# M4 Release Implementation Plan

> For agentic workers: executed subagent-driven. Each task runs in a fresh session. Two critical
> reviewers and an arbiter review each task.

**Start (user decision U-GO-M4, 2026-10-25, binding):** M4 execution, Task 0 included, starts as
soon as M3.1 is complete, without waiting for the user. This replaces the earlier rule that M4
starts only after the user has run and reviewed M1b..M3. The M3 release still waits for the user's
E6 checklist. Every Task 12 publication step stays its own `STOP: ask the user` step, unchanged.

**Task order (user decision U-M4ORDER, 2026-10-25, binding):** packaging (Task 8) moves after
release preparation, and release preparation (Task 10) is split into three stages. The order is
0 → 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5a → 5b → 6 → 7a → 7b → P (only when Task 0 records an M3.1 E7/R7 miss) → 9 →
10-alpha → 10-beta → 10-final → 8 → 11 → 12 (gated). The task numbers are kept, so Task 8 runs
after Task 10-final; the task texts below stand in this order (Task 9, Task 10 with its three
stages, Task 8, Task 11, Task 12; the conditional Task P stays at the end).
- **10-alpha:** every code repository at version `1.0.0-alpha`; the rawreel README and the spec
  cover the lossless FFmpeg sources (U-FF) and `rawreel.json` (U-IO2-config); run instructions for
  a development build. It is not a review stop: execution goes on to 10-beta.
- **10-beta:** version `1.0.0-beta`; the full docs, the [U4] texts and the licence notes; a draft
  of the release notes; fixes for any feedback on the alpha.
- **10-final:** version `1.0.0`; the final release notes; the ledger.
- Task 8 then packages version 1.0.0, Task 11 verifies the release candidate, and Task 12 stays
  gated.
- In the texts of Tasks 0–7b and in the change-log sections, "Task 10" means these stages: the
  version change and the FFmpeg-source and `rawreel.json` docs are 10-alpha; the other docs, the
  [U4] texts, the licence notes and the release-notes draft are 10-beta; version 1.0.0, the final
  release notes and the ledger are 10-final.

**Dispatch:** every task dispatch includes this header, "Global Constraints" and "Conventions for
every task" together with the task text. Tasks refer to the inner loop, the Per-task checks, the
Checkpoint checks and the privacy lint by these names (U-PROC).

**Goal (exit criteria E1–E6, design v2 §1):** rawreel v1.0. FFmpeg container files (any container
FFmpeg demuxes, lossless video codecs only: user decision U-FF) and `lavfi:`
generators open as sources through FFmpeg loaded at run time (never linked), checked against
`ffmpeg` itself (`framemd5`, `rawvideo`, `extractplanes`, swscale display colour); `rawreel-cli` and
`rawreel-gui` use them end to end; rawreel is packaged as a Windows zip (with or without the
bundled LGPL FFmpeg, `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`; with the third-party sources archive when bundled),
verified from a local dry run; and v1.0.0 is published through the gated release (Task 12, only
after the user's explicit go-aheads). v1.0 supports Windows only (user decision U-WIN); the code
stays portable, and the Linux package, Linux tests and Linux release work are v1.x.
The end of Task 11 is "v1.0.0 release candidate"; only the end of Task 12 is "v1.0 published".

**Architecture:**
- **rawreel-workspace:** the superbuild pins the custom_ffmpeg v1.0.0 LGPL shared archives
  (`LINKAGE runtime PREBUILT`) and the LAME 3.100 source tarball (when the shipped build contains
  LAME, U-FF); the licence gate allows `LGPL-2.0-or-later` and `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` for
  run-time-loaded libraries only, each only while a pinned archive needs it (Task 0); `SHIPPED`
  entries produce the shipped `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt`; the superbuild option `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`
  (default `ON`) decides whether FFmpeg ships; the prefix gets a small CMake package
  `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk` that names FFmpeg's headers, libraries and programs.
- **dvv-platform:** `LibrarySearch::DllDirectory` (absolute path; dependencies from the library's
  own folder). **dvv-media:** `formatAlias` (`yuvj*` names) and the public colour-query header
  `uri_colour.h`.
- **dvv-components (new repository):** `dvv_add_component()` and the conformance test; the FFmpeg
  run-time binding `dvv_ffmpeg` (one `extern "C"` wrapper header, a function table resolved through
  `DynamicLibrary`, six libraries checked: versions, LGPL licence, no `--enable-gpl`/`--enable-nonfree`);
  the component `ffmpeg` with the source providers `ffmpeg` (containers; a lossless codec allowlist
  checked at open, U-FF; decode on one worker thread per source) and `lavfi` (filter-graph
  generators); the oracle suite against the prefix's `ffmpeg`.
- **rawreel:** a shared startup (FFmpeg load → capabilities → component registry) for CLI and GUI;
  `rawreel-cli --version` and About with the FFmpeg status line; the Windows minidump; packaging,
  the third-party sources archive and `release.yml` (workflow_dispatch, dry run first).

**Tech Stack:** C++23 (MSVC v145; Clang 19 with libstdc++ 14 for the lint and the Linux sanity
build only), CMake 4.3 (presets, FetchContent, ExternalProject superbuild, CPack ZIP), doctest, Vulkan 1.4 (dvv-gpu), wxWidgets 3.3.4 (static),
FFmpeg n8.1.3 from the custom_ffmpeg v1.0.0 `lgpl` shared release (headers at build time, six
shared libraries loaded at run time, `ffmpeg`/`ffprobe` as test oracle), GitHub Actions
(`dvv-ci.yml`, `release.yml`, `gh attestation`).

**Design (binding):** the approved M4 design v2 (the U-SCOPE re-scope of the approved M4 design)
with the v2 arbiter's 32 fixes and the arbiter's verification; where design v2 says "as v1", the v1
text it names is binding. Its decisions log comes with it (the "M4-design", "M4-rescope" and
"M4-v2-arbiter" lines). Analysis and metrics of every kind are v1.x (design v2 §13): no task builds
them or adds hooks for them. User decision U-FF (2026-10-25, binding) overrides the design where
they differ: the design's MPEG-2 (`.m2v`) fixtures are replaced by lossless ones, and the U4
patent note and the BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance depend on Task 0's codec-scope record.

## Global Constraints
- **Codec scope (user decision U-FF, binding):**
  - The `ffmpeg` source provider accepts any container FFmpeg can demux, including AVI, MKV, MOV,
    MP4 and NUT.
  - It decodes only lossless video codecs. The allowlist is checked at open time, on the FFmpeg
    codec name of the chosen stream: `rawvideo`, `v210`, `v410`, `r210`, `ffv1`, `huffyuv`,
    `ffvhuff`, `utvideo`, `magicyuv`, `png`, `qtrle`. Any other codec is refused with `Unsupported`
    "codec <name> is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)".
  - MPEG-family codecs (MPEG-1/2/4, H.264, HEVC) and other lossy codecs are out of scope for
    M4/v1.0. No fixture, test or document of M4 relies on decoding one; a lossy clip appears only
    to check the refusal.
  - No bypass through `lavfi:` (controller decision on the U-FF review): the `lavfi` provider
    refuses any graph that uses `movie`, `amovie` or another filter that opens a file or URL
    (`LAVFI_FILE_FILTERS`, Task 5b) with `Unsupported` "lavfi: movie/amovie are not supported;
    open the file directly". So the [U4] sentence "rawreel itself opens only lossless video
    codecs" is true.
  - The runtime allowlist applies whatever FFmpeg build is bundled. Task 0 records whether the
    pinned build contains patent-encumbered decoders and SVT-AV1, and whether a lossless-only build
    can be pinned instead. No such decoders in the bundled build → the U4 patent note is dropped
    (Tasks 0, 10-beta, 12). No SVT-AV1 in any pinned archive → the BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance
    is dropped (Tasks 1, 10-beta).
- **Platform scope (user decision U-WIN, binding):** v1.0 supports Windows only; Linux may follow
  in v1.x.
  - The code stays portable in every step: Linux branches (`*_posix`, X11/GTK paths, `#if` blocks,
    CMake `else()` branches) are written, kept compiling and kept in the CMake, but not run. No
    Windows-only shortcut in code that is meant to be portable.
  - No Linux test runs: no WSL ctest or workflow preset, no lavapipe, no WSLg/Xvfb GUI run, no
    Linux ASan/TSan. Risk accepted: Windows has no TSan, so data races are found only by review
    and Windows ASan until Linux returns.
  - The **Linux sanity build** is the checkpoint build (U-PROC, U-PROC-tidy): the WSL
    `ninja-clang` configure + build of each changed repository in the Checkpoint checks, plus the
    workspace build in the final task (Task 11). No task runs a Linux build of its own. Our
    portability bugs it finds are fixed; toolchain or third-party failures are recorded and
    reported, not fixed.
  - Linux release artefacts are v1.x: no Linux package, Linux FFmpeg bundling, Linux job in
    `release.yml`, Linux package smoke or glibc baseline. The Linux custom_ffmpeg pin stays (Task
    1): the clang prefix needs FFmpeg's headers for the checkpoint builds.
  - Existing green CI Linux jobs are left as they are unless they break; new CI work adds no Linux
    test job.
- **Toolchains:**
  - C++23.
  - MSVC v145 (`vs2026`, `vs2026-asan`): every build and test.
  - Clang 19 with libstdc++ 14 in WSL Ubuntu 24.04: clang-format, and `ninja-clang` only for the
    checkpoint build (clang-tidy's compile database and the Linux sanity build); no tests.
  - Warnings are errors for our code. Third-party sources compiled into a dvv target never fail the
    build on warnings. Third-party headers are always SYSTEM.
- **Test cycles (U-TEST, user decision 2026-10-24, binding).** Three cycles; this rule overrides
  every task text below.
  - **Sanity cycle:** after any change and before every commit (WIP and `fix:` commits too), run
    `pwsh rawreel-workspace/tools/sanity.ps1 [-Repo <name>,...] [-NoGui]` from the workspace root:
    an incremental `vs2026` build and `ctest --preset vs2026-sanity` (label `sanity`) per
    repository, one PASS/FAIL line each, exit 1 on any failure. It is not a substitute for the
    Per-task or Checkpoint checks.
  - **Per-task cycle:** every per-task Debug run (the Per-task checks, inner loops, task steps) uses
    `ctest --preset vs2026-small` (every test except label `package`). Where a task text still says
    `ctest --preset vs2026` or `ctest --preset vs2026 -E "\.package$"` without `-R`, read
    `ctest --preset vs2026-small`; targeted `-R` runs (race checks ×10) stay as written.
  - **Full cycle:** package and install tests (label `package`, e.g. `dvv_media.package`) run only
    at the checkpoints and at the milestone end (`ctest --preset vs2026` / the workspace workflows,
    `package` included, and `vs2026-release`).
- **Test failures (U-BATCH, user decision 2026-10-24, binding).** (1) Run the complete test set of
  the stage (per-task or checkpoint) without stopping at the first failure; a stage that cannot run,
  e.g. after a build failure, is recorded and the other stages still run. (2) Collect every failure
  and error in one list in the report. (3) Review the list together: merge duplicates, group by
  root cause and plan one combined set of fixes. (4) One agent applies all the fixes. (5) Only then
  re-run the complete set once. Never fix and re-run after each single failure. Applies to
  implementers, checkpoints and fix waves; "fixed … and the check repeated" in a task text means
  this procedure.
- **Workspace root:** never create files in the workspace root folder itself (only the
  repositories, `_build` and `_third_party` live there). Scratch output goes only under the scratch
  folder the brief names (or the `$env:TEMP` folder a task names). Beware PowerShell redirects such
  as `> name` or `Out-File name` with a relative path.
- **Git:**
  - Branch `m4` in every existing repository: `rawreel-workspace`, `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`,
    `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media` and `rawreel`, each created by Task 0 from its latest
    milestone head (expected: `m2` for dvv-base, dvv-registry and dvv-platform, with M3 beta's
    test-label commit,
    or `m3` where M3.1 created one, e.g. M3.1 Task B's F1 branch in dvv-platform; `m3` for the
    other four). Every repository changes in M4 (at the latest, the version in
    Task 10-alpha).
  - The new repository `dvv-components` is created by Task 3 with `git init -b m4`, local only.
  - Nothing is pushed, no remote is added and no GitHub repository is created before Task 12's
    explicit user go-ahead (Gate A, then Gate B; user decision [U5]). Even then, every push, tag
    and GitHub write (repository create, rename, archive, topics, workflow run, release create or
    delete) is its own `STOP: ask the user` step, answered with an explicit yes at that time.
  - Commits use the noreply identity that bootstrap copies and end with the trailer
    `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Privacy:** no machine paths, user names, host names or personal e-mails in committed files or
  messages, and none in the "Changes during execution" records (use paths relative to the workspace
  root). Run the privacy lint in commits mode before finishing a task.
- **Shells:**
  - Windows: the PowerShell tool only. Run git from PowerShell; never run git inside WSL (except
    the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only` of the format and tidy commands).
  - Linux (clang-format, the checkpoint build, `bootstrap.sh` only):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && …'` from the workspace root.
  - WSL has no passwordless sudo; nothing is installed in it. No display or GUI run in WSL.
- **GPU and display tests:**
  - Exit 77 means skip, unless `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1`.
  - NVIDIA on Windows with `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync` and `DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED=1`. Zero
    validation messages. GPU tests must run, not skip.
- **FFmpeg tests:**
  - FFmpeg-dependent tests exit 77 when the capability is missing, unless `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1`.
    Every Windows test run (Per-task and checkpoint) sets `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` (the "GPU and
    FFmpeg env" below), so FFmpeg tests must run, not skip.
  - Deliberate no-FFmpeg CTest entries set `ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=unset:"`
    and their own `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR`; the other tests point `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR` at the prefix's FFmpeg
    runtime directory (`DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR`; nothing is copied or committed).
  - FFmpeg paths in CMake come only from Task 1's package: `find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk CONFIG
    REQUIRED)` gives `DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM`, `DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR`, `DVV_FFMPEG_COMMAND` (on Linux
    with `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`) and the rest of Task 1's list. dvv-components calls it in its top-level
    `CMakeLists.txt` (Task 4), rawreel in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (Task 7a). No task guesses the
    archive layout with `find_program` or a `PATH_SUFFIXES`.
  - Tests with the label `oracle` (Task 6) run only in the non-sanitizer preset `vs2026` (and in
    the non-sanitizer CI jobs).
- **FFmpeg boundary (design D3):** FFmpeg is never linked. dvv-components compiles against the
  headers of the pinned custom_ffmpeg archive through one `extern "C"` wrapper header and resolves
  every function through `DynamicLibrary`. No FFmpeg source is copied into any repository; no
  system FFmpeg is used; no FFmpeg header is included anywhere else.
- **Licence gate and bundling:** `LGPL-2.0-or-later` and `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` are allowed for
  `runtime` and `tool` linkage only (controller decisions; BSD-3-Clause-Clear is flagged "USER MAY
  WANT TO REVISIT"). Each allowance is added only while a pinned custom_ffmpeg archive contains the
  component that needs it (LAME, SVT-AV1; Task 0's record, U-FF). `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` (superbuild
  option, default `ON`) is used for all work and dry runs; user decision [U4] never blocks a task
  (asked at Task 0, needed at the latest at Gate A). [U5] is asked only at Task 12.
- **Long runs:** a tool call stops after 10 minutes, so bootstraps, workflow presets, the
  checkpoint build and long test runs use `run_in_background`. The WSL checkpoint build may run in
  parallel with a Windows run. The Per-task checks run once, on a task's final commit.
- **Per-task checks (U-PROC, binding).** Every task runs them once, on its final commit, in each
  repository it changed (`<repo>`). Nothing else runs per task: no `vs2026` Release, no
  `vs2026-asan`, no clang-tidy, no WSL configure or build, no Linux sanity build. Failures are
  handled per U-BATCH (below):
  fixed together in a new commit, then the complete check runs once more. `<GPU and FFmpeg env>` is
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
  1. Windows Debug build and every test except label `package` (U-TEST), GPU and FFmpeg tests on
     NVIDIA (`run_in_background: true`):
     `<GPU and FFmpeg env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026-small --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
     Expected: `100% tests passed`, no `Skipped` line for a `gpu` or `ffmpeg` test, 0 validation
     messages.
  2. Debug race check, only in a risky task that names it (in M4: Task P): its concurrency tests
     ×10 in Debug,
     `<GPU and FFmpeg env>; Push-Location <repo>; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "<regex>" --repeat until-fail:10 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`.
  3. clang-format on the files the task changed (WSL, no build). `<base>` is the repository's head
     before the task's first commit (record `git -C <repo> rev-parse HEAD` when the task starts):
     `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`.
     `git diff <base>` compares the working tree (run it after `git add -A`), so it works before and
     after the commit. In a repository the task creates, `git ls-files -z "*.h" "*.cpp"` replaces it.
     A repository without `.clang-format` (rawreel-workspace) has no format check. Expected
     `FORMAT-OK`; a reported file is fixed with `clang-format -i` in WSL.
  4. Privacy lint, commits mode:
     `cmake -DREPO=<repo> -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
     Expected `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.
- **Checkpoints (U-PROC):** Task 3, Task 5b, Task 7b, Task 9, Task 11 (final). The conditional Task P
  (after 7b) falls under the Task 9 checkpoint. Task 12 is the gated publication: it adds no code
  and runs after the final checkpoint, so it has no checkpoint of its own.
  After U-M4ORDER the Task 9 checkpoint covers Task P (when it ran) and Task 9 only. Task 8's
  packaging work, which the Task 9 checkpoint covered before, is now covered by the Task 11 final
  checkpoint, which runs directly after Task 8 (Release with `rawreel.packaging_scripts`, ASan,
  clang-tidy and the Linux sanity build of `packaging.cmake`). Tasks 10-alpha, 10-beta and
  10-final fall under the Task 11 checkpoint too, as Task 10 did.
- **Checkpoint checks (U-PROC, U-PROC-tidy, binding).** A checkpoint task runs them after its own
  steps (its Per-task checks included), on its final commits, in every repository changed since the
  previous checkpoint (`<repo>`). `<cp-base>` is the repository's head at the previous checkpoint
  (the `m4` branch start for the first one): the controller records the heads in its ledger at each
  checkpoint and names them in the brief; a repository created since then uses `git ls-files` in
  place of the `git diff`. The two Windows runs go in one background call per repository (never two
  package tests or GUI selftests at once); the WSL build may run in parallel.
  1. Windows Release:
     `<GPU and FFmpeg env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; ctest --preset vs2026-release --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
  2. Windows ASan, then the ASan race check ×10 of each risky task since the previous checkpoint:
     `<GPU and FFmpeg env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|AddressSanitizer'; Pop-Location` and
     `<GPU and FFmpeg env>; Push-Location <repo>; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R "<regex>" --repeat until-fail:10 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`.
     Expected: all passed (the `oracle` tests only in `vs2026`), no sanitizer report.
  3. clang-tidy on the C++ sources changed since `<cp-base>`, after one WSL `ninja-clang` configure +
     build with no tests (`run_in_background: true`). This build is also the Linux sanity build:
     `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <cp-base> HEAD -- <tidy pathspecs> | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/<repo>/ninja-clang --quiet && echo TIDY-OK'`,
     where `<tidy pathspecs>` is `":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp"`,
     and for **dvv-components** its own list (the files of its compile database):
     `":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/components/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/ffmpeg/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/oracle/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp"`.
     Expected `TIDY-OK`. A compile or link error in our code is a portability bug and is fixed; a
     toolchain or third-party failure is recorded in the task's report (and the plan file's
     `## Changes during execution`) and not fixed.
  A checkpoint failure is fixed (a `fix:` commit, then the Per-task checks and the failed checkpoint
  check again) before the next task starts. The final task's checkpoint covers everything: every
  repository of the milestone, clang-tidy on all sources (`git ls-files -z` with the same pathspecs
  replaces the `git diff`), and the ASan race checks of every risky task of the milestone.
- **Spec §9.4** applies to every managed type (design v2 §6).
- **Task order and parallelism (binding):** one task at a time per repository, working tree and
  branch. Two tasks may run at the same time only when they touch disjoint repositories, neither
  builds the other's repositories (the dvv repositories build their dependencies in-tree from the
  sibling folders), and neither rewrites the third-party prefix the other builds against. Every M4
  task fails at least one of these tests against its neighbours, so the tasks run one after the
  other: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5a → 5b → 6 → 7a → 7b → P (only when Task 0 records an M3.1 E7/R7
  miss) → 9 → 10-alpha → 10-beta → 10-final → 8 → 11 → 12 (gated) (user decision U-M4ORDER).
  Dependencies: Task 9 needs Tasks 3, 6 and 7a (and P when it ran); it defines `release.yml`
  against Task 8's planned script interface (Task 8 Produces) and does not run it. 10-alpha needs
  Tasks 1–9; 10-beta needs 10-alpha; 10-final needs 10-beta. Task 8 needs 10-final (it packages
  version 1.0.0 with the final README and notes) and Task 9's `release.yml`, whose Windows-job
  commands it runs first. Task 11 needs Task 8; Task 12 needs Task 11. Within a task, the WSL
  checkpoint build may run in parallel with a Windows run of the same step (background calls).
- **Workspace workflow groups:** G1 Tasks 0–3 (run in Task 3), G2 Tasks 4–7b (run in Task 7b), G3
  Tasks P (when it runs), 9, 10-alpha, 10-beta, 10-final, 8 and 11 (run in Task 11); then the gated
  Task 12.
- **Plan file:** this plan lives in
  `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m4-release.md` (Task 0 adds it). Facts a task
  measures go to its `## Changes during execution` section.
- **Earlier milestones:** M4 builds on M1b, M2 and M3 as planned; their code is not visible while
  this plan is written. Each task's Step 1 compares the names it consumes with the real code. A name
  that differs is adapted in this task (not in the earlier repository's API) and noted in the commit
  message body.

## Conventions for every task

Conventions used by every task below (repeated here so each task text can point at them by name;
each task also repeats the commands it needs):
- Run every command from the workspace root (the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`). Use the
  PowerShell tool on Windows. Linux runs (clang-format, the checkpoint build, `bootstrap.sh`) as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. Git runs only from PowerShell, except the read-only `git ls-files` and
  `git diff --name-only` inside the WSL format and tidy commands.
- **GPU and FFmpeg env** (in the same tool call as the run). Windows:
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
- **Inner loop** (Windows, foreground): `<GPU and FFmpeg env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026-small --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
- **Sanity cycle** (U-TEST): after any change and before every commit,
  `pwsh rawreel-workspace/tools/sanity.ps1 [-Repo <name>,...] [-NoGui]`
  from the workspace root (PASS/FAIL per repository). dvv-components joins it once Task 3 adds it to
  `repos.txt`; Task 3 labels its fast unit test `sanity` and its package test `package`, and every
  new package or install test of M4 gets the label `package` (so `vs2026-small` leaves it out).
- **Test cycles and failures** (Global Constraints, U-TEST and U-BATCH): per-task Debug runs use
  `ctest --preset vs2026-small`; package tests run only at the checkpoints and in Tasks 10-alpha,
  10-beta, 10-final and 11;
  failures are collected for the whole stage, fixed together, and the complete set re-run once.
- **Workspace root:** never create files in the workspace root folder itself; scratch output only
  under the folder the brief or task names.
- **Per-task checks** (U-PROC; once, on the task's final commit; Global Constraints has the
  commands): the Windows Debug build and every test except label `package` (`vs2026-small`, U-TEST;
  GPU and FFmpeg env), the Debug race
  check in a risky task, clang-format on the changed files (`FORMAT-OK`; new files must be
  `git add`ed first) and the privacy lint. No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL configure or
  build, no Linux test run.
- **Checkpoint checks** (U-PROC, U-PROC-tidy; only in the tasks of the Checkpoints list; Global
  Constraints has the commands and each checkpoint task repeats them): vs2026 Release, the
  vs2026-asan workflow with the ASan race checks of the risky tasks since the last checkpoint, and
  clang-tidy on the files changed since the last checkpoint after one WSL `ninja-clang` configure +
  build with no tests (`TIDY-OK`; also the Linux sanity build). **dvv-components** uses its own
  clang-tidy file list, the files of its compile database (`tests/compile_fail`, `tests/package` and
  `tests/cmake` are not in it):
  `":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/components/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/ffmpeg/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/oracle/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp"`.
  A compile or link error in our code is a portability bug: fix it (a `fix: …` commit with the
  trailer) before the next task. A toolchain or third-party failure is recorded in the task's report
  (and the plan file's `## Changes during execution` when the task writes one) and not fixed.
- **Privacy lint** after every commit:
  `cmake -DREPO=<repo> -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.
- Every commit message ends with the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
  Nothing is pushed; no remote is added; no GitHub repository is created.

---

# M4 plan, part 1: Tasks 0-4

### Task 0: Branches, pins, checks, the U4 question

**Goal:** Start M4 on branch `m4` in the seven existing repositories, taken from their finished
milestone heads, put this plan into rawreel-workspace, and record every fact the later tasks pin or
rely on: the custom_ffmpeg v1.0.0 archives (SHA256, attestation, layout, libraries, licence,
headers, dependencies, linker version; for the Linux archive only what its pin needs), the LAME
3.100 tarball SHA256, the codec scope of the pinned build (user decision U-FF: patent-encumbered
decoders, SVT-AV1, whether a lossless-only build can be pinned instead), the lossless fixture facts
(the `Sequential` fixture's packets, probe scores, the lossy encoder for the refusal clip), CPack's
ZIP generator, the wx user-path check, the M3.1 records that decide Task P, and the [U4] question
(asked, never waited for).

**Files:** only the M4 plan file `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m4-release.md`
(created in Step 3; the results go to its `## Changes during execution` section in Step 11).
Downloads go to `_build/m4-task0/` (outside every repository; deleted by nobody, reused by Task 1).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M3 complete (its final Task 12 committed) and M3.1 complete (U-GO-M4: M4 starts when
  M3.1 is done; its records are in the M3 plan file). The seven repositories sit side by side with
  clean trees, each on its latest milestone branch. The M3 plan file
  `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-m3-player.md` with its
  `## Changes during execution` section (M3.1 Task 11b (and Task C) recorded the performance outcome
  and any E7/R7 miss there). The bootstrapped prefixes `_third_party/msvc` and `_third_party/clang`.
  GitHub CLI `gh` (read-only use: `gh release view/download`, `gh attestation verify`).
- Produces:
  - branch `m4` in `rawreel-workspace`, `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`,
    `dvv-media` and `rawreel`;
  - the M4 plan file in rawreel-workspace;
  - recorded facts (each used later):
    - custom_ffmpeg asset names, URLs and SHA256 for Windows and Linux (→ Task 1 `deps/versions.cmake`);
    - the attestation result: signer workflow, source repository, `sourceRepositoryDigest` and ref
      (→ Task 9 `release.yml`, the sources archive checkout);
    - archive layout: the folder holding `bin/`, `include/`, `lib/`, `share/` and `build-info.txt`,
      and the exact licence file paths relative to the extracted archive (→ Task 1 `LICENSE_FILES`,
      Task 8 staging);
    - the six library file names and majors (avutil, avcodec, avformat, avfilter, swscale,
      swresample), whether avdevice/postproc files exist, `ffmpeg -version` (version string,
      configuration without `--enable-gpl`/`--enable-nonfree`), `ffmpeg -L` licence lines (→ Tasks
      1, 4, 7a, 8);
    - the demuxers, muxers, encoders and filters the fixtures need are present (→ Tasks 5a, 5b, 6,
      7a);
    - `dumpbin /dependents` and the linker version of each DLL (→ R6, Task 8 VC runtime check);
    - the LAME 3.100 tarball URL and SHA256 (→ Task 1);
    - codec scope (U-FF, → Tasks 1, 5a, 10, 12): the patent-encumbered decoders of the Windows
      build (H.264, HEVC, AAC, AV1, MPEG-1/2/4: a list or "none"); SVT-AV1 in the Windows and in
      the Linux archive (yes/no each); LAME in the Windows and in the Linux archive (yes/no each);
      the lossless-only build: "pinned <asset>" or "none available"; the allowlist decoders
      present; the consequences: "U4 patent note: kept / dropped", "BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance:
      kept / dropped", "LAME allowance: kept / dropped", "LAME: present / absent";
    - the lossless fixture facts (U-FF, → Tasks 5a, 7a, 11): the `Sequential` fixture choice
      (`seq.avi`, `seq.nut` or `seq.mkv`) with its `ffprobe` packet lines and its `framemd5` line
      count; whether AVI and NUT packets carry `pts`; the 4096-byte probe scores of the AVI, MKV,
      MOV and NUT fixtures; the lossy encoder for the refusal clip (`mpeg4` present or not);
    - CPack's `ZIP` generator present (→ Task 8);
    - whether the wx libraries of the local MSVC prefix contain user-home paths (→ Task 8 dry run);
    - M3.1 run: "yes" or "no" (an M3.1 "Task 11b: perf" record exists or not; → Task 10 spec §12
      row, Task 11 E5 item 18);
    - Task P: "not needed" or "needed: <item>" (→ controller);
    - [U4]: "asked, pending (default `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=ON`)" or the user's answer (→ Tasks 8, 12);
    - the baseline workspace test count.
- Constraint for the whole plan: nothing is pushed, no GitHub repository is created, no remote is
  added.

**Behaviour:**
- If a repository is missing, has uncommitted changes, or its checked-out branch is not its latest
  milestone branch (the highest of `m3`, `m2`, `m1b` that exists in it), stop and report it to the
  controller. Never create `m4` from anything else.
- If an asset is missing, a SHA256 differs from the release's `SHA256SUMS`, the attestation does
  not verify, any of the six libraries is missing, `ffmpeg -version` shows `--enable-gpl` or
  `--enable-nonfree`, or the licence files name a licence outside the SPDX expression of Task 1
  (`LGPL-2.1-or-later AND LGPL-2.0-or-later AND BSD-3-Clause AND BSD-2-Clause AND
  BSD-3-Clause-Clear AND MIT AND Zlib`), stop and report it: Task 1 cannot pin the archive.
- **Task P** is needed when the M3.1 record names an E7/R7 miss (the 4K 10-bit 60 fps `perf` run of
  M3.1 Task 11b) that M3.1 did not fix. Read it; do not re-run `perf`. Report the item to the controller.
- **[U4]** is put to the controller for the user in the task report, once, without waiting (text in
  Step 10). Nothing in M4 waits for it.
- **Codec scope (U-FF; Step 7).** The runtime allowlist (Global Constraints) applies in every case;
  this step only decides what is pinned and what the documents say:
  - A lossless-only build can be pinned when the custom_ffmpeg v1.0.0 release (or a later release of
    the same FFmpeg n8.1.3, attested by the same workflow) has an LGPL shared asset for Windows
    whose video decoders are all on the allowlist (Step 7 Part A's "video decoders outside the
    allowlist: none"; this also rules out VVC, VC-1, H.263, MJPEG, ProRes and the like, which the
    patent regex does not name), which holds every allowlist decoder, and which passes
    every check of Steps 4–5 (six libraries, LGPL, no `--enable-gpl`/`--enable-nonfree`, no
    `MISSING` fixture component). Then it is pinned instead (its asset, SHA256 and licence files
    go to Task 1); the Linux pin follows the same variant when it exists for Linux, else stays the
    full build (headers only, never shipped in v1.0). Otherwise record "none available": M4 does
    not build or publish a custom_ffmpeg variant (custom_ffmpeg is a separate project; the
    controller reports it as an open point for the user).
  - The bundled (Windows) build has no patent-encumbered decoder and no video decoder outside the
    allowlist → "U4 patent note: dropped": the
    [U4] question (Step 10), the README accept text and release notes (Task 10) and the Gate A
    [U4] text (Task 12) leave the patent sentence out. Otherwise "kept".
  - No pinned archive (Windows or Linux) contains SVT-AV1 → "BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance:
    dropped": Task 1 leaves `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` out of the gate, the SPDX expression and the
    tests, and Task 10 out of the spec. Otherwise "kept".
  - LAME has two facts (review fix: the gate covers every pinned archive, the source pin only the
    shipped one):
    - "LAME allowance: kept / dropped": dropped only when no pinned archive (Windows or Linux)
      contains LAME; then Task 1 leaves `LGPL-2.0-or-later` out of the gate. A Linux full pin with
      LAME keeps it.
    - "LAME: present / absent" (the shipped Windows build): absent → Task 1 adds no LAME source pin
      and no `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_*`, and Tasks 8–10 leave LAME out of the sources archive, its smoke
      step 10 and the texts. Otherwise "present".
  - Expected with the full v1.0.0 `shared-lgpl` build: patent-encumbered decoders present (H.264,
    HEVC, AAC, AV1, MPEG-1/2/4), SVT-AV1 present, LAME present, so all four are "kept"/"present".
- Expected values (for comparison only; record what you see): FFmpeg `n8.1.3`; majors avutil 60,
  avcodec 62, avformat 62, avfilter 11, swscale 9, swresample 6 (FFmpeg 8.x); Windows names
  `avutil-60.dll` …, Linux `libavutil.so.60` …; the asset names
  `custom_ffmpeg-1.0.0-windows-x64-shared-lgpl.zip` and `custom_ffmpeg-1.0.0-linux-x64-shared-lgpl.tar.xz`;
  the LAME 3.100 tarball SHA256 `ddfe36cab873794038ae2c1210557ad34857a4b6bdc515785d1da9e175b1da1e`.
  If the majors differ, later tasks use the recorded ones.

**Code** (helpers for Steps 5 and 9; paste them first in the same tool call):

```powershell
# dumpbin of the newest MSVC toolset (no machine path is printed or recorded).
$vs = & "${env:ProgramFiles(x86)}/Microsoft Visual Studio/Installer/vswhere.exe" -latest -property installationPath
$dumpbin = (Get-ChildItem "$vs/VC/Tools/MSVC/*/bin/Hostx64/x64/dumpbin.exe" | Sort-Object FullName | Select-Object -Last 1).FullName
# A path relative to the Task 0 folder, with forward slashes.
function Rel([string] $path) { [IO.Path]::GetRelativePath((Resolve-Path '_build/m4-task0'), $path).Replace('\', '/') }
```

**Tests first:** none (no code). The checks below are the test.

**Steps:**

```powershell
Get-Content rawreel-workspace/repos.txt
Test-Path dvv-components
```

dvv-platform, with M3 beta's test-label commit, or `m3` where M3.1 created one; `m3` for the
others). `repos.txt` lists `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`,
`dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media`, `rawreel` (comment lines aside). `Test-Path dvv-components` is `False`.

Copy the approved M4 plan (the assembled plan file the controller names in the dispatch) to

`## Changes during execution` section, and commit it:

```powershell
Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
```

- [ ] **Step 4: custom_ffmpeg assets, SHA256 and attestation**

```powershell
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force _build/m4-task0 | Out-Null
gh release view v1.0.0 -R BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg --json assets --jq '.assets[].name'
gh release download v1.0.0 -R BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg -D _build/m4-task0 --clobber -p '*windows-x64-shared-lgpl*' -p '*linux-x64-shared-lgpl*' -p 'SHA256SUMS*'
Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0 -File -Filter 'custom_ffmpeg-*' | ForEach-Object { "$((Get-FileHash $_.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant())  $($_.Name)" }
Get-Content _build/m4-task0/SHA256SUMS* | Select-String 'shared-lgpl'
foreach ($f in Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0 -File -Filter 'custom_ffmpeg-*') {
    "== $($f.Name)"
    $json = gh attestation verify $f.FullName -R BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg --format json
    "exit=$LASTEXITCODE"
    ($json | ConvertFrom-Json) | ForEach-Object { $_.verificationResult.signature.certificate } |
        Select-Object sourceRepositoryURI, sourceRepositoryDigest, sourceRepositoryRef, buildSignerURI | Format-List
}
```

Expected: the asset list contains the Windows `.zip` and the Linux `.tar.xz` of the `shared-lgpl`
build (and `SHA256SUMS`); each computed SHA256 equals its `SHA256SUMS` line; `exit=0` twice; the
certificate shows `https://github.com/BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg`, a 40-hex digest, the ref
`refs/tags/v1.0.0` and the release workflow. If the certificate properties have other names, print
`($json | ConvertFrom-Json) | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8` and record the source repository, digest,
ref and signer from it.

- [ ] **Step 5: Windows archive facts** (one call: paste the **Code** helpers first)

```powershell
$zip = Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0 -File -Filter '*windows-x64-shared-lgpl.zip' | Select-Object -First 1
Remove-Item -Recurse -Force _build/m4-task0/win -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Expand-Archive -Path $zip.FullName -DestinationPath _build/m4-task0/win
Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -Depth 3 -Directory | ForEach-Object { Rel $_.FullName }
Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -File | Where-Object { $_.Extension -in '.dll','.exe','.txt' -or $_.FullName -match '[\\/]licenses?[\\/]' } | ForEach-Object { Rel $_.FullName }
Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -Filter 'version*.h' | Select-String -Pattern '#define LIB(AVUTIL|AVCODEC|AVFORMAT|AVFILTER|SWSCALE|SWRESAMPLE)_VERSION_(MAJOR|MINOR|MICRO)\s' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() }
foreach ($h in 'libavutil/avutil.h','libavutil/frame.h','libavutil/pixdesc.h','libavcodec/avcodec.h','libavformat/avformat.h','libavfilter/avfilter.h','libavfilter/buffersink.h','libswscale/swscale.h','libswresample/swresample.h') { "$h " + [bool](Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -File | Where-Object { $_.FullName.Replace('\','/') -like "*/include/$h" }) }
$ff = (Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -Filter ffmpeg.exe | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
& $ff -hide_banner -version
& $ff -hide_banner -L | Select-Object -First 8
# The demuxers, muxers, encoders and filters the fixtures of Tasks 5a, 5b, 6 and 7a use.
$need = [ordered]@{ '-formats' = 'lavfi','v210','concat','rawvideo','matroska','avi','mov','mp4','nut','framemd5'; '-encoders' = 'ffv1','ffvhuff','huffyuv','utvideo','magicyuv','png','v210','pcm_s16le','rawvideo'; '-filters' = 'testsrc2','extractplanes','scale','format','setparams','setpts','select','sine','concat' }
foreach ($k in $need.Keys) { $out = (& $ff -hide_banner $k 2>&1) -join "`n"; foreach ($n in $need[$k]) { if ($out -notmatch "(?m)^\s*\S+\s+([^\s,]+,)*$([regex]::Escape($n))[\s,]") { "MISSING $k $n" } } }
Get-Content (Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -Filter build-info.txt | Select-Object -First 1).FullName | Select-Object -First 40
foreach ($d in Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -Filter *.dll) {
    "== $($d.Name)"
    & $dumpbin /nologo /dependents $d.FullName | Select-String '\.dll\s*$' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() } | Where-Object { $_ -notlike 'Dump of file*' }
    & $dumpbin /nologo /headers $d.FullName | Select-String 'linker version' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() }
}
```

Expected: one folder holds `bin/`, `include/`, `lib/` and `share/` (record it, relative to the
extracted archive; it may be the archive root); `bin/` holds `avutil-60.dll`, `avcodec-62.dll`,
`avformat-62.dll`, `avfilter-11.dll`, `swscale-9.dll`, `swresample-6.dll` (and possibly
`avdevice-*.dll`; never `postproc-*.dll`), `ffmpeg.exe` and `ffprobe.exe`; every listed header is
`True`; the version line reads `ffmpeg version n8.1.3 …`; `configuration:` has neither
`--enable-gpl` nor `--enable-nonfree`; `-L` names the GNU Lesser General Public License; no
`MISSING` line (a missing demuxer, muxer, encoder or filter would surface tasks later as an
unexplained fixture failure: stop and report it); each DLL
lists only FFmpeg siblings and system DLLs (KERNEL32, the UCRT `api-ms-win-*`, and the VC runtime
`VCRUNTIME140.dll`/`MSVCP140.dll` if built with MSVC) and one `<major>.<minor> linker version`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Linux archive layout (for its pin only)**

The Linux archive is pinned (Task 1) because the clang prefix needs FFmpeg's headers for the lint
configure and the Linux sanity build; nothing of it is run or shipped in v1.0 (U-WIN). Only the
listing is read (PowerShell; CMake reads `.tar.xz`):

```powershell
$txz = Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0 -File -Filter '*linux-x64-shared-lgpl.tar.xz' | Select-Object -First 1
$entries = cmake -E tar tf $txz.FullName
$entries | Where-Object { $_ -match '/$' } | Where-Object { ($_ -split '/').Count -le 4 } | Sort-Object
$entries | Where-Object { $_ -match '(/lib/lib(avutil|avcodec|avformat|avfilter|swscale|swresample)\.so[^/]*$)|(/bin/ff(mpeg|probe)$)|(build-info\.txt$)|(include/libavutil/avutil\.h$)' } | Sort-Object
$entries | Where-Object { $_ -match '[\\/]licenses?[\\/].+[^/]$' } | Sort-Object
```

Expected: the same folder structure as Windows (the same top folder holding `bin/`, `include/`,
`lib/`, `share/` and `build-info.txt`), `lib/` with `libavutil.so.60` and the other five, and the
licence files (record them: Task 1's Linux `LICENSE_FILES`). Record "SVT-AV1 in the Linux archive:
yes" when a licence file path matches `(?i)svt` (U-FF, Step 7), else "no"; likewise "LAME in the
Linux archive: yes" when one matches `(?i)lame|mp3lame`, else "no". No `readelf`/`ldd`
facts are recorded: the Linux baseline is v1.x (U-WIN).

- [ ] **Step 7: Codec scope and lossless fixture facts (U-FF)** (Windows archive programs; one
  call: paste the **Code** helpers first)

Part A, the codec scope of the pinned build (Behaviour "Codec scope"):

```powershell
$bin = Split-Path (Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -Filter ffmpeg.exe | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
$names = { param($list) $list | ForEach-Object { if ($_ -match '^\s[VAS][A-Z.]{5}\s+(\S+)\s') { $Matches[1] } } | Where-Object { $_ -ne '=' } }
$dec = & $names (& "$bin/ffmpeg.exe" -hide_banner -decoders)
$enc = & $names (& "$bin/ffmpeg.exe" -hide_banner -encoders)
$patent = @($dec | Where-Object { $_ -match '^(h264|hevc|aac|av1|libdav1d|mpeg1video|mpeg2video|mpeg4|mpeg1|mpeg2)(_|$)' })
"patent-encumbered decoders: " + ($patent.Count ? ($patent -join ' ') : 'none')
$allow = 'rawvideo','v210','v410','r210','ffv1','huffyuv','ffvhuff','utvideo','magicyuv','png','qtrle'
foreach ($c in $allow) { if ($c -notin $dec) { "MISSING allowlist decoder $c" } }
$video = & $names ((& "$bin/ffmpeg.exe" -hide_banner -decoders) | Where-Object { $_ -match '^\sV' })
$other = @($video | Where-Object { $_ -notin $allow })
"video decoders outside the allowlist: " + ($other.Count ? "$($other.Count) (first: $(($other | Select-Object -First 8) -join ' '))" : 'none')
$svtLicence = [bool](Get-ChildItem _build/m4-task0/win -Recurse -File | Where-Object { (Rel $_.FullName) -match '(?i)licen[cs]es?/.*svt' })
"SVT-AV1 (Windows): " + ((('libsvtav1' -in $enc) -or $svtLicence) ? 'yes' : 'no')
"LAME (Windows): " + (('libmp3lame' -in $enc) ? 'yes' : 'no')
"mpeg4 encoder: " + (('mpeg4' -in $enc) ? 'yes' : 'no')
gh release list -R BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg --limit 20
gh release view v1.0.0 -R BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg --json assets --jq '.assets[].name'
```

Expected: the decoder list (with the full `shared-lgpl` build: `h264`, `hevc`, `aac`, `av1` or
`libdav1d`, `mpeg1video`, `mpeg2video`, `mpeg4` and their hardware variants); no `MISSING
allowlist decoder` line (a missing one would only mean that codec opens with "no decoder for
<name>"; record it and go on); `video decoders outside the allowlist:` a count for the full build
(record "none" or the count); `SVT-AV1 (Windows): yes`, `LAME (Windows): yes`; the `mpeg4`
encoder line. A lossless-only candidate is an LGPL shared Windows asset whose name marks a
lossless or minimal variant (e.g. `lossless`, `minimal`, `intra`) in v1.0.0 or a later release of
the same FFmpeg version. If one is listed: download it to `_build/m4-task0/lossless/`, run Step 4's
SHA256 and attestation commands and Step 5's commands on it (extracted to
`_build/m4-task0/win-lossless`), then Part A again with `$bin` in that folder; it is pinned only
when every Behaviour condition holds, and Part B and the rest of the task then use its programs.
Otherwise record "lossless-only build: none available". Then record the four consequences
(Behaviour) with the Linux SVT-AV1 and LAME facts of Step 6.

Part B, the lossless fixtures (the throwaway copies of Task 5a's fixture commands):

```powershell
$t = '_build/m4-task0'; $ff = "$bin/ffmpeg.exe"; $fp = "$bin/ffprobe.exe"
function Gen([string] $size, [int] $frames, [string[]] $rest) { & $ff -hide_banner -loglevel error -nostdin -y -f lavfi -i "testsrc2=s=${size}:r=25" -frames:v $frames @rest }
Gen 352x288 30 '-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-c:v','rawvideo',"$t/seq.avi"
Gen 352x288 30 '-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-c:v','rawvideo',"$t/seq.nut"
Gen 352x288 30 '-vf','setpts=floor(N/2)/(25*TB)','-fps_mode','passthrough','-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-c:v','ffv1','-level','3',"$t/seq.mkv"
Gen 192x144 10 '-pix_fmt','yuv422p','-c:v','huffyuv',"$t/lossless_huffyuv.avi"
Gen 192x144 10 '-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-c:v','utvideo',"$t/lossless_utvideo.mkv"
Gen 192x144 10 '-pix_fmt','yuv422p10le','-c:v','v210',"$t/lossless_v210.mov"
Gen 192x144 10 '-pix_fmt','yuv444p','-c:v','magicyuv',"$t/lossless_magicyuv.nut"
if ('mpeg4' -in $enc) { Gen 64x48 5 '-pix_fmt','yuv420p','-c:v','mpeg4',"$t/lossy_mpeg4.mp4" }
# Task 5a case 14 (a): every HFYU FourCC in the first 4096 bytes becomes XVID (codec mpeg4).
$b = [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes((Resolve-Path "$t/lossless_huffyuv.avi")); $n = 0
for ($i = 0; $i -le [Math]::Min(4096, $b.Length) - 4; $i++) { if ($b[$i] -eq 0x48 -and $b[$i+1] -eq 0x46 -and $b[$i+2] -eq 0x59 -and $b[$i+3] -eq 0x55) { $b[$i] = 0x58; $b[$i+1] = 0x56; $b[$i+2] = 0x49; $b[$i+3] = 0x44; $n++ } }
[IO.File]::WriteAllBytes((Join-Path (Resolve-Path $t) 'lossy_xvid.avi'), $b); "HFYU replaced: $n"
foreach ($f in 'seq.avi','seq.nut','seq.mkv','lossless_huffyuv.avi','lossless_utvideo.mkv','lossless_v210.mov','lossless_magicyuv.nut','lossy_mpeg4.mp4','lossy_xvid.avi') {
    if (-not (Test-Path "$t/$f")) { "== $f not made"; continue }
    $p = @(& $fp -v error -select_streams v:0 -show_entries packet=pts,dts,flags -of csv=p=0 "$t/$f")
    $pts = $p | ForEach-Object { ($_ -split ',')[0] }
    "== $f packets=$($p.Count) ptsNA=$(@($pts | Where-Object { $_ -eq 'N/A' }).Count) duplicatePts=$(@($pts | Where-Object { $_ -ne 'N/A' } | Group-Object | Where-Object Count -gt 1).Count) codec=" + (& $fp -v error -select_streams v:0 -show_entries stream=codec_name,pix_fmt -of csv=p=0 "$t/$f")
    $p | Select-Object -First 6
    & $fp -hide_banner -v debug -probesize 4096 -i "$t/$f" 2>&1 | Select-String 'probed with size' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() }
    if ($f -like 'seq.*') { & $ff -hide_banner -loglevel error -nostdin -y -i "$t/$f" -map 0:v:0 -fps_mode passthrough -f framemd5 "$t/$f.framemd5"; "framemd5 entries: " + @(Get-Content "$t/$f.framemd5" | Where-Object { $_ -notmatch '^#' }).Count }
}
```

Expected and the choice:
- `seq.avi` and `seq.nut`: 30 packets. Record `ptsNA` and `duplicatePts`. Likely 0 and 0 for both:
  libavformat fills `pts` from `dts` for a codec without reordering. That holds even for AVI,
  which stores no timestamps.
- `seq.mkv`: 30 packets, `ptsNA=0`, `duplicatePts=15` (pairs of frames share a timestamp; Matroska
  accepts equal timestamps).
- Every `seq.*` file: `framemd5 entries: 30`.
- **The `Sequential` fixture:**
  - the first of `seq.avi`, `seq.nut` with `ptsNA > 0` (a lossless stream with missing
    timestamps: D5's first trigger);
  - else `seq.mkv` when `duplicatePts > 0` (D5's second trigger: two packets with equal `pts`);
  - record "Sequential fixture: <file> (<trigger>)". Tasks 5a and 7a use that file's command;
    their `long.<ext>` uses the same container and timestamp options with FFV1.
  - If no candidate qualifies, or the chosen file has fewer than 30 `framemd5` entries, stop and
    report it: the `Sequential` path of D5 would never run (design arbiter #8).
- `lossless_*`: codecs `huffyuv`, `utvideo`, `v210`, `magicyuv` with `yuv422p`, `yuv420p`,
  `yuv422p10le`, `yuv444p`; one `probed with size` line each with score > 50 (expected 100: AVI,
  Matroska, MOV and NUT probe on their headers); record `ptsNA` per container (expected 0).
- `lossy_mpeg4.mp4`: codec `mpeg4` when the encoder exists. Otherwise "not made": record "mpeg4
  encoder: no", and Task 5a patches an AVI's FourCC instead.
- `lossy_xvid.avi` (review fix: the always-on refusal input is checked here): `HFYU replaced: 2`
  (strh and strf; at least 1), ffprobe exits 0 and prints `codec=mpeg4,…`, one `probed with size`
  line with score > 50. Record "patched AVI: codec mpeg4, opens: yes". If ffprobe fails to open it
  or names another codec, stop and report it: Task 5a case 14 (a) would not test the allowlist.
- Note: `seq.nut` can never qualify (NUT stores a timestamp for every frame); it is kept only
  because the user named NUT as an example. The decisive candidates are `seq.avi` and `seq.mkv`.
