### Task 1: Superbuild: FFmpeg, gate, notices, `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`

**Goal:** Pin the custom_ffmpeg v1.0.0 LGPL shared archives and the LAME 3.100 source tarball,
allow `LGPL-2.0-or-later` and `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` for run-time-loaded libraries (each only when
Task 0's codec-scope record keeps it, user decision U-FF), mark the
entries whose code ships (`SHIPPED`) so `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt` lists exactly them (plus the
Visual C++ runtime on Windows), add the superbuild option `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` (default `ON`), and
publish the prefix's FFmpeg to the dvv repositories as the CMake package `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk`
(design v2 §9, D3, D19, D20, D27; controller decisions in §2).

**Files** (all in rawreel-workspace):
- Modify `cmake/DvvSpdx.cmake`: the two licences join `DVV_SPDX_RUNTIME_ONLY`.
- Modify `cmake/DvvThirdParty.cmake`: the `SHIPPED` flag of `dvv_third_party()`; the new
  `dvv_source_pin()`.
- Modify `deps/versions.cmake`: `SHIPPED` on the five shipped entries; the two `custom_ffmpeg`
  declarations; the LAME pin.
- Modify `deps/CMakeLists.txt`: the option `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`; two notices files; writing the
  `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk` package and the state file `dvv-third-party-state.cmake` into the prefix.
- Create `deps/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake.in`: the package template.
- Modify `deps/write-notices.cmake`: `HEADER` and `MSVC_RUNTIME` arguments.
- Modify `bootstrap.ps1` (`-BundleFfmpeg ON|OFF`) and `bootstrap.sh` (`--bundle-ffmpeg ON|OFF`):
  pass the option and include it in the stamp.
- Create `tests/deps/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/deps/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer/main.cpp`
  and `tests/deps/run-ffmpeg-sdk-consumer.cmake`; modify `CMakeLists.txt` (CTest
  `workspace.ffmpeg_sdk`).
- Modify `tests/spdx/test_spdx.cmake`, `tests/spdx/test_third_party_gate.cmake`,
  `tests/deps/test_superbuild_scripts.cmake`, `tests/deps/test_versions.cmake`.
- Modify `README.md`: bootstrap's FFmpeg option, the notices files, the prefix's FFmpeg.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 0's record in the M4 plan file (asset names, URLs, SHA256, licence file paths,
  LAME URL and SHA256; the codec-scope record: the pinned asset, "BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance:
  kept/dropped", "LAME allowance: kept/dropped", "LAME: present/absent") and the downloads in
  `_build/m4-task0/`; the M1b superbuild
  (`dvv_third_party`, `dvv_spdx_evaluate`, `write-notices.cmake`, bootstrap stamps).
- Produces (for Tasks 3–12):
  - Superbuild cache option **`DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`** (BOOL, default `ON`), set through
    `bootstrap.ps1 -BundleFfmpeg ON|OFF` / `bootstrap.sh --bundle-ffmpeg ON|OFF` (default `ON`). It
    drives custom_ffmpeg's `SHIPPED` (so the notices) and the `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` value of
    `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk`, which Task 8 reads for staging the Windows zip's `ffmpeg/`, the sources
    archive, the manifest and the smoke variants. Changing it reruns the superbuild (it is part of the stamp)
    without rebuilding anything but the notices and the package file.
  - `dvv_third_party(... SHIPPED)`: global property `DVV_THIRD_PARTY_<name>_SHIPPED` (`TRUE`/`FALSE`).
    Shipped entries: nlohmann_json, Vulkan-Headers (with Vulkan-Hpp), VulkanMemoryAllocator,
    vk-bootstrap, wxWidgets, and custom_ffmpeg when `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` is on.
  - `dvv_source_pin(<name> VERSION <v> URL <url> SHA256 <hash>)`: global properties
    `DVV_SOURCE_PIN_NAMES` and `DVV_SOURCE_PIN_<name>_{VERSION,URL,SHA256}`; one pin, `lame` 3.100.
  - Prefix files (in `_third_party/<toolchain>/`):
    - `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt`: the shipped entries only (first line `Third-party software in the
      rawreel packages`), on Windows followed by the Visual C++ runtime paragraph. Task 8 ships it.
    - `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES-all.txt`: every entry (developer reference, never shipped).
    - `custom_ffmpeg/`: the extracted archive (PREBUILT).
    - `lib/cmake/dvv_ffmpeg_sdk/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake`: `find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk CONFIG
      REQUIRED)` (through the presets' `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH`) sets `DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT`,
      `DVV_FFMPEG_INCLUDE_DIR`, `DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR` (Windows `<root>/bin`, Linux `<root>/lib`:
      the directory a test passes as `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR`), `DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES` (the six files
      the loader opens, absolute: Windows `avutil-60.dll` …, Linux `libavutil.so.60` …),
      `DVV_FFMPEG_SHIPPED_FILES` (those six plus their Linux links, sorted), `DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM`,
      `DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM`, `DVV_FFMPEG_COMMAND` and `DVV_FFPROBE_COMMAND` (lists that run the
      programs; on Linux through `cmake -E env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=<runtime dir>`),
      `DVV_FFMPEG_LICENSE_DIR`, `DVV_FFMPEG_BUILD_INFO`, `DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_VERSION` (`1.0.0`),
      `DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_URL`, `DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_SHA256`, `DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_REPOSITORY`
      (`https://github.com/BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg`), `DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_TAG` (`v1.0.0`),
      `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` (`ON`/`OFF`), `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_VERSION`, `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL`,
      `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256`; and the target **`dvv::ffmpeg_headers`** (INTERFACE IMPORTED, the
      include directory as a system directory).
    - **`dvv-third-party-state.cmake`** (for `cmake -P` scripts, which cannot load the package
      because it creates a target; Task 8's packaging, its scripts and Task 9's `release.yml` read
      it), exactly these variables:
      `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` (`ON`/`OFF`), `DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY` (the platform's `SHIPPED` names,
      sorted; `custom_ffmpeg` only when bundled), `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION`,
      `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_URL`, `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_SHA256` (the platform's pin),
      `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_DIR` (`<prefix>/custom_ffmpeg`, through `CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR`),
      `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL`, `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256`. Same values as `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk`; one
      producer (this task).
  - Licence gate: `LGPL-2.0-or-later` and `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` in `DVV_SPDX_RUNTIME_ONLY`.
  - CTest `workspace.ffmpeg_sdk` (Debug only, not under sanitizers).
- Not here: the dvv-components wiring (Task 3), CI changes (Task 9).

**Behaviour:**
- **Gate.** `dvv_spdx_evaluate`: `LGPL-2.0-or-later` and `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` are allowed for
  `runtime` and `tool`, rejected for `static` ("not allowed for static linkage: …"). Nothing else
  changes (`LGPL-2.0-only` stays rejected; `LGPL-2.0-or-later WITH WxWindows-exception-3.1` stays
  allowed everywhere).
- **Codec scope (U-FF; Task 0's record).** The text and code below assume the full v1.0.0
  `shared-lgpl` build with "BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance: kept" and "LAME: present" (expected).
  - **"BSD-3-Clause-Clear allowance: dropped"** (no pinned archive contains SVT-AV1):
    - leave `BSD-3-Clause-Clear` out of `DVV_SPDX_RUNTIME_ONLY`, out of both custom_ffmpeg SPDX
      expressions and out of `ffmpeg_spdx` in `test_spdx.cmake`;
    - its three `expect` lines become `expect("BSD-3-Clause-Clear" runtime 0)`, `… tool 0)` and
      `… static 0)`;
    - drop SVT-AV1 from the `DvvSpdx.cmake` and `versions.cmake` comments;
    - the first commit becomes `build: allow LGPL-2.0-or-later for run-time libraries` (body
      without SVT-AV1 and the flag).
  - **"LAME allowance: dropped"** (no pinned archive, Windows or Linux, contains LAME): likewise
    leave `LGPL-2.0-or-later` out (its `expect` lines `runtime 0` and `tool 0`) and out of both
    SPDX expressions. A Linux full pin with LAME keeps the allowance even when the Windows build has
    none.
  - **"LAME: absent"** (the shipped Windows build has no LAME): add neither the
    `dvv_source_pin(lame …)` declaration nor the LAME tests and variables (`DVV_LAME_SOURCE_*`);
    the function `dvv_source_pin()` and its own tests stay. If both allowances are dropped, skip
    Step 4's first commit.
  - **A lossless-only build pinned**: its asset name, URL, SHA256 and licence files replace the
    Windows (and, when Task 0 pinned one, the Linux) values; the SPDX expression is the one of its
    recorded licence files.
  - The runtime codec allowlist (Task 5a) is the same in every case.
- **`SHIPPED`.** A flag of `dvv_third_party`; with `LINKAGE tool` it is a configuration error
  `dvv_third_party(<name>): SHIPPED needs LINKAGE static or runtime (a tool is never shipped)`.
  Recorded as `DVV_THIRD_PARTY_<name>_SHIPPED` for the platforms the entry is built on, like the
  other keys.
- **`dvv_source_pin`.** Requires `VERSION`, `URL`, `SHA256` (`dvv_source_pin(<name>): <key> is
  required`); `SHA256` is 64 lower-case hex digits (`dvv_source_pin(<name>): SHA256 must be 64
  lower-case hex digits`); unknown arguments fail (`unexpected '<args>'`); a name is pinned once
  (`dvv_source_pin(<name>): pinned twice`). Nothing is downloaded by the superbuild; Task 8's
  sources-archive script downloads it with `file(DOWNLOAD … EXPECTED_HASH SHA256=…)`.
- **`DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`.** `deps/CMakeLists.txt` declares the option before it includes
  `versions.cmake`. `versions.cmake` adds `SHIPPED` to custom_ffmpeg when the variable is undefined
  (the test scripts include the file without it) or true.
- **Notices.** `third_party_notices` writes both files after every dependency: `-all` with every
  entry and the header `Third-party software used to build and test rawreel and the dvv libraries
  (developer reference; not shipped)`; the shipped file with the shipped entries, the header
  `Third-party software in the rawreel packages` and, when the superbuild compiler is MSVC, the
  Visual C++ runtime paragraph (exact text in **Code**). `write-notices.cmake` without `HEADER`
  keeps today's first line.
- **`dvv_ffmpeg_sdk`.** `deps/CMakeLists.txt` writes the package file at configure time with
  `configure_file(@ONLY)`. The release repository and tag come from the custom_ffmpeg URL
  (`^(https://github\.com/[^/]+/[^/]+)/releases/download/([^/]+)/`; another URL form is a
  configuration error). The package finds the archive's top folder at consumption time (the single
  `include/libavutil/avutil.h` under `<prefix>/custom_ffmpeg`), so the layout recorded by Task 0
  needs no code. Any missing piece sets `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_FOUND` to `FALSE` with
  `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_NOT_FOUND_MESSAGE` naming it.
- **Bootstrap.** `-BundleFfmpeg` takes `ON` or `OFF` (case-insensitive, normalised to upper case),
  default `ON`; the line `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=<value>` is appended to the hashed listing, and
  `-DDVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=<value>` goes to the superbuild configure. `bootstrap.sh --bundle-ffmpeg
  ON|OFF` likewise; another value exits 2 with `--bundle-ffmpeg must be ON or OFF`.

**Code:**

`cmake/DvvSpdx.cmake` (replace the `DVV_SPDX_RUNTIME_ONLY` lines):

```cmake
# Allowed only for libraries that are loaded at run time or used as build tools. LGPL-2.0-or-later
# (LAME) and BSD-3-Clause-Clear (SVT-AV1) occur only inside custom_ffmpeg's run-time-loaded
# libraries (controller decisions, M4; BSD-3-Clause-Clear is flagged for the user: USER MAY WANT TO
# REVISIT, spec §17).
set(DVV_SPDX_RUNTIME_ONLY LGPL-2.1-only LGPL-2.1-or-later LGPL-3.0-only LGPL-3.0-or-later
    LGPL-2.0-or-later BSD-3-Clause-Clear)
```

`cmake/DvvThirdParty.cmake` (header comment line for the flag, inserted after `PREBUILT`; then the
changes inside `dvv_third_party`; then the new function at the end of the file):

```cmake
#   SHIPPED        the entry's code or files are in a rawreel package: only shipped entries go
#                  into THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt (M4 design D19). Not with LINKAGE tool.
```

```cmake
    cmake_parse_arguments(PARSE_ARGV 1 arg "PREBUILT;SHIPPED"
                          "VERSION;URL;SHA256;SPDX;LINKAGE;ONLY_ON;POST_INSTALL"
                          "LICENSE_FILES;DEPENDS;CMAKE_ARGS;CONFIGS")
    # (after the CONFIGS checks)
    if(arg_SHIPPED AND arg_LINKAGE STREQUAL "tool")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_third_party(${name}): SHIPPED needs LINKAGE static or runtime "
                            "(a tool is never shipped)")
    endif()
    # (the recorded keys)
    foreach(key IN ITEMS VERSION URL SHA256 SPDX LINKAGE LICENSE_FILES DEPENDS PREBUILT CMAKE_ARGS
                         POST_INSTALL CONFIGS SHIPPED)
```

```cmake
# dvv_source_pin(<name> VERSION <v> URL <url> SHA256 <hash>)
# A source archive that is pinned but never downloaded, built or shipped by the superbuild (M4
# design D27: the LAME tarball of rawreel's third-party sources archive). Records the global
# properties DVV_SOURCE_PIN_<name>_VERSION, _URL and _SHA256 and appends <name> to
# DVV_SOURCE_PIN_NAMES; a name is pinned once.
function(dvv_source_pin name)
    cmake_parse_arguments(PARSE_ARGV 1 arg "" "VERSION;URL;SHA256" "")
    if(arg_UNPARSED_ARGUMENTS)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_source_pin(${name}): unexpected '${arg_UNPARSED_ARGUMENTS}'")
    endif()
    foreach(required IN ITEMS VERSION URL SHA256)
        if(NOT arg_${required})
            message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_source_pin(${name}): ${required} is required")
        endif()
    endforeach()
    if(NOT arg_SHA256 MATCHES "^[0-9a-f]+$")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_source_pin(${name}): SHA256 must be 64 lower-case hex digits")
    endif()
    string(LENGTH "${arg_SHA256}" hash_length)
    if(NOT hash_length EQUAL 64)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_source_pin(${name}): SHA256 must be 64 lower-case hex digits")
    endif()
    get_property(pinned GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SOURCE_PIN_NAMES)
    if(name IN_LIST pinned)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_source_pin(${name}): pinned twice")
    endif()
    set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY DVV_SOURCE_PIN_NAMES "${name}")
    foreach(key IN ITEMS VERSION URL SHA256)
        set_property(GLOBAL PROPERTY "DVV_SOURCE_PIN_${name}_${key}" "${arg_${key}}")
    endforeach()
endfunction()
```

`deps/versions.cmake`: add the word `SHIPPED` (on its own line, after `LINKAGE static`) to
`nlohmann_json`, `Vulkan-Headers`, `VulkanMemoryAllocator`, `vk-bootstrap` and both `wxWidgets`
declarations. Then append (the `<…>` values are Task 0's record):

```cmake
# FFmpeg (spec §2, §13.2; M4 design D3, §9): the custom_ffmpeg "lgpl" shared release, loaded at run
# time and never linked. dvv-components compiles against its headers (through dvv_ffmpeg_sdk, written
# by deps/CMakeLists.txt); its six run-time libraries ship in the Windows package's ffmpeg/ folder
# only with DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG (user decision U4); its ffmpeg and ffprobe programs serve the tests
# as an oracle. The Linux archive is pinned for the clang prefix (headers for the lint and the
# Linux sanity build); v1.0 has no Linux package (user decision U-WIN; Linux packaging is v1.x). Contents: FFmpeg (LGPL-2.1-or-later), LAME
# (LGPL-2.0-or-later), libvpx, libwebp, Opus, Ogg, Vorbis (BSD-3-Clause), SVT-AV1
# (BSD-3-Clause-Clear, with the AOM patent licence), dav1d (BSD-2-Clause), zlib (Zlib), libvpl, AMF
# and ffnvcodec headers (MIT); on Linux also libva and libdrm (MIT). Patent grants (AOM, the libvpx
# and libwebp PATENTS files) are not licence expressions; they are licence files, so they reach the
# notices. The release's build attestation was verified when these were pinned (M4 Task 0) and is
# verified again by rawreel's release workflow.
# The test scripts include this file without DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG, which counts as ON.
if(NOT DEFINED DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG OR DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG)
    set(custom_ffmpeg_shipped SHIPPED)
else()
    set(custom_ffmpeg_shipped "")
endif()
dvv_third_party(custom_ffmpeg
    VERSION 1.0.0
    URL https://github.com/BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg/releases/download/v1.0.0/<Windows asset name>
    SHA256 <Windows SHA256>
    SPDX "LGPL-2.1-or-later AND LGPL-2.0-or-later AND BSD-3-Clause AND BSD-2-Clause \
AND BSD-3-Clause-Clear AND MIT AND Zlib"
    LINKAGE runtime
    LICENSE_FILES <licence files of the Windows archive, relative to the extracted folder>
    ONLY_ON Windows
    PREBUILT
    ${custom_ffmpeg_shipped})

dvv_third_party(custom_ffmpeg
    VERSION 1.0.0
    URL https://github.com/BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg/releases/download/v1.0.0/<Linux asset name>
    SHA256 <Linux SHA256>
    SPDX "LGPL-2.1-or-later AND LGPL-2.0-or-later AND BSD-3-Clause AND BSD-2-Clause \
AND BSD-3-Clause-Clear AND MIT AND Zlib"
    LINKAGE runtime
    LICENSE_FILES <licence files of the Linux archive, relative to the extracted folder>
    ONLY_ON Linux
    PREBUILT
    ${custom_ffmpeg_shipped})

# LAME 3.100 source tarball (M4 design D27): LAME is inside custom_ffmpeg's libraries; the tarball is
# pinned only for rawreel's third-party sources archive and is never downloaded by the superbuild,
# built or shipped in a package.
dvv_source_pin(lame
    VERSION 3.100
    URL https://downloads.sourceforge.net/project/lame/lame/3.100/lame-3.100.tar.gz
    SHA256 <LAME SHA256>)
```

The extracted folder is `<prefix>/custom_ffmpeg` (ExternalProject drops a single top-level folder
of the archive). `LICENSE_FILES` entries are paths or globs relative to it, e.g.
`share/licenses/*` when the archive root holds `share/`, or `<folder>/share/licenses/*` when Task 0
recorded a nested folder; every licence file of the archive must be covered (including the patent
files), and `build-info.txt` is not a licence file.

`deps/CMakeLists.txt` (changes):

```cmake
# (before include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/versions.cmake"))
# User decision U4 (M4 design §2, D3, D27): ship custom_ffmpeg's LGPL libraries in the rawreel
# packages. OFF keeps FFmpeg for development and tests but leaves it out of the shipped notices and,
# through dvv_ffmpeg_sdk, out of the packages and the sources archive.
option(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG "Ship custom_ffmpeg's LGPL libraries in the rawreel packages" ON)

# (in the per-entry loop: add SHIPPED to the keys read, then replace the list(APPEND notice_entries …))
    ExternalProject_Get_Property(${name} SOURCE_DIR)
    list(JOIN LICENSE_FILES "|" licence_files)
    set(entry "${name}|${VERSION}|${SPDX}|${URL}|${SOURCE_DIR}|${licence_files}")
    list(APPEND notice_entries "${entry}")
    if(SHIPPED)
        list(APPEND shipped_entries "${entry}")
        list(APPEND shipped_names "${name}")
    endif()

# (replaces the third_party_notices target)
# THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt holds what the packages ship (M4 design D19); THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES-all.txt
# every dependency, as a developer reference that is never shipped. $<SEMICOLON> keeps each list in
# one command-line argument.
string(REPLACE ";" "$<SEMICOLON>" notice_entries "${notice_entries}")
string(REPLACE ";" "$<SEMICOLON>" shipped_entries "${shipped_entries}")
if(MSVC)
    set(msvc_runtime ON)
else()
    set(msvc_runtime OFF)
endif()
add_custom_target(third_party_notices ALL
    COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" "-DOUTPUT=${prefix}/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES-all.txt"
            "-DENTRIES=${notice_entries}"
            "-DHEADER=Third-party software used to build and test rawreel and the dvv libraries \
(developer reference; not shipped)"
            -P "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/write-notices.cmake"
    COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" "-DOUTPUT=${prefix}/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt"
            "-DENTRIES=${shipped_entries}"
            "-DHEADER=Third-party software in the rawreel packages"
            "-DMSVC_RUNTIME=${msvc_runtime}"
            -P "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/write-notices.cmake"
    DEPENDS ${names}
    VERBATIM)

# dvv_ffmpeg_sdk: the prefix's FFmpeg for dvv-components and rawreel (M4 design §9).
foreach(key IN ITEMS VERSION URL SHA256)
    get_property(ffmpeg_${key} GLOBAL PROPERTY "DVV_THIRD_PARTY_custom_ffmpeg_${key}")
    get_property(lame_${key} GLOBAL PROPERTY "DVV_SOURCE_PIN_lame_${key}")
endforeach()
if(NOT ffmpeg_URL MATCHES "^(https://github\\.com/[^/]+/[^/]+)/releases/download/([^/]+)/")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "custom_ffmpeg's URL is not a GitHub release asset: ${ffmpeg_URL}")
endif()
set(ffmpeg_repository "${CMAKE_MATCH_1}")
set(ffmpeg_tag "${CMAKE_MATCH_2}")
if(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG)
    set(bundle_value ON)
else()
    set(bundle_value OFF)
endif()
configure_file("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake.in"
               "${prefix}/lib/cmake/dvv_ffmpeg_sdk/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake" @ONLY)

# What the prefix ships, for rawreel's packaging and its cmake -P scripts (M4 design D19, D27, §9),
# which cannot load dvv_ffmpeg_sdk (it creates a target). The same values as the package. Written at
# configure time; the PREBUILT extract steps replace only <prefix>/<name>, never this file.
list(SORT shipped_names)
file(WRITE "${prefix}/dvv-third-party-state.cmake"
"# Generated by the rawreel-workspace superbuild (deps/CMakeLists.txt); do not edit.
set(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG ${bundle_value})
set(DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY \"${shipped_names}\")
set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION \"${ffmpeg_VERSION}\")
set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_URL \"${ffmpeg_URL}\")
set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_SHA256 \"${ffmpeg_SHA256}\")
set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_DIR \"\${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/custom_ffmpeg\")
set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL \"${lame_URL}\")
set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256 \"${lame_SHA256}\")
")
```

`deps/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake.in` (complete):

```cmake
# FFmpeg of the third-party prefix: custom_ffmpeg @ffmpeg_VERSION@, an LGPL shared build (spec §2,
# §13.2; M4 design D3, §9). Written by the rawreel-workspace superbuild from
# deps/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake.in; do not edit. FFmpeg is never linked: dvv-components compiles
# against these headers and loads the libraries at run time; ffmpeg and ffprobe serve the tests.
#
# Sets DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT, DVV_FFMPEG_INCLUDE_DIR, DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR, DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES (the
# six libraries the loader opens), DVV_FFMPEG_SHIPPED_FILES (their files and links),
# DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM, DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM, DVV_FFMPEG_COMMAND and DVV_FFPROBE_COMMAND (lists that run
# the programs), DVV_FFMPEG_LICENSE_DIR, DVV_FFMPEG_BUILD_INFO, DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_VERSION,
# DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_URL, DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_SHA256, DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_REPOSITORY,
# DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_TAG, DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG (ON or OFF), DVV_LAME_SOURCE_VERSION,
# DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL and DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256, and the target dvv::ffmpeg_headers (INTERFACE
# IMPORTED: the include directory, which consumers see as a system directory).

set(DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_VERSION "@ffmpeg_VERSION@")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_URL "@ffmpeg_URL@")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_SHA256 "@ffmpeg_SHA256@")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_REPOSITORY "@ffmpeg_repository@")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_TAG "@ffmpeg_tag@")
set(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG @bundle_value@)
set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_VERSION "@lame_VERSION@")
set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL "@lame_URL@")
set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256 "@lame_SHA256@")

# The archive's top folder: the one with include/libavutil/avutil.h (M4 Task 0 recorded the layout).
cmake_path(SET _dvv_ffmpeg_extracted NORMALIZE "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/../../../custom_ffmpeg")
file(GLOB_RECURSE _dvv_ffmpeg_headers LIST_DIRECTORIES false "${_dvv_ffmpeg_extracted}/avutil.h")
list(FILTER _dvv_ffmpeg_headers INCLUDE REGEX "/include/libavutil/avutil\\.h$")
list(LENGTH _dvv_ffmpeg_headers _dvv_ffmpeg_count)
if(NOT _dvv_ffmpeg_count EQUAL 1)
    set(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_FOUND FALSE)
    set(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_NOT_FOUND_MESSAGE
        "expected one include/libavutil/avutil.h in ${_dvv_ffmpeg_extracted}, found "
        "${_dvv_ffmpeg_count}; run bootstrap")
    return()
endif()
string(REGEX REPLACE "/include/libavutil/avutil\\.h$" "" DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT "${_dvv_ffmpeg_headers}")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_INCLUDE_DIR "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/include")
if(WIN32)
    set(DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/bin")
else()
    set(DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/lib")
endif()
set(DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/bin/ffmpeg${CMAKE_EXECUTABLE_SUFFIX}")
set(DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/bin/ffprobe${CMAKE_EXECUTABLE_SUFFIX}")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_LICENSE_DIR "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/share/licenses")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_BUILD_INFO "${DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT}/build-info.txt")
if(WIN32)
    set(DVV_FFMPEG_COMMAND "${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}")
    set(DVV_FFPROBE_COMMAND "${DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM}")
else()
    # The programs find their libraries without a RUNPATH, too.
    set(DVV_FFMPEG_COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -E env "LD_LIBRARY_PATH=${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}"
                           "${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}")
    set(DVV_FFPROBE_COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -E env "LD_LIBRARY_PATH=${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}"
                            "${DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM}")
endif()

# The six libraries of M4 design D3 (no avdevice, no postproc).
set(DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES "")
set(DVV_FFMPEG_SHIPPED_FILES "")
foreach(_dvv_library IN ITEMS avutil avcodec avformat avfilter swscale swresample)
    if(WIN32)
        file(GLOB _dvv_files LIST_DIRECTORIES false "${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}/${_dvv_library}-*.dll")
        set(_dvv_loaded ${_dvv_files})
    else()
        file(GLOB _dvv_files LIST_DIRECTORIES false "${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}/lib${_dvv_library}.so*")
        set(_dvv_loaded ${_dvv_files})
        list(FILTER _dvv_loaded INCLUDE REGEX "/lib${_dvv_library}\\.so\\.[0-9]+$")
    endif()
    list(LENGTH _dvv_loaded _dvv_count)
    if(NOT _dvv_count EQUAL 1)
        set(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_FOUND FALSE)
        set(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_NOT_FOUND_MESSAGE
            "expected one ${_dvv_library} library in ${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}, found ${_dvv_count}")
        return()
    endif()
    list(APPEND DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES ${_dvv_loaded})
    list(APPEND DVV_FFMPEG_SHIPPED_FILES ${_dvv_files})
endforeach()
list(SORT DVV_FFMPEG_SHIPPED_FILES)
foreach(_dvv_file IN ITEMS "${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}" "${DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM}" "${DVV_FFMPEG_LICENSE_DIR}"
                           "${DVV_FFMPEG_BUILD_INFO}")
    if(NOT EXISTS "${_dvv_file}")
        set(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_FOUND FALSE)
        set(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_NOT_FOUND_MESSAGE "${_dvv_file} does not exist; run bootstrap")
        return()
    endif()
endforeach()

if(NOT TARGET dvv::ffmpeg_headers)
    add_library(dvv::ffmpeg_headers INTERFACE IMPORTED)
    set_target_properties(dvv::ffmpeg_headers PROPERTIES
        INTERFACE_INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES "${DVV_FFMPEG_INCLUDE_DIR}")
endif()
```

If Task 0 recorded the licences in another folder than `share/licenses`, or the programs elsewhere
than `bin/`, change those two lines to the recorded paths (relative to `DVV_FFMPEG_ROOT`).

`deps/write-notices.cmake` (header comment and the two additions):

```cmake
# Writes a notices file. Called by deps/CMakeLists.txt with
#   -DOUTPUT=<file> -DENTRIES=<name|version|spdx|url|source dir|licence file|...;...>
#   [-DHEADER=<first line>] [-DMSVC_RUNTIME=ON]
# A licence file is a path or a glob relative to the source directory; a glob must match.
# MSVC_RUNTIME appends the paragraph for the Visual C++ runtime DLLs that the Windows package carries
# next to the executables (M4 design D17, D19).
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
if(NOT DEFINED HEADER)
    set(HEADER "Third-party software used by rawreel and the dvv libraries")
endif()
set(text "${HEADER}\n")
# (the entries loop is unchanged)
if(MSVC_RUNTIME)
    string(APPEND text "\n==============================================================\n"
                       "Microsoft Visual C++ runtime\n"
                       "Files: vcruntime140.dll, vcruntime140_1.dll, msvcp140.dll and the other "
                       "Visual C++ runtime DLLs next to the executables\n"
                       "Licence: Microsoft Software License Terms for Microsoft Visual Studio "
                       "(Distributable Code)\n"
                       "Source: https://visualstudio.microsoft.com/license-terms/\n")
endif()
file(WRITE "${OUTPUT}" "${text}")
```

`bootstrap.ps1` (changes):

```powershell
.PARAMETER BundleFfmpeg
    ON (default) or OFF: whether the rawreel packages ship custom_ffmpeg's LGPL libraries (superbuild
    option DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG; user decision U4). FFmpeg is installed for development and tests
    either way.
# param block: add
    [ValidateSet('ON', 'OFF')]
    [string] $BundleFfmpeg = 'ON'
# after param: $BundleFfmpeg = $BundleFfmpeg.ToUpperInvariant()
# after the $listing assignment:
$listing += "DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=$BundleFfmpeg`n"
# the configure line:
$configure = @('-S', (Join-Path $workspaceRepo 'deps'), '-B', $build, "-DDVV_THIRD_PARTY_PREFIX=$prefix",
    "-DDVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=$BundleFfmpeg")
```

`bootstrap.sh` (changes):

```sh
# Usage: ./bootstrap.sh [--toolchain clang] [--no-clone] [--force] [--bundle-ffmpeg ON|OFF]
bundle_ffmpeg=ON
        --bundle-ffmpeg) bundle_ffmpeg=$2; shift 2 ;;
case "$bundle_ffmpeg" in
    ON|OFF) ;;
    *) echo "--bundle-ffmpeg must be ON or OFF" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac
hash=$(cd "$workspace_repo" && {
    find deps cmake/DvvThirdParty.cmake cmake/DvvSpdx.cmake -type f | LC_ALL=C sort | xargs sha256sum
    echo "DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=$bundle_ffmpeg"
} | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
cmake -S "$workspace_repo/deps" -B "$build" -G Ninja "-DCMAKE_C_COMPILER=$c_compiler" \
    "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=$compiler" "-DDVV_THIRD_PARTY_PREFIX=$prefix" \
    "-DDVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=$bundle_ffmpeg"
```

`tests/deps/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer/CMakeLists.txt`:

```cmake
# Consumer of dvv_ffmpeg_sdk, the FFmpeg package of the third-party prefix (M4 design §9). Built and
# run by run-ffmpeg-sdk-consumer.cmake.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk_consumer LANGUAGES CXX)

find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk REQUIRED CONFIG)
list(LENGTH DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES count)
if(NOT count EQUAL 6)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES has ${count} entries, expected 6")
endif()
foreach(file IN LISTS DVV_FFMPEG_LIBRARY_FILES DVV_FFMPEG_SHIPPED_FILES
             ITEMS "${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}" "${DVV_FFPROBE_PROGRAM}" "${DVV_FFMPEG_BUILD_INFO}"
                   "${DVV_FFMPEG_LICENSE_DIR}" "${DVV_FFMPEG_INCLUDE_DIR}/libavutil/avutil.h")
    if(NOT EXISTS "${file}")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "${file} does not exist")
    endif()
endforeach()
if(NOT DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG MATCHES "^(ON|OFF)$")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG is '${DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG}'")
endif()
foreach(hash IN ITEMS "${DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_SHA256}" "${DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256}")
    if(NOT hash MATCHES "^[0-9a-f]+$")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "bad SHA256 '${hash}'")
    endif()
endforeach()
if(NOT DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_REPOSITORY STREQUAL "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg" OR
   NOT DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_TAG STREQUAL "v${DVV_FFMPEG_RELEASE_VERSION}")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "source ${DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_REPOSITORY} ${DVV_FFMPEG_SOURCE_TAG}")
endif()
execute_process(COMMAND ${DVV_FFMPEG_COMMAND} -hide_banner -version
                RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE version ERROR_VARIABLE error)
if(NOT status EQUAL 0 OR NOT version MATCHES "configuration:" OR
   version MATCHES "--enable-(gpl|nonfree)")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "ffmpeg -version failed or is not an LGPL build:\n${version}${error}")
endif()
string(REGEX MATCH "^[^\n]*" first_line "${version}")
message(STATUS "ffmpeg-sdk: ${first_line}; DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=${DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG}")

add_executable(consumer main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(consumer PRIVATE dvv::ffmpeg_headers)
target_compile_features(consumer PRIVATE cxx_std_23)
```

`tests/deps/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer/main.cpp`:

```cpp
// FFmpeg's version headers compile as C++ inside extern "C" (M4 design D3); nothing of FFmpeg is
// linked.
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>

extern "C" {
#include <libavcodec/version_major.h>
#include <libavformat/version_major.h>
#include <libavutil/version.h>
}

int main() {
    std::printf("avutil %d avcodec %d avformat %d\n", LIBAVUTIL_VERSION_MAJOR,
                LIBAVCODEC_VERSION_MAJOR, LIBAVFORMAT_VERSION_MAJOR);
    return 0;
}
```

`tests/deps/run-ffmpeg-sdk-consumer.cmake`:

```cmake
# Builds tests/deps/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer against the third-party prefix and runs it (M4 design §9).
# Called by workspace.ffmpeg_sdk with -DSOURCE_DIR, -DBUILD_DIR, -DGENERATOR, -DPLATFORM (may be
# empty), -DCXX_COMPILER and -DPREFIX_PATH (comma-separated).
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
foreach(variable IN ITEMS SOURCE_DIR BUILD_DIR GENERATOR CXX_COMPILER PREFIX_PATH)
    if(NOT DEFINED ${variable} OR "${${variable}}" STREQUAL "")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "run-ffmpeg-sdk-consumer.cmake needs -D${variable}=<value>")
    endif()
endforeach()
string(REPLACE "," ";" prefix_path "${PREFIX_PATH}")
file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${BUILD_DIR}")
# A list value cannot pass through a command line intact; an initial-cache file keeps it whole.
file(WRITE "${BUILD_DIR}-cache.cmake" "set(CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \"${prefix_path}\" CACHE PATH \"\")\n")
set(configure "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -S "${SOURCE_DIR}" -B "${BUILD_DIR}" -G "${GENERATOR}"
              "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=${CXX_COMPILER}" -C "${BUILD_DIR}-cache.cmake")
if(PLATFORM)
    list(APPEND configure -A "${PLATFORM}")
endif()
execute_process(COMMAND ${configure} RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE out ERROR_VARIABLE err)
if(NOT status EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "FAIL: configuring the consumer failed:\n${out}${err}")
endif()
string(REGEX MATCH "ffmpeg-sdk: [^\n]*" line "${out}")
message(STATUS "${line}")
execute_process(COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --build "${BUILD_DIR}" --config Release
                RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE out ERROR_VARIABLE err)
if(NOT status EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "FAIL: building the consumer failed:\n${out}${err}")
endif()
set(consumer "")
foreach(candidate IN ITEMS "${BUILD_DIR}/Release/consumer.exe" "${BUILD_DIR}/Release/consumer"
                           "${BUILD_DIR}/consumer.exe" "${BUILD_DIR}/consumer")
    if(EXISTS "${candidate}" AND NOT consumer)
        set(consumer "${candidate}")
    endif()
endforeach()
execute_process(COMMAND "${consumer}" RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE out)
if(NOT status EQUAL 0 OR NOT out MATCHES "^avutil [0-9]+ avcodec [0-9]+ avformat [0-9]+")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "FAIL: the consumer printed '${out}' (exit ${status})")
endif()
message(STATUS "ffmpeg sdk consumer passed: ${out}")
```

`CMakeLists.txt` (append after the wx consumer block):

```cmake
# custom_ffmpeg in the prefix (M4 design §9): the dvv_ffmpeg_sdk package finds FFmpeg's headers,
# libraries and programs, the ffmpeg program is an LGPL build, and FFmpeg's headers compile as C++.
# Not under sanitizers.
if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)
    string(REPLACE ";" "," ffmpeg_prefix_path "${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}")
    add_test(NAME workspace.ffmpeg_sdk CONFIGURATIONS Debug
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                     -DSOURCE_DIR=${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/deps/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer
                     -DBUILD_DIR=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/ffmpeg-sdk-consumer
                     -DGENERATOR=${CMAKE_GENERATOR}
                     -DPLATFORM=${CMAKE_GENERATOR_PLATFORM}
                     -DCXX_COMPILER=${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}
                     -DPREFIX_PATH=${ffmpeg_prefix_path}
                     -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/deps/run-ffmpeg-sdk-consumer.cmake)
    set_tests_properties(workspace.ffmpeg_sdk PROPERTIES TIMEOUT 600)
endif()
```

`tests/spdx/test_spdx.cmake` (append before the final message):

```cmake
# custom_ffmpeg (M4): LAME (LGPL-2.0-or-later) and SVT-AV1 (BSD-3-Clause-Clear) are allowed only for
# run-time-loaded libraries (controller decisions).
expect("LGPL-2.0-or-later" runtime 1)
expect("LGPL-2.0-or-later" tool 1)
expect("LGPL-2.0-or-later" static 0)
expect("LGPL-2.0-only" runtime 0)
expect("BSD-3-Clause-Clear" runtime 1)
expect("BSD-3-Clause-Clear" tool 1)
expect("BSD-3-Clause-Clear" static 0)
expect("LGPL-2.0-or-later WITH WxWindows-exception-3.1" static 1)
set(ffmpeg_spdx "LGPL-2.1-or-later AND LGPL-2.0-or-later AND BSD-3-Clause AND BSD-2-Clause AND \
BSD-3-Clause-Clear AND MIT AND Zlib")
expect("${ffmpeg_spdx}" runtime 1)
expect("${ffmpeg_spdx}" static 0)
```

`tests/spdx/test_third_party_gate.cmake` (append before the final message; `|` separates
commands in probe code, so the messages use spaces):

```cmake
# SHIPPED (M4 design D19): only an entry whose code or files are in a package; a tool never ships.
set(code "set(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME Windows)")
decl(code probe MIT tool SHIPPED)
expect("${code}" 1 "dvv_third_party\\(probe\\): SHIPPED needs LINKAGE static or runtime")
set(code "set(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME Windows)")
decl(code shipped MIT runtime PREBUILT SHIPPED)
decl(code kept MIT static)
string(APPEND code [[|get_property(a GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_THIRD_PARTY_shipped_SHIPPED)]]
                   [[|get_property(b GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_THIRD_PARTY_kept_SHIPPED)]]
                   [[|message(STATUS "shipped=<${a}> kept=<${b}>")]])
expect("${code}" 0 "shipped=<TRUE> kept=<FALSE>")

# dvv_source_pin (M4 design D27).
set(pin "dvv_source_pin(lame VERSION 3.100 URL https://example.com/lame.tgz")
string(CONCAT code "set(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME Windows)|${pin} SHA256 ${hash})"
                   [[|get_property(n GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SOURCE_PIN_NAMES)]]
                   [[|get_property(u GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SOURCE_PIN_lame_URL)]]
                   [[|get_property(v GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SOURCE_PIN_lame_VERSION)]]
                   [[|message(STATUS "pin=<${n} ${v} ${u}>")]])
expect("${code}" 0 "pin=<lame 3.100 https://example.com/lame.tgz>")
expect("set(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME Windows)|${pin})" 1 "dvv_source_pin\\(lame\\): SHA256 is required")
string(TOUPPER "${hash}" upper_hash)
string(REPLACE "0" "A" upper_hash "${upper_hash}")
expect("set(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME Windows)|${pin} SHA256 ${upper_hash})" 1
       "dvv_source_pin\\(lame\\): SHA256 must be 64 lower-case hex digits")
expect("set(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME Windows)|${pin} SHA256 ${hash})|${pin} SHA256 ${hash})" 1
       "dvv_source_pin\\(lame\\): pinned twice")
```

`tests/deps/test_superbuild_scripts.cmake` (after the existing write-notices block): a function
`expect_no_file_text(<file> <regex>)` (fails when the text matches), then:

```cmake
# write-notices.cmake: a custom first line and the Visual C++ runtime paragraph (M4 design D17, D19).
execute_process(COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" "-DOUTPUT=${notices}" "-DENTRIES=${entries}"
                        "-DHEADER=Third-party software in the rawreel packages" -DMSVC_RUNTIME=ON
                        -P "${deps}/write-notices.cmake"

expect_file_text("${notices}" "Licence: Microsoft Software License Terms for Microsoft Visual Studio")
execute_process(COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" "-DOUTPUT=${notices}" "-DENTRIES=${entries}"
                        -DMSVC_RUNTIME=OFF -P "${deps}/write-notices.cmake"
                RESULT_VARIABLE status)

expect_no_file_text("${notices}" "Visual C\\+\\+ runtime")
```

`tests/deps/test_versions.cmake`: the probe mode also takes `-DBUNDLE=ON|OFF`, sets
`DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` to it before including `versions.cmake`, and checks after the existing name
check:
- the shipped names (entries whose `DVV_THIRD_PARTY_<name>_SHIPPED` is true), sorted, equal
  `Vulkan-Headers;VulkanMemoryAllocator;nlohmann_json;vk-bootstrap;wxWidgets` plus `custom_ffmpeg`
  (sorted in) when `BUNDLE` is `ON`; otherwise `FATAL_ERROR "shipped for <platform> (DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=<b>):
  <list>, expected <list>"`;
- `DVV_SOURCE_PIN_NAMES` is exactly `lame`;
- it prints `<platform> (DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=<b>): <names>; shipped: <shipped>`.
The driver runs the probe for each platform with `-DBUNDLE=ON` and `-DBUNDLE=OFF` (four runs).
Use `list(SORT)` with `CASE INSENSITIVE` off (plain `list(SORT)`) on both lists before comparing.

rawreel packages`; `cmake -P rawreel-workspace/tests/deps/test_versions.cmake` → `shipped for

```powershell
build: allow LGPL-2.0-or-later and BSD-3-Clause-Clear for run-time libraries

LAME (LGPL-2.0-or-later) and SVT-AV1 (BSD-3-Clause-Clear) occur only inside custom_ffmpeg's
libraries, which rawreel loads at run time and never links (controller decisions, M4 design v2 §2;
BSD-3-Clause-Clear is flagged for the user). Both stay rejected for static linking.

build: mark shipped third-party entries and pin source archives

dvv_third_party(... SHIPPED) marks an entry whose code is in a rawreel package (never a tool);
dvv_source_pin() pins a source archive the superbuild never downloads. write-notices.cmake takes
its first line and, for Windows packages, a Visual C++ runtime paragraph.
```

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File rawreel-workspace/bootstrap.ps1 -Toolchain msvc *> _build/m4-t1-bootstrap-msvc.log; "exit=$LASTEXITCODE"
wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel-workspace && ./bootstrap.sh --toolchain clang > ../_build/m4-t1-bootstrap-clang.log 2>&1; echo exit=$?'
```

each log, within a few minutes: only custom_ffmpeg is downloaded and extracted; no other
dependency is rebuilt (no compiler lines in the log). A failing archive SHA256 means a wrong pin
(compare with Task 0).

```powershell
foreach ($t in 'msvc','clang') {
    foreach ($f in 'THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt','THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES-all.txt') {
        $names = [regex]::Matches((Get-Content -Raw "_third_party/$t/$f"), '(?m)^=+\r?\n(.+?)\r?$') | ForEach-Object { $_.Groups[1].Value }
        "== $t ${f}: " + ($names -join ' | ')
    }
    "== $t sdk: " + (Test-Path "_third_party/$t/lib/cmake/dvv_ffmpeg_sdk/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake")
    Select-String -Path "_third_party/$t/lib/cmake/dvv_ffmpeg_sdk/dvv_ffmpeg_sdkConfig.cmake" -Pattern '^set\(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
    "== $t state: " + (Test-Path "_third_party/$t/dvv-third-party-state.cmake")
    Select-String -Path "_third_party/$t/dvv-third-party-state.cmake" -Pattern '^set\((DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG|DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY|DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION)' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
}
```

- `state: True`, `set(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG ON)`,

  and `set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION "1.0.0")` for both.

```powershell
build: add custom_ffmpeg and the DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG option

The custom_ffmpeg v1.0.0 LGPL shared archives (runtime linkage, prebuilt; attestation verified at
pinning) and the LAME 3.100 source pin. THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt lists only the shipped entries (and
the Visual C++ runtime on Windows); THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES-all.txt keeps every entry. The superbuild
option DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG (default ON; bootstrap -BundleFfmpeg / --bundle-ffmpeg) decides whether
FFmpeg ships, and the prefix gets the dvv_ffmpeg_sdk package with FFmpeg's headers, libraries and
programs, and dvv-third-party-state.cmake with what the packages ship for the packaging scripts.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C rawreel-workspace commit -F -
cmake -DREPO=rawreel-workspace -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake
```

Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

**Expected results:** the gate, gate-declaration, superbuild-script and versions tests pass on
Windows; both prefixes hold custom_ffmpeg, the two notices files, `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk` and
`dvv-third-party-state.cmake` (the clang prefix from the WSL `bootstrap.sh` run);
the workspace has one more CTest test (`workspace.ffmpeg_sdk`, `vs2026`, Debug); the
`OFF` variant drops custom_ffmpeg from the shipped notices only. No C++ lint (the workspace has no
`.clang-format`; keep the consumer in the style of `tests/deps/wx-consumer/main.cpp`).

**Commit:** three commits in rawreel-workspace: `build: allow LGPL-2.0-or-later and
BSD-3-Clause-Clear for run-time libraries`, `build: mark shipped third-party entries and pin source
archives`, `build: add custom_ffmpeg and the DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG option`.

**Notes:**
- Design v2 §9 (`deps/versions.cmake`, `DvvThirdParty.cmake`, `DvvSpdx.cmake`,
  `write-notices.cmake`, `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`), D3, D19, D20, D27, §2 (controller decisions; [U4]
  default), arbiter fixes 1, 2 and 3 (the wiring stays in Task 3).
- Note (plan choice): the LAME pin is a `dvv_source_pin()` declaration in `versions.cmake`, so the
  superbuild never downloads it and the gate's "every dependency is declared" rule stays true; the
  mechanism of the download is Task 8's `file(DOWNLOAD … EXPECTED_HASH)`.
- Note (plan choice): the prefix publishes FFmpeg through a generated package `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk`, so
  dvv-components (Task 4), the fixtures (Tasks 5a, 5b, 6, 7a) and packaging (Task 8) read one set of
  paths instead of each guessing the archive layout. It finds the archive's top folder by its
  `include/libavutil/avutil.h`, so Task 0's layout needs no code.
- Note (plan choice): the Visual C++ runtime paragraph is written only when the superbuild compiler
  is MSVC: only the Windows package carries the runtime DLLs (D17).
- Note (plan choice): `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` is part of the bootstrap stamp, so flipping it reruns the
  superbuild (only the notices, the package file and the state file change). The option does not
  remove FFmpeg from the prefix: development, tests and the oracle always need it.
- Note (plan choice): the default `ON` is written in three places: the `option()` in
  `deps/CMakeLists.txt`, `bootstrap.ps1` (`$BundleFfmpeg = 'ON'`) and `bootstrap.sh`
  (`bundle_ffmpeg=ON`), because bootstrap always passes the value. A [U4] decline changes all three
  (Task 12), so CI and `release.yml`, which call bootstrap without the flag, follow it.
- Note (plan choice): the prefix gets two generated files with the same facts from one producer:
  the package `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk` for CMake projects and `dvv-third-party-state.cmake` for `cmake -P`
  scripts (Task 8), which cannot load a package that creates a target.
- Note (plan choice): `SHIPPED` with `LINKAGE tool` is a configuration error; validation layers,
  SPIRV-Tools, doctest and slang are not shipped and get no `SHIPPED`.
- Risk: if a custom_ffmpeg licence file names a licence outside the SPDX expression, Task 0 stopped
  already; a gate change needs the controller.
- Note (U-FF): the allowances follow what the pinned archives contain (Behaviour "Codec scope"):

  configure, sanity builds). The WSL script tests and the Linux workspace presets of plan v2 are

  sanity build).

### Task 2: Platform and media additions (`DllDirectory`; `formatAlias`, `uri_colour.h`)

**Goal:** dvv-platform loads a run-time library by absolute path with its dependencies taken from
the library's own folder (`LibrarySearch::DllDirectory`, design v2 §4.1, M1b F9), for the FFmpeg
loader. dvv-media gains `formatAlias` for FFmpeg's full-range `yuvj*` names (§4.3) and the public
header `uri_colour.h`, the M3 colour-query parser moved out of the private `detail` header without
behaviour change (§4.3, D28), so the ffmpeg provider uses the same parser as Y4M.

**Files:**
- dvv-platform (branch `m4`):
  - Modify `include/dvv/platform/dynamic_library.h`: `LibrarySearch::DllDirectory` and the
    documented error.
  - Modify `src/dynamic_library.cpp`, `src/dynamic_library_detail.h`, `src/dynamic_library_win32.cpp`,
    `src/dynamic_library_posix.cpp`: the absolute-path check and the Windows flags.
  - Create `tests/test_dependency.cpp`, `tests/test_dependent.cpp`; modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`
    and `tests/test_dynamic_library.cpp`.
  - Modify `README.md`.
- dvv-media (branch `m4`):
  - Modify `include/dvv/media/pixel_format.h` and the source file that implements `findFormat`
    (M2 Task 2; find it in Step 1): `FormatAlias`, `formatAlias`.
  - Create `include/dvv/media/uri_colour.h` (complete below) and `src/uri_colour.cpp` (the moved
    implementation); modify `CMakeLists.txt` (add `src/uri_colour.cpp` to `dvv_media`).
  - Modify `src/sources/uri_params.h`, `src/sources/uri_params.cpp`: remove the moved parts,
    include the public header; update every call site of the three moved names in `src/` and
    `tests/`.
  - Create `tests/test_uri_colour.cpp`; modify `tests/test_pixel_format.cpp`,
    `tests/test_uri_params.cpp`, `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/package/main.cpp`.
  - Modify `README.md`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M1b `DynamicLibrary`/`LibrarySearch` (`dynamic_library.h`, shown in Step 1); dvv-base
  `dvv::paths::executablePath()` (`dvv/base/paths.h`, M0); M2 `findFormat`, `FormatId`,
  `ColourRange`; M3 `src/sources/uri_params.h` (`COLOUR_PARAMETERS`, `colourFromQuery`,
  `colourParameters`, private, namespace `dvv::media::detail`), `validateColour`, `ColourParams`
  (with `kr`/`kb`), dvv-base `Uri`/`parseUri`.
- Produces (Task 4 relies on the first, Task 5a on the others):

```cpp
// dvv-platform: include/dvv/platform/dynamic_library.h (changed parts)
enum class LibrarySearch : std::uint8_t {
    Default,          // Windows LoadLibraryExW(name, nullptr, 0); Linux dlopen(RTLD_NOW|RTLD_LOCAL)
    SystemDirectory,  // Windows LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32 only (not app dir, cwd, PATH; an
                      // already loaded module of the same name is returned as is);
                      // Linux as Default. `name` must be a bare file name.
    // `name` is an absolute path (else Errc::Internal). Windows: LoadLibraryExW with
    // LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR | LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_APPLICATION_DIR |
    // LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32: the library's dependencies come only from its own folder, the
    // executable's folder and System32 ('/' is accepted and passed on as '\'). Linux: dlopen of the
    // path (RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL); dependencies through the library's RUNPATH or sonames already
    // loaded.
    DllDirectory,
};
// DynamicLibrary::open: Errc::Internal also for a DllDirectory name that is not an absolute path
// ("DynamicLibrary::open: '<name>' must be an absolute path with LibrarySearch::DllDirectory").
```

```cpp
// dvv-media: include/dvv/media/pixel_format.h (addition)
// FFmpeg's deprecated full-range names (M4 design D4): what a decoder output named yuvj420p,
// yuvj422p or yuvj444p is in the catalogue, with the range its name implies.
struct FormatAlias {
    FormatId format = 0;
    ColourRange range = ColourRange::Unspecified;
    bool operator==(const FormatAlias&) const = default;
};
// yuvj420p -> {yuv420p, Full}, yuvj422p -> {yuv422p, Full}, yuvj444p -> {yuv444p, Full}; nullopt
// for every other name, catalogue names included. Exact, case-sensitive. findFormat() does not know
// these names. Used by the FFmpeg providers.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<FormatAlias> formatAlias(std::string_view name) noexcept;
```

```cpp
// dvv-media: include/dvv/media/uri_colour.h (complete)
#pragma once

// The colour query parameters of source URIs (spec §6.3, §7.2; M3 design D21, M4 design D28): range,
// matrix (with kr and kb for matrix=custom), transfer and primaries. Every provider that takes
// colour overrides parses them here (raw, synthetic and Y4M in dvv-media, the ffmpeg provider in
// dvv-components), and resolved URIs write them back with colourParameters().

#include <array>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/uri.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media {

// The colour query parameters every provider accepts.
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 6> COLOUR_PARAMETERS{
    "range", "matrix", "kr", "kb", "transfer", "primaries"};
// range=full|limited; matrix=bt601|bt709|bt2020|rgb|custom (bt2020 -> Bt2020Ncl); kr=, kb= (decimal
// floats, only with matrix=custom and then both required); transfer=srgb|bt1886|linear|pq|hlg;
// primaries=bt601-525|bt601-625|bt709|bt2020; absent -> Unspecified. Errc::Format: "bad range 'x'
// (full, limited)", "bad matrix 'x' (bt601, bt709, bt2020, rgb, custom)", "bad transfer 'x' (srgb,
// bt1886, linear, pq, hlg)", "bad primaries 'x' (bt601-525, bt601-625, bt709, bt2020)",
// "matrix=custom needs kr and kb", "kr and kb need matrix=custom", "bad kr 'x'", "bad kb 'x'", or
// validateColour()'s message.
[[nodiscard]] Result<ColourParams> colourFromQuery(const Uri& uri);
// The resolved-URI form of a colour: the fields that are not Unspecified, in the order range,
// matrix, kr, kb, transfer, primaries, with the canonical names above; kr and kb (Custom only) as
// std::format("{}", value) (shortest text that reads back to the same float).
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> colourParameters(
    const ColourParams& colour);

}  // namespace dvv::media
```

  The comments are those of M3's private header; if the real M3 code worded them differently,
  keep the real wording (the move changes no behaviour and no text).
- Not built: `dvv::platform::executablePath()` (see Notes); dvv-gpu is not touched (design v2 §4.2).

**Behaviour:**
- **`DllDirectory`** (dvv-platform):
  - `open` checks, after the existing empty-name and NUL checks: `search == DllDirectory` and
    `!detail::isAbsolutePath(name)` → `Internal` with the message above.
  - `detail::isAbsolutePath(std::string_view) noexcept`, per platform: Windows `X:\…` or `X:/…`
    (an ASCII letter, `:`, a slash) or a UNC path (two leading slashes of either kind); Linux a
    leading `/`. `C:x.dll` (drive-relative) is not absolute.
  - Windows `loadLibrary`: for `DllDirectory`, every `/` of the wide path becomes `\` and the flags
    are the three of the header comment (`LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR` needs a fully
    qualified path with backslashes). The error dialog suppression and the message format stay.
  - Linux: no change in `loadLibrary` (the path goes to `dlopen` as is).
  - A failed load stays `Unsupported "'<name>' could not be loaded: <system message>"`.
- **`formatAlias`** (dvv-media): a fixed table of the three names; the target id is
  `findFormat("<yuv*p name>")` (catalogue lookups; never interns). `findFormat` is unchanged.
- **`uri_colour.h`** (dvv-media): the three names move from `dvv::media::detail` (private header)
  to `dvv::media` (public header) with the same declarations, comments and behaviour. The
  definitions move from `src/sources/uri_params.cpp` (library `dvv_media_sources`) to the new
  `src/uri_colour.cpp` in the core library `dvv_media`, so dvv-components needs only `dvv::media`.
  `src/sources/uri_params.h` includes `dvv/media/uri_colour.h` and no longer declares them; call
  sites use `dvv::media::colourFromQuery` (or the unqualified name inside `dvv::media`). No
  `using`-aliases in `detail`. No `Errc` value or message changes.

**Code** (dvv-platform; the Windows flags are easy to get wrong):

```cpp
// src/dynamic_library_win32.cpp, inside loadLibrary (replaces the flags line)
    std::wstring path = *wide;
    DWORD flags = 0;
    switch (search) {
    case LibrarySearch::Default:
        break;
    case LibrarySearch::SystemDirectory:
        flags = LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32;
        break;
    case LibrarySearch::DllDirectory:
        // LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR needs a fully qualified path with backslashes.
        std::ranges::replace(path, L'/', L'\\');
        flags = LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR | LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_APPLICATION_DIR |
                LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32;
        break;
    }
    // ... LoadLibraryExW(path.c_str(), nullptr, flags) as before
```

```cpp
// tests/test_dependent.cpp: a run-time library that needs test_dependency at load time.
#if defined(_WIN32)
#define DVV_TEST_EXPORT __declspec(dllexport)
#define DVV_TEST_IMPORT __declspec(dllimport)
#else
#define DVV_TEST_EXPORT __attribute__((visibility("default")))
#define DVV_TEST_IMPORT
#endif

extern "C" DVV_TEST_IMPORT int dvvTestDependencyValue();

extern "C" DVV_TEST_EXPORT int dvvTestDependentValue() {
    return dvvTestDependencyValue() + 2;
}
```

```cmake
# tests/CMakeLists.txt (after dvv_platform_test_module)
# LibrarySearch::DllDirectory: a run-time library whose dependency sits in its own folder, away from
# the test executable and the working directory, so only a search of the library's own folder
# finds it. $<CONFIG> in the folder keeps multi-config generators from adding their own subfolder.
set(dll_directory "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dll-directory/$<CONFIG>")
add_library(dvv_platform_test_dependency SHARED test_dependency.cpp)
add_library(dvv_platform_test_dependent MODULE test_dependent.cpp)
target_link_libraries(dvv_platform_test_dependent PRIVATE dvv_platform_test_dependency)
set_target_properties(dvv_platform_test_dependency dvv_platform_test_dependent PROPERTIES
    RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY "${dll_directory}"
    LIBRARY_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY "${dll_directory}"
    BUILD_RPATH "$ORIGIN")
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_platform_test_dependency)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_platform_test_dependent)
# (in the existing target_compile_definitions(dvv_platform_tests ...) list, add)
#   "DVV_PLATFORM_TEST_DEPENDENT=\"$<TARGET_FILE:dvv_platform_test_dependent>\""
# (and) add_dependencies(dvv_platform_tests dvv_platform_test_dependent)
```

**Notes:**
- Design v2 §4.1 (v1 §4.1 text), §4.3, D4, D28, §8.1 dvv-platform and dvv-media rows, arbiter fix 4.
- Note (plan choice): `executablePath()` is not added to dvv-platform. dvv-base already provides
  `dvv::paths::executablePath()` (M0, `dvv/base/paths.h`: `GetModuleFileNameW` /
  `readlink("/proc/self/exe")`, `Errc::Io` on failure), which is the contract design v2 §4.1 asks
  for; a second copy would add a duplicate API. Task 4 uses the dvv-base function. If Step 1 does
  not find it, add `include/dvv/platform/process.h` with
  `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::filesystem::path> executablePath();` (namespace `dvv::platform`, same
  behaviour) and a test that its file name equals the test executable's.
- Note (plan choice): `findFormat` does not learn the `yuvj*` names; only `formatAlias` maps them.
  Design §4.3 says "findFormat gains the aliases … reported with a Full hint through formatAlias";
  the simplest reading that keeps the range is one lookup that returns both. If `findFormat` knew
  them, a raw or Y4M `fmt=yuvj420p` would silently open as limited range, and custom-format names
  would collide with them.
- Note (plan choice): the moved definitions go into the core library `dvv_media`, not
  `dvv_media_sources`: the latter is a component library linked as a whole archive, which
  dvv-components must not link a second time.
- Note (plan choice): `DllDirectory`'s absolute-path check is lexical (no file-system access); a
  drive-relative Windows path (`C:x.dll`) is refused, because `LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR`
  needs a fully qualified path.
- Risk: on Windows, the first test case only proves the folder search if the dependency is not
  already loaded; the case opens it with the default search first (which must fail) and never calls
  `keepLoaded`.
