### Task 3: dvv-components skeleton, conformance, workspace wiring

**Goal:** Create the local repository `dvv-components` (package `dvv_components` 0.1.0, namespace
`dvv::components`) with the standard skeleton, `dvv_add_component()`/`dvv_components_link()`
(design v2 §4.4, without `SHADERS`), the component conformance test of spec §12 (deferred since
M1a) over a test component and the M2 source providers, a compile-fail case, a package test and the
inactive `ci.yml`; then wire it into the workspace after dvv-media and before rawreel, and run the
G1 workspace workflow.

**Files:**
- dvv-components (new, `git init -b m4`):
  - `.clang-format`, `.clang-tidy`, `.gitattributes`, `.gitignore`, `LICENSE`: byte copies of the
    dvv-media files (MIT, "The rawreel authors").
  - `CMakePresets.json`: includes the workspace presets.
  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: the dvv-media file with `repo: dvv-media` replaced by
    `repo: dvv-components` (inactive until published).
  - `README.md`: description, build instructions, how to add a component.
  - `CMakeLists.txt`: dependencies, the umbrella target `dvv_components`, install and export.
  - `cmake/DvvComponent.cmake` (complete below), `cmake/dvv_componentsConfig.cmake.in`.
  - `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/main.cpp`, `tests/test_conformance.cpp`,
    `tests/components/echo_provider.h`, `tests/components/echo_provider.cpp`,
    `tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`,
    `tests/cmake/option-project/{CMakeLists.txt,a.cpp,b.cpp,main.cpp}`,
    `tests/cmake/run-component-option-test.cmake`,
    `tests/package/{CMakeLists.txt,main.cpp,run-package-test.cmake}`.
- rawreel-workspace: `repos.txt`, `cmake/dvv-versions.cmake`, `CMakeLists.txt`,
  `cmake/DvvPresets.json`, `README.md`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (bootstrapped prefixes); M2 dvv-media (`dvv::media`, `dvv::media_sources` with
  the providers `raw`, `synthetic`, `y4m`; `ISourceProvider`, `SourceRequest`, `Match`,
  `FormatGuess`, `OpenTicket`, `OpenContext`, `source_factory.h`); M1a/M1b dvv-registry
  (`ComponentRegistry::create`, `list`, `createComponent<I>`, `ComponentStatus`, `DVV_REGISTER`,
  `Capabilities`, `require`, `Kind`, `dvv_link_components()` from `DvvComponents.cmake`); dvv-base
  (`Managed`, `CreationKey`, `LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount()`, `ManagedObject::ownerId()`,
  `finishWithLeakCheck`, `DVV_CMAKE_DIR`, `run-compile-fail.cmake`).
- Produces (CMake; Tasks 4, 5a, 5b, 6, 7a, 8 rely on these):
  - package `dvv_components` 0.1.0; umbrella target `dvv_components` (INTERFACE, alias
    `dvv::components`, export name `components`, `INTERFACE dvv::media`). An application links
    `dvv::components` and calls `dvv_components_link(<exe>)`. Task 4 adds `dvv::ffmpeg` to it.
  - `DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR` (cache, internal; in-tree `<repo>/cmake`, installed the package's
    config folder) and `${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvComponent.cmake` with
    `dvv_add_component(<id> KIND <Kind> SOURCES <file>... [LINK <target>...] [REGISTERS <component id>...])`
    and `dvv_components_link(<executable>)` (behaviour in the file's header below).
  - Global properties `DVV_COMPONENTS_REGISTERS` (`<component id>=<Kind>` per registered id) and
    `DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES` (enabled component libraries).
  - Rule: component libraries are added in the top-level `CMakeLists.txt` at the marked place,
    before the install rules and before `add_subdirectory(tests)`; the conformance test and the
    installed package then list them automatically (Task 5a adds
    `dvv_add_component(ffmpeg KIND Source SOURCES … LINK … REGISTERS ffmpeg)`, Task 5b adds
    `lavfi` to its `REGISTERS`).
  - Test executable `dvv_components_tests` (CTest `dvv_components.unit`): unit tests and the
    conformance cases; it links every enabled component (`dvv_components_link`) and
    `dvv::media_sources`; generated header `expected_components.h`
    (`dvv::components::testing::CMAKE_COMPONENTS`). Later tasks add their unit test files to it.
  - Compile-fail harness `tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp` with case 1; Task 5a appends
    case 2 (`FfmpegSourceProvider` constructed directly) to the source, the case list and the
    pattern list.
  - CTest `dvv_components.cmake_functions`, `dvv_components.compile_fail.1`,
    `dvv_components.package` (not under sanitizers, `RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree`).
  - Workspace: `dvv-components` in `repos.txt`, `dvv_components` declared in `dvv-versions.cmake`,
    made available and sibling-checked in the workspace build, `FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_COMPONENTS`
    in the presets. rawreel does not declare `dvv_components` yet (Task 7a adds it to rawreel's
    dependency list and sibling check). The nightly workspace CI (`workspace.yml`) bootstraps from
    `repos.txt`, so it gains the repository through this task (design §9 "workspace.yml gains
    dvv-components"); Task 9 changes only its `env`.
- Component rules for every component library (spec §9.4, design v2 §6): `final`,
  `Managed<T>`, constructed only by the registry (`CreationKey<ComponentRegistry>`), static
  `info()`, `schema()`, `probe()`; constructors must not call FFmpeg or do I/O (the conformance test
  creates every component with all capabilities but without loading FFmpeg).

**Behaviour:**
- **`dvv_add_component`** (exact rules in the file header below): one option per component
  library, `DVV_COMPONENT_<ID>` (default `ON`); `KIND` must be a `dvv::Kind` name; the library id
  matches `[a-z][a-z0-9_]*`; each `REGISTERS` id matches `[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]*` and is registered at
  most once across all libraries. Errors stop configuration with the messages in the file.
- **Test component `test.echo`** (library id `test_echo`): `final` `ISourceProvider` and
  `Managed<TestEchoProvider>`; `info() = {"test.echo", "Conformance test source", Kind::Source, 1}`;
  empty schema; `probe(caps) = require(caps, {"test.echo"})`; `schemes() = {"test-echo"}`; `match`
  returns `{0, {}, "test component"}`; `open` returns `Unsupported "test component"`. It is added
  only when dvv-components is the top-level project (`PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL`): in the workspace build
  rawreel links every listed component library, and a test component must never reach an
  application.
- **Conformance** (doctest, `tests/test_conformance.cpp`): see Tests first. The capabilities used
  are `ALL_CAPABILITIES = {"ffmpeg", "ffmpeg.lavfi", "test.echo"}` (every capability a v1.0
  component probes for; later components add theirs here).
- **Installed package:** the config finds the five dvv dependencies, includes the targets, sets
  `DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR`, includes `DvvComponent.cmake` and appends the package's component
  libraries (`dvv::component_<id>`) to `DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES` once per configure.

**Code** (CMake; the component rules, the export and the generated list are easy to get wrong):

`dvv-components/cmake/DvvComponent.cmake` (complete):

```cmake
# Component libraries (spec §9.1; M4 design §4.4). Installed with dvv_components and included by its
# package configuration; in-tree the dvv_components project includes it. Needs
# dvv_set_compiler_options() (dvv_base, DvvCompilerOptions.cmake) and dvv_link_components()
# (dvv_registry, DvvComponents.cmake).
#
# dvv_add_component(<id> KIND <Kind> SOURCES <file>... [LINK <target>...]
#                   [REGISTERS <component id>...])
#   Option DVV_COMPONENT_<ID> (default ON; <ID> is <id> in upper case). When ON: a STATIC library
#   dvv_component_<id> (alias dvv::component_<id>, export name component_<id>) from SOURCES, linking
#   dvv::media PUBLIC and the LINK targets PRIVATE, with the dvv compiler options. REGISTERS (default
#   <id>) are the component ids its DVV_REGISTER lines register; each is recorded with KIND in the
#   global property DVV_COMPONENTS_REGISTERS as "<component id>=<Kind>", which the conformance test
#   compares with the registry (spec §12 "registry == enabled CMake set"), and the library is
#   appended to the global property DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES. When OFF: nothing is created or
#   recorded. <id>: [a-z][a-z0-9_]*; component ids: [a-z][a-z0-9_.-]*, each registered once; KIND: a
#   dvv::Kind name. No SHADERS in v1.0 (it comes with the first shader component, v1.x).
# dvv_components_link(<executable>)
#   dvv_link_components(<executable> <every library in DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES>): each as a whole
#   archive, so its DVV_REGISTER lines survive linking.
include_guard(GLOBAL)

function(dvv_add_component id)
    # Inside the function: include_guard(GLOBAL) skips a second include of this file (the installed
    # package found again from another directory), so a file-scope list would be empty there.
    set(DVV_COMPONENT_KINDS Source FormatDetector Exporter Tool Metric Analyser Processor)
    cmake_parse_arguments(PARSE_ARGV 1 arg "" "KIND" "SOURCES;LINK;REGISTERS")
    if(arg_UNPARSED_ARGUMENTS)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_component(${id}): unexpected '${arg_UNPARSED_ARGUMENTS}'")
    endif()
    if(NOT id MATCHES "^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_component(${id}): the id must match [a-z][a-z0-9_]*")
    endif()
    if(NOT arg_KIND IN_LIST DVV_COMPONENT_KINDS)
        list(JOIN DVV_COMPONENT_KINDS ", " kinds)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_component(${id}): KIND must be one of ${kinds}")
    endif()
    if(NOT arg_SOURCES)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_component(${id}): SOURCES is required")
    endif()
    if(NOT arg_REGISTERS)
        set(arg_REGISTERS "${id}")
    endif()
    string(TOUPPER "${id}" upper)
    option(DVV_COMPONENT_${upper} "Build the ${id} component library" ON)
    if(NOT DVV_COMPONENT_${upper})
        message(STATUS "dvv component ${id}: off (DVV_COMPONENT_${upper})")
        return()
    endif()
    get_property(registered GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_REGISTERS)
    foreach(component IN LISTS arg_REGISTERS)
        if(NOT component MATCHES "^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]*$")
            message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_component(${id}): component id '${component}' must match "
                                "[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]*")
        endif()
        foreach(entry IN LISTS registered)
            string(REGEX REPLACE "=[A-Za-z]+$" "" registered_id "${entry}")
            if(registered_id STREQUAL component)
                message(FATAL_ERROR
                        "dvv_add_component(${id}): component '${component}' is registered twice")
            endif()
        endforeach()
    endforeach()

    set(target dvv_component_${id})
    add_library(${target} STATIC ${arg_SOURCES})
    add_library(dvv::component_${id} ALIAS ${target})
    set_target_properties(${target} PROPERTIES EXPORT_NAME component_${id})
    target_link_libraries(${target} PUBLIC dvv::media PRIVATE ${arg_LINK})
    dvv_set_compiler_options(${target})
    foreach(component IN LISTS arg_REGISTERS)
        set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_REGISTERS "${component}=${arg_KIND}")
    endforeach()
    set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES ${target})
endfunction()

function(dvv_components_link target)
    get_property(libraries GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES)
    dvv_link_components(${target} ${libraries})
endfunction()
```

`dvv-components/CMakeLists.txt` (Task 3 state):

```cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_components VERSION 0.1.0 DESCRIPTION "dvv component libraries" LANGUAGES CXX)

option(DVV_BUILD_TESTS "Build the dvv tests" ${PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL})

# The dvv dependencies: inside the workspace build cmake/dvv-versions.cmake has declared them first,
# so these declarations are ignored there (spec §3.1). Stand-alone builds take them from the sibling
# folders (FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_<NAME> in the workspace presets), from installed packages or from
# Git.
include(FetchContent)
foreach(dependency IN ITEMS base registry platform gpu media)
    FetchContent_Declare(dvv_${dependency}
        GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-${dependency}.git"
        GIT_TAG main
        FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
endforeach()
# A stand-alone build tests only this repository (the normal variable hides the cache entry from
# the dependencies' option()); as a subproject the parent's value is kept, and the workspace
# build tests every repository.
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    set(DVV_BUILD_TESTS OFF)
endif()
FetchContent_MakeAvailable(dvv_base dvv_registry dvv_platform dvv_gpu dvv_media)
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    unset(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
endif()
if(NOT DVV_CMAKE_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_base did not define DVV_CMAKE_DIR")
endif()
include("${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvCompilerOptions.cmake")
include(GNUInstallDirs)
include(CMakePackageConfigHelpers)

# Higher repositories use "${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvComponent.cmake" (in-tree here, installed
# through the package configuration).
set(DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake" CACHE INTERNAL
    "dvv-components CMake modules")
include("${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvComponent.cmake")

# What an application links besides dvv_components_link(<exe>).
add_library(dvv_components INTERFACE)
add_library(dvv::components ALIAS dvv_components)
set_target_properties(dvv_components PROPERTIES EXPORT_NAME components)
target_link_libraries(dvv_components INTERFACE dvv::media)

# Component libraries (dvv_add_component) go here, before the install rules and the tests: the
# installed package and the conformance test list them.

get_property(component_libraries GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES)
install(TARGETS dvv_components ${component_libraries} EXPORT dvv_componentsTargets)
set(config_dir ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/dvv_components)
install(EXPORT dvv_componentsTargets NAMESPACE dvv:: DESTINATION ${config_dir})
set(DVV_COMPONENTS_INSTALLED_LIBRARIES "")
foreach(library IN LISTS component_libraries)
    string(REGEX REPLACE "^dvv_" "dvv::" exported "${library}")
    list(APPEND DVV_COMPONENTS_INSTALLED_LIBRARIES "${exported}")
endforeach()
configure_package_config_file(cmake/dvv_componentsConfig.cmake.in
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_componentsConfig.cmake INSTALL_DESTINATION ${config_dir})
write_basic_package_version_file(${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_componentsConfigVersion.cmake
    COMPATIBILITY SameMinorVersion)
install(FILES
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_componentsConfig.cmake
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_componentsConfigVersion.cmake
    cmake/DvvComponent.cmake
    DESTINATION ${config_dir})

if(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
    enable_testing()
    add_subdirectory(tests)
endif()
```

`dvv-components/cmake/dvv_componentsConfig.cmake.in`:

```cmake
@PACKAGE_INIT@

include(CMakeFindDependencyMacro)
find_dependency(dvv_base 0.1)
find_dependency(dvv_registry 0.1)
find_dependency(dvv_platform 0.1)
# dvv_gpu needs the pinned Vulkan-Headers of the third-party prefix in CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH.
find_dependency(dvv_gpu 0.1)
find_dependency(dvv_media 0.1)

include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/dvv_componentsTargets.cmake")

# dvv_add_component() and dvv_components_link(), and the component libraries this package was built
# with (its DVV_COMPONENT_<ID> options), which dvv_components_link() links as whole archives.
set(DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}")
include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/DvvComponent.cmake")
get_property(_dvv_components_listed GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_PACKAGE_LISTED)
if(NOT _dvv_components_listed)
    set_property(GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_PACKAGE_LISTED TRUE)
    set_property(GLOBAL APPEND PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_LIBRARIES @DVV_COMPONENTS_INSTALLED_LIBRARIES@)
endif()

check_required_components(dvv_components)
```

`dvv-components/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (Task 3 state):

```cmake
find_package(doctest 2.5 REQUIRED CONFIG)

# The conformance test component test.echo exists only when dvv-components is the top-level
# project: in the workspace build rawreel links every listed component library, and a test
# component must never reach an application.
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    dvv_add_component(test_echo KIND Source SOURCES components/echo_provider.cpp
                      REGISTERS test.echo)
    if(TARGET dvv_component_test_echo)
        target_include_directories(dvv_component_test_echo PRIVATE
                                   ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/components)
    endif()
endif()

# The enabled components (spec §12: the registry equals the enabled CMake set) as
# dvv::components::testing::CMAKE_COMPONENTS, one {"<component id>", dvv::Kind::<Kind>} each.
get_property(registers GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_COMPONENTS_REGISTERS)
list(SORT registers)
list(LENGTH registers expected_count)
set(expected_init "{}")
if(expected_count GREATER 0)
    set(expected_init "{{\n")
    foreach(entry IN LISTS registers)
        string(REGEX MATCH "^(.+)=([A-Za-z]+)$" unused "${entry}")
        string(APPEND expected_init "    {\"${CMAKE_MATCH_1}\", dvv::Kind::${CMAKE_MATCH_2}},\n")
    endforeach()
    string(APPEND expected_init "}}")
endif()
file(CONFIGURE OUTPUT ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/expected_components.h CONTENT [[
// Generated by dvv-components/tests/CMakeLists.txt: the component ids that dvv_add_component()
// enabled in this configuration, with their kinds (sorted by id).
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/registry/component_info.h"

namespace dvv::components::testing {

struct ExpectedComponent {
    std::string_view id;
    dvv::Kind kind;
};

inline constexpr std::array<ExpectedComponent, @expected_count@> CMAKE_COMPONENTS@expected_init@;

}  // namespace dvv::components::testing
]] @ONLY)

add_executable(dvv_components_tests
    main.cpp
    test_conformance.cpp)
target_include_directories(dvv_components_tests PRIVATE
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/components)
target_link_libraries(dvv_components_tests PRIVATE dvv::components doctest::doctest)
# Every enabled component and the M2 source providers, as whole archives (their DVV_REGISTER lines).
dvv_components_link(dvv_components_tests)
dvv_link_components(dvv_components_tests dvv::media_sources)
# doctest otherwise only forward-declares std::ostream, which breaks printing std types.
target_compile_definitions(dvv_components_tests PRIVATE DOCTEST_CONFIG_USE_STD_HEADERS)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_components_tests)
add_test(NAME dvv_components.unit COMMAND dvv_components_tests)
# The timeout turns a hang into a failure long before the CI job limit.
set_tests_properties(dvv_components.unit PROPERTIES
    TIMEOUT 300
    ENVIRONMENT DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail
    ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "${DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT}")

# dvv_add_component() and dvv_components_link() at the CMake level: options, recorded ids and
# errors, in configure-only runs of a stub project.
add_test(NAME dvv_components.cmake_functions
         COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                 -DPROJECT_DIR=${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/option-project
                 -DWORK_DIR=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/cmake-functions
                 -DGENERATOR=${CMAKE_GENERATOR}
                 -DPLATFORM=${CMAKE_GENERATOR_PLATFORM}
                 -DCXX_COMPILER=${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}
                 -DCOMPONENT_MODULE=${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvComponent.cmake
                 -DREGISTRY_MODULE=${dvv_registry_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/DvvComponents.cmake
                 -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/run-component-option-test.cmake)
set_tests_properties(dvv_components.cmake_functions PROPERTIES TIMEOUT 300)

# Compile-fail tests (spec §9.4). Case 0 is the control and must build; each other case must fail to
# compile with its pattern. Task 5a appends case 2 (FfmpegSourceProvider).
set(compile_fail_source ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp)
add_executable(dvv_components_compile_ok ${compile_fail_source})
target_compile_definitions(dvv_components_compile_ok PRIVATE DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE=0)
target_include_directories(dvv_components_compile_ok PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/components)
target_link_libraries(dvv_components_compile_ok PRIVATE dvv::media)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_components_compile_ok)
if(MSVC)
    set(compile_fail_patterns "C2248")
else()
    set(compile_fail_patterns "private constructor")
endif()
foreach(case IN ITEMS 1)
    set(target dvv_components_compile_fail_${case})
    add_executable(${target} EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL ${compile_fail_source})
    target_compile_definitions(${target} PRIVATE DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE=${case})
    target_include_directories(${target} PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/components)
    target_link_libraries(${target} PRIVATE dvv::media)
    dvv_set_compiler_options(${target})
    math(EXPR pattern_index "${case} - 1")
    list(GET compile_fail_patterns ${pattern_index} pattern)
    # cmake --build needs the top-level tree; in the workspace build dvv-components is a subproject.
    add_test(NAME dvv_components.compile_fail.${case}
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                     -DBUILD_DIR=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}
                     -DCONFIG=$<CONFIG>
                     -DTARGET=${target}
                     "-DPATTERN=${pattern}"
                     -P ${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/run-compile-fail.cmake)
    set_tests_properties(dvv_components.compile_fail.${case} PROPERTIES
                         RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree)
endforeach()

# Package test (spec §3.1): install, then build a consumer with find_package(dvv_components) and
# another with dvv_components in-tree. Skipped for sanitizer builds, whose consumers would need the
# same runtime flags.
if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)
    string(REPLACE ";" "," deps_prefix_path "${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}")
    add_test(NAME dvv_components.package
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                     -DBUILD_DIR=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}
                     -DCONFIG=$<CONFIG>
                     -DPREFIX=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/package-prefix
                     -DSOURCE_DIR=${dvv_components_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DBASE_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_base_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DREGISTRY_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_registry_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DPLATFORM_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_platform_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DGPU_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_gpu_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DMEDIA_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_media_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DCONSUMER_SOURCE=${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/package
                     -DCONSUMER_BUILD=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/package-consumer
                     -DGENERATOR=${CMAKE_GENERATOR}
                     -DPLATFORM=${CMAKE_GENERATOR_PLATFORM}
                     -DCXX_COMPILER=${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}
                     -DDEPS_PREFIX_PATH=${deps_prefix_path}
                     -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/package/run-package-test.cmake)
    # Package and compile-fail tests of every dvv repository drive the same build tree in the
    # workspace build, so they share one lock name.
    set_tests_properties(dvv_components.package PROPERTIES RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree)
endif()
```

```cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

include("${COMPONENT_MODULE}")

    dvv_add_component(probe_b KIND Source SOURCES b.cpp REGISTERS probe.same)

    dvv_add_component(probe_b KIND Tool SOURCES b.cpp)

else()

message(STATUS "registers=<${registers}> libraries=<${libraries}> probe_a_target=<${probe_a_target}> "
```

`a.cpp`, `b.cpp`: one function each (`int probeA() { return 1; }`, `int probeB() { return 2; }`);
`main.cpp`: `int main() { return 0; }`.

`tests/cmake/run-component-option-test.cmake`:

```cmake
# Configures tests/cmake/option-project once per case and checks what dvv_add_component() and
# dvv_components_link() did (M4 design §4.4). Called by dvv_components.cmake_functions with
# -DPROJECT_DIR, -DWORK_DIR, -DGENERATOR, -DPLATFORM (may be empty), -DCXX_COMPILER,
# -DCOMPONENT_MODULE and -DREGISTRY_MODULE.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
foreach(variable IN ITEMS PROJECT_DIR WORK_DIR GENERATOR CXX_COMPILER COMPONENT_MODULE
                          REGISTRY_MODULE)
    if("${${variable}}" STREQUAL "")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "pass -D${variable}")
    endif()
endforeach()
file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${WORK_DIR}")

# check(<case> <0|1 expected failure> <expected output regex> [-D<name>=<value>...])
function(check case expect_failure expected_text)
    set(command "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -S "${PROJECT_DIR}" -B "${WORK_DIR}/${case}" -G "${GENERATOR}"
        "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=${CXX_COMPILER}" "-DCOMPONENT_MODULE=${COMPONENT_MODULE}"
        "-DREGISTRY_MODULE=${REGISTRY_MODULE}" "-DCASE=${case}" ${ARGN})
    if(PLATFORM)
        list(APPEND command -A "${PLATFORM}")
    endif()
    execute_process(COMMAND ${command} RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE out ERROR_VARIABLE err)
    # CMake wraps long messages; compare with every run of white space folded into one space.
    string(REGEX REPLACE "[ \t\r\n]+" " " output "${out}${err}")
    if(expect_failure AND status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "FAIL: case ${case} configured")
    elseif(NOT expect_failure AND NOT status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "FAIL: case ${case} failed:\n${out}${err}")
    elseif(NOT output MATCHES "${expected_text}")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "FAIL: case ${case} lacks '${expected_text}':\n${out}${err}")
    endif()
endfunction()

set(link_a "\\$<LINK_LIBRARY:WHOLE_ARCHIVE,dvv_component_probe_a>")
set(link_b "\\$<LINK_LIBRARY:WHOLE_ARCHIVE,dvv_component_probe_b>")
check(default 0 "registers=<probe\\.a=Source;probe\\.b=Source;probe_b=Tool> libraries=<dvv_component_probe_a;dvv_component_probe_b> probe_a_target=<yes> app_links=<${link_a};${link_b}>")
check(probe_a_off 0 "dvv component probe_a: off \\(DVV_COMPONENT_PROBE_A\\).*registers=<probe_b=Tool> libraries=<dvv_component_probe_b> probe_a_target=<no> app_links=<${link_b}>"
      -DDVV_COMPONENT_PROBE_A=OFF)
check(bad_kind 1 "dvv_add_component\\(probe_bad\\): KIND must be one of Source, FormatDetector, Exporter, Tool, Metric, Analyser, Processor")
check(bad_id 1 "dvv_add_component\\(Probe-Bad\\): the id must match")
check(twice 1 "dvv_add_component\\(probe_b\\): component 'probe\\.same' is registered twice")
message(STATUS "component option tests passed")
```

`tests/test_conformance.cpp` (doctest; `ALL_CAPABILITIES` and `M2_PROVIDERS = {raw, synthetic,
y4m}` (kind `Source`) as file-local constants; `expected()` = `CMAKE_COMPONENTS` plus

2. `conformance: every component is available, valid, created and destroyed cleanly` — for each

   `schema != nullptr` and `schema->resolve({})` has a value; `!info.name.empty()`;

   has `ownerId() == registry->objectId()` and whose `schemes()` is not empty; after the component
   is destroyed `liveCount()` equals the value taken before.

   why` — `ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{})->list()` has the same ids as
   `expected()`; every status is available or has a non-empty `unavailable->reason`; the three M2

   reason contains `test.echo`.

```cpp
// Compile-fail cases for the components of dvv-components (spec §9.4). DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE selects
// one:
//   0  control: the component's header compiles
//   1  constructing a component with a forged registry key (CreationKey's constructor is private)
// Task 5a appends case 2 (FfmpegSourceProvider constructed directly).
#include <type_traits>

#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "echo_provider.h"

using dvv::components::testing::TestEchoProvider;

int main() {
#if DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 0
    static_assert(std::is_final_v<TestEchoProvider>);
    static_assert(!std::is_copy_constructible_v<TestEchoProvider>);
    return 0;
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 1
    TestEchoProvider provider(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry>(
                                  dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<TestEchoProvider>()),
                              dvv::ParameterValues{});
    return 0;
#endif
}
```

```powershell
feat: add dvv-components with dvv_add_component and the conformance test

The repository mirrors dvv-media (presets from rawreel-workspace, reusable CI, package test, MIT
licence) and depends on dvv-base, dvv-registry, dvv-platform, dvv-gpu and dvv-media.

- dvv_add_component() builds one static library per component (option DVV_COMPONENT_<ID>) and
  records the component ids it registers; dvv_components_link() links every enabled one as a whole
  archive.
- The conformance test (spec §12) checks that the registry holds exactly the enabled components
  and the built-in source providers, and that each is valid, available with its capabilities, owned
  by the registry and destroyed without a leak; a test component exists only in stand-alone builds.
- A compile-fail case proves a component cannot be built with a forged registry key.
```

**Notes:**
- Design v2 §3 (dvv-components targets and files), §4.4 (`dvv_add_component` without `SHADERS`),
  §6, §8.1 unit row (conformance), §9 "Wiring" (Task 3, not Task 1), arbiter fixes 3 and 17.
- Note (plan choice): the umbrella target `dvv::components` (INTERFACE) gives the package an
  exported target from its first commit and gives applications one name to link; Task 4 adds
  `dvv::ffmpeg` to it.
- Note (plan choice): `LINK` targets are linked `PRIVATE` (static libraries still pass them on to
  the final link); a component's public surface is `dvv::media`.
- Note (plan choice): the test component `test.echo` is built only when dvv-components is the
  top-level project, because `dvv_components_link()` links every listed component into rawreel in
  the workspace build. The workspace build still runs the conformance cases over the M2 providers
  and every real component.
- Note (plan choice): "option OFF removes a component from both" is tested at the CMake level
  (`dvv_components.cmake_functions`: the recorded ids, the library and the executable's link list
  lose it); at run time the expected list and the linked set come from the same global properties,
  so they cannot differ.
- Note (plan choice): `KIND` is recorded and checked against the registry by the conformance test;
  v1.0 components are all `Source`, so the test creates them through `ISourceProvider` and fails
  clearly for any other kind until an interface exists (v1.x).
- Risk: the package test builds dvv-gpu and dvv-media from source for the in-tree consumer and takes
  several minutes; that is expected.

### Task 4: FFmpeg run-time binding

**Goal:** dvv-components loads FFmpeg at run time and never links it (design v2 D3, §4.4): one
`extern "C"` wrapper header, a function table resolved from the six libraries through
`DynamicLibrary` with `LibrarySearch::DllDirectory`, the search order with final explicit sources,
the checks (major/minor versions against the headers, LGPL licence, no `--enable-gpl`/
`--enable-nonfree`), `keepLoaded`, FFmpeg's log routed to the dvv logger, the AVERROR mapping of
§7, the public status header `ffmpeg.h`, and a check that no binary imports an FFmpeg library.

**Files** (dvv-components, branch `m4`):
- Create `include/dvv/components/ffmpeg.h` (complete below).
- Create `src/ffmpeg/ffmpeg_headers.h` (complete below), `src/ffmpeg/api.h` (complete below),
  `src/ffmpeg/loader_detail.h` (complete below).
- Create `src/ffmpeg/checks.cpp` (`refusalReason`, `planSearch`, `sourceName`, `libraryFileName`,
  `versionText`), `src/ffmpeg/error.cpp` (`errcForAverror`, `ffmpegError`, the deleters),
  `src/ffmpeg/loader.cpp` (`loadFfmpeg`, `addFfmpegCapabilities`, `ffmpeg::api()`, the log
  callback).
- Create `cmake/check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake` (complete below); modify `cmake/DvvComponent.cmake`
  (`dvv_check_no_ffmpeg_imports()`).
- Modify `CMakeLists.txt`: `find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk)`, `dvv_ffmpeg_internal`, `dvv_ffmpeg`,
  the umbrella link, install rules.
- Modify `.clang-tidy`: public members named after FFmpeg functions.
- Tests: create `tests/test_ffmpeg_checks.cpp`, `tests/ffmpeg_main.cpp`, `tests/test_ffmpeg_load.cpp`,
  `tests/ffmpeg_support.h`, `tests/run-import-check-selftest.cmake`; modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`
  and `tests/package/main.cpp`.
- Modify `README.md`: a section "FFmpeg".

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk CONFIG REQUIRED)`: `dvv::ffmpeg_headers`,
  `DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR`, `DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM`); Task 2 (`LibrarySearch::DllDirectory`); Task 3
  (`dvv::components`, `DvvComponent.cmake`, `dvv_components_tests`, `DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR`);
  dvv-base (`getEnvironment`, `dvv::paths::executablePath`, `Logger`, `LogLevel`, `MemorySink`,
  `logLevelName`, `Error`, `Errc`, `makeError`); dvv-media (`pathFromUtf8`, `utf8FromPath`);
  dvv-registry (`Capabilities`); dvv-platform (`DynamicLibrary`). Task 0's record (majors, version
  string).
- Produces (public, `include/dvv/components/ffmpeg.h`, complete; Task 7a's startup and Task 5a/5b
  rely on it):

```cpp
#pragma once

// FFmpeg status and loading (spec §2, §13.2; M4 design D3, §4.4): the only public FFmpeg header of
// dvv-components, with no FFmpeg type. FFmpeg is never linked: loadFfmpeg() opens the six libraries
// of an LGPL shared build at run time, checks them, resolves every function the components use and
// never unloads them.

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>

#include "dvv/registry/capabilities.h"

namespace dvv::components {

struct FfmpegStatus {
    bool core = false;    // capability "ffmpeg": avutil, avcodec, avformat, swscale, swresample
    bool lavfi = false;   // capability "ffmpeg.lavfi": core and avfilter
    std::string reason;   // why not; empty when both are available (with core only: why not lavfi)
    std::string version;  // av_version_info(), e.g. "n8.1.3"
    std::string licence;  // avutil_license()
    std::filesystem::path directory;                 // where the libraries were loaded from
    std::array<std::uint32_t, 6> libraryVersions{};  // <lib>_version() of avutil, avcodec,
                                                     // avformat, swscale, swresample, avfilter
};

// An explicit FFmpeg directory: absolute, final like DVV_FFMPEG_DIR (no fallthrough). The v1.0
// executables pass {} (a command-line option is v1.x).
struct FfmpegLoadOptions {
    std::optional<std::filesystem::path> directory;
};

// Loads once per process (later calls return the first result, whatever their options;
// thread-safe) and never unloads. Search: options.directory, else DVV_FFMPEG_DIR (an empty value
// counts as unset), else <exe dir>/ffmpeg, then <exe dir>/../lib/ffmpeg. An explicit directory is
// final: a failure there leaves FFmpeg off with the reason. Every library passes the checks of
// design D3 (refusalReason) and every function of the table resolves. Installs the av_log callback.
// Adds nothing to a registry.
[[nodiscard]] const FfmpegStatus& loadFfmpeg(const FfmpegLoadOptions& options = {});
// Adds "ffmpeg" when status.core and "ffmpeg.lavfi" when status.lavfi.
void addFfmpegCapabilities(const FfmpegStatus& status, Capabilities& caps);

namespace detail {
// What the loader learned about one library; pure checks, unit-tested without FFmpeg.
struct LibraryFacts {
    std::string name;              // "avutil", "avcodec", ...
    unsigned runtimeVersion = 0;   // <lib>_version() (AV_VERSION_INT packing)
    unsigned compiledVersion = 0;  // LIB<NAME>_VERSION_INT of the headers
    std::string licence;           // <lib>_license()
    std::string configuration;     // <lib>_configuration()
};
// The first problem in `libraries` (in order), worded to follow "FFmpeg in <dir> (<source>) ", or
// nullopt when every library passes: major == compiled major, minor >= compiled minor, licence
// starts with "LGPL", configuration contains neither "--enable-gpl" nor "--enable-nonfree".
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::string> refusalReason(std::span<const LibraryFacts> libraries);
}  // namespace detail

}  // namespace dvv::components
```

- Produces (internal to dvv-components, for Tasks 5a, 5b; never installed):
  - target `dvv_ffmpeg` (STATIC, alias `dvv::ffmpeg`, export name `ffmpeg`, installed and exported;
    `PUBLIC dvv::registry`; the public include directory with `ffmpeg.h`), linked into the umbrella
    `dvv::components`. Applications (Task 7a) link `dvv::components` and call
    `dvv_components_link()`.
  - target `dvv_ffmpeg_internal` (INTERFACE, not installed or exported): `src/` and FFmpeg's
    headers as system headers. Component libraries and tests link
    `$<BUILD_INTERFACE:dvv_ffmpeg_internal>` (Task 5a:
    `dvv_add_component(ffmpeg KIND Source SOURCES … LINK dvv::ffmpeg $<BUILD_INTERFACE:dvv_ffmpeg_internal> REGISTERS ffmpeg)`).
  - `src/ffmpeg/ffmpeg_headers.h`: the only place that includes FFmpeg headers; add an FFmpeg
    header there when a provider needs one.
  - `src/ffmpeg/api.h`: namespace `dvv::components::ffmpeg`: `enum class Library`, the X-macro list
    `DVV_FFMPEG_FUNCTIONS(X)` with `X(<Library>, <function>)` lines (Tasks 5a/5b add the functions
    they call; an added function must be exported by that library, or loading fails with "has
    no …"), `struct Api` (one pointer member per function, named like the function), `const Api&
    api()`, the RAII handles `FormatContextPtr`, `CodecContextPtr`, `FramePtr`, `PacketPtr`,
    `FilterGraphPtr`, `FilterInOutPtr`, `DictionaryPtr`, `errcForAverror`, `ffmpegError`.
  - Test executable `dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests` (doctest suites `ffmpeg`, `ffmpeg-absent`,
    `ffmpeg-relative`) with CTest entries `dvv_components.ffmpeg` (label `ffmpeg`, prefix FFmpeg,
    skip 77 unless `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1`), `dvv_components.ffmpeg_absent` and
    `dvv_components.ffmpeg_relative` (label `ffmpeg`, `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=unset:`). Tasks 5a/5b add
    their test files to this executable: suite `ffmpeg` for cases that need FFmpeg, suite
    `ffmpeg-absent` for "unavailable without FFmpeg" cases. doctest's `--test-suite` filter matches
    the whole suite name (no wildcard here), so later cases use exactly `TEST_SUITE("ffmpeg")`
    (their case names carry a prefix such as `ffmpeg:` or `lavfi:`), never a suite of their own.
    `tests/ffmpeg_support.h`:
    `ffmpegRuntimeDirectory()`, `ffmpegProgram()` (from the CTest environment variables
    `DVV_TEST_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR`, `DVV_TEST_FFMPEG_PROGRAM`). The CMake variable list
    `ffmpeg_test_environment` in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` holds the common environment entries.
  - CMake function `dvv_check_no_ffmpeg_imports(<test name> <target>...)` (in `DvvComponent.cmake`,
    installed) and the script `${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake`
    (installed): Task 7a adds the test `rawreel.no_ffmpeg_imports` for `rawreel_cli` and
    `rawreel_gui`.
  - Capabilities: `ffmpeg`, `ffmpeg.lavfi` (provider `probe`s: `require(caps, {"ffmpeg"})` /
    `{"ffmpeg.lavfi"}`).

```cpp
// src/ffmpeg/loader_detail.h (complete)
#pragma once

// Pure parts of the FFmpeg loader (M4 design D3), unit-tested without FFmpeg.

#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/components/ffmpeg.h"

namespace dvv::components::detail {

enum class SearchSource : std::uint8_t { Options, Environment, Default };

struct SearchPlan {
    std::vector<std::filesystem::path> directories;  // in order; exactly one for an explicit source
    SearchSource source = SearchSource::Default;
    std::string error;  // non-empty: FFmpeg stays off with this reason, nothing is searched
};

// options.directory, else `environment` (DVV_FFMPEG_DIR; nullopt or "" counts as unset), else
// exe_dir/ffmpeg and exe_dir/../lib/ffmpeg (lexically normal; none when exe_dir is empty). An
// explicit source must be absolute: "the FFmpeg directory must be absolute (got '<p>')",
// "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR must be absolute (got '<value>')".
[[nodiscard]] SearchPlan planSearch(const FfmpegLoadOptions& options,
                                    const std::optional<std::string>& environment,
                                    const std::filesystem::path& exe_dir);
// "the given directory", "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR", "default search".
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view sourceName(SearchSource source) noexcept;
// Windows "<library>-<major>.dll", Linux "lib<library>.so.<major>".
[[nodiscard]] std::string libraryFileName(std::string_view library, unsigned major);
// AV_VERSION_INT packing as "<major>.<minor>.<micro>", e.g. "62.3.100".
[[nodiscard]] std::string versionText(unsigned packed);

}  // namespace dvv::components::detail
```

**Behaviour:**
- **Search** (`planSearch`, then the loader):
  - `planSearch.error` set → `status.reason = error`; nothing is opened.
  - Explicit source (`Options`, `Environment`): the one directory is used, whatever is there.
  - Default search: the first directory where `libraryFileName("avutil", <compiled avutil major>)`
    is a regular file; none → `"FFmpeg not found (looked in <d1> and <d2>; set DVV_FFMPEG_DIR to
    the folder of an LGPL FFmpeg shared build)"` (without an executable folder: `"FFmpeg not found
    (the executable's folder is unknown; set DVV_FFMPEG_DIR …)"`). A found directory is final like
    an explicit one.
  - `<where>` below is `"FFmpeg in <dir> (<sourceName>)"`, `<dir>` as UTF-8 (`utf8FromPath`).
- **Opening**, in the order avutil, swresample, swscale, avcodec, avformat, avfilter (each after
  the FFmpeg libraries it depends on), each `DynamicLibrary::open(utf8FromPath(dir /
  libraryFileName(name, compiled major)), LibrarySearch::DllDirectory)`:
  - a core library fails → `reason = "<where>: <DynamicLibrary message>"`, FFmpeg off;
  - avfilter fails → core may still load; `lavfi` stays off with
    `reason = "<where>: libavfilter is not available: <message>"`.
- **Resolving**: every `DVV_FFMPEG_FUNCTIONS` entry from its library's handle. A missing core
  function → `reason = "<where>: <file name> has no <function>"`, FFmpeg off; a missing avfilter
  function → lavfi off with that reason; every avfilter member is then null and avfilter is not
  kept loaded.
- **Checks**: `LibraryFacts` of every opened library (avfilter only when it resolved), in the
  `libraryVersions` order (avutil, avcodec, avformat, swscale, swresample, avfilter) →
  `refusalReason`; a refusal → `reason = "<where> <refusal>"`, FFmpeg off (a GPL avfilter refuses
  everything: the GPL check covers every opened library).
- **`refusalReason`** (first library in order; per library: version, then licence, then
  configuration):
  - `"has no libraries to check"` for an empty span;
  - major differs: `"has <name> <runtime>, but rawreel was built for <compiled> (the major version
    must match)"`; minor below: `"has <name> <runtime>, older than the <compiled> rawreel was built
    for"` (versions through `versionText`; micro is ignored);
  - licence not starting with `LGPL`: starting with `GPL` → `"is a GPL build (<name>: <licence>);
    rawreel uses only LGPL builds"`, otherwise `"is not an LGPL build (<name>: <licence>); rawreel
    uses only LGPL builds"`;
  - configuration contains `--enable-gpl` → `"is a GPL build (<name> configured with
    --enable-gpl); rawreel uses only LGPL builds"`; contains `--enable-nonfree` → `"is a nonfree
    build (<name> configured with --enable-nonfree); rawreel uses only LGPL builds"`.
- **Success**: `keepLoaded()` on every kept handle (never unloaded; process infrastructure,
  design §6); `api.av_log_set_level(AV_LOG_WARNING)`; `api.av_log_set_callback(&logCallback)`;
  `status = {core = true, lavfi = <avfilter ok>, reason = "" or the lavfi reason, version =
  av_version_info(), licence = avutil_license(), directory = <dir, lexically normal>,
  libraryVersions = <lib>_version() (0 for an unloaded avfilter)}`.
- **`loadFfmpeg`**: `std::call_once` on a never-destroyed state object (function-local `static
  auto* state = new State`); the table is published with an `std::atomic<bool>` (release on store,
  acquire in `api()`). Later calls return the same `FfmpegStatus` object.
- **`ffmpeg::api()`**: throws `std::logic_error("FFmpeg is not loaded")` unless `core`; avfilter
  members are null unless `lavfi`.
- **Log** (`logCallback`): levels from `AV_LOG_PANIC` to `AV_LOG_WARNING` are formatted with
  `av_log_format_line2` (thread-local `print_prefix`, 1024-byte buffer, trailing newlines removed)
  and written as `Logger::instance().write(LogLevel::Warn, "FFmpeg: <line>")` — capped at warning,
  even for FFmpeg errors; other levels are dropped; nothing goes to stderr.
- **Errors** (`errcForAverror`, design v2 §7): `AVERROR(ENOMEM)` → `OutOfMemory`;
  `AVERROR(ENOENT)`, `(EIO)`, `(EACCES)`, `(EPERM)`, `(EISDIR)`, `(ENOTDIR)`, `(ENXIO)`, `(ENODEV)`,
  `(EROFS)`, `(ENOSPC)`, `(EFBIG)`, `(ENAMETOOLONG)`, `(EMFILE)` → `Io`; `AVERROR_INVALIDDATA` →
  `Format`; `AVERROR_DECODER_NOT_FOUND`, `AVERROR_DEMUXER_NOT_FOUND`, `AVERROR_STREAM_NOT_FOUND`,
  `AVERROR_PATCHWELCOME`, `AVERROR(ENOSYS)` → `Unsupported`; anything else (`AVERROR_EOF` and
  `AVERROR_BUG` included) → `Internal`. `ffmpegError(call, averror)` → that code with
  `"<call> failed: <av_strerror text> (AVERROR <averror>)"`, or `"<call> failed (AVERROR
  <averror>)"` when FFmpeg is not loaded or `av_strerror` fails. The providers turn `AVERROR_EOF`
  and the decoder's `EAGAIN` into their own control flow; they never report them through this
  mapping.
- **RAII deleters** call `avformat_close_input(&p)`, `avcodec_free_context(&p)`,
  `av_frame_free(&p)`, `av_packet_free(&p)`, `avfilter_graph_free(&p)`, `avfilter_inout_free(&p)`,
  `av_dict_free(&p)` through `api()`; a null pointer is a no-op.
- **Import check**: `check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake` fails when a binary's import table names one of
  the exact base names `avutil avcodec avformat avfilter avdevice swscale swresample postproc`
  (Windows `<name>.dll` or `<name>-<n>.dll`, any case, not as part of a longer name; Linux
  `[lib<name>.so…]`).

**Code:**

`src/ffmpeg/ffmpeg_headers.h` (complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The only include of FFmpeg's C headers (M4 design D3): they have no extern "C" of their own. The
// include directory is a system directory (dvv::ffmpeg_headers), so their warnings are not ours.
// Never use a C-only macro or an inline helper that calls an exported function (it would need
// FFmpeg linked): AVRational{1, AV_TIME_BASE} instead of AV_TIME_BASE_Q, api().av_strerror instead
// of av_err2str. A violation shows up as an unresolved symbol when linking.

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdint>  // UINT64_C and friends, which FFmpeg's common.h requires in C++

extern "C" {
#include <libavcodec/avcodec.h>
#include <libavcodec/version.h>
#include <libavfilter/avfilter.h>
#include <libavfilter/buffersink.h>
#include <libavfilter/buffersrc.h>
#include <libavfilter/version.h>
#include <libavformat/avformat.h>
#include <libavformat/version.h>
#include <libavutil/avutil.h>
#include <libavutil/dict.h>
#include <libavutil/error.h>
#include <libavutil/frame.h>
#include <libavutil/log.h>
#include <libavutil/opt.h>
#include <libavutil/pixdesc.h>
#include <libavutil/version.h>
#include <libswresample/swresample.h>
#include <libswresample/version.h>
#include <libswscale/swscale.h>
#include <libswscale/version.h>
}
```

`src/ffmpeg/api.h` (complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The FFmpeg function table of dvv_ffmpeg (M4 design D3, §4.4): every FFmpeg function dvv-components
// calls, resolved at load time from the six libraries; RAII handles; the AVERROR mapping (§7).
// Internal to dvv-components; never installed.

#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <source_location>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "ffmpeg/ffmpeg_headers.h"

namespace dvv::components::ffmpeg {

enum class Library : std::uint8_t { Avutil, Avcodec, Avformat, Swscale, Swresample, Avfilter };

// X(<Library>, <function>): every FFmpeg function dvv-components calls. Add a line when a provider
// needs another function; it must be exported by that library. Avfilter's entries are null unless
// FfmpegStatus::lavfi.
// NOLINTBEGIN(bugprone-macro-parentheses)
#define DVV_FFMPEG_FUNCTIONS(X)               \
    X(Avutil, avutil_version)                 \
    X(Avutil, avutil_license)                 \
    X(Avutil, avutil_configuration)           \
    X(Avutil, av_version_info)                \
    X(Avutil, av_log)                         \
    X(Avutil, av_log_set_callback)            \
    X(Avutil, av_log_set_level)               \
    X(Avutil, av_log_format_line2)            \
    X(Avutil, av_strerror)                    \
    X(Avutil, av_frame_alloc)                 \
    X(Avutil, av_frame_free)                  \
    X(Avutil, av_frame_unref)                 \
    X(Avutil, av_dict_set)                    \
    X(Avutil, av_dict_free)                   \
    X(Avutil, av_get_pix_fmt_name)            \
    X(Avutil, av_pix_fmt_desc_get)            \
    X(Avutil, av_opt_set)                     \
    X(Avutil, av_opt_set_bin)                 \
    X(Avutil, av_rescale_q)                   \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_version)               \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_license)               \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_configuration)         \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_find_decoder)          \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_alloc_context3)        \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_free_context)          \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_parameters_to_context) \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_open2)                 \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_send_packet)           \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_receive_frame)         \
    X(Avcodec, avcodec_flush_buffers)         \
    X(Avcodec, av_packet_alloc)               \
    X(Avcodec, av_packet_free)                \
    X(Avcodec, av_packet_unref)               \
    X(Avformat, avformat_version)             \
    X(Avformat, avformat_license)             \
    X(Avformat, avformat_configuration)       \
    X(Avformat, avformat_open_input)          \
    X(Avformat, avformat_close_input)         \
    X(Avformat, avformat_find_stream_info)    \
    X(Avformat, av_find_best_stream)          \
    X(Avformat, av_read_frame)                \
    X(Avformat, avformat_seek_file)           \
    X(Avformat, av_probe_input_format3)       \
    X(Avformat, av_match_ext)                 \
    X(Swscale, swscale_version)               \
    X(Swscale, swscale_license)               \
    X(Swscale, swscale_configuration)         \
    X(Swresample, swresample_version)         \
    X(Swresample, swresample_license)         \
    X(Swresample, swresample_configuration)   \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_version)             \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_license)             \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_configuration)       \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_graph_alloc)         \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_graph_free)          \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_graph_parse_ptr)     \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_graph_config)        \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_graph_create_filter) \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_get_by_name)         \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_inout_alloc)         \
    X(Avfilter, avfilter_inout_free)          \
    X(Avfilter, av_buffersink_get_frame)

// One pointer per function, typed from the headers and named like the function.
struct Api {
#define DVV_FFMPEG_MEMBER(library_id, symbol) decltype(&::symbol) symbol = nullptr;
    DVV_FFMPEG_FUNCTIONS(DVV_FFMPEG_MEMBER)
#undef DVV_FFMPEG_MEMBER
};
// NOLINTEND(bugprone-macro-parentheses)

// The loaded table. Throws std::logic_error("FFmpeg is not loaded") unless FfmpegStatus::core;
// callers exist only where the capability does (a provider's probe requires it).
[[nodiscard]] const Api& api();

// RAII handles; each deleter calls FFmpeg's free function through api(), null is a no-op.
struct FormatContextDeleter { void operator()(AVFormatContext* context) const noexcept; };
struct CodecContextDeleter { void operator()(AVCodecContext* context) const noexcept; };
struct FrameDeleter { void operator()(AVFrame* frame) const noexcept; };
struct PacketDeleter { void operator()(AVPacket* packet) const noexcept; };
struct FilterGraphDeleter { void operator()(AVFilterGraph* graph) const noexcept; };
struct FilterInOutDeleter { void operator()(AVFilterInOut* inout) const noexcept; };
struct DictionaryDeleter { void operator()(AVDictionary* dictionary) const noexcept; };
using FormatContextPtr = std::unique_ptr<AVFormatContext, FormatContextDeleter>;
using CodecContextPtr = std::unique_ptr<AVCodecContext, CodecContextDeleter>;
using FramePtr = std::unique_ptr<AVFrame, FrameDeleter>;
using PacketPtr = std::unique_ptr<AVPacket, PacketDeleter>;
using FilterGraphPtr = std::unique_ptr<AVFilterGraph, FilterGraphDeleter>;
using FilterInOutPtr = std::unique_ptr<AVFilterInOut, FilterInOutDeleter>;
using DictionaryPtr = std::unique_ptr<AVDictionary, DictionaryDeleter>;

// M4 design §7: AVERROR(ENOMEM) -> OutOfMemory; AVERROR(ENOENT, EIO, EACCES, EPERM, EISDIR,
// ENOTDIR, ENXIO, ENODEV, EROFS, ENOSPC, EFBIG, ENAMETOOLONG, EMFILE) -> Io; AVERROR_INVALIDDATA ->
// Format; AVERROR_DECODER_NOT_FOUND, AVERROR_DEMUXER_NOT_FOUND, AVERROR_STREAM_NOT_FOUND,
// AVERROR_PATCHWELCOME, AVERROR(ENOSYS) -> Unsupported; anything else -> Internal.
[[nodiscard]] Errc errcForAverror(int averror) noexcept;
// errcForAverror(averror) with "<call> failed: <av_strerror text> (AVERROR <averror>)", or
// "<call> failed (AVERROR <averror>)" when FFmpeg is not loaded or av_strerror has no text.
[[nodiscard]] Error ffmpegError(std::string_view call, int averror,
                                std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());

}  // namespace dvv::components::ffmpeg
```

`src/ffmpeg/loader.cpp` (the parts that are easy to get wrong; the rest follows **Behaviour**):

```cpp
namespace dvv::components {
namespace {

using ffmpeg::Api;
using ffmpeg::Library;

struct LibraryInfo {
    Library library;
    std::string_view name;
    unsigned compiledVersion;
};
// The FfmpegStatus::libraryVersions order.
constexpr std::array<LibraryInfo, 6> LIBRARIES{{
    {Library::Avutil, "avutil", LIBAVUTIL_VERSION_INT},
    {Library::Avcodec, "avcodec", LIBAVCODEC_VERSION_INT},
    {Library::Avformat, "avformat", LIBAVFORMAT_VERSION_INT},
    {Library::Swscale, "swscale", LIBSWSCALE_VERSION_INT},
    {Library::Swresample, "swresample", LIBSWRESAMPLE_VERSION_INT},
    {Library::Avfilter, "avfilter", LIBAVFILTER_VERSION_INT},
}};
// Each library after the FFmpeg libraries it depends on.
constexpr std::array<Library, 6> LOAD_ORDER{Library::Avutil,  Library::Swresample,
                                            Library::Swscale, Library::Avcodec,
                                            Library::Avformat, Library::Avfilter};

// Process infrastructure (design §6): created once, never destroyed, like the Logger.
struct LoaderState {
    std::once_flag once;
    FfmpegStatus status;
    Api api;
    std::atomic<bool> loaded{false};
    std::vector<platform::DynamicLibrary> kept;  // keepLoaded(); never unloaded
};
LoaderState& loaderState() {
    static auto* const state = new LoaderState;
    return *state;
}

// FFmpeg's log goes to the dvv logger, capped at warning; nothing reaches stderr (design D3).
void logCallback(void* object, int level, const char* format, va_list args) {
    if (level < AV_LOG_PANIC || level > AV_LOG_WARNING) {
        return;
    }
    thread_local int print_prefix = 1;
    std::array<char, 1024> line{};
    loaderState().api.av_log_format_line2(object, level, format, args, line.data(),
                                          static_cast<int>(line.size()), &print_prefix);
    std::string_view text(line.data());
    while (!text.empty() && (text.back() == '\n' || text.back() == '\r')) {
        text.remove_suffix(1);
    }
    if (!text.empty()) {
        Logger::instance().write(LogLevel::Warn, std::format("FFmpeg: {}", text));
    }
}

// Resolves every table entry from the opened libraries (index = Library). A missing function is
// appended to `missing_core` or, for avfilter, `missing_lavfi`.
void resolve(Api& api, const std::array<std::optional<platform::DynamicLibrary>, 6>& opened,
             std::vector<std::string>& missing_core, std::vector<std::string>& missing_lavfi) {
// NOLINTBEGIN(bugprone-macro-parentheses)
#define DVV_FFMPEG_RESOLVE(library_id, symbol)                                                  \
    if (const auto& handle = opened[static_cast<std::size_t>(Library::library_id)]; handle) {   \
        api.symbol = handle->function<decltype(api.symbol)>(#symbol);                            \
        if (api.symbol == nullptr) {                                                            \
            (Library::library_id == Library::Avfilter ? missing_lavfi : missing_core)            \
                .push_back(std::format("{} has no {}",                                           \
                                       libraryFile(Library::library_id), #symbol));              \
        }                                                                                       \
    }
    DVV_FFMPEG_FUNCTIONS(DVV_FFMPEG_RESOLVE)
#undef DVV_FFMPEG_RESOLVE
// NOLINTEND(bugprone-macro-parentheses)
}

}  // namespace

const FfmpegStatus& loadFfmpeg(const FfmpegLoadOptions& options) {
    LoaderState& state = loaderState();
    std::call_once(state.once, [&] {
        load(state, options);  // fills state.status, state.api and state.kept per Behaviour
        if (state.status.core) {
            state.loaded.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
        }
    });
    return state.status;
}

namespace ffmpeg {
const Api& api() {
    LoaderState& state = loaderState();
    if (!state.loaded.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        throw std::logic_error("FFmpeg is not loaded");
    }
    return state.api;
}
}  // namespace ffmpeg

}  // namespace dvv::components
```

`libraryFile(Library)` is `detail::libraryFileName(name, compiledVersion >> 16)` of the `LIBRARIES`
entry. `load()` follows **Behaviour** step by step (plan, directory, open, resolve, facts, refusal,
success); when lavfi ends up off it sets every avfilter member back to null with a second X-macro
pass and drops the avfilter handle (unloaded) before keeping the others.

`cmake/check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake` (complete):

```cmake
# Fails when a binary imports an FFmpeg library (M4 design D3: FFmpeg is loaded at run time and never
# linked). Installed with dvv_components; dvv_check_no_ffmpeg_imports() adds a CTest test for it.
#   cmake -DBINARIES=<file>[,<file>...] (-DDUMPBIN=<dumpbin> | -DREADELF=<readelf>)
#         -P check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake
#   cmake -DLISTING=<text file> -P check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake   (checks a saved listing)
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
set(ffmpeg_libraries avutil avcodec avformat avfilter avdevice swscale swresample postproc)

# check_listing(<what> <dumpbin /dependents or readelf -d output>)
function(check_listing what listing)
    # The leading space lets a name at the very start pass the "not part of a longer name" rule.
    string(TOLOWER " ${listing}" listing)
    foreach(name IN LISTS ffmpeg_libraries)
        # Windows: "avutil-60.dll" or "avutil.dll", never "myavutil.dll"; Linux: "[libavutil.so.60]".
        if(listing MATCHES "[^a-z0-9_]${name}(-[0-9]+)?\\.dll" OR
           listing MATCHES "\\[lib${name}\\.so(\\.[0-9]+)*\\]")
            message(FATAL_ERROR "${what} imports the FFmpeg library ${name}; FFmpeg must be loaded "
                                "at run time, never linked (M4 design D3)")
        endif()
    endforeach()
endfunction()

if(DEFINED LISTING)
    file(READ "${LISTING}" listing)
    check_listing("${LISTING}" "${listing}")
    message(STATUS "no FFmpeg import in ${LISTING}")
    return()
endif()
if(NOT BINARIES)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "pass -DBINARIES=<file>[,<file>...] or -DLISTING=<text file>")
endif()
string(REPLACE "," ";" binaries "${BINARIES}")
foreach(binary IN LISTS binaries)
    if(DUMPBIN)
        execute_process(COMMAND "${DUMPBIN}" /nologo /dependents "${binary}"
                        RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE listing ERROR_VARIABLE error)
    elseif(READELF)
        execute_process(COMMAND "${READELF}" -d "${binary}"
                        RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE listing ERROR_VARIABLE error)
    else()
        message(FATAL_ERROR "pass -DDUMPBIN=<dumpbin> or -DREADELF=<readelf>")
    endif()
    if(NOT status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "reading the imports of ${binary} failed (${status}): ${error}")
    endif()
    check_listing("${binary}" "${listing}")
endforeach()
list(LENGTH binaries count)
message(STATUS "no FFmpeg import in ${count} binaries")
```

`cmake/DvvComponent.cmake` (append; also extend the header comment):

```cmake
# dvv_check_no_ffmpeg_imports(<test name> <target>...)
#   Adds a CTest test that fails when the binary of one of the targets imports an FFmpeg library
#   (M4 design D3), reading the import tables with dumpbin (MSVC, next to the compiler) or readelf.
function(dvv_check_no_ffmpeg_imports test_name)
    if(MSVC)
        cmake_path(GET CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER PARENT_PATH compiler_dir)
        find_program(DVV_DUMPBIN dumpbin HINTS "${compiler_dir}" NO_DEFAULT_PATH REQUIRED)
        set(tool "-DDUMPBIN=${DVV_DUMPBIN}")
    else()
        find_program(DVV_READELF NAMES llvm-readelf-19 llvm-readelf readelf REQUIRED)
        set(tool "-DREADELF=${DVV_READELF}")
    endif()
    set(binaries "")
    foreach(target IN LISTS ARGN)
        list(APPEND binaries "$<TARGET_FILE:${target}>")
    endforeach()
    list(JOIN binaries "," binaries)
    add_test(NAME ${test_name}
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} "${tool}" "-DBINARIES=${binaries}"
                     -P "${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake")
endfunction()
```

`CMakeLists.txt` (insert after the `dvv_components` umbrella, before the component marker; then
extend the install rules):

```cmake
# FFmpeg of the third-party prefix: headers at build time, libraries at run time (M4 design D3).
find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk REQUIRED CONFIG)

# dvv_ffmpeg's private headers and FFmpeg's headers (system headers), for dvv_ffmpeg, the component
# libraries and the tests only. Never installed or exported, so no FFmpeg header reaches an
# application: link it as $<BUILD_INTERFACE:dvv_ffmpeg_internal>.
add_library(dvv_ffmpeg_internal INTERFACE)
target_include_directories(dvv_ffmpeg_internal INTERFACE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/src)
target_link_libraries(dvv_ffmpeg_internal INTERFACE dvv::ffmpeg_headers)

# The FFmpeg run-time binding (M4 design D3, §4.4): loader, function table, AVERROR mapping and log
# routing. Public part: dvv/components/ffmpeg.h (status and loading; no FFmpeg types).
add_library(dvv_ffmpeg STATIC
    src/ffmpeg/checks.cpp
    src/ffmpeg/error.cpp
    src/ffmpeg/loader.cpp)
add_library(dvv::ffmpeg ALIAS dvv_ffmpeg)
set_target_properties(dvv_ffmpeg PROPERTIES EXPORT_NAME ffmpeg)
target_include_directories(dvv_ffmpeg PUBLIC
    $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
    $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}>)
target_link_libraries(dvv_ffmpeg
    PUBLIC dvv::registry
    PRIVATE dvv::media dvv::platform $<BUILD_INTERFACE:dvv_ffmpeg_internal>)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_ffmpeg)
target_link_libraries(dvv_components INTERFACE dvv::ffmpeg)

# (install rules)
install(TARGETS dvv_components dvv_ffmpeg ${component_libraries} EXPORT dvv_componentsTargets)
install(DIRECTORY include/ DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR})
# (and add cmake/check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake to the install(FILES … DESTINATION ${config_dir}))
```

`tests/CMakeLists.txt` (additions; `ffmpeg_test_environment` is reused by Tasks 5a/5b):

```cmake
# The FFmpeg binding's pure parts (no FFmpeg needed): in the unit test executable.
target_sources(dvv_components_tests PRIVATE test_ffmpeg_checks.cpp)
target_link_libraries(dvv_components_tests PRIVATE dvv::ffmpeg dvv_ffmpeg_internal)

# FFmpeg tests (label ffmpeg; M4 design §3, §8.1): one executable, one CTest entry per loader
# directory (FFmpeg loads once per process): the prefix's FFmpeg, an existing empty directory and a
# relative DVV_FFMPEG_DIR. Without FFmpeg the first exits 77 unless DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1; the
# deliberate no-FFmpeg entries unset it.
add_executable(dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests
    ffmpeg_main.cpp
    test_ffmpeg_load.cpp)
target_include_directories(dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR})
target_link_libraries(dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests PRIVATE dvv::components dvv_ffmpeg_internal
                                                          doctest::doctest)
target_compile_definitions(dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests PRIVATE DOCTEST_CONFIG_USE_STD_HEADERS)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests)
file(MAKE_DIRECTORY ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/no-ffmpeg)
set(ffmpeg_test_environment ${DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT}
    "DVV_TEST_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR=set:${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}"
    "DVV_TEST_FFMPEG_PROGRAM=set:${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}")
foreach(mode IN ITEMS available absent relative)
    if(mode STREQUAL "available")
        set(name dvv_components.ffmpeg)
        set(suite ffmpeg)
        set(environment ${ffmpeg_test_environment} "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR=set:${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}")
    elseif(mode STREQUAL "absent")
        set(name dvv_components.ffmpeg_absent)
        set(suite ffmpeg-absent)
        set(environment ${ffmpeg_test_environment} "DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=unset:"
                        "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR=set:${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/no-ffmpeg")
    else()
        set(name dvv_components.ffmpeg_relative)
        set(suite ffmpeg-relative)
        set(environment ${ffmpeg_test_environment} "DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=unset:"
                        "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR=set:ffmpeg-relative")
    endif()
    add_test(NAME ${name} COMMAND dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests --test-suite=${suite})
    set_tests_properties(${name} PROPERTIES
        LABELS ffmpeg
        SKIP_RETURN_CODE 77
        TIMEOUT 600
        ENVIRONMENT DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail
        ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "${environment};DVV_FFMPEG_TEST_MODE=set:${mode}")
endforeach()

# No binary imports an FFmpeg library (M4 design D3), and the check itself works.
dvv_check_no_ffmpeg_imports(dvv_components.no_ffmpeg_imports
                            dvv_components_tests dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests)
add_test(NAME dvv_components.no_ffmpeg_imports_selftest
         COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                 -DSCRIPT=${DVV_COMPONENTS_CMAKE_DIR}/check-no-ffmpeg-imports.cmake
                 -DWORK_DIR=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/import-check-selftest
                 -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/run-import-check-selftest.cmake)
```

`tests/ffmpeg_main.cpp` (doctest main of `dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests`):

```cpp
#define DOCTEST_CONFIG_IMPLEMENT
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/environment.h"
#include "dvv/base/leak_check.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/components/ffmpeg.h"

// DVV_FFMPEG_TEST_MODE "available" (default): FFmpeg must load from DVV_FFMPEG_DIR; without it the
// executable exits 77 (skipped) unless DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1. "absent" and "relative" run the cases
// that expect FFmpeg to stay off.
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().setDetailed(true);
    const std::string mode = dvv::getEnvironment("DVV_FFMPEG_TEST_MODE").value_or("available");
    if (mode == "available") {
        const auto& status = dvv::components::loadFfmpeg();
        if (!status.core || !status.lavfi) {
            std::fprintf(stderr, "FFmpeg is not available: %s\n", status.reason.c_str());
            return dvv::getEnvironment("DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG") == "1" ? 1 : 77;
        }
    }
    doctest::Context context(argc, argv);
    const int result = context.run();
    if (context.shouldExit()) {
        return result;
    }
    return dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(result);
}
```

`tests/ffmpeg_support.h`: header-only, namespace `dvv::components::testing`;
`ffmpegRuntimeDirectory()` and `ffmpegProgram()` return `dvv::media::pathFromUtf8` of
`DVV_TEST_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR` / `DVV_TEST_FFMPEG_PROGRAM` (an empty path when unset).

`tests/run-import-check-selftest.cmake`: writes saved listings into `WORK_DIR` and runs the script
on each with `-DLISTING=` (pass = exit 0 and `no FFmpeg import`; fail = non-zero exit and `imports
the FFmpeg library <name>`):
- clean Windows (`KERNEL32.dll`, `MSVCP140.dll`, `myavutil.dll`, `libavutil_extra.so`) → pass;
- `    avcodec-62.dll` → fail `avcodec`; `    AVFILTER.DLL` → fail `avfilter`; `    postproc-58.dll`
  → fail `postproc`;
- clean Linux (` 0x0000000000000001 (NEEDED)  Shared library: [libstdc++.so.6]` and a `RUNPATH`
  line `[$ORIGIN]`) → pass;
- `(NEEDED) Shared library: [libswresample.so.6]` → fail `swresample`;
  `(NEEDED) Shared library: [libavdevice.so]` → fail `avdevice`.
It ends with `message(STATUS "import check self-test passed")`.

**Tests first:**

`tests/test_ffmpeg_checks.cpp` (in `dvv_components_tests`; no FFmpeg loaded). Notation: `v(M, m,
u)` is `(M << 16) | (m << 8) | u`; `facts()` returns six `LibraryFacts` named avutil, avcodec,
avformat, swscale, swresample, avfilter with runtime = compiled = `v(60, 8, 100)`, licence `"LGPL
version 2.1 or later"`, configuration `"--prefix=/x --enable-shared --disable-static"`.
1. `refusalReason: an LGPL build of the compiled versions passes` — `refusalReason(facts())` is
   `nullopt`; `refusalReason({})` is `"has no libraries to check"`.
2. `refusalReason: a GPL or nonfree build is refused` — with swscale's licence `"GPL version 2 or
   later"`: `"is a GPL build (swscale: GPL version 2 or later); rawreel uses only LGPL builds"`;
   avcodec's licence `"nonfree and unredistributable"`: `"is not an LGPL build (avcodec: nonfree and
   unredistributable); rawreel uses only LGPL builds"`; avformat's configuration `"--enable-shared
   --enable-gpl"`: `"is a GPL build (avformat configured with --enable-gpl); rawreel uses only LGPL
   builds"`; avfilter's configuration `"--enable-nonfree"`: `"is a nonfree build (avfilter
   configured with --enable-nonfree); rawreel uses only LGPL builds"`.
3. `refusalReason: versions must match the headers` — avformat compiled `v(62, 3, 100)`: runtime
   `v(61, 3, 100)` → `"has avformat 61.3.100, but rawreel was built for 62.3.100 (the major version
   must match)"`; runtime `v(62, 2, 100)` → `"has avformat 62.2.100, older than the 62.3.100 rawreel
   was built for"`; runtime `v(62, 4, 0)` and `v(62, 3, 99)` → `nullopt`.
4. `refusalReason: the first problem in order is reported` — avutil runtime minor below and
   swscale GPL → the avutil message.
5. `planSearch: explicit directories are final and must be absolute` — with `A` an absolute
   directory (`"X:/opt/ff"` on Windows, `"/opt/ff"` on Linux): options `{A}`, environment
   `"/elsewhere"` → `directories == {A}`, `source == Options`, no error; options `{"ffmpeg"}` →
   error `"the FFmpeg directory must be absolute (got 'ffmpeg')"`; no options, environment = A's
   UTF-8 → `{A}`, `Environment`; environment `"rel/dir"` → error `"DVV_FFMPEG_DIR must be absolute
   (got 'rel/dir')"`; `sourceName(Options) == "the given directory"`, `sourceName(Environment) ==
   "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR"`, `sourceName(Default) == "default search"`.
6. `planSearch: the default search looks next to the executable` — environment `nullopt` and `""`
   alike, exe directory `E = A / "bin"` → `{E / "ffmpeg", A / "lib" / "ffmpeg"}` (compared after
   `lexically_normal()`), `Default`, no error; exe directory empty → no directories, `Default`, no
   error.
7. `libraryFileName and versionText` — Windows `libraryFileName("avutil", 60) == "avutil-60.dll"`,
   `("swresample", 6) == "swresample-6.dll"`; Linux `"libavutil.so.60"`, `"libswresample.so.6"`;
   `versionText(v(62, 3, 100)) == "62.3.100"`.
8. `errcForAverror maps the codes of the design` — `AVERROR(ENOMEM)` → `OutOfMemory`;
   `AVERROR(ENOENT)`, `AVERROR(EIO)`, `AVERROR(EACCES)` → `Io`; `AVERROR_INVALIDDATA` → `Format`;
   `AVERROR_DECODER_NOT_FOUND`, `AVERROR_DEMUXER_NOT_FOUND`, `AVERROR_STREAM_NOT_FOUND`,
   `AVERROR_PATCHWELCOME`, `AVERROR(ENOSYS)` → `Unsupported`; `AVERROR_EOF`, `AVERROR_BUG`, `-12345`
   → `Internal`.
9. `addFfmpegCapabilities follows the status` — `{core false, lavfi false}` → no names;
   `{true, false}` → `{"ffmpeg"}`; `{true, true}` → `{"ffmpeg", "ffmpeg.lavfi"}`.

`tests/test_ffmpeg_load.cpp` (in `dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests`):
- `TEST_SUITE("ffmpeg")`:
  1. `loader: the prefix's FFmpeg loads and passes the checks` — `status = loadFfmpeg()`:
     `core`, `lavfi`, `reason.empty()`, `licence.starts_with("LGPL")`, `version` equals Task 0's
     recorded version string (`"n8.1.3"` expected), `std::filesystem::equivalent(status.directory,
     ffmpegRuntimeDirectory())`; `libraryVersions[i] >> 16` equals `LIBAVUTIL_VERSION_MAJOR`,
     `LIBAVCODEC_VERSION_MAJOR`, `LIBAVFORMAT_VERSION_MAJOR`, `LIBSWSCALE_VERSION_MAJOR`,
     `LIBSWRESAMPLE_VERSION_MAJOR`, `LIBAVFILTER_VERSION_MAJOR` in that order; every `Api` member is
     non-null (an X-macro pass with `CHECK_MESSAGE(api.<f> != nullptr, "<f>")`).
  2. `loader: later calls return the first result` — `&loadFfmpeg() ==
     &loadFfmpeg(FfmpegLoadOptions{.directory = std::filesystem::path("relative")})` and `core`
     is still true.
  3. `loader: FFmpeg's log reaches the dvv logger, capped at warning` — add a `MemorySink`; call
     `api().av_log(nullptr, AV_LOG_ERROR, "dvv-test-error %d\n", 1)`, `api().av_log(nullptr,
     AV_LOG_WARNING, "dvv-test-warning\n")`, `api().av_log(nullptr, AV_LOG_INFO,
     "dvv-test-info\n")`; the sink has a line containing `FFmpeg: dvv-test-error 1` and the level
     name `logLevelName(LogLevel::Warn)`, a line containing `FFmpeg: dvv-test-warning` with the same
     level name, no line containing `dvv-test-info`, no line containing
     `logLevelName(LogLevel::Error)`; then `Logger::instance().clearSinks()`.
  4. `loader: ffmpegError names the call and maps the code` —
     `ffmpegError("avformat_open_input", AVERROR(ENOENT))`: code `Io`, message starts with
     `"avformat_open_input failed: "`, ends with `"(AVERROR -2)"` and has text in between;
     `ffmpegError("avcodec_send_packet", AVERROR_INVALIDDATA)`: code `Format`, ends with
     `std::format("(AVERROR {})", AVERROR_INVALIDDATA)`.
  5. `loader: the capabilities follow the status` — `addFfmpegCapabilities(loadFfmpeg(), caps)`
     gives `has("ffmpeg")` and `has("ffmpeg.lavfi")`.
- `TEST_SUITE("ffmpeg-absent")`:
  6. `loader: an empty FFmpeg directory leaves FFmpeg off with the reason` — `!core`, `!lavfi`,
     `reason` contains `(DVV_FFMPEG_DIR)`, `no-ffmpeg` and `could not be loaded`; `version` empty;
     `addFfmpegCapabilities` adds nothing; `CHECK_THROWS_AS(ffmpeg::api(), std::logic_error)`.
- `TEST_SUITE("ffmpeg-relative")`:
  7. `loader: a relative DVV_FFMPEG_DIR is refused` — `reason == "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR must be absolute
     (got 'ffmpeg-relative')"`, `!core`.

`tests/package/main.cpp` (addition): `dvv::components::FfmpegStatus status; dvv::Capabilities caps;
dvv::components::addFfmpegCapabilities(status, caps);` and fail unless `caps.names().empty()` (the
installed `dvv::ffmpeg` links).

First failures: the build fails (`dvv/components/ffmpeg.h` missing). With the headers and stub
definitions (returning defaults), it builds; the unit cases 1–9 fail; `dvv_components.ffmpeg` exits
1 under `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` ("FFmpeg is not available"); cases 6 and 7 fail (empty reason); the
import-check tests already pass.

```powershell
dvv_ffmpeg opens avutil, swresample, swscale, avcodec, avformat and avfilter from DVV_FFMPEG_DIR or
next to the executable (an explicit directory is final), with each library's dependencies taken
from its own folder, and refuses a library whose major or minor version differs from the headers,
whose licence is not LGPL or whose configuration enables GPL or nonfree code. Every function the
components call is resolved once into a table; the libraries stay loaded. FFmpeg's log goes to the
dvv logger as warnings. dvv/components/ffmpeg.h reports the status without FFmpeg types; AVERROR
codes map to dvv errors. A test fails when a binary imports an FFmpeg library.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-components commit -F -
cmake -DREPO=dvv-components -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake
```

Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

**Expected results:** dvv-components stand-alone: 9 CTest tests (`unit`, `cmake_functions`,
`compile_fail.1`, `package`, `ffmpeg`, `ffmpeg_absent`, `ffmpeg_relative`, `no_ffmpeg_imports`,
`no_ffmpeg_imports_selftest`; 8 under sanitizers, no `package`), all passing in vs2026 Debug with
`DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1`; `dvv_components.unit` has 12 cases, `dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests` 7
(5 + 1 + 1); `FORMAT-OK`; privacy lint clean. (Release, `vs2026-asan` with its 8 tests, clang-tidy
and the Linux build of the Linux-only code: Task 5b checkpoint.)

**Commit:** `feat: load FFmpeg at run time through a checked function table` (dvv-components).

**Notes:**
- Design v2 D3 (with its changes a–c: no `sws_*`, six libraries opened and checked, exact import
  base names), §4.4 (`ffmpeg.h` as v1, binding minus `sws_*`/`SwsContext`), §5.5 (never unloaded),
  §6 (library state is process infrastructure), §7 (AVERROR rows), §8.1 unit and ffmpeg rows, D21
  (`DVV_FFMPEG_DIR`; `--ffmpeg-dir` is v1.x), arbiter fixes 18, 28 (one CTest entry per loader
  directory, `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=unset:`), 31 (exact base names).
- Note (plan choice): `FfmpegLoadOptions::directory` stays (design v2 §4.4 "as v1", §4.6
  `startComponents(const FfmpegLoadOptions&)`), documented as an explicit, final directory that the
  v1.0 executables do not set.
- Note (plan choice): when avfilter alone fails to load or resolve, `core` stays on and only
  `lavfi` is off, with `reason` saying why (capabilities `ffmpeg` = five libraries, `ffmpeg.lavfi` =
  + avfilter, v1 D3); a version, licence or configuration refusal of any opened library turns
  everything off.
- Note (plan choice): a default-search directory counts as found when the avutil file exists in it;
  from then on it is final like an explicit directory, so a broken bundled FFmpeg is reported
  instead of silently skipped.
- Note (plan choice): the X-macro table starts with the functions the two providers of Tasks 5a/5b
  are expected to need; they add or remove lines (the test "every member non-null" keeps the table
  honest on Windows; the Linux table is compiled by the sanity builds and tested in v1.x).
- Note (plan choice): `ffmpegError` maps `AVERROR_EOF` to `Internal`; the providers handle end of
  stream themselves (D6 "the lavfi graph ended" is `Io` by its own rule).
- Note (plan choice): the import check is a reusable CMake function, so Task 7a checks
  `rawreel-cli` and `rawreel-gui` with the same script.
- Risk: errno values in `AVERROR(e)` are the platform's; the Windows archive's DLLs and our MSVC
  build share the C runtime numbering for the listed codes, so the Windows table holds (the
  Linux numbering is checked when Linux tests return, v1.x).
- Risk: clang-tidy may report a diagnostic inside an FFmpeg macro expanded in our code (e.g.
  `AVERROR_INVALIDDATA`); if it does, wrap that use in a small function with a targeted `NOLINT`,
  never disable a check for the repository.
