# M4 plan, part 2: Tasks 5a, 5b, 6, 7a, 7b

Source: the approved design `prog/m4-design-v2.md` with `prog/m4-design-v2-arbiter.md` and its
verification. Tasks 0-4 (part 1) and 8-12 + P (part 3) are written separately; each task below
names what it consumes from them (flagged where assumed) and what it produces for them.

---

### Task 5a: `ffmpeg` provider, container fixtures and the `framemd5` parser

**Goal:** The `ffmpeg` source provider in dvv-components: files of any container FFmpeg can demux
(AVI, MKV, MOV, MP4, NUT, …) open through M2's `SourceFactory` when their video codec is on the
lossless allowlist (user decision U-FF; any other codec is refused at open with a clear error), are
indexed once at open, decode on one worker thread per source into
catalogue originals, and match `ffmpeg -f framemd5` frame by frame. It also takes the M3 colour keys
(design v2 D28), refuses frames that change size or format, and stops queued work when it is
destroyed. The shared worker (ring, stop flag, frame check, copy) is reused by Task 5b's `lavfi`
source.

**Execution notes** (repeated in every M4 task of this part):
- Run every command from the workspace root (the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`). Use the
  PowerShell tool on Windows. Linux runs as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. Git runs only from PowerShell, except the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only`
  inside the WSL format and tidy commands. Branch `m4`. Nothing is pushed; no remote is added; no repository is created.
- **Windows test env** (`<Windows test env>`; in the same tool call as the run):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
  With `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` an FFmpeg-dependent test that would skip (77) fails instead (design v2
  §3).
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Windows runs use `run_in_background: true`; at a checkpoint the
  WSL checkpoint build may run in parallel with a Windows run.
- **Per-task checks** (U-PROC; once, on the task's final commit; one background call for Windows;
  `<base>` = the dvv-components head before this task):
  - Windows Debug: `<Windows test env>; Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026-small --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`
    Expected: `100% tests passed`, no `Skipped` line for an `ffmpeg` or `gpu` test.
  - clang-format on the changed files (new files must be `git add`ed first):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd dvv-components && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`
    Expected: `FORMAT-OK`.
  - No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL build here: they run in the Checkpoint checks of the
    checkpoint tasks (Global Constraints: Tasks 5b, 7b, 9, 11).
- **Privacy lint** after every commit:
  `cmake -DREPO=dvv-components -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.
- Every commit message ends with the trailer
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- The earlier tasks' code is not visible while this plan is written. Step 1 compares every name this
  task consumes with the real code. A name that differs is adapted in this task (not in the earlier

- **Two sessions (task size):** the controller dispatches this task as two fresh sessions on the

```cpp
#pragma once

// Pure rules of the FFmpeg providers (design v2 §4.4, D5, D28); no FFmpeg calls, unit-tested
// without FFmpeg. FFmpeg enum types come from the dvv_ffmpeg wrapper header.

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/uri.h"           // or wherever dvv-base declares Uri (Step 1)
#include "dvv/media/types.h"
#include "ffmpeg/ffmpeg_headers.h"  // Task 4's extern "C" wrapper

namespace dvv::components::detail {

// D5: max(1, min(8, 256 MiB / frame_bytes)) frames; frame_bytes 0 counts as 1.
[[nodiscard]] std::size_t ringCapacity(std::uint64_t frame_bytes) noexcept;

// User decision U-FF (binding): the lossless video codecs v1.0 decodes, by FFmpeg codec name
// (avcodec_get_name of the stream's codec_id). MPEG-family and other lossy codecs are out of scope.
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 11> LOSSLESS_CODECS{
    "rawvideo", "v210", "v410", "r210", "ffv1", "huffyuv",
    "ffvhuff", "utvideo", "magicyuv", "png", "qtrle"};
// Ok when `codec_name` is in LOSSLESS_CODECS (exact match); else Errc::Unsupported
// "codec <codec_name> is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)".
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> checkLosslessCodec(std::string_view codec_name);

// D5 mode of the index scan: true (Sequential) when any pts is AV_NOPTS_VALUE or two pts are equal
// (any order); false (indexed) otherwise, also for an empty list. Unit-tested here, because the
// lossless fixtures may exercise only one of the two triggers (Task 0 Step 7).
[[nodiscard]] bool sequentialMode(std::span<const std::int64_t> pts);

// §4.4 match: 90 when score > 50 (AVPROBE_SCORE_EXTENSION) and the demuxer is not excluded; 70 when
// 0 < score <= 50, the demuxer is not excluded and `file_extension` (without the dot, any case) is
// one of the comma-separated `demuxer_extensions`; else 0. Excluded demuxer names: "rawvideo",
// "yuv4mpegpipe", "image2pipe" (raw and Y4M stay with their providers, spec §7.2).
[[nodiscard]] int matchConfidence(int score, std::string_view demuxer_name,
                                  std::string_view demuxer_extensions,
                                  std::string_view file_extension) noexcept;

// What the container decides (D28); fps already reduced.
struct ContainerFacts {
    media::FormatId format = 0;
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    media::Rational fps{25, 1};
};
// D28 query check of an `ffmpeg` URI. Allowed names: fmt, size, fps, COLOUR_PARAMETERS (and impl).
// Errc::Format: "unknown parameter '<name>'"; colourFromQuery's errors unchanged; "fmt is set by
// the container (<stream format name>)" when fmt names another format (findFormat for catalogue
// names, formatAlias for yuvj*: fmt=yuvj420p equals a yuv420p stream only through formatAlias; an
// unknown name or "@<json>" also differs); "size is set by the container (<W>x<H>)"
// for another or unparsable WxH; "fps is set by the container (<N or N/D>)" for another or
// unparsable fps (N, N/D or I.F, compared as reduced rationals). Returns the query's colour (the
// D28 override; Unspecified fields are not overridden).
[[nodiscard]] Result<media::ColourParams> checkContainerQuery(const Uri& uri,
                                                              const ContainerFacts& facts);
// The query colour's non-Unspecified fields replace `mapped` (matrix carries kr/kb with it); then
// validateColour. Errc::Format with validateColour's message.
[[nodiscard]] Result<media::ColourParams> overrideColour(const media::ColourParams& mapped,
                                                         const media::ColourParams& query);

// The binding colour mapping table (design v1 §4.4, kept by v2): BT470BG/SMPTE170M -> Bt601,
// BT709 -> Bt709, BT2020_NCL -> Bt2020Ncl, RGB -> Rgb; MPEG -> Limited, JPEG -> Full; trc
// BT709/SMPTE170M/BT2020_10/BT2020_12 -> Bt1886, IEC61966_2_1 -> Srgb, SMPTE2084 -> Pq,
// ARIB_STD_B67 -> Hlg, LINEAR -> Linear; primaries BT709 -> Bt709, BT470BG -> Bt601_625,
// SMPTE170M -> Bt601_525, BT2020 -> Bt2020; anything else -> Unspecified.
[[nodiscard]] media::ColourParams mapColour(AVColorSpace space, AVColorRange range,
                                            AVColorTransferCharacteristic transfer,
                                            AVColorPrimaries primaries) noexcept;

// A decoder/graph output pix_fmt name -> catalogue format: findFormat, or formatAlias (yuvj*:
// the yuv*p entry with fullRange = true). nullopt when neither knows the name.
struct CatalogueFormat { media::FormatId format = 0; bool fullRange = false; };
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<CatalogueFormat> catalogueFormat(std::string_view pix_fmt_name);

// "N" when den == 1, else "N/D" (M2's resolved-URI form).
[[nodiscard]] std::string fpsText(media::Rational fps);
// num/den reduced by the gcd; den > 0.
[[nodiscard]] media::Rational reduced(media::Rational fps) noexcept;

}  // namespace dvv::components::detail
```

- Produces (private, `src/sources/frame_ring.h` and `src/sources/av_worker.h`; Task 5b reuses them
  unchanged):

```cpp
// frame_ring.h — the last decoded frames of one source (D5); used only on its worker.
namespace dvv::components::detail {
class FrameRing {
public:
    explicit FrameRing(std::size_t capacity);            // capacity >= 1
    [[nodiscard]] const AVFrame* find(std::int64_t index) const noexcept;  // nullptr when absent
    // Takes the frame's references (av_frame_move_ref into a ring-owned AVFrame). When full, the
    // entry with the lowest index is dropped first.
    void put(std::int64_t index, AVFrame& frame);
    void clear() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::int64_t> oldest() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::int64_t> newest() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t capacity() const noexcept;
};
}

// av_worker.h — one worker thread per FFmpeg/lavfi source (design v2 §5.4).
namespace dvv::components::detail {
// The stop condition of one worker (design v2 D5, §5.4): its own flag, set by ~AvWorker, or a
// process-wide stopFfmpegSources() since the worker was created (ffmpeg::stopGeneration() changed).
struct StopCheck {
    const std::atomic<bool>* flag = nullptr;
    std::uint64_t generation = 0;
    // flag->load(std::memory_order_acquire) || ffmpeg::stopGeneration() != generation
    [[nodiscard]] bool operator()() const noexcept;
};

// The decode or filter state of one source. Called only on the worker thread.
class IFrameSupplier {
public:
    virtual ~IFrameSupplier() = default;
    // The frame of `index`, owned by the supplier's ring and valid until the next call. Checks
    // `stopped()` before each packet read, decode and filter step (Errc::Internal "source closed").
    // Every frame it decodes or pulls passes `checkFrame` before it enters the ring.
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Result<const AVFrame*> frame(std::int64_t index,
                                                       const StopCheck& stopped) = 0;
};

// §5.4 frame check: width, height and format equal the descriptor's, else Errc::Format
// "frame <index> changed to <w>x<h> <pix_fmt name>".
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> checkFrame(const AVFrame& frame, std::int64_t index,
                                      const media::FrameDesc& desc, int expected_av_format);

// Copies the planes of `frame` row by row (linesize may be padded or negative) into a HostFrame of
// the tight catalogue geometry, allocated through ctx, and publishes it. Errc::Internal when the
// catalogue plane count differs from av_pix_fmt_count_planes.
[[nodiscard]] Result<media::FrameRef> copyAndPublish(const AVFrame& frame,
                                                     const media::FrameDesc& desc,
                                                     media::ProduceContext& ctx);

class AvWorker {
public:
    AvWorker(std::unique_ptr<IFrameSupplier> supplier, media::FrameDesc desc);
    // Sets `stopping`, then destroys the pool: every queued request runs, sees the flag and
    // completes with Internal "source closed"; then the supplier (and its AV objects) dies.
    ~AvWorker();
    AvWorker(const AvWorker&) = delete;
    AvWorker& operator=(const AvWorker&) = delete;

    // ISource::produce body: hop to the worker, supplier->frame(index), copyAndPublish, then hop
    // to ctx.io() before returning (never return on the worker thread: the caller may drop the
    // last reference to the source there, and the destructor would join its own thread).
    [[nodiscard]] Task<Result<media::FrameRef>> produce(std::int64_t index,
                                                        media::ProduceContext& ctx);
    // Test seam (stop test): the same path without a ProduceContext; resumes on `resume_on`.
    [[nodiscard]] Task<Result<void>> fetch(std::int64_t index, IExecutor& resume_on);
    // Requests that entered produce/fetch and have not left the worker yet (tests).
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t queued() const noexcept;

private:
    std::atomic<bool> stopping_{false};          // design v2 D5/§5.4: the stop flag
    StopCheck stop_{&stopping_, ffmpeg::stopGeneration()};   // the flag or stopFfmpegSources()
    std::atomic<std::size_t> queued_{0};
    media::FrameDesc desc_;
    int av_format_ = -1;                         // the AVPixelFormat of desc_ (frame check)
    std::unique_ptr<IFrameSupplier> supplier_;   // declared before pool_: destroyed after it
    std::shared_ptr<IExecutor> pool_;            // ExecutorFactory::makeThreadPool(1)
};
}
```

- Produces (private, `src/sources/ffmpeg_provider.h`, namespace `dvv::components`):

```cpp
class FfmpegSourceProvider final : public media::ISourceProvider,
                                   public Managed<FfmpegSourceProvider> {
public:
    FfmpegSourceProvider(CreationKey<ComponentRegistry> key, const ParameterValues& values) noexcept;
    static ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "ffmpeg", .name = "FFmpeg container files", .kind = Kind::Source,
                .version = 1};
    }
    static const ParameterSchema& schema();                    // empty
    static std::expected<void, Unavailable> probe(const Capabilities& caps) {
        return require(caps, {"ffmpeg"});
    }
    std::span<const std::string_view> schemes() const noexcept override;   // {"file"}
    media::Match match(const media::SourceRequest&, std::span<const std::byte> head) const override;
    Task<Result<std::unique_ptr<media::ISource>>> open(media::OpenTicket, const media::SourceRequest&,
                                                       const media::FormatGuess&,
                                                       const media::OpenContext&) override;
};
// FfmpegSource final : media::ISource, Managed<FfmpegSource>; constructor
// (CreationKey<media::SourceFactory>, media::SourceInfo, std::unique_ptr<detail::AvWorker>);
// produce() checks the index, then forwards to the worker.
```

- Produces (public, added to Task 4's `include/dvv/components/ffmpeg.h`; defined in `dvv_ffmpeg`,
  `src/ffmpeg/loader.cpp`, so an application can call it whatever components are enabled; Task 7a
  calls it):

```cpp
// Ends the current and queued work of every FFmpeg and lavfi source (design v2 D5, §5.4: exit is
// bounded): from now on their workers complete each request with Internal "source closed". Sources
// created afterwards work normally. The applications call it before the Session or CLI shutdown,
// because the store's queued Produce steps keep their sources alive (M2 R11) and the Session waits
// for in-flight acquisitions. Thread-safe; increments a process-wide generation.
void stopFfmpegSources() noexcept;
```
  and in `src/ffmpeg/api.h`: `[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t stopGeneration() noexcept;` (namespace
  `dvv::components::ffmpeg`; a `std::atomic<std::uint64_t>` read with acquire, incremented with
  release by `stopFfmpegSources()`).
- CMake (top-level `CMakeLists.txt`; Task 5b appends its sources and `lavfi` to `REGISTERS`; the
  `LINK` list is Task 4's: `dvv_ffmpeg_internal` brings `src/` and FFmpeg's headers, and
  `uri_colour.h` is in `dvv::media`, which every component links PUBLIC):

```cmake
# FFmpeg source providers (design v2 §4.4): loaded at run time, never linked (D3).
dvv_add_component(ffmpeg KIND Source
    SOURCES src/sources/ffmpeg_rules.cpp src/sources/frame_ring.cpp src/sources/av_worker.cpp
            src/sources/container_supplier.cpp src/sources/ffmpeg_provider.cpp
    LINK dvv::ffmpeg $<BUILD_INTERFACE:dvv_ffmpeg_internal>
    REGISTERS ffmpeg)
```
  (Never `dvv::media_sources` here: executables link it as a whole archive through
  `dvv_link_components`, and CMake rejects a library linked both plainly and as a whole archive;
  never the internal target without `$<BUILD_INTERFACE:…>`: `install(EXPORT)` would fail.)
- Produces for later tasks: provider id `ffmpeg` (Tasks 7a, 7b, 8); the resolved-URI form
  `file:<path>?fmt=<name>&size=WxH&fps=<fps>[&<colour keys>]&impl=ffmpeg` (Tasks 7a, 7b); the
  fixture script `tests/oracle/make-container-fixtures.cmake` and the `framemd5` parser (Task 5b
  reuses the run helper pattern); `AvWorker`/`FrameRing`/`checkFrame`/`copyAndPublish` (Task 5b);
  the allowlist and its error text "codec <name> is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)"
  (Tasks 7a, 10, 11).

**Behaviour:**
- **`match(request, head)`** (no I/O, design v2 §4.4):
  - Copy `head` into a buffer of `head.size() + AVPROBE_PADDING_SIZE` bytes, the padding zeroed
    (`av_probe_input_format3` reads past `buf_size`; an ASan test uses a 1-byte head).
  - `AVProbeData{.filename = <UTF-8 file name of uri.path>, .buf = <buffer>, .buf_size = <head
    size>}`; `fmt = av_probe_input_format3(&pd, 1, &score)`.
  - `fmt == nullptr` → `{0, {}, "FFmpeg does not recognise the file"}`.
  - Else `c = matchConfidence(score, fmt->name, fmt->extensions ? fmt->extensions : "", <extension
    of the file name without the dot>)`; why: 90 → `"FFmpeg demuxer <name> (score <s>)"`, 70 →
    `"FFmpeg demuxer <name> by extension (score <s>)"`, 0 → `"FFmpeg demuxer <name> left to its
    own provider"` for an excluded demuxer, else `"FFmpeg probe score <s> is too low"`. The guess
    stays empty (the format is known only after decoding).
- **`open(ticket, request, guess, ctx)`** (`co_await resumeOn(ctx.io)` first; all FFmpeg work of
  open runs on the I/O pool, design v2 §5.1):
  1. Nothing of the query is checked before the file is open; `checkContainerQuery` runs in
     step 9 with the stream's facts (unknown names, colour values and the equality rules together).
  2. Open: `avformat_open_input(&fc, "file:" + uri.path, nullptr, &opts)` with
     `opts = {protocol_whitelist=file}` (D3; the `file:` prefix keeps a Windows drive letter from
     being read as a protocol; FFmpeg's file protocol takes UTF-8 names on Windows).
     `avformat_find_stream_info(fc, nullptr)`. Errors → `ffmpegError(call, averror)` (§7:
     `AVERROR(ENOENT)`/`EIO` → `Io`, `AVERROR_INVALIDDATA` → `Format`, `DEMUXER_NOT_FOUND` →
     `Unsupported`, `ENOMEM` → `OutOfMemory`, others `Internal` with the `<call> failed: <text>
     (AVERROR <n>)` message).
  3. **Stream choice** (D5): `s = av_find_best_stream(fc, AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO, -1, -1, nullptr, 0)`;
     when `s` has `AV_DISPOSITION_ATTACHED_PIC`, take the first video stream without it;
     `AVERROR_STREAM_NOT_FOUND` or none left → `Unsupported` "no video stream". Every other
     stream gets `discard = AVDISCARD_ALL`.
  3b. **Codec allowlist** (U-FF, binding): `checkLosslessCodec(avcodec_get_name(
     stream->codecpar->codec_id))` → its `Unsupported` "codec <name> is not supported in v1.0
     (lossless codecs only)" ends the open (the format context is closed; no index scan, no
     decoder lookup). It runs before step 7, so the message is the same whether or not the build
     has a decoder for that codec. It uses the codec id the demuxer reports: an AVI FourCC such as
     `XVID`, `DIVX`, `H264` or `MJPG`, an MP4/MOV `avc1`/`hvc1`/`mp4v` sample entry, a Matroska
     `V_MPEG4/ISO/AVC` codec id or an MPEG elementary stream all name a codec outside the list and
     are refused.
  4. **Index scan** (D5; demux only, uncancellable, R7): read every packet with `av_read_frame`;
     packets of other streams and packets with `AV_PKT_FLAG_DISCARD` are ignored; for each other
     packet record `pts` and `flags & AV_PKT_FLAG_KEY`. `AVERROR_EOF` ends the scan; other errors
     → `ffmpegError`. No indexed packet → `Format` "no video frames".
  5. **Mode** (D5; the pure rule `detail::sequentialMode(pts list)` of `ffmpeg_rules`, unit case
     9): any packet with `pts == AV_NOPTS_VALUE`, or two packets with equal `pts` →
     `Sequential`, `frameCount` = the number of indexed packets (parsers deliver one frame per
     packet; a decoder that delivers fewer frames hits the "not produced" error). Otherwise
     indexed: entries sorted by `pts` (presentation order), `frameCount` = their number, `access =
     RandomCostly`.
  6. Reopen the format context (close + open as in step 2, stream discards again) so decoding
     starts at the beginning; this is also the `Sequential` restart (D5).
  7. Decoder (an allowlisted codec, step 3b): `avcodec_find_decoder(codecpar->codec_id)` (null →
     `Unsupported` "no decoder for <avcodec_get_name(id)>"), `avcodec_alloc_context3`,
     `avcodec_parameters_to_context`,
     `thread_count = 0` (FFmpeg's own threads), `pkt_timebase = stream->time_base`,
     `avcodec_open2`. Software decoding only.
  8. Frame 0: the supplier decodes index 0 (into its ring). `catalogueFormat(av_get_pix_fmt_name(
     frame->format))` → none → `Unsupported` "decoder output <name> is not in the catalogue".
     `desc = {format, colour (step 10), frame->width, frame->height}`.
  9. `fps = reduced(av_guess_frame_rate(fc, stream, nullptr))`; `num <= 0 || den <= 0` → 25/1 and
     the warning "frame rate unknown, 25 fps assumed". Then
     `checkContainerQuery(uri, {format, width, height, fps})` → its `Format` errors (D28); its
     value is the query colour.
  10. Colour: `mapped = mapColour(frame->colorspace, frame->color_range, frame->color_trc,
      frame->color_primaries)`; each field still Unspecified takes the `codecpar` value through the
      same table; an alias (`fullRange`) sets `range = Full` when still Unspecified;
      `colour = overrideColour(mapped, query colour)`; `desc.colour = internColour(colour)`.
  11. `resolvedUri = formatUri(Uri{"file", "", <uri.path as given>, {fmt=<descriptor(format).name>,
      size=<W>x<H>, fps=<fpsText(fps)>, <colourParameters(query colour)…>, impl=ffmpeg}})` (M2's
      raw provider builds its URI the same way; the Y4M pattern of M3: only the query's colour
      keys).
  12. `SourceInfo{desc, fps, frameCount, access (RandomCostly or Sequential), deterministic =
      true, resolvedUri, provider = "ffmpeg", warnings}` with `reader = "ffmpeg"` (U-IO2: FFmpeg's
      own file I/O; see "Containers"); ring capacity `ringCapacity(geometry(
      format, w, h)->bytes)`; the supplier (format context, decoder, index, ring with frame 0) moves
      into `std::make_unique<AvWorker>(…)` (the worker thread starts here); the source is
      `std::make_unique<FfmpegSource>(media::SourceFactory::issueKey<FfmpegSource>(std::move(ticket)),
      info, std::move(worker))`.
- **Containers (U-FF):** open, the index scan, seek and the `Sequential` rules are the same for every
  container. Nothing is keyed by the demuxer name, except the `match` exclusions
  (`rawvideo`, `yuv4mpegpipe`, `image2pipe`).
  - AVI (`avi` demuxer, probe score 100) stores no timestamps. Its packet `dts` comes from the frame
    position in its index, and libavformat fills `pts` from `dts` for codecs without reordering
    (every allowlisted codec). So an AVI is normally indexed (`RandomCostly`); Task 0 Step 7
    recorded it. Keyframe flags come from the AVI index, and `avformat_seek_file` seeks through it.
  - An AVI without an index (`idx1`/OpenDML) is indexed by the scan of step 4 as well. An AVI whose
    packets lack `pts` takes the `Sequential` path like any other container.
  - MKV, MOV/MP4 and NUT carry timestamps. MOV and MP4 share FFmpeg's `mov` demuxer, so the MOV
    fixtures of cases 9 and 13 also cover MP4 (no separate lossless MP4 fixture).
  - File reads (U-IO2): the provider reads through FFmpeg's own file protocol, not through
    dvv-platform's `FileReader`, so `SourceRequest::fileReads` (`io.fileReads` of `rawreel.json`)
    does not apply and is ignored; `SourceInfo::reader` is `"ffmpeg"` (the lavfi provider of Task 5b
    sets `"lavfi"`), so `info` and the Info pane print `reads: ffmpeg`. Step 1 checks the field name
    and how M3.1's `info` prints it.
- **`FfmpegSource::produce(i, ctx)`:** `i` outside `[0, frameCount)` → `Internal` "frame <i> is
  outside [0, <F>)"; else `co_return co_await worker_->produce(i, ctx)`. Concurrent calls (the
  store's prefetch, K = 4) queue on the worker (§5.4: "`produce` is called only by the store the
  source is attached to; concurrent calls queue on the worker").
- **Indexed path** (`ContainerSupplier::frame(i)`, D5):
  - In the ring → return it.
  - `last` = the newest index decoded since the last seek (none after a seek or at start). When
    `last` exists and `last < i <= last + capacity` → decode forward. Otherwise seek: `k` = the
    last keyframe entry with `pts[k] <= pts[i]` (entry 0 when none is flagged);
    `avformat_seek_file(fc, s, INT64_MIN, pts[k], pts[k], 0)`, then `avcodec_flush_buffers` after
    **every** `avformat_seek_file`, `ring.clear()`, decode forward.
  - Decode forward: loop { check `stopped()` (→ `Internal` "source closed"); `avcodec_receive_frame`:
    a frame → `j` = the entry whose `pts == frame->pts` (binary search; a frame with
    `AV_NOPTS_VALUE` or an unknown `pts` is dropped with a debug log line); after a seek to entry
    `k`, a frame with `j < k` (an open-GOP leading frame that may reference the previous GOP) is
    dropped too: not put into the ring, `last` unchanged; `checkFrame(frame, j)`;
    `ring.put(j)`; `last = j`; `j == i` → return; `j > i` → `Format` "frame <i> not produced by the
    decoder". `AVERROR(EAGAIN)` → check `stopped()`, then read packets (`av_read_frame`, skipping
    other streams and `AV_PKT_FLAG_DISCARD`) and `avcodec_send_packet`; at the demuxer's
    `AVERROR_EOF` send the drain packet (`nullptr`) once. `AVERROR_EOF` from the decoder → `Format`
    "frame <i> not produced by the decoder" and the next request seeks (the drained decoder needs a
    flush). }
- **`Sequential` path** (D5, D6 rule): output frames are numbered 0, 1, 2… in decoder order since
  the last restart. In the ring → return it. `i` > the newest → decode forward (any distance).
  Otherwise (behind the ring) → restart: close and reopen the `AVFormatContext` (step 6 of open)
  and `avcodec_flush_buffers` (no timestamp seek), `ring.clear()`, then decode forward. The
  decoder's end before frame `i` → `Format` "frame <i> not produced by the decoder".
  `best_effort_timestamp` is not used.
- **Stop flag** (design v2 D5, §5.4): `AvWorker::~AvWorker` sets `stopping_` before the pool is
  destroyed; `stopFfmpegSources()` changes the process-wide generation. `StopCheck` (`stop_`)
  sees either; the supplier checks it before every packet read, decode call and (5b) filter pull;
  `produce`/`fetch` check it when they start on the worker. Every request that sees it completes with
  `Internal` "source closed" (the Session already drops results of a closed source). In the store
  path a source with queued Produce steps is not destroyed (M2 R11), so the destructor's flag bounds
  only a source without queued work; application exit is bounded by `stopFfmpegSources()` (Task
  7a); close and Reopen drain the old source's work in the background (the Session drops its
  results).
- **Frame check** (§5.4): every decoded frame passes `checkFrame` before it enters the ring;
  `AVFrame::format` is compared with the `AVPixelFormat` of frame 0 (an alias decoder keeps its
  `yuvj*` value). A difference → `Format` "frame <j> changed to <w>x<h> <av_get_pix_fmt_name>".
- **Copy** (`copyAndPublish`): `g = geometry(desc.format, desc.width, desc.height)`;
  `ctx.allocateHost(desc, g.bytes, 64, 0)`; for each plane `p < g.planeCount`: `rows` rows of
  `rowBytes` from `frame.data[p] + r * frame.linesize[p]` (signed arithmetic: linesize may be
  negative) to `data() + g.planes[p].offset + r * g.planes[p].stride`; then `ctx.publish`. The
  catalogue names are FFmpeg's (M2 D3), so plane `p` of the catalogue format is `data[p]` (gbrp:
  G, B, R in both).
- **Worker threading** (§5.1, §5.4): decoder state (format context, codec context, index, ring,
  `last`) is touched only on the worker after open. `produce` posts with `co_await
  resumeOn(*pool_)`; the result is computed there; `queued_` is decremented there; then
  `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io())` before `co_return` (code below).
- **Errors** (§7): as in steps 2–10 (step 3b: `Unsupported` with the allowlist text); ring and
  copy errors as above; `AVERROR(ENOMEM)` → `OutOfMemory`.

**Code** (binding: the worker hand-off and the destructor order):

```cpp
// src/sources/av_worker.cpp
AvWorker::AvWorker(std::unique_ptr<IFrameSupplier> supplier, media::FrameDesc desc)
    : desc_(desc), supplier_(std::move(supplier)),
      pool_(ExecutorFactory::makeThreadPool(1)) {}   // the source's own worker (D5)

AvWorker::~AvWorker() {
    stopping_.store(true, std::memory_order_release);  // §5.4: before joining the worker
    pool_.reset();       // runs every queued resumption (each sees stopping_), then joins
    supplier_.reset();   // the AV objects die after the worker thread is gone
}

Task<Result<media::FrameRef>> AvWorker::produce(std::int64_t index, media::ProduceContext& ctx) {
    queued_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    co_await resumeOn(*pool_);
    Result<media::FrameRef> result = [&]() -> Result<media::FrameRef> {
        if (stop_()) {
            return makeError(Errc::Internal, "source closed");
        }
        auto frame = supplier_->frame(index, stop_);
        if (!frame) {
            return std::unexpected(frame.error());
        }
        return copyAndPublish(**frame, desc_, ctx);
    }();
    queued_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    // Leave the worker before returning: the store may release the last reference to the source
    // in our continuation, and ~AvWorker on the worker thread would join itself.
    co_await resumeOn(ctx.io());
    co_return result;
}
```
(`fetch` is the same with `resume_on` instead of `ctx.io()` and without the copy. Use the
project's error helpers (`makeError`/`std::unexpected`) as M2's sources do. If Step 1 found that a
destroyed pool drops posted items, `~AvWorker` posts a no-op that sets a `std::promise` and waits
for it before `pool_.reset()`.)

**Tests first:**
- `tests/test_ffmpeg_rules.cpp` (`dvv_components_tests`, `TEST_SUITE("ffmpeg_rules")`, no FFmpeg):
  1. `ring capacity`: `ringCapacity(352 * 288 * 3 / 2) == 8`; `ringCapacity(0) == 8`;
     `ringCapacity(40ull << 20) == 6`; `ringCapacity(7680ull * 4320 * 6) == 1`;
     `ringCapacity(256ull << 20) == 1`.
  2. `match confidence` (pure inputs; a low score with a known extension is the extension rule,
     whatever the codec): `(60, "matroska,webm", "mkv,mk3d,mka,mks,webm", "mkv") == 90`;
     `(100, "avi", "avi", "bin") == 90`; `(25, "image2", "bmp,png,tiff", "png") == 70`;
     `(25, "image2", "bmp,png,tiff", "PNG") == 70`; `(25, "image2", "bmp,png,tiff", "bin") == 0`;
     `(50, "image2", "bmp,png,tiff", "png") == 70`; `(0, "image2", "bmp,png,tiff", "png") == 0`;
     `(100, "rawvideo", "yuv,rgb", "yuv") == 0`; `(100, "yuv4mpegpipe", "y4m", "y4m") == 0`;
     `(60, "image2pipe", "", "png") == 0`; `(25, "image2", "", "png") == 0`.
  3. `container query`: facts `{findFormat("yuv420p10le"), 64, 48, {25, 1}}`:
     `file:x.mkv?matrix=bt601&range=full` → `{.matrix = Bt601, .range = Full}`;
     `?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=64x48&fps=25` → ok, all Unspecified; `?fps=50/2` → ok;
     `?fps=25.0` → ok; `?impl=ffmpeg` → ok; `?fmt=gray` → `Format` containing
     `"fmt is set by the container (yuv420p10le)"`; `?fmt=@x.json` → `Format` (fmt);
     `?size=32x24` → `Format` containing `"size is set by the container (64x48)"`; `?size=64*48`
     → `Format` (size); `?fps=30` → `Format` containing `"fps is set by the container (25)"`;
     `?w=64` → `Format` `"unknown parameter 'w'"`; `?transfer=gamma` → `Format` containing
     `"bad transfer"`; `?matrix=custom&kr=0.212&kb=0.087` → `Custom`, kr `0.212f`, kb `0.087f`.
  4. `colour override`: mapped `{Bt709, Limited, Bt1886, Bt709}` with query `{.matrix = Bt601}` →
     `{Bt601, Limited, Bt1886, Bt709}`; with query `{.matrix = Custom, .kr = 0.212f, .kb = 0.087f}`
     → Custom with kr/kb; with `ColourParams{}` → unchanged.
  5. `colour mapping table`: every row of the table (one `CHECK` per input value, 19 rows) and one
     unknown value per field (`AVCOL_SPC_FCC`, `AVCOL_RANGE_UNSPECIFIED`, `AVCOL_TRC_GAMMA22`,
     `AVCOL_PRI_BT470M`) → Unspecified.
  6. `catalogue format`: `"yuv420p"` → yuv420p, `fullRange` false; `"yuvj420p"` → yuv420p,
     `fullRange` true; `"yuvj444p"` → yuv444p; `"gbrp10le"` → gbrp10le; `"bgr0"` → nullopt.
  7. `fps text`: `fpsText({25, 1}) == "25"`, `fpsText({30000, 1001}) == "30000/1001"`;
     `reduced({50, 2}) == Rational{25, 1}`; `reduced({60000, 2002}) == Rational{30000, 1001}`.
  8. `lossless codec allowlist` (U-FF): each of the 11 names of `LOSSLESS_CODECS` → ok;
     `"h264"`, `"hevc"`, `"mpeg1video"`, `"mpeg2video"`, `"mpeg4"`, `"av1"`, `"mjpeg"`, `"prores"`,
     `"FFV1"` (case matters) and `""` → `Unsupported`; the `"h264"` message contains
     `"codec h264 is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)"`.
  9. `sequential mode` (review fix): `{0, 1, 2}` → false; `{}` → false; `{2, 0, 1}` → false
     (unsorted, unique); `{0, AV_NOPTS_VALUE, 2}` → true (D5's first trigger, which a lossless
     fixture may never produce); `{0, 0, 1, 1}` → true; `{3, 1, 3}` → true.
- `tests/test_framemd5.cpp` (`dvv_components_tests`, `TEST_SUITE("framemd5")`): `parseFramemd5`
  on an in-memory text with the six header lines (`#format: frame checksums`, `#version: 2`,
  `#hash: MD5`, `#tb 0: 1/25`, `#media_type 0: video`, `#codec_id 0: rawvideo`, then
  `#dimensions 0: 352x288`, `#sar 0: 1/1`, `#stream#, dts,        pts, duration,     size, hash`)
  and three lines `0,          0,          0,        1,   152064, 0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef`
  → 3 entries with `pts` 0, 1, 2, `size` 152064 and the hashes; a line with 5 fields → `Format`;
  a hash that is not 32 hex digits → `Format`; lines of stream 1 are ignored.
- `tests/ffmpeg/framemd5.h` (test helper):
  `struct FrameHash { std::int64_t pts; std::uint64_t size; std::string md5; };`
  `[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::vector<FrameHash>> parseFramemd5(std::string_view text);`
  (stream 0 only; fields split at `,` and trimmed);
  `[[nodiscard]] std::string md5Hex(std::span<const std::byte> bytes);` (through
  `api().av_md5_sum`; FFmpeg must be loaded, so only the ffmpeg tests call it).
- `tests/ffmpeg/ffmpeg_harness.h` (as M2's dvv-media `tests/source_harness.h`; read it in Step 1):
  a registry created with `Capabilities` filled by `addFfmpegCapabilities(loadFfmpeg(), caps)` and
  every linked component (`dvv_components_link(dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests)` plus
  `dvv_link_components(… dvv::media_sources)`), cpu and io pools of 4 threads, a `SourceFactory`
  and a host-only `FrameStore`; `open(uri)` (REQUIRE success), `tryOpen(uri)`,
  `originalBytes(source, index)` (attach once per source, `acquireOriginal(…, Api::HostPlanes,
  hostDomain(), …, requireExact = true)`, copy of the tight bytes), `fixture(name)` = the path of a
  fixture file under `DVV_FFMPEG_FIXTURES`, `fixtureUri(name)` = `file:` + its UTF-8 path.
- `tests/ffmpeg/test_ffmpeg_provider.cpp` (`dvv_components_ffmpeg_tests`,
  `TEST_SUITE("ffmpeg")`, so `dvv_components.ffmpeg` runs them; fixtures from
  `make-container-fixtures.cmake`):
  1. `ffmpeg: FFV1 MKV matches framemd5` — for every format `F` of the FFV1 list (below):
     open `ffv1_<F>.mkv` → `info().provider == "ffmpeg"`, `descriptor(desc.format).name == F`,
     352×288, `frameCount == 10`, `access == RandomCostly`, `fps == {25, 1}`; for i in 0..9
     `md5Hex(originalBytes(i)) == hashes[i].md5` of `ffv1_<F>.framemd5` (10 entries). A failure
     prints the format, frame, our hash and the reference.
  2. `ffmpeg: a Sequential stream matches framemd5 forward, backward and random` — `<seq>` (the
     lossless `Sequential` fixture Task 0 Step 7 chose: `seq.avi` or `seq.nut` (rawvideo, packets
     without `pts`) or `seq.mkv` (FFV1, pairs of equal `pts`)): `access == Sequential`,
     `frameCount == 30`; frames in the order 0..29, then 29, 3, 17, 0, 28, 5 (each through a fresh
     `acquireOriginal`; 3 and 0 are behind the ring and restart), then the 30 frames in a fixed
     shuffled order (seed 1, `std::mt19937` + `std::shuffle`); every hash equals `seq.framemd5`
     (30 entries, in file order).
  3. `ffmpeg: seek and flush on the indexed path` — `ffv1_yuv420p.mkv`: frames 9, 2, 8, 0, 5 in
     that order (9 → 2 is behind the ring → seek + flush) equal their hashes.
  4. `ffmpeg: concurrent produce calls from one store` — `ffv1_yuv420p10le.mkv` attached to one
     host-only store whose cpu pool has 4 threads: `whenAll` of 10 `acquireOriginal` tasks for
     frames 9..0 → every hash equals the reference (run under `vs2026-asan` at the Task 5b checkpoint; TSan
     returns with Linux, v1.x).
  5. `ffmpeg: destroying a Sequential source with 4 queued far-back requests returns within 1 s` —
     build the `AvWorker` of `long.<ext>` (3000 FFV1 frames, `Sequential` like `<seq>`) through
     the test-only factory function `detail::makeContainerWorkerForTest(path)` (declared in
     `container_supplier.h`, implemented with the same code as `open`); start four `fetch` calls for 2999, 1500, 2999, 1500 on four
     `std::thread`s (each `syncWait(worker->fetch(i, *testPool))`, the result stored); wait until
     `worker->queued() == 4`; `t0 = steady_clock::now()`; `worker.reset()`; `elapsed <
     1000 ms`; join the threads; every result is `Internal` containing "source closed".
  6. `ffmpeg: a frame that changes size is Format` — `concat_sizes.mkv` (PNG, `gray`; frames 0..4
     352×288, 5..9 176×144; PNG carries its size in every frame, so the decoder reports the
     change): frames 0..4 succeed; frame 5 → `Format` containing
     `"frame 5 changed to 176x144 gray"`.
  7. `ffmpeg: colour from the container and the D28 override` — `ffv1_yuv420p.mkv` (tagged BT.709,
     limited, trc bt709, primaries bt709) → `colourParams(desc.colour) == {Bt709, Limited, Bt1886,
     Bt709}`; resolved URI equals `file:<path>?fmt=yuv420p&size=352x288&fps=25&impl=ffmpeg`.
     `<uri>?matrix=bt601` → `{Bt601, Limited, Bt1886, Bt709}`; its resolved URI equals
     `file:<path>?fmt=yuv420p&size=352x288&fps=25&matrix=bt601&impl=ffmpeg`; reopening that
     resolved URI gives the same `desc` (same `ColourId`) and frame 3's hash (the in-process Reopen
     round trip).
  8. `ffmpeg: fmt, size and fps must equal the stream` — `<uri>?fmt=yuv420p&size=352x288&fps=25`
     opens; `?fmt=gray` → `Format` containing "fmt is set by the container"; `?size=176x144` →
     `Format` (size); `?fps=30` → `Format` (fps).
  9. `ffmpeg: match with a 1-byte head` — `provider.match({parseUri("file:x.mkv")}, one byte 0x1A)`
     runs (ASan checks the padding) and returns 0 or a valid confidence; with the first 4096 bytes
     of `lossless_huffyuv.avi` and name `clip.avi` → 90 and the reason contains
     `"FFmpeg demuxer avi"` (Task 0 recorded the AVI score with a 4096-byte probe, expected 100);
     with the same head and name `clip.bin` → 90 (the content probe needs no extension); the same
     two checks with the head of `lossless_v210.mov` (name `clip.mov`) → 90. The extension rule
     (score ≤ 50) is covered by the unit case `match confidence`: no lossless fixture probes that
     low.
  10. `ffmpeg: raw and Y4M files stay with their providers` — a 3-frame `x_16x16_gray.yuv` written by
      the test opens with provider `raw`; a Y4M written by the test opens with `y4m`.
  11. `ffmpeg: errors` — a missing file → `Io`; a text file `notes.txt` with `impl=ffmpeg` →
      `Unsupported` (match 0) and no crash; `audio_only.mka` (fixture) → `Unsupported` "no video
      stream".
  12. `ffmpeg: stopFfmpegSources ends queued store requests within 1 s` — a harness of its own;
      open `long.<ext>` (3000 frames, `Sequential`) and attach it; on a `std::thread`,
      `syncWait` of `acquireOriginal(… 2999 …)` (seconds of decoding); after 200 ms, `t0 =
      steady_clock::now()`, `stopFfmpegSources()`; join the thread; `elapsed < 1000 ms`; the result
      is `Internal` containing "source closed" (whether the request was decoding or still
      queued). Then a source opened afterwards works: `<seq>` frame 0 equals its hash (a new
      worker takes the new generation). Detach and destroy everything.
  13. `ffmpeg: lossless codecs in AVI, MKV, MOV and NUT open and match framemd5` (U-FF) — for each
      of `lossless_huffyuv.avi` (`yuv422p`), `lossless_utvideo.mkv` (`yuv420p`),
      `lossless_v210.mov` (`yuv422p10le`) and `lossless_magicyuv.nut` (`yuv444p`): `provider ==
      "ffmpeg"`, `descriptor(desc.format).name` as given, 192×144, `frameCount == 10`, `access ==
      RandomCostly` (or `Sequential` for a container whose packets Task 0 Step 7 recorded without
      `pts`), `fps == {25, 1}`; frames 0..9, then 7 (in the 8-frame ring, which holds 2..9), then 1
      and 0 (behind the ring: a seek through the container's index, then the ring), each `md5Hex`
      equal to its entry in `lossless_<codec>.framemd5`. A
      failure prints the file, frame, our hash and the reference.
  14. `ffmpeg: a lossy codec is refused at open` (U-FF) — (a) always: the test reads the first
      4096 bytes of `lossless_huffyuv.avi`, replaces every `HFYU` FourCC in them with `XVID` (the
      AVI demuxer then reports codec `mpeg4`), writes the file to the test's temporary folder with
      the rest unchanged and opens it: `match` gives 90 (AVI is a container FFmpeg demuxes);
      `tryOpen` → `Unsupported` whose message contains
      `"codec mpeg4 is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)"`; no crash. (b) When Task 0
      recorded "mpeg4 encoder: yes": `lossy_mpeg4.mp4` (a real 5-frame MPEG-4 Part 2 clip) gives
      the same `Unsupported` message. A refused open leaves no source and no worker thread
      (`LifetimeRegistry` live count unchanged).
- `tests/oracle/make-container-fixtures.cmake` (CTest `dvv_components.ffmpeg_fixtures`,
  `FIXTURES_SETUP dvv_components_ffmpeg_fixtures`; arguments `-DFFMPEG=${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}
  -DOUT=<dir> -DLIBDIR=${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}` from Task 4's `find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk)`; on
  Linux every command runs through `cmake -E env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=<LIBDIR>`). The output directory
  is keyed by the FFmpeg version (`<OUT>/<version>/`, `OUT` = `<build>/ffmpeg-fixtures`; `<version>`
  = the third token of `ffmpeg -version` line 1, e.g. `n8.1.3`, with every character outside
  `[A-Za-z0-9._-]` removed) with a `done.stamp`; an existing stamp skips the work. The script writes the absolute version folder it
  used into `<OUT>/current.txt`; the harness's `fixture(name)` resolves names through it
  (`DVV_FFMPEG_FIXTURES` = `OUT`, known at configure time). Without the program: print "ffmpeg program missing" and exit 0
  without a stamp (the provider cases then see no fixtures: 77, or a failure under
  `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1`). Every command has `-hide_banner -nostdin -y`; every reference command
  has `-fps_mode passthrough`; a failing command is `FATAL_ERROR` with its output. Commands:
  - FFV1/MKV, for each `F` in `gray gray10le gray12le gray16le yuv420p yuv422p yuv444p yuv420p10le
    yuv422p10le yuv444p10le yuv420p12le yuv422p12le yuv444p12le yuv420p16le yuv444p16le gbrp
    gbrp10le gbrp16le`:
    `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=352x288:r=25 -frames:v 10 -pix_fmt <F> -c:v ffv1 -level 3 ffv1_<F>.mkv`
    (`yuv420p` additionally `-colorspace bt709 -color_range tv -color_trc bt709 -color_primaries bt709`;
    `gbrp` with `-c:v ffvhuff` instead of `-c:v ffv1 -level 3`, because FFmpeg's FFV1 encoder lists
    no 8-bit `gbrp` and would pick another format; the file keeps the name `ffv1_gbrp.mkv` so the
    loop stays one list),
    then `ffmpeg -i ffv1_<F>.mkv -map 0:v:0 -fps_mode passthrough -f framemd5 ffv1_<F>.framemd5`.
  - The `Sequential` fixture (D5, arbiter FIX 8; U-FF: lossless). Only the candidate Task 0 Step 7
    chose is written, as `<seq>` and `long.<ext>`:
    - `seq.avi` (rawvideo in AVI, packets without `pts`):
      `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=352x288:r=25 -frames:v 30 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v rawvideo seq.avi`;
      `long.avi`: `-frames:v 3000 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v ffv1 -level 3`, same container.
    - `seq.nut` (rawvideo in NUT, packets without `pts`): the same two commands with `.nut`.
    - `seq.mkv` (FFV1 in MKV, pairs of frames with equal `pts`, D5's second trigger):
      `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=352x288:r=25 -frames:v 30 -vf "setpts=floor(N/2)/(25*TB)" -fps_mode passthrough -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v ffv1 -level 3 seq.mkv`;
      `long.mkv`: the same with `-frames:v 3000`.
    - then `ffmpeg -i <seq> -map 0:v:0 -fps_mode passthrough -f framemd5 seq.framemd5` (30
      entries); `long.<ext>` has no framemd5.
  - Lossless codecs per container (U-FF), 10 frames each at `testsrc2=s=192x144:r=25`, each
    followed by `ffmpeg -i <file> -map 0:v:0 -fps_mode passthrough -f framemd5 <file stem>.framemd5`:
    `-pix_fmt yuv422p -c:v huffyuv lossless_huffyuv.avi`; `-pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v utvideo
    lossless_utvideo.mkv`; `-pix_fmt yuv422p10le -c:v v210 lossless_v210.mov`; `-pix_fmt yuv444p
    -c:v magicyuv lossless_magicyuv.nut`.
  - `concat` size change (§5.4): `part_a.mkv` = `testsrc2=s=352x288:r=25`, 5 frames, `-pix_fmt
    gray -c:v png`; `part_b.mkv` the same at `s=176x144`; a list file
    `concat.txt` with `file 'part_a.mkv'` and `file 'part_b.mkv'`;
    `ffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i concat.txt -c copy concat_sizes.mkv`.
  - The lossy refusal clip (U-FF), only when Task 0 recorded "mpeg4 encoder: yes":
    `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25 -frames:v 5 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v mpeg4 lossy_mpeg4.mp4`
    (no framemd5; it only has to be refused).
  - `audio_only.mka`: `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i sine=frequency=440:duration=1 -c:a pcm_s16le audio_only.mka`.
- Conformance (Task 3's test): the generated `CMAKE_COMPONENTS` gains `ffmpeg` by itself (from
  `REGISTERS`); add the capability-specific assertions: with `Capabilities{}` its status is
  unavailable and the reason names `ffmpeg`; with `Capabilities{"ffmpeg", "ffmpeg.lavfi"}` it is
  available and `createComponent<ISourceProvider>("ffmpeg")` gives a `ManagedObject` whose live
  count returns to the old value after destruction.
- Compile-fail (Task 3's block, next case number 2): `#include "sources/ffmpeg_provider.h"` and
  `dvv::components::FfmpegSourceProvider provider(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry>(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<dvv::components::FfmpegSourceProvider>()), dvv::ParameterValues{});`
  → patterns `C2248` (MSVC) and `private constructor` (Clang) (design v2 §6). The loop becomes
  `foreach(case IN ITEMS 1 2)` with one more pattern in `compile_fail_patterns`, and the control
  target and every case target also get `target_link_libraries(<target> PRIVATE dvv::ffmpeg
  dvv_ffmpeg_internal)`, so case 2 fails on the private constructor, not on a missing include.
- First failures: `dvv_components_tests` does not compile (no `ffmpeg_rules.h`, no
  `framemd5.h`); after the stubs, the rules cases fail; the provider cases fail with "no source
  provider for scheme" until the component exists.

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Step 1: read and check the consumed names.** `git -C dvv-components log --oneline -5`,
  `git -C dvv-media log --oneline -5`; read dvv-components' `CMakeLists.txt`,
  `cmake/DvvComponent.cmake`, `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, Task 4's `src/ffmpeg/` (wrapper header,
  function list, `api()`, handles, `ffmpegError`), `include/dvv/components/ffmpeg.h`; dvv-media's
  `include/dvv/media/{source.h,source_factory.h,uri_colour.h,pixel_format.h,types.h}`,
  `src/sources/raw_file.cpp` (resolved URI), `tests/source_harness.{h,cpp}`; dvv-base's `Uri` and
  thread pool. Note the differences and the two dvv-base facts for the commit body. Read Task 0's
  U-FF records (Step 7): the `Sequential` fixture choice (`<seq>` = `seq.avi`, `seq.nut` or
  `seq.mkv`; `<ext>` its extension) and its trigger, whether AVI/NUT packets carry `pts`, the
  probe scores of the AVI, MKV, MOV and NUT fixtures (each > 50), and "mpeg4 encoder: yes/no".
  `<seq>` and `<ext>` are constants in the fixture script and the tests; no record → stop and ask
  the controller.
- [ ] **Step 2: unit tests first.** Write `test_ffmpeg_rules.cpp`, `framemd5.{h,cpp}`,
  `test_framemd5.cpp`, stub `ffmpeg_rules.{h,cpp}` (declarations, functions returning defaults),
  the CMake lines. Run
  `Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "dvv_components\.unit" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: builds; the `ffmpeg_rules` cases fail; the `framemd5` cases fail.
- [ ] **Step 3: implement `ffmpeg_rules` and the parser.** Same command. Expected:
  `dvv_components.unit` passes.
- [ ] **Step 4: fixtures.** Write `make-container-fixtures.cmake` and its CTest entry. Run
  `<Windows test env>; Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "dvv_components\.ffmpeg_fixtures" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: passes; the version folder holds 18 `ffv1_*.mkv` + 18 `ffv1_*.framemd5`, `<seq>`,
  `seq.framemd5`, `long.<ext>`, the four `lossless_*` files + their four `.framemd5`,
  `part_a.mkv`, `part_b.mkv`, `concat.txt`, `concat_sizes.mkv`, `lossy_mpeg4.mp4` (only with
  "mpeg4 encoder: yes"), `audio_only.mka`, `done.stamp`. If the FFV1 encoder refuses a
  format (or decodes it as another `pix_fmt`, seen in Step 6's case 1), drop that format from both
  lists and record it in the commit body. Then check `long.<ext>` once (prefix programs, same
  call): `ffprobe` shows the same `Sequential` trigger as `<seq>` (a `pts` of `N/A` or pairs of
  equal `pts`), and `ffmpeg -benchmark -i long.<ext> -f null -` takes at least 2 s (cases 5 and
  12 need seconds of decoding). If it is faster, use `s=704x576` for `long.<ext>` (same 3000
  frames) and record it; if the trigger differs, stop and report it.
- [ ] **Step 5: provider tests first.** Write the harness and `test_ffmpeg_provider.cpp`; register
  the ffmpeg test entry's `FIXTURES_REQUIRED dvv_components_ffmpeg_fixtures` and
  `DVV_FFMPEG_FIXTURES=set:<dir>` (appended to its `ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION` list). Declare
  `stopFfmpegSources()` with an empty stub. Run
  `<Windows test env>; Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "dvv_components\.ffmpeg$" -V | Select-String 'test cases:|Test #|FAILED'; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: the doctest summary line shows 19 test cases (Task 4's 5 + these 14) and the new
  cases fail ("no source provider for scheme" or no provider `ffmpeg`). A summary of 5 cases
  means the new cases are in another suite: fix the suite name first.
- [ ] **Step 6: implement** the ring, the worker, the supplier, the provider and the component (in
  that order), `stopFfmpegSources()`/`stopGeneration()`, the conformance assertions and the
  compile-fail case. Run Step 5's command, then
  `ctest --preset vs2026 -R "dvv_components\." --output-on-failure` in the same call. Expected:
  `test cases: 19 | 19 passed` for `dvv_components.ffmpeg`; every `dvv_components.*` test passes,
  none skipped.
- [ ] **Step 7: commit.**
  ```powershell
  git -C dvv-components add -A
  @'
  feat: add the ffmpeg source provider

  Files of any container FFmpeg demuxes (AVI, MKV, MOV, MP4, NUT) through FFmpeg loaded at run
  time, lossless video codecs only: an allowlist checked at open refuses every other codec with
  "codec <name> is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)". Probe with padding and the
  extension rule, protocol_whitelist=file, an index scan at open (Sequential for packets without
  pts or with equal pts), one worker thread per source with a ring of the last decoded frames,
  seek with a flush, a Sequential restart that reopens the file, a stop flag, a per-frame size and
  format check, the container colour mapping with the colour keys of the URI, and a resolved URI
  that reopens with impl=ffmpeg. Fixtures (FFV1/MKV, huffyuv/utvideo/v210/magicyuv in
  AVI/MKV/MOV/NUT, a lossless Sequential stream, a PNG concat size change, a refused MPEG-4
  clip) and a framemd5 parser check the originals against ffmpeg.

  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  '@ | git -C dvv-components commit -F -
  ```
  Add the Step 1 differences and any dropped FFV1 format to the body.
- [ ] **Step 8: Per-task checks** (Execution notes; once, on the final commit; clang-format
  findings go into a `style: …` commit first). Expected: `FORMAT-OK`; `100% tests passed` in
  vs2026 Debug with `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1`; `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

**Expected results:**
- `dvv_components.unit` gains 10 cases (9 rules incl. the U-FF allowlist and the `Sequential`
  mode rule, 1 framemd5 with three error subcases) plus the conformance changes.
- `dvv_components.ffmpeg` gains 14 provider cases in suite `ffmpeg` (19 in all);
  `dvv_components.ffmpeg_fixtures` is a new CTest entry; one more compile-fail test
  (`dvv_components.compile_fail.2`).
- No fixture uses an MPEG-family or other lossy codec, except the refusal check of case 14 (U-FF).
- At the end of 5a-1: `vs2026` green (19 doctest cases in `dvv_components.ffmpeg`, every
  `dvv_components.*` test passing, none skipped). At the end of 5a-2: the Per-task checks green
  (`vs2026` Debug, `FORMAT-OK`, privacy lint); no skip. (Release, `vs2026-asan` and clang-tidy:
  Task 5b checkpoint.)

**Commit:** `feat: add the ffmpeg source provider` (dvv-components, end of 5a-1); 5a-2 adds a
`style: …` or `fix: …` commit only when the Per-task checks find something.

**Notes:**
- Design references: v2 D3, D4, D5 (stream choice, `Sequential` count and restart, stop flag), D28,
  §4.4 `ffmpeg` (a)–(d), §5.1, §5.4, §6, §7, §8.1 ffmpeg row, §8.3 Task 5a fixtures; v1 §4.4 colour
  table and match rule; arbiter v2 FIX 5, 8, 9, 13, 14, 19, 23; user decision U-FF (allowlist,
  containers, lossless fixtures).
- Note (plan choice): the D28 equality checks of `fmt`/`size`/`fps` reimplement M2's size and fps
  parsing in `ffmpeg_rules` (M2's `parseSize`/`parseFps` stay private in dvv-media; only the colour
  parser became public in Task 2). Same accepted forms (`WxH`; `N`, `N/D`, `I.F`).
- Note (plan choice): the stop flag lives in `AvWorker`, which each `FfmpegSource`/`LavfiSource`
  owns; the worker owns the thread, so the flag and the join sit together.
- Note (plan choice): the stop test drives `AvWorker` directly (`fetch`, `queued()`), because
  `ProduceContext` can only be made by a `FrameStore`, and a store's Produce step holds the source
  (M2 R11), so a source attached to a store is never destroyed with requests queued.
  `long.<ext>` (3000 FFV1 frames, `Sequential`; Step 4 checks it takes at least 2 s to decode)
  makes the four requests take seconds, so the 1 s bound shows the flag works.
- Note (plan choice): two packets with equal `pts` also select `Sequential` (the decoded-frame →
  index mapping needs unique `pts`); a decoded frame with an unknown `pts` is dropped with a debug
  line. `seq.mkv`, when Task 0 chose it, exercises the equal-`pts` trigger; no fixture exercises
  the dropped frame.
- Note (U-FF): the design's `-bf 2` `.m2v` fixtures are replaced. Lossless codecs never reorder
  frames, so libavformat usually fills a missing `pts` from `dts` (even in AVI, which stores no
  timestamps). Task 0 therefore tries the user's examples (rawvideo in AVI or NUT) for packets
  without `pts` first and falls back to equal `pts` in MKV, D5's other trigger of the same
  `Sequential` path. The case intent is unchanged: a `Sequential` source matches `framemd5`
  forward, backward and at random. The `match` extension rule (score ≤ 50) is now covered only by
  the unit case, because every lossless fixture container probes at 100.
- Note (U-FF): the allowlist check runs on the demuxer's codec id before any decoder lookup, so
  case 14 (a) needs no lossy encoder and no lossy decoder. It works with a lossless-only FFmpeg
  build too; (b) adds a real MPEG-4 clip when the pinned `ffmpeg` can encode one (the LGPL build
  has no H.264 software encoder).
- Note (plan choice): after the index scan the format context is reopened instead of seeking to
  the start (the same code as the `Sequential` restart, D5).
- Note (plan choice): the `match` 1-byte-head case runs in the ffmpeg tests (it needs the loaded
  library; ASan runs them too), not in the unit executable as §8.1 lists it.
- Note (plan choice): frame rate = `av_guess_frame_rate`, reduced; an unknown rate gives 25/1 and a
  warning.
- Risk: if `ffmpeg -f concat … -c copy` refuses parts of different sizes (or the PNG parts in
  MKV), use `.nut` for the three files (`part_a.nut`, `part_b.nut`, `concat_sizes.nut`) and record
  it. PNG is used because it carries the frame size in every frame. FFV1, rawvideo and the other
  allowlisted codecs take the size from the container, so a size change would not reach the
  decoder.
- Note: FFV1 tags its output as limited range (`ffv1enc` declares `AVCOL_RANGE_MPEG`); the hash
  cases do not depend on it.
- Note (arbiter decision, plan v2): in the store path the source is destroyed only after its last
  `produce` finished (M2 R11: the Produce step holds `shared_ptr<ISource>`), so the destructor's
  flag cannot shorten a close, Reopen or exit while far-back `Sequential` requests are queued. Exit
  is bounded by `stopFfmpegSources()` (case 12; Task 7a calls it before the shutdown); close and
  Reopen drain in the background and the Session drops their results. A per-source stop through
  `ISource` (called by `FrameStore::detach`) would also bound close/Reopen but changes dvv-media
  and the M3 Session; it is not built.

---

### Task 5b: `lavfi` provider and the lavfi reference fixtures

**Goal:** The `lavfi` source provider: `lavfi:<graph>` URIs build an in-process libavfilter graph
whose single video output ends in a `buffersink`; frames are pulled in order on the source's worker
(Task 5a's `AvWorker`), restart from the start when a request is behind the ring, end with `Io` when
a finite graph ends, take their colour from frame 0, and equal the same graph run through
`ffmpeg … -f rawvideo` byte for byte.

**Execution notes** (repeated in every M4 task of this part):
- Run every command from the workspace root (the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`). Use the
  PowerShell tool on Windows. Linux runs as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. Git runs only from PowerShell, except the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only`
  inside the WSL format and tidy commands. Branch `m4`. Nothing is pushed; no remote is added; no repository is created.
- **Windows test env** (`<Windows test env>`; in the same tool call as the run):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Windows runs use `run_in_background: true`; at a checkpoint the
  WSL checkpoint build may run in parallel with a Windows run.
- **Per-task checks** (U-PROC; once, on the task's final commit; one background call for Windows;

```cpp
// Filters that open a file or URL (a source such as movie/amovie, or a file, URL, plugin or model
// path option). A lavfi graph that uses one is refused, so FFmpeg decodes no file inside a graph
// and the U-FF allowlist cannot be bypassed. FFmpeg 8 names; Step 1 checks the list against the
// pinned build.
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 44> LAVFI_FILE_FILTERS{
    "movie", "amovie", "subtitles", "ass", "sendcmd", "asendcmd", "zmq", "azmq", "metadata",
    "ametadata", "drawtext", "lut1d", "lut3d", "frei0r", "frei0r_src", "ladspa", "lv2",
    "sofalizer", "arnndn", "dnn_classify", "dnn_detect", "dnn_processing", "sr", "derain",
    "signature", "vidstabdetect", "vidstabtransform", "psnr", "ssim", "ssim360", "xpsnr",
    "vmafmotion", "libvmaf", "ocr", "fsync", "find_rect", "cover_rect", "removelogo",
    "libplacebo", "program_opencl", "openclsrc", "firequalizer", "whisper", "lensfun"};
// (44 names; the unit case checks that none is empty, so a wrong count cannot add "".)
// Ok unless `filter_name` (exact, case-sensitive) is in LAVFI_FILE_FILTERS. "movie"/"amovie" →
// Errc::Unsupported "lavfi: movie/amovie are not supported; open the file directly"; any other
// listed name → Errc::Unsupported "lavfi: filter <name> opens files or URLs and is not supported;
// open the file directly".
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> checkLavfiFilter(std::string_view filter_name);
```
- Produces (private, `src/sources/graph_supplier.h`, namespace `dvv::components::detail`):

```cpp
// One lavfi graph (design v2 D6, §4.4 lavfi). Built on open and on every restart; used only on the
// source's worker after open.
class GraphSupplier final : public IFrameSupplier {
public:
    // Builds the graph (code below). Errors: Errc::Format "the lavfi graph is invalid: <text>
    // (AVERROR <n>)" for a parse or configure error; Errc::Format "the lavfi graph has an
    // unconnected input" / "the lavfi graph needs exactly one output"; Errc::Unsupported "the lavfi
    // graph output is not a video output (<text>)" when the sink cannot be linked;
    // Errc::OutOfMemory for ENOMEM.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<std::unique_ptr<GraphSupplier>> create(std::string graph,
                                                                       std::size_t ring_capacity);
    // Pulls frame 0 (open only): its size, format, colour and the sink's frame rate.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<const AVFrame*> first();
    [[nodiscard]] AVRational frameRate() const;       // av_buffersink_get_frame_rate
    void setExpected(const media::FrameDesc& desc, int av_format);   // for checkFrame
    [[nodiscard]] Result<const AVFrame*> frame(std::int64_t index,
                                               const StopCheck& stopped) override;
};
// Test seam (stop test): an AvWorker over GraphSupplier for `graph`, with the same open code.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::unique_ptr<AvWorker>> makeGraphWorkerForTest(std::string graph);
```

- Produces (private, `src/sources/lavfi_provider.cpp`): `LavfiSourceProvider final :
  media::ISourceProvider, Managed<LavfiSourceProvider>` with `info() = {.id = "lavfi", .name =
  "FFmpeg filter graphs (lavfi)", .kind = Kind::Source, .version = 1}`, empty schema, `probe` =
  `require(caps, {"ffmpeg.lavfi"})`, `schemes() = {"lavfi"}`; `LavfiSource final : media::ISource,

```cmake
            src/sources/container_supplier.cpp src/sources/ffmpeg_provider.cpp

```
  (The `LINK` list stays Task 4's and Task 5a's; never `dvv::media_sources`.)
- Produces for later tasks: provider id `lavfi` (Tasks 7a, 7b, 8: `info`/`dump`/`bench` of
  `lavfi:` URIs, the `smoke` step, the package smoke); `SourceInfo` of a lavfi stream:
  `frameCount = nullopt`, `access = Sequential`, `resolvedUri = "lavfi:" + <graph as given>` (Task
  7b's View → Format… rule tests the `lavfi:` prefix); the `Io` message "the lavfi graph ended after
  <n> frames".

**Behaviour:**
- **`match`:** scheme `lavfi` → confidence 100, why "lavfi filter graph"; the guess stays empty; no
  validation (open reports errors).
- **`open(ticket, request, guess, ctx)`** (`co_await resumeOn(ctx.io)` first):
  1. The graph is `uri.path` exactly as `parseUri` gave it; empty → `Format` "lavfi: URI without a
     graph". Any query parameter other than `impl` → `Format` "lavfi: URIs take no query (the rest
     of the URI is the graph)" (design v2 D28: `lavfi:` URIs take no query).
  2. `GraphSupplier::create(graph, 8)` (the ring is resized in step 4), then `first()`: end of
     stream at once → `Format` "the lavfi graph produced no frame".
  3. Frame 0 decides (§4.4 lavfi, D6): `catalogueFormat(av_get_pix_fmt_name(frame->format))` →
     none → `Unsupported` "graph output <name> is not in the catalogue (append ,format=<a catalogue
     name>)"; `desc = {format, colour, frame->width, frame->height}`; colour = `mapColour(
     frame->colorspace, frame->color_range, frame->color_trc, frame->color_primaries)`, an alias sets
     `range = Full` when still Unspecified (arbiter v2 FIX 6); `fps = reduced(frameRate())`, an
     invalid rate → 25/1 with the warning "frame rate unknown, 25 fps assumed".
  4. `setExpected(desc, frame->format)`; ring capacity `ringCapacity(geometry(...)->bytes)` (8 for
     the small test graphs);
     frame 0 stays in the ring as index 0.
  5. `SourceInfo{desc, fps, frameCount = nullopt, access = Sequential, deterministic = true
     (documented: seeded generators only, D6), resolvedUri = "lavfi:" + graph, provider = "lavfi",
     warnings}`, `reader = "lavfi"` (no file is read; `fileReads` is ignored, U-IO2); the supplier moves into an `AvWorker`; the source is made with
     `issueKey<LavfiSource>`.
  6. Graphs have command-line trust (§7, arbiter v2 FIX 25) except for files and URLs: a graph that
     uses a filter of `LAVFI_FILE_FILTERS` (`movie`, `amovie`, `subtitles`, `sendcmd`, `zmq`,
     `metadata`, `drawtext`, …) is refused in `GraphSupplier::build` before any filter is created
     or initialised (`movie`/`amovie` open their input in their init): `Unsupported` "lavfi:
     movie/amovie are not supported; open the file directly" (other names: "lavfi: filter <name>
     opens files or URLs and is not supported; open the file directly"). So no file is decoded
     inside a graph and the U-FF allowlist has no bypass (U-FF review fix, controller decision).
     No protocol whitelist is applied inside a graph (`protocol_whitelist=file` applies only to the
     `ffmpeg` provider's own `AVFormatContext`); with no file filter, none is needed.
- **`LavfiSource::produce(i, ctx)`:** `i < 0` → `Internal` "frame <i> is outside [0, unbounded)";
  else `co_return co_await worker_->produce(i, ctx)`.
- **`GraphSupplier::frame(i, stopped)`** (D6; frames are numbered 0, 1, 2… in pull order since the
  last build; `produced_` = the next number):
  - In the ring → return it.
  - `ended_` and `i >= produced_` → `Io` "the lavfi graph ended after <produced_> frames".
  - `i < ring.oldest()` (behind the ring) → restart: `build()` again (a new graph from the text,
    `produced_ = 0`, `ended_ = false`), `ring.clear()`.
  - Pull forward while `produced_ <= i`: check `stopped()` (→ `Internal` "source closed");
    `av_buffersink_get_frame(sink, tmp)`; `AVERROR_EOF` → `ended_ = true`, `Io` "the lavfi graph
    ended after <produced_> frames"; another error → `ffmpegError("av_buffersink_get_frame", e)`;
    `checkFrame(tmp, produced_, desc, av_format)` (§5.4: a size or format change → `Format` "frame
    <j> changed to <w>x<h> <fmt>"); `ring.put(produced_, tmp)`; `++produced_`.
  - Return `ring.find(i)`.
- The player pauses with the infobar on the `Io` end (M3); the CLI's `dump`/`bench` are bounded by
  their arguments (D6).

**Code** (binding: the graph build; it follows FFmpeg's own lavfi input device: parse with both
open lists, then link the single open output to a `buffersink`):

```cpp
// src/sources/graph_supplier.cpp
Result<void> GraphSupplier::build() {
    graph_.reset(api().avfilter_graph_alloc());
    if (!graph_) {
        return std::unexpected(ffmpegError("avfilter_graph_alloc", AVERROR(ENOMEM)));
    }
    AVFilterInOut* inputs = nullptr;
    AVFilterInOut* outputs = nullptr;
    const auto free_lists = [&] {
        api().avfilter_inout_free(&inputs);
        api().avfilter_inout_free(&outputs);
    };
    // Parse only (no filter created yet), check every filter name (U-FF review fix: no filter that
    // opens a file or URL; movie/amovie would open their input in their init), then apply.
    AVFilterGraphSegment* segment = nullptr;
    int parsed = api().avfilter_graph_segment_parse(graph_.get(), text_.c_str(), 0, &segment);
    if (parsed < 0) {
        api().avfilter_graph_segment_free(&segment);
        return std::unexpected(graphError("avfilter_graph_segment_parse", parsed));
    }
    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < segment->nb_chains; ++c) {
        const AVFilterChain* chain = segment->chains[c];
        for (std::size_t f = 0; f < chain->nb_filters; ++f) {
            const char* name = chain->filters[f]->filter_name;
            if (auto allowed = checkLavfiFilter(name != nullptr ? name : ""); !allowed) {
                api().avfilter_graph_segment_free(&segment);
                return std::unexpected(std::move(allowed).error());
            }
        }
    }
    // The same result as avfilter_graph_parse_ptr with empty open lists (create, options, init,
    // link): the unconnected pads come back in inputs/outputs.
    parsed = api().avfilter_graph_segment_apply(segment, 0, &inputs, &outputs);
    api().avfilter_graph_segment_free(&segment);
    if (parsed < 0) {
        free_lists();
        return std::unexpected(graphError("avfilter_graph_segment_apply", parsed));  // Format/ENOMEM
    }
    if (inputs != nullptr) {
        free_lists();
        return makeError(Errc::Format, "the lavfi graph has an unconnected input");
    }
    if (outputs == nullptr || outputs->next != nullptr) {
        free_lists();
        return makeError(Errc::Format, "the lavfi graph needs exactly one output");
    }
    AVFilterContext* sink = nullptr;
    int result = api().avfilter_graph_create_filter(&sink, api().avfilter_get_by_name("buffersink"),
                                                    "out", nullptr, nullptr, graph_.get());
    if (result >= 0) {
        result = api().avfilter_link(outputs->filter_ctx, static_cast<unsigned>(outputs->pad_idx),
                                     sink, 0);
        if (result < 0) {
            free_lists();
            return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                             std::format("the lavfi graph output is not a video output ({})",
                                         errorText(result)));   // av_strerror text
        }
    }
    free_lists();
    if (result < 0) {
        return std::unexpected(ffmpegError("avfilter_graph_create_filter", result));
    }
    result = api().avfilter_graph_config(graph_.get(), nullptr);
    if (result < 0) {
        return std::unexpected(graphError("avfilter_graph_config", result));
    }
    sink_ = sink;   // owned by graph_
    produced_ = 0;
    ended_ = false;
    return {};
}
```
(`graphError(call, e)`: `AVERROR(ENOMEM)` → `OutOfMemory`; any other → `Format` "the lavfi graph is
invalid: <av_strerror text> (AVERROR <n>)". Use the project's error helpers as Task 5a does.)

  the manifest under `DVV_LAVFI_FIXTURES`):
  1. `lavfi: frames equal ffmpeg -f rawvideo` — for each manifest line: `open("lavfi:" + graph)` →
     `provider == "lavfi"`, `frameCount == nullopt`, `access == Sequential`, 352×288, the format
     name after `format=`, `fps == {25, 1}`, `resolvedUri == "lavfi:" + graph`; frames 0..23 equal
     the `geometry(...)->bytes`-sized slices of `lavfi_<k>.raw` (a failure prints graph, frame and
     the first differing byte offset).
  2. `lavfi: a request behind the ring restarts the graph` — graph 1: frames 20, 2, 23, 0 in that
     order equal the reference (2 and 0 are behind the 8-frame ring).
  3. `lavfi: concurrent produce calls from one store` — graph 1: `whenAll` of 12 acquires (frames
     11..0) on a store with a 4-thread cpu pool; all equal (`vs2026-asan` at this task's checkpoint; TSan
     returns with Linux, v1.x).
  4. `lavfi: a finite graph ends with Io` — `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25:d=0.4,format=yuv420p`:
     frames 0..9 succeed; frame 10 → `Io` containing "the lavfi graph ended after 10 frames";
     frame 12 → the same `Io`; frame 0 afterwards succeeds and equals the first frame 0 (it lies
     behind the 8-frame ring, which holds 2..9, so the graph is rebuilt after its end).
  5. `lavfi: colour from frame 0` —
     `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p,setparams=colorspace=bt709:range=pc` →
     `colourParams(desc.colour).matrix == Bt709` and `.range == Full`.
  6. `lavfi: a size change is Format` —
     `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,scale=w='if(lt(n,3),64,32)':h='if(lt(n,3),48,24)':eval=frame,format=yuv420p`:
     frames 0..2 succeed; frame 3 → `Format` containing "frame 3 changed to 32x24 yuv420p".
  7. `lavfi: errors` — `lavfi:nosuchfilter` → `Format` containing "the lavfi graph is invalid";
     `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48,format=bgr0` → `Unsupported` containing "not in the catalogue";
     `lavfi:` (empty graph) → `Format`; `lavfi:testsrc2?fmt=gray` → `Format` containing "take no
     query"; `lavfi:sine` → `Unsupported` containing "not a video output".
  8. `lavfi: destroying the source with queued requests stops it` —
     `makeGraphWorkerForTest("testsrc2=s=1920x1080:r=25,format=yuv444p16le")`; four `fetch` calls
     (2000, 1000, 2000, 1000) on four `std::thread`s; wait until `queued() == 4`; `reset()` returns
     within 1000 ms; every result is `Internal` containing "source closed".
  9. `lavfi: graphs that open files or URLs are refused` (U-FF review fix) — `<rel>` = the path of
     Task 5a's fixture `lossless_huffyuv.avi` relative to the current directory, with `/`
     separators (no drive letter, so no `:` escaping inside the graph):
     `lavfi:movie=<rel>` → `Unsupported` whose message equals "lavfi: movie/amovie are not
     supported; open the file directly" (without the check this graph opens and plays that file, so
     the case fails); `lavfi:amovie=<rel>` → the same message;
     `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,drawtext=textfile=x.txt,format=yuv420p` → `Unsupported`
     containing "lavfi: filter drawtext opens files or URLs"; each refused open leaves no source and
     no worker (`LifetimeRegistry` live count unchanged); the control
     `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p` still opens.

  ```powershell
  a request behind the ring rebuilds the graph, a finite graph ends with an Io error, the colour
  ```

```powershell
$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; ctest --preset vs2026-release --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|AddressSanitizer'; Pop-Location
```

- `dvv_components.ffmpeg` gains 9 `lavfi:` cases in suite `ffmpeg` (28 in all: Task 5a's 19 incl.
  its two U-FF cases, + 9, case 9 being the file-filter refusal);
  `dvv_components.unit` gains the case `lavfi file filters`;
  `dvv_components.lavfi_fixtures` is a new CTest entry; the conformance case lists `ffmpeg` and
  `lavfi` besides the M2 providers.
- Per-task checks: vs2026 Debug green, `FORMAT-OK`; no skip. Checkpoint: Release and `vs2026-asan`
  green, `TIDY-OK`, the Linux build of Tasks 4-5b compiles (no Linux run, U-WIN).

**Commit:** `feat: add the lavfi source provider` (dvv-components).

**Notes:**
- Design references: v2 D6, §4.4 `lavfi` (colour from frame 0; command-line trust), §5.4 (stop flag,
  frame check), §6, §7 (graph end, catalogue, trust), §8.1 ffmpeg row, §8.3 Task 5b fixtures and the
  lavfi row; v1 §4.4 lavfi; arbiter v2 FIX 6, 14, 25.
- Note (plan choice): graph parse and configure errors are `Format` "the lavfi graph is invalid:
  …" (a typo in a user graph is an input error; §7 names no row for it); a graph whose output
  cannot feed a `buffersink` (audio) is `Unsupported`.
- Note (plan choice): the resolved URI is `lavfi:` plus the graph exactly as given (no `impl`: one
  provider serves the scheme), so it reopens without guessing.
- Note (plan choice): six reference graphs cover the copy of planar 8/10/16-bit, semi-planar and
  packed layouts; the provider's general correctness does not depend on the format, and Task 6's
  oracle covers every catalogue format's unpack.
- Note (plan choice): case 8 (stop test) is not in the design's 5b test idea; it costs one case and
  checks that the shared stop flag also bounds the graph supplier.
- Note (U-FF review fix, controller decision): design v2 §7 and arbiter FIX 25 gave graphs full
  command-line trust, so `lavfi:movie=clip.mp4` would decode H.264 inside rawreel and bypass the
  U-FF allowlist. The provider now refuses every filter of `LAVFI_FILE_FILTERS` on the parsed
  names, before any filter exists. It is a name list (one rule, unit-tested) rather than an option
  scan, because positional arguments (`movie=<file>`) are resolved only when a filter is created.
  With it the [U4] sentence "rawreel itself opens only lossless video codecs" holds: files open only
  through the `ffmpeg` provider and its allowlist (or the raw and Y4M providers), and graphs read
  no files.
