### Task 6: Oracle suite: unpack and display colour

**Goal:** The FFmpeg oracle of design v2 §8.3 (exit criterion E2): for every catalogue format that
FFmpeg reads, our CPU unpack (M2 `reference.h`) equals FFmpeg's `extractplanes` bit for bit; for the
§8.3 display rows, M3's `toDisplayReference` + `encodeSrgb8` stays within ±1 code value of swscale's
`accurate_rnd+full_chroma_int+bitexact` rgb24. The fixtures come from the prefix's `ffmpeg` program;
the test executable reads them through our raw provider and compares in C++. It needs no FFmpeg
library. It runs after Task 5b (one task at a time per repository: it edits dvv-components'
`tests/CMakeLists.txt` and builds dvv-media in-tree).

**Execution notes** (repeated in every M4 task of this part):
- Run every command from the workspace root (the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`). Use the
  PowerShell tool on Windows. Linux runs as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. Git runs only from PowerShell, except the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only`
  inside the WSL format and tidy commands. Branch `m4`. Nothing is pushed; no remote is added; no repository is created.
- **Windows test env** (`<Windows test env>`; in the same tool call as the run):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Windows runs use `run_in_background: true`; at a checkpoint the
  WSL checkpoint build may run in parallel with a Windows run.
- **Per-task checks** (U-PROC; once, on the task's final commit; one background call for Windows;
  `<base>` = the dvv-components head before this task):
  - Windows Debug: `<Windows test env>; Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026-small --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`
    Expected: `100% tests passed`, no `Skipped` line for an `ffmpeg` or `gpu` test.
  - clang-format on the changed files (new files must be `git add`ed first):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd dvv-components && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`
    Expected: `FORMAT-OK`.
  - No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL build here: they run in the Checkpoint checks of the
    checkpoint tasks (Global Constraints: Tasks 5b, 7b, 9, 11).
- **Privacy lint** after every commit:
  `cmake -DREPO=dvv-components -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.
- Every commit message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Step 1 compares every consumed name with the real code; a differing name is adapted here and noted
  in the commit body.

**Files** (dvv-components, branch `m4`):
- Create `tests/oracle/make-fixtures.cmake`: the clips, the `extractplanes` planes, the swscale
  rgb24 outputs and `manifest.txt` (FIXTURES_SETUP).
- Create `tests/oracle/oracle_harness.h`, `tests/oracle/oracle_harness.cpp`: reads a clip frame
  through the raw provider (host-only store); reads the manifest.
- Create `tests/oracle/oracle_main.cpp`: doctest main with the skip rule.
- Create `tests/oracle/test_oracle_unpack.cpp`, `tests/oracle/test_oracle_display.cpp`.
- Modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`: the executable `dvv_components_oracle_tests` and two CTest entries,
  only without `DVV_SANITIZE`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (check in Step 1):
  - Task 3: the dvv-components test layout (`tests/CMakeLists.txt`, doctest, `DVV_SANITIZE`,
    `dvv_set_compiler_options`, the test timeout convention).
  - Task 1 through Task 4's top-level `find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk CONFIG REQUIRED)`:
    `DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM` (custom_ffmpeg's `ffmpeg` program) and `DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR` (its
    library folder, for `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` on Linux). The fixture key is the third token of
    `ffmpeg -version` line 1 (e.g. `n8.1.3`), with every character outside `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`
    removed (Task 5a's rule).
  - M2 dvv-media: `catalogue()`, `descriptor()` (name, model, component count),
    `findFormat`, `unpackedFormat`, `geometry`, `unpack` (`CodeValues{format, width, height,
    components[k]}`), `unpackToPlanes`; the raw provider in `dvv::media_sources`
    (`file:<path>?fmt=<F>&size=WxH&impl=raw`), `SourceFactory`, `FrameStore::create` (host-only),
    `attach`, `acquireOriginal`, `ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})`; M2's
    `tests/source_harness.cpp` as the model for reading original bytes.
  - M3 dvv-media (`display.h`): `toDisplayReference(format, resolved, code_values)`,
    `encodeSrgb8`, `ColourParams{matrix, range, transfer, primaries}`.
  - From Tasks 2, 4, 5a and 5b only Task 4's CMake variables above: no FFmpeg library is loaded.
- Produces: CTest `dvv_components.oracle_fixtures` (`FIXTURES_SETUP dvv_components_oracle`) and
  `dvv_components.oracle` (labels `ffmpeg;oracle`), present only in non-sanitizer builds; Task 9's
  CI runs the `oracle` label in the non-sanitizer jobs; the "not covered" list (empty unless this
  task records a format) for the README and the spec §12 table (Task 10).

**Behaviour:**
- **Clips** (§8.3 Task 6): for every catalogue format `F` except v210:
  `ffmpeg -hide_banner -nostdin -y -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=352x288:r=25 -frames:v 10 -pix_fmt <F> -sws_flags accurate_rnd+bitexact -fps_mode passthrough -f rawvideo clip_<F>.raw`.
  v210: `ffmpeg … -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=384x288:r=25 -frames:v 10 -pix_fmt yuv422p10le -c:v v210 -f rawvideo clip_v210.raw`.
  A format the program cannot write is listed as not covered (recorded; Step 4).
- **Reference planes:** one run per component letter `c` (one output per run, so the order is
  ours): `ffmpeg … -f rawvideo -pix_fmt <F> -video_size 352x288 -framerate 25 -i clip_<F>.raw -sws_flags accurate_rnd+bitexact -vf "<step,>extractplanes=<c>" -fps_mode passthrough -f rawvideo <F>_<c>.raw`.
  v210 reads with `-f v210 -video_size 384x288 -framerate 25 -i clip_v210.raw` (the v210 decoder
  gives yuv422p10le; no step). Letters by model in our component order: YUV `y u v`, RGB `r g b`
  (+ `a` with alpha), gray `y`.
- **Per-format reference table** (binding; arbiter v2 FIX 30; "step" = the lossless same-depth
  planar conversion before `extractplanes`):

| Formats | Step | Letters |
|---|---|---|
| gray gray10le gray12le gray16le | – | y |
| gray16be | `format=gray16le` | y |
| yuv420p yuv422p yuv444p yuv420p10le yuv422p10le yuv444p10le yuv420p12le yuv422p12le yuv444p12le yuv420p16le yuv444p16le | – | y u v |
| yuv420p10be | `format=yuv420p10le` | y u v |
| nv12 nv21 | `format=yuv420p` | y u v |
| p010le | `format=yuv420p10le` | y u v |
| p016le | `format=yuv420p16le` | y u v |
| yuyv422 uyvy422 | `format=yuv422p` | y u v |
| y210le | `format=yuv422p10le` | y u v |
| v210 (384×288) | – (decoded by the v210 decoder) | y u v |
| rgb24 bgr24 | `format=gbrp` | r g b |
| rgba bgra argb | `format=gbrap` | r g b a |
| rgb48le rgb48be | `format=gbrp16le` | r g b |
| rgba64le | `format=gbrap16le` | r g b a |
| x2rgb10le | `format=gbrp10le` | r g b |
| gbrp gbrp10le gbrp16le | – | r g b |

  (37 formats; not covered: none planned.)
- **Display rows** (§8.3, arbiter v2 FIX 29; transfer sRGB, primaries BT.709): for `F` in
  `yuv444p yuv444p10le`, `M` in `bt601 bt709`, `R` in `limited full`:
  `ffmpeg … -f rawvideo -pix_fmt <F> -video_size 352x288 -framerate 25 -i clip_<F>.raw -vf "scale=in_color_matrix=<M>:in_range=<tv|pc>:out_range=pc:flags=accurate_rnd+full_chroma_int+bitexact,format=rgb24" -fps_mode passthrough -f rawvideo display_<F>_<M>_<R>.rgb`;
  for `gray gray10le gbrp` (full range only):
  `… -vf "scale=in_range=pc:out_range=pc:flags=accurate_rnd+full_chroma_int+bitexact,format=rgb24" … display_<F>_full.rgb`.
  11 rows.
- **Manifest** (`manifest.txt`, written last, then `done.stamp`): `unpack|<F>|<W>|<H>|10|<clip
  file>|<letters without separators>` per covered format; `display|<F>|<M or ->|<limited or
  full>|<rgb file>` per display row. The C++ tests take everything from it.
- **Fixture directory and skip** (§8.3: never committed; keyed by the FFmpeg version):
  `<build>/oracle-fixtures/<version>/` (`<version>`: the sanitised third token of `ffmpeg -version`
  line 1, Task 5a's rule); `done.stamp` present → nothing to do. Without the program:
  message "ffmpeg program missing; oracle fixtures not made", exit 0, no stamp. On Linux each command
  runs through `cmake -E env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=<LIBDIR>`. A failing command is a
  `FATAL_ERROR` naming the command and its output, except the clip of a format the program cannot
  write (logged, the format left out of the manifest).
- **Test skip rule** (`oracle_main.cpp`, design v2 §3: the oracle tests exit 77 when the prefix's
  `ffmpeg` program is missing): `DVV_ORACLE_FIXTURES` unset or without `done.stamp` → with
  `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` print "oracle fixtures missing (DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1)" and return 1, else
  print "oracle fixtures missing: skipped" and return 77; otherwise run doctest.
- **Unpack comparison:** for each `unpack` line, open `file:<clip>?fmt=<F>&size=<W>x<H>&impl=raw`
  through the harness; for frames 0..9: `ours` = `unpackToPlanes(F, W, H, original, planes)` into a
  buffer of `geometry(unpackedFormat(F), W, H)->bytes`; `ref` = for each component `k` the frame's
  slice of `<F>_<letter k>.raw` (slice size = plane `k`'s bytes in that geometry; the file size must
  be 10 × the slice size, else fail with "reference plane <file> has <n> bytes, expected <m>"),
  concatenated in component order. Equal bytes, else the failure names format, frame, component,
  x, y, our value and the reference value of the first difference (§8.3 "A failure prints the frame,
  position, value and reference").
- **Display comparison:** for each `display` line: `params = {matrix (bt601 → Bt601, bt709 → Bt709,
  "-" → Rgb), range (limited/full), Srgb, Bt709}`; per frame and pixel, `codes = unpack(F, …)`,
  values `{c0, c1, c2}` at the pixel (gray: `{c0}`), `rgb = toDisplayReference(F, params, values)`,
  `encodeSrgb8(rgb[0..2])` vs the three bytes of the rgb24 file; `|diff| <= 1` for every channel.
  Count the misses; a row fails when any channel misses, printing the count, the first miss
  (frame, x, y, channel, our value, reference) and the largest difference.
- **Coverage check:** the set of `unpack` formats in the manifest plus the C++ constant
  `NOT_COVERED` (empty unless Step 4 records one, each with its reason) equals the catalogue names,
  so no format can drop out silently.

**Tests first:**
- `tests/oracle/test_oracle_unpack.cpp` (`TEST_SUITE("oracle")`):
  1. `oracle: every catalogue format is covered or listed` — as above.
  2. `oracle: unpack equals extractplanes` — every `unpack` line (one `SUBCASE` per format).
- `tests/oracle/test_oracle_display.cpp`:
  3. `oracle: display colour within 1 of swscale` — every `display` line (one `SUBCASE` per row; 11
     rows).
- `tests/oracle/oracle_harness.h`:
  `struct OracleLine { std::string kind, format, matrix, range, file, letters; std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0, frames = 0; };`
  `[[nodiscard]] std::vector<OracleLine> readManifest();` (from `DVV_ORACLE_FIXTURES`);
  `class OracleHarness { public: OracleHarness(); [[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::vector<std::byte>> originalBytes(std::string_view uri, std::int64_t index); };`
  (registry with `dvv::media_sources` linked through `dvv_link_components`, cpu and io pools of 2
  threads, `SourceFactory`, host-only `FrameStore`; one attach per URI).
- CMake (`tests/CMakeLists.txt`):

```cmake
# FFmpeg oracle (design v2 §8.3): fixtures from the prefix's ffmpeg program, compared in C++ through
# our raw provider. Non-sanitizer builds only (§8.1: the sanitizers already cover unit and ffmpeg).
if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)
    add_executable(dvv_components_oracle_tests
        oracle/oracle_main.cpp oracle/oracle_harness.cpp
        oracle/test_oracle_unpack.cpp oracle/test_oracle_display.cpp)
    target_link_libraries(dvv_components_oracle_tests PRIVATE dvv::media doctest::doctest)
    dvv_link_components(dvv_components_oracle_tests dvv::media_sources)
    dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_components_oracle_tests)
    set(oracle_dir "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/oracle-fixtures")
    add_test(NAME dvv_components.oracle_fixtures
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} "-DFFMPEG=${DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM}" "-DOUT=${oracle_dir}"
                     "-DLIBDIR=${DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR}"
                     -P "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/oracle/make-fixtures.cmake")
    set_tests_properties(dvv_components.oracle_fixtures PROPERTIES
        LABELS "ffmpeg;oracle" FIXTURES_SETUP dvv_components_oracle TIMEOUT 600)
    add_test(NAME dvv_components.oracle COMMAND dvv_components_oracle_tests)
    set_tests_properties(dvv_components.oracle PROPERTIES
        LABELS "ffmpeg;oracle" FIXTURES_REQUIRED dvv_components_oracle SKIP_RETURN_CODE 77
        TIMEOUT 600 ENVIRONMENT "DVV_ORACLE_FIXTURES=${oracle_dir}")
endif()
```
  (The script appends the version folder itself and writes the absolute folder it used into
  `${oracle_dir}/current.txt`; `oracle_main.cpp` reads `DVV_ORACLE_FIXTURES/current.txt` to find it.
  Use Task 3's names for the doctest target and the compiler-options function if they differ.)

**Expected results:**
- Two new CTest entries in `vs2026` (`dvv_components.oracle_fixtures`, `dvv_components.oracle`
  with 3 cases: 37 unpack subcases, 11 display subcases), green in Debug; none in `vs2026-asan`.
  (Release with the oracle, `vs2026-asan` and clang-tidy: Task 7b checkpoint.)
- E2 met on Windows for the non-sanitizer configuration (U-WIN: the Linux oracle run is v1.x; no
  Linux sanity build, no Linux-only code here).

**Commit:** `test: add the FFmpeg oracle for unpack and display colour` (dvv-components).

**Notes:**
- Design references: v2 §1 E2, §3 (tests, skip rule), §8.1 oracle row, §8.3 (fixtures for Task 6,
  table rows "extractplanes" and display, the planar step list), §8.7, R2; M2 D4 ("FFmpeg's
  `extractplanes` oracle joins in M4"); arbiter v2 FIX 23, 29, 30.
- Note (plan choice): every clip is raw (no Y4M); the design allows either, and raw covers every
  catalogue format with one provider.
- Note (U-FF): the oracle uses only rawvideo and v210 (both on the lossless allowlist) and no lossy
  codec, so user decision U-FF changes nothing here. Task 0 checks the `v210` and `rawvideo`
  encoders and formats this task needs (Step 5's list).
- Note (plan choice): the v210 clip is 384×288 instead of 352×288. FFmpeg's v210 encoder aligns
  rows to 48 pixels (128 bytes); at 384 the encoder's row size (1024 bytes) is the unpadded size, so
  the raw file has no padding that our catalogue geometry and FFmpeg could disagree on.
- Note (plan choice): one `ffmpeg` run per extracted plane keeps the plane order ours, whatever
  order `extractplanes` gives its output pads.
- Note (plan choice): `-sws_flags accurate_rnd+bitexact` is set on the clip and planar-step runs so
  any generic swscale path is exact; the unscaled converters ignore it.
- Note (plan choice): `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` turns the missing-program skip into a failure, as for
  every FFmpeg-dependent test (design v2 §3); the trigger stays the program, not the capability.
- Risk: if the custom_ffmpeg build lacks the `v210` demuxer, read the clip with
  `-f rawvideo -c:v v210 -video_size 384x288` instead and record it; if the program cannot write
  `x2rgb10le` or `y210le`, that format goes to `NOT_COVERED` with the program's message.

---

### Task 7a: Startup, CLI, crash handler and the CLI end-to-end tests

**Goal:** rawreel loads FFmpeg at startup in both executables (FFmpeg load → capabilities →
component registry, shared by App and CLI), links the `ffmpeg` component (providers `ffmpeg` and
`lavfi`) into both, prints the FFmpeg status line in `rawreel-cli --version` (which never fails for
a missing Vulkan loader or device), appends the status line to the CLI's open errors when FFmpeg is
unavailable, and installs the Windows crash handler (minidump) in both executables. The §8.4
end-to-end tests prove `info`, `dump` and `bench` on `ffmpeg` and `lavfi` URIs.

**Execution notes** (repeated in every M4 task of this part):
- Run every command from the workspace root (the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`). Use the
  PowerShell tool on Windows. Linux runs as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. Git runs only from PowerShell, except the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only`
  inside the WSL format and tidy commands. Branch `m4`. Nothing is pushed; no remote is added; no repository is created.
- **Windows test env** (`<Windows test env>`; in the same tool call as the run):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
- `rawreel_gui` and its CTest entries exist only without `DVV_SANITIZE` (`vs2026`); no Linux
  display or GUI run (U-WIN).
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Windows runs use `run_in_background: true`; at a checkpoint the
  WSL checkpoint build may run in parallel with a Windows run.
- **Per-task checks** (U-PROC; once, on the task's final commit; one background call for Windows;
  `<base>` = the rawreel head before this task):
  - Windows Debug: `<Windows test env>; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026-small --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`
    Expected: `100% tests passed`, no `Skipped` line for an `ffmpeg` or `gpu` test.
  - clang-format on the changed files (new files must be `git add`ed first):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`
    Expected: `FORMAT-OK`.
  - No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL build here: they run in the Checkpoint checks of the
    checkpoint tasks (Global Constraints: Tasks 5b, 7b, 9, 11).

  `cmake -DREPO=rawreel -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

- Step 1 compares every consumed name with the real code; a differing name is adapted here and noted

- Produces (`src/core/startup.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Startup shared by rawreel-gui and rawreel-cli (design M4 v2 §4.6): the FFmpeg load, the
// capabilities (FFmpeg's only; "gpu.compute" returns in v1.x with the analysers) and the component
// registry that feeds the SourceFactory of the Session (GUI) and of the CLI commands.

#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/components/ffmpeg.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"   // the real header names: Step 1

namespace rawreel {

struct Startup {
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::ComponentRegistry> components;
    dvv::Capabilities caps;
    dvv::components::FfmpegStatus ffmpeg;
};

// loadFfmpeg(options), addFfmpegCapabilities, ComponentRegistry::create(caps). Never fails because
// of FFmpeg: a missing or refused FFmpeg only leaves the capabilities off.
[[nodiscard]] Startup startComponents(const dvv::components::FfmpegLoadOptions& options);

// One line for rawreel-cli --version and About (design D21/D23):
//   core and lavfi: "FFmpeg <version> (<licence>) from <directory as UTF-8>"
//   core only:      the same + ", lavfi not available"
//   otherwise:      "FFmpeg: not available (<reason>)"
// The directory is printed at run time only.
[[nodiscard]] std::string ffmpegStatusLine(const dvv::components::FfmpegStatus& status);

// The open-error text of the CLI and the GUI (design v2 §4.6): describe(error), plus "; " +
// ffmpegStatusLine(status) when error.code == Errc::Unsupported and either !status.core, or `uri`
// starts with "lavfi:" and !status.lavfi.
[[nodiscard]] std::string openErrorText(const dvv::Error& error, std::string_view uri,
                                        const dvv::components::FfmpegStatus& status);

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/core/version_text.h`, complete; Task 7b adds `aboutText` here):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Version lines of rawreel-cli --version and the About dialog (design M4 v2 D23).

#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/json.h"   // the real header of dvv::json::Value: Step 1

namespace rawreel {

struct VulkanStatus {
    bool available = false;
    std::string device;   // e.g. "NVIDIA GeForce …" or "llvmpipe (…)"
    std::string api;      // "1.4.318"
    std::string reason;   // when not available
};
struct VersionReport {
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> packages;  // packageVersions()
    std::string vulkan;                                         // vulkanStatusLine(…)
    std::string ffmpeg;                                         // ffmpegStatusLine(…)
};

// {name, version} for rawreel, dvv-base, dvv-registry, dvv-platform, dvv-gpu, dvv-media,
// dvv-components, wxWidgets, in this order, from rawreel_core's compile definitions. wxWidgets is
// "not part of this build" in builds without wx (the sanitizer presets).
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> packageVersions();
// "Vulkan: <device>, API <api>" or "Vulkan: not available (<reason>)".
[[nodiscard]] std::string vulkanStatusLine(const VulkanStatus& status);
// "<name> <version>\n" per package, then the Vulkan line and the FFmpeg line, each ending in "\n".
[[nodiscard]] std::string versionText(const VersionReport& report);
// {"<key>": "<version>", …, "vulkan": "<line>", "ffmpeg": "<line>"}; key = the package name in lower
// case with '-' replaced by '_' (M2's keys "rawreel", "dvv_base", … stay; new: "dvv_components",
// "wxwidgets").
[[nodiscard]] dvv::json::Value versionJson(const VersionReport& report);

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/gui/crash_handler.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Windows minidump on an unhandled exception (design M4 v2 D22; spec §11). wx-free; compiled into
// rawreel_core. Other platforms: both functions do nothing.

#include <string_view>

namespace rawreel {

class CrashHandler {
public:
    // Call first thing in main()/App::OnInit(). Loads dbghelp.dll from System32
    // (LibrarySearch::SystemDirectory, kept loaded) and installs the unhandled-exception filter that
    // writes <userDataDir("rawreel")>/crashes/<exe>-<yyyymmdd-hhmmss>-<pid>.dmp (MiniDumpNormal),
    // prints "rawreel: crash dump <path>" on stderr, logs an error line and ends the process with
    // the exception code. A failure to load dbghelp only logs a warning. `exe`: "rawreel-cli" or
    // "rawreel-gui".
    static void install(std::string_view exe);
    // Test hook (design D22): with RAWREEL_TEST_CRASH=1 raises an access violation
    // (RaiseException(EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION), not abort); otherwise returns.
    static void crashIfRequested();
};

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- CMake (top-level `CMakeLists.txt`; insert next to M2's `rawreel_dvv_version` loop; the wx part
  after the block that runs `find_package(wxWidgets …)`):

```cmake
# Versions for rawreel-cli --version and About (design M4 v2 D23), on rawreel_core so both use them.
set(rawreel_versions "RAWREEL_VERSION=\"${PROJECT_VERSION}\"")
foreach(package IN ITEMS dvv_base dvv_registry dvv_platform dvv_gpu dvv_media dvv_components)
    rawreel_dvv_version(${package} version)
    string(TOUPPER "${package}" upper)
    list(APPEND rawreel_versions "RAWREEL_${upper}_VERSION=\"${version}\"")
endforeach()
target_compile_definitions(rawreel_core PRIVATE ${rawreel_versions})
target_sources(rawreel_core PRIVATE src/core/startup.cpp src/core/version_text.cpp
                                    src/gui/crash_handler.cpp)
target_link_libraries(rawreel_core PUBLIC dvv::components)   # ffmpeg.h, loadFfmpeg (Task 4 name)
target_link_libraries(rawreel_cli PRIVATE rawreel_core)
dvv_components_link(rawreel_cli)           # the ffmpeg component (providers ffmpeg, lavfi)
# … after the wx block:
if(DEFINED wxWidgets_VERSION)
    target_compile_definitions(rawreel_core PRIVATE RAWREEL_WX_VERSION="${wxWidgets_VERSION}")
else()
    target_compile_definitions(rawreel_core PRIVATE RAWREEL_WX_VERSION="not part of this build")
endif()
if(TARGET rawreel_gui)
    dvv_components_link(rawreel_gui)
endif()
```
  (Remove the `cli_versions` definitions from `rawreel_cli_core`; it reads the versions through
  `packageVersions()`. If wx's package sets its version under another variable, use it.)
- Produces for later tasks:
  - Task 7b: `startup.h`, `version_text.h`, `App::startup()` (`const Startup&`), the FFmpeg status
    in the GUI log at startup ("ffmpeg: <status line>", Info), the CTest fixture `rawreel_ffmpeg`
    (folder `${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/cli-ffmpeg-fixtures` with `clip.mkv`), the variables
    `DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR`/`DVV_FFMPEG_PROGRAM` of the `find_package(dvv_ffmpeg_sdk)` in
    `tests/CMakeLists.txt`.
  - Task 8 (packaging, smoke): `rawreel-cli --version` lines: `rawreel <PROJECT_VERSION>` first,
    then the dvv packages and `wxWidgets <v>`, `Vulkan: …`, and `FFmpeg <version> (<licence>) from
    <dir>` (bundled: `<dir>` inside the extracted package) or `FFmpeg: not available (<reason>)`;
    exit 0 without Vulkan; `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR=<empty dir>` gives "not available" even with an
    `ffmpeg/` folder next to the exe (Task 4's final explicit source). `info`/`dump` of
    `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p` work from the package. `RAWREEL_TEST_CRASH=1` and
    the dump folder `<userDataDir>/crashes`. Task 8 adds `/DEBUG /PDBALTPATH:%_PDB%` and the symbols
    zip; the crash handler needs no PDB at run time.
  - Task 10 (README): the dump folder and the note that `abort`/`__fastfail` are not captured.

**Behaviour:**
- **`startComponents(options)`:** `status = loadFfmpeg(options)`; `caps = Capabilities{}`;
  `addFfmpegCapabilities(status, caps)`; `components = ComponentRegistry::create(caps)` (as M3's App
  receives it); returns `{components, caps, status}`.
- **CLI `run` common part** (M2 `info`/`dump`, M2 Task 14 `bench`): step 2
  `registry = ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})` becomes `startup = startComponents({})`
  and the factory uses `*startup.components`; the shutdown order keeps the registry's place (step 5:
  reset `startup.components` with the component registry) and starts with
  `dvv::components::stopFfmpegSources()` (queued prefetch work of an FFmpeg source then ends at
  once instead of decoding on). An open error is printed as
  `error: <openErrorText(error, uri, startup.ffmpeg)>` (stderr), or with `--json` as
  `errorJson(errcName(code), openErrorText(…))`; exit codes unchanged (`exitCodeFor`; `Unsupported`
  → 3, `Format` → 2).
- **CLI `--version`** (design D21, D23): `startup = startComponents({})`; Vulkan:
  `GpuRegistry::create({.applicationName = "rawreel-cli"})` (as M2's `dump --via gpu` creates it) →
  an error gives `VulkanStatus{.available = false, .reason = describe(error)}`; no `bestDevice()` →
  reason "no usable Vulkan 1.3 device"; else device name and API version of the best device; the
  GPU registry is destroyed right after. Print `versionText(report)` (stdout) or
  `versionJson(report)` with `--json`. Exit 0 in every case, also without a Vulkan loader.
- **Main (CLI):** `CrashHandler::install("rawreel-cli")` first, then M2's `setUpLogging()`, then
  `CrashHandler::crashIfRequested()`, then the M2 flow.
- **App::OnInit (GUI):** `CrashHandler::install("rawreel-gui")` before step 1; step 5 becomes
  `startup_ = startComponents({})` with `DVV_LOG_INFO("ffmpeg: {}", ffmpegStatusLine(
  startup_.ffmpeg))`; `components()` returns `startup_.components` (the Session gets it as
  `SessionOptions::components`); `OnExit` first calls `dvv::components::stopFfmpegSources()` (before
  the Session shutdown, which waits for in-flight acquisitions: design v2 D5 "exit is bounded"),
  then releases `startup_` where M3 released `components`.
  Nothing else in the GUI changes here (About, the status line in the infobar and the selftest
  steps are Task 7b).
- **`CrashHandler`** (Code below; D22): Windows only. The filter is guarded against re-entry; the
  folder is created in the filter (`CreateDirectoryW` for `<userDataDir>` and `crashes`,
  `ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS` ignored), so a run without a crash creates nothing; the file name uses the
  local time and the process id; the dump is written before anything that may allocate (the stderr
  line and the log line come after); the filter returns `EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER`, so the process
  ends with the exception code and no Windows Error Reporting dialog appears.
- **Errors (§7):** "Crash (Windows) → minidump + log line; process ends with the exception code";
  every other row is the providers' (Tasks 4–5b).

**Code** (binding: the platform calls of the crash handler):

```cpp
// src/gui/crash_handler.cpp
#include "gui/crash_handler.h"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define NOMINMAX
#include <windows.h>
#include <dbghelp.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cwchar>
#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/logger.h"                      // real names: Step 1
#include "dvv/base/paths.h"
#include "dvv/platform/dynamic_library.h"

namespace rawreel {
namespace {

using WriteDump = BOOL(WINAPI*)(HANDLE, DWORD, HANDLE, MINIDUMP_TYPE,
                                PMINIDUMP_EXCEPTION_INFORMATION,
                                PMINIDUMP_USER_STREAM_INFORMATION, PMINIDUMP_CALLBACK_INFORMATION);

struct CrashState {               // process infrastructure: set once by install()
    std::wstring base;            // <userDataDir("rawreel")>
    std::wstring folder;          // <base>\crashes
    std::wstring exe;             // L"rawreel-cli"
    WriteDump writeDump = nullptr;
    std::atomic<bool> entered{false};
};
CrashState& crashState() {
    static CrashState state;
    return state;
}

LONG WINAPI onUnhandledException(EXCEPTION_POINTERS* pointers) {
    CrashState& s = crashState();
    if (s.entered.exchange(true) || s.writeDump == nullptr) {
        return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    }
    CreateDirectoryW(s.base.c_str(), nullptr);    // ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS is fine
    CreateDirectoryW(s.folder.c_str(), nullptr);
    SYSTEMTIME t{};
    GetLocalTime(&t);
    wchar_t path[1024];
    // WORD fields promote to int: cast each to unsigned for %u (MSVC C4477 under /W4 /WX).
    const int length = swprintf(path, std::size(path), L"%ls\\%ls-%04u%02u%02u-%02u%02u%02u-%lu.dmp",
                                s.folder.c_str(), s.exe.c_str(), static_cast<unsigned>(t.wYear),
                                static_cast<unsigned>(t.wMonth), static_cast<unsigned>(t.wDay),
                                static_cast<unsigned>(t.wHour), static_cast<unsigned>(t.wMinute),
                                static_cast<unsigned>(t.wSecond), GetCurrentProcessId());
    if (length <= 0) {
        return EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER;
    }
    const HANDLE file = CreateFileW(path, GENERIC_WRITE, 0, nullptr, CREATE_NEW,
                                    FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        return EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER;
    }
    MINIDUMP_EXCEPTION_INFORMATION exception{GetCurrentThreadId(), pointers, FALSE};
    const BOOL written = s.writeDump(GetCurrentProcess(), GetCurrentProcessId(), file,
                                     MiniDumpNormal, &exception, nullptr, nullptr);
    CloseHandle(file);
    char utf8[4096];
    if (written && WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, path, -1, utf8, static_cast<int>(sizeof utf8), nullptr,
                                       nullptr) > 0) {
        char line[4200];
        const int n = std::snprintf(line, sizeof line, "rawreel: crash dump %s\n", utf8);
        DWORD out = 0;
        if (n > 0) {
            WriteFile(GetStdHandle(STD_ERROR_HANDLE), line, static_cast<DWORD>(n), &out, nullptr);
        }
        // After the dump is on disk: the log line may allocate.
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("crash: exception 0x{:08X}, dump {}",
                      static_cast<unsigned>(pointers->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionCode), utf8);
        dvv::Logger::flush();
    }
    return EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER;  // ends the process with the exception code, no WER dialog
}

}  // namespace

void CrashHandler::install(std::string_view exe) {
    auto library = dvv::platform::DynamicLibrary::open(
        "dbghelp.dll", dvv::platform::LibrarySearch::SystemDirectory);
    if (!library) {
        DVV_LOG_WARN("crash dumps are off: {}", dvv::describe(library.error()));
        return;
    }
    auto base = dvv::paths::userDataDir("rawreel");
    if (!base) {
        DVV_LOG_WARN("crash dumps are off: {}", dvv::describe(base.error()));
        return;
    }
    CrashState& s = crashState();
    s.writeDump = library->function<WriteDump>("MiniDumpWriteDump");
    if (s.writeDump == nullptr) {
        DVV_LOG_WARN("crash dumps are off: MiniDumpWriteDump not found");
        return;
    }
    library->keepLoaded();
    s.base = base->wstring();
    s.folder = (*base / "crashes").wstring();
    s.exe = std::wstring(exe.begin(), exe.end());   // ASCII names only
    SetUnhandledExceptionFilter(&onUnhandledException);
}

void CrashHandler::crashIfRequested() {
    wchar_t value[2] = {};
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"RAWREEL_TEST_CRASH", value, 2) == 1 && value[0] == L'1') {
        RaiseException(EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION, 0, 0, nullptr);
    }
}

}  // namespace rawreel
#else
namespace rawreel {
void CrashHandler::install(std::string_view /*exe*/) {}
void CrashHandler::crashIfRequested() {}
}  // namespace rawreel
#endif
```
(If `userDataDir` returns a plain path instead of a `Result`, drop the check. Link `dbghelp` only
at run time: no `dbghelp.lib`. `DbgHelp.h` comes from the Windows SDK.)

**Tests first:**
- `tests/test_startup.cpp` (`rawreel_core_tests`, `TEST_SUITE("core")`; no FFmpeg is loaded):
  1. `startup: FFmpeg status line` — `FfmpegStatus{.core = true, .lavfi = true, .version = "n8.1.3",
     .licence = "LGPL version 2.1 or later", .directory = "ffmpeg"}` →
     `"FFmpeg n8.1.3 (LGPL version 2.1 or later) from ffmpeg"`; with `.lavfi = false` → the same +
     `", lavfi not available"`; `FfmpegStatus{.reason = "DVV_FFMPEG_DIR must be absolute"}` →
     `"FFmpeg: not available (DVV_FFMPEG_DIR must be absolute)"`.
  2. `startup: open error text` — `e = Unsupported "no source provider for scheme 'lavfi'"`, status
     not available → `describe(e) + "; FFmpeg: not available (…)"`; the same with `core = lavfi =
     true` → `describe(e)`; `Io` with FFmpeg unavailable → `describe(e)`; uri `"lavfi:testsrc2"`,
     `core = true`, `lavfi = false` → appended; uri `"file:x.yuv"`, `core = true`, `lavfi = false`
     → not appended.
  3. `version: Vulkan line` — `{true, "llvmpipe", "1.4.318"}` → `"Vulkan: llvmpipe, API 1.4.318"`;
     `{.reason = "no Vulkan loader"}` → `"Vulkan: not available (no Vulkan loader)"`.
  4. `version: package list` — names in the order rawreel, dvv-base, dvv-registry, dvv-platform,

     `RAWREEL_VERSION` as seen by the test (pass it to `rawreel_core_tests` too).
  5. `version: text and JSON` — a report with packages `{{"rawreel", "0.4.0"}, {"dvv-base",
     "0.4.0"}}`, Vulkan `"Vulkan: x, API 1.3.0"`, FFmpeg `"FFmpeg: not available (y)"` →
     `versionText == "rawreel 0.4.0\ndvv-base 0.4.0\nVulkan: x, API 1.3.0\nFFmpeg: not available (y)\n"`;
     `versionJson` has `"rawreel" == "0.4.0"`, `"dvv_base" == "0.4.0"`, `"vulkan"`, `"ffmpeg"`.
- `tests/cli/cli_ffmpeg_common.cmake` (included after `cli_common.cmake`; arguments
  `-DFIXTURES=<folder>`): `require_ffmpeg()` runs `--version`; when its output contains

  (missing → the same skip rule).
- `tests/cli/make-ffmpeg-fixtures.cmake` (CTest `rawreel.cli_ffmpeg_fixtures`, label `ffmpeg`,

  - `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25 -frames:v 12 -pix_fmt yuv420p10le -colorspace bt709 -c:v ffv1 -level 3 clip.mkv`
  - the lossless `Sequential` fixture (U-FF), the candidate Task 0 Step 7 chose (`<seq>`), at
    64×48: `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25 -frames:v 30 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v rawvideo seq.avi`
    (or the same with `seq.nut`), or
    `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25 -frames:v 30 -vf "setpts=floor(N/2)/(25*TB)" -fps_mode passthrough -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v ffv1 -level 3 seq.mkv`

- `tests/cli/run-cli-ffmpeg.cmake` (CTest `rawreel.cli_e2e.ffmpeg`, label `ffmpeg`,

  3. `info clip.mkv?fmt=gray` → exit 2; stderr contains `fmt is set by the container`.

- `tests/cli/run-cli-ffmpeg-gpu.cmake` (CTest `rawreel.cli_e2e.ffmpeg_gpu`, labels `gpu;ffmpeg`,

  properties): `ENV{DVV_FFMPEG_DIR}` = `relative/ffmpeg`; `--version` → exit 0, stdout contains
  `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR must be absolute`.
- `tests/cli/run-cli-version.cmake` (CTest `rawreel.cli_e2e.version`, label `ffmpeg`, environment
  `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR=<dir>`): `require_ffmpeg()`; `--version` → exit 0; stdout's first line is
  `rawreel <version>` (pass `-DVERSION=${PROJECT_VERSION}`); it has a line starting `Vulkan: ` and a
  line matching `^FFmpeg n8\.1\.3 \(LGPL` (Task 0's recorded FFmpeg version wins if it differs);
  `--version --json` parses and has the keys `rawreel`, `dvv_components`, `wxwidgets`, `vulkan`,
  `ffmpeg`.
- `tests/cli/run-cli-crash.cmake` (CTest `rawreel.cli_e2e.crash`, only `if(WIN32 AND NOT
  DVV_SANITIZE)` (MSVC ASan installs its own exception handling); design v2 §8.4):
  `ENV{RAWREEL_TEST_CRASH}=1`, `ENV{LOCALAPPDATA}=${WORK_DIR}/appdata`; `execute_process(rawreel-cli
  --version)` → the exit code is not `0`; stderr has `rawreel: crash dump <path>`; that file exists,
  its size is > 0 and its name matches `rawreel-cli-[0-9]{8}-[0-9]{6}-[0-9]+\.dmp`; when Step 1

  The script records before the run whether the dump's `crashes` folder (and its `rawreel` parent)

  else()

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Step 1: read and check.** `git -C rawreel log --oneline -5`, `git -C dvv-components log
  --oneline -8`; read rawreel's `CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `src/cli/{main,commands,
  output}.cpp`, `src/gui/app.{h,cpp}`, `tests/cli/cli_common.cmake`, `tests/cli/run-cli-gpu.cmake`;
  dvv-components' `include/dvv/components/ffmpeg.h`, its exported targets and `dvv_components_link`;
  dvv-gpu's `gpu_info.h`; dvv-base's `paths` (`userDataDir` on Windows: `LOCALAPPDATA` or the
  known-folder API?). Note the differences for the commit body. Read Task 0's U-FF records in the
  M4 plan file (the `Sequential` fixture choice `<seq>`, "mpeg4 encoder: yes/no"); `<seq>` is a
  constant in the fixture and e2e scripts.
- [ ] **Step 2: unit tests first.** Write `test_startup.cpp`, the headers and stub `.cpp` files, the
  CMake lines (dependency, sources, definitions, links). Run
  `Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "rawreel\.core_unit" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: builds; the 5 new cases fail.
- [ ] **Step 3: implement `startup` and `version_text`.** Same command. Expected:
  `rawreel.core_unit` passes.
- [ ] **Step 4: e2e tests first.** Write the fixture script, the common file, the six `run-cli-*`
  scripts (the crash script included) and their seven CTest entries (the fixture entry counts as
  one). Run
  `<Windows test env>; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "rawreel\.cli_(ffmpeg_fixtures|e2e\.(ffmpeg|version|crash))" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: the fixture test passes; `ffmpeg`, `version` and `crash` fail as described;
  `ffmpeg_missing` and `ffmpeg_relative` may already fail on the missing status line.
- [ ] **Step 5: implement** the CLI changes (startup in `run`, `--version`, the error text), the
  crash handler and its two calls, the App changes. Run Step 4's command, then
  `ctest --preset vs2026 -R "rawreel\." --output-on-failure` in the same call. Expected: every
  `rawreel.*` test passes (the GUI selftests `smoke`, `player`, `lost` still pass; the App now loads
  FFmpeg), none skipped.
- [ ] **Step 6: Linux sanity build:** none in this task (U-PROC). The Linux-only code it adds (the
  non-Windows no-op `CrashHandler`, the `ffmpeg` component linked into both executables on Linux) is
  compiled by the Task 7b checkpoint build; keep it portable.
- [ ] **Step 7: commit.**
  ```powershell
  git -C rawreel add -A
  @'
  feat: load FFmpeg at startup and add the crash handler

  rawreel-gui and rawreel-cli share one startup: the FFmpeg load (DVV_FFMPEG_DIR or the folder next
  to the executable), its capabilities and the component registry, with the ffmpeg component
  (providers ffmpeg and lavfi) linked into both. rawreel-cli --version lists every package, the
  Vulkan device (or why there is none, still exit 0) and the FFmpeg status line, which is also
  appended to an unsupported open when FFmpeg is unavailable. On Windows an unhandled exception
  writes a minidump to the user data folder. End-to-end tests cover info, dump and bench on FFV1,
  a lossless Sequential stream and lavfi inputs, the colour keys and their Reopen round trip, the
  refusal of a lossy codec, the missing-FFmpeg cases and the crash dump.

  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  '@ | git -C rawreel commit -F -
  ```
  Add the Step 1 differences and the `userDataDir` fact to the body.
- [ ] **Step 8: Per-task checks** (Execution notes; once, on the final commit). Expected:
  `FORMAT-OK`; `100% tests passed` in vs2026 Debug; privacy lint clean.

**Expected results:**
- `rawreel.core_unit` gains 5 cases (vs2026 Debug; ASan at the Task 7b checkpoint).
- New CTest entries: `rawreel.cli_ffmpeg_fixtures`, `rawreel.cli_e2e.ffmpeg`, `.ffmpeg_gpu`,
  `.ffmpeg_missing`, `.ffmpeg_relative`, `.version` and `rawreel.no_ffmpeg_imports` (output `no
  FFmpeg import in 2 binaries`, 1 in `vs2026-asan`) in both presets, and `.crash` in `vs2026`
  only; all pass in vs2026 Debug, none skipped with `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` and `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1`
  (Release and `vs2026-asan`: Task 7b checkpoint).
- The M3 GUI selftests still pass in `vs2026` Debug. The Linux build of `rawreel`: Task 7b
  checkpoint.

**Commit:** `feat: load FFmpeg at startup and add the crash handler` (rawreel).

**Notes:**
- Design references: v2 E3, D21, D22, D23, §4.6 (startup, seams, unavailable-FFmpeg message,
  CLI), §7 (rows for `lavfi:`/container without FFmpeg, crash), §8.1 rawreel core unit and CLI rows,
  §8.4; v1 D22 (dump path, dbghelp from System32, `MiniDumpNormal`); arbiter v2 FIX 10, 16, 18, 22,
  28, 31.
- Note (plan choice): `version_text.{h,cpp}` is a new core file (the design lists only
  `startup.{h,cpp}`): the version lines are shared by the CLI and About and unit-tested without wx.
- Note (plan choice): `crash_handler.{h,cpp}` stay at the design's path `src/gui/` but are wx-free
  and compiled into `rawreel_core`, so the CLI uses the same code.
- Note (plan choice): the CLI lists wxWidgets from the build's wx package version (it does not link
  wx); builds without wx print "not part of this build".
- Note (plan choice): the dump folder is created in the filter, so a run without a crash leaves no
  folder; the filter returns `EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER` (no WER dialog on CI or for users).
- Note (plan choice): the crash test reads the dump path from the stderr line, so it passes whether
  or not `userDataDir` honours `LOCALAPPDATA`, and it always deletes the dump.
- Note (plan choice): the §8.4 fixtures are regenerated on every run (64×48, a few frames) and not
  keyed by version.
- Risk: the log line inside the filter could deadlock if the crash happened inside the logger on
  the same thread; the dump is already on disk then. Accepted (D22 asks for the log line).

---

### Task 7b: GUI integration: About, the lavfi Format… message, the `smoke` and `player` steps

**Goal:** rawreel-gui gains Help → About (a `wxGenericAboutDialog` owned by the frame, with the D23
versions, the MIT licence, the notices file name and the FFmpeg status line, its text from a pure
`aboutText`), the View → Format… message for `lavfi:` streams (D28), the FFmpeg status line in the
infobar/log when an open fails because FFmpeg is unavailable, and the selftest steps of D29
(`smoke`: a `lavfi:` URI through the Open URI hook, About through its hook) and §8.5 (`player`: the
drop of an FFV1/MKV fixture). It runs the G2 workspace workflow.

**Execution notes** (repeated in every M4 task of this part):
- Run every command from the workspace root (the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`). Use the
  PowerShell tool on Windows. Linux runs as
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. Git runs only from PowerShell, except the read-only `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only`
  inside the WSL format and tidy commands. Branch `m4`. Nothing is pushed; no remote is added; no repository is created.
- **Windows test env** (`<Windows test env>`; in the same tool call as the run):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'`.
- `rawreel_gui` exists only without `DVV_SANITIZE`, and its GUI selftest CTest entries only on
  Windows (`if(TARGET rawreel_gui AND WIN32)`, M1b/M3); on Linux rawreel-gui builds only through
  the M1b stub viewport. No Linux GUI run: the existing CI Linux jobs keep their `xvfb-run` wrapper
  for the display tests but register and run no GUI selftest (U-WIN).
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Windows runs use `run_in_background: true`; at a checkpoint the
  WSL checkpoint build may run in parallel with a Windows run.
- **Per-task checks** (U-PROC; once, on the task's final commit; one background call for Windows;
  `<base>` = the rawreel head before this task):
  - Windows Debug: `<Windows test env>; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026-small --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`
    Expected: `100% tests passed`, no `Skipped` line for an `ffmpeg` or `gpu` test.
  - clang-format on the changed files (new files must be `git add`ed first):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`
    Expected: `FORMAT-OK`.
  - No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL build here: they run in the Checkpoint checks of the

**Behaviour:**
- **About** (design v2 §4.7, D23; arbiter v2 FIX 11): `execute(Command::About)` (always available)
  → `showAbout(*this)`: `report = {packageVersions(), vulkanStatusLine(status), ffmpegStatusLine(
  wxGetApp().startup().ffmpeg)}`, where `status` comes from `context_.lock()`: a context →
  `{true, <deviceInfo name>, <API version as a.b.c>}`, none → `{.reason = "no GPU device is in
  use"}`; `text = aboutText(report)`; `wxAboutDialogInfo info; info.SetName("rawreel");
  info.SetVersion(<rawreel version>); info.SetDescription(wxString::FromUTF8(text));` (no
  `SetLicence`: it would add a second dialog); `wxGenericAboutDialog dialog(info, this);` (owned by
  the frame on all platforms, never `wxAboutBox`); store `lastAboutText_ = text`, `++aboutShown_`;
  `showModal(dialog)`.
- **Open URI** (M3 case, changed only in how the dialog is shown): the `wxTextEntryDialog` goes
  through `showModal(dialog)` so the `uri` hook can set the value and close it with
  `EndModal(wxID_OK)` from `CallAfter`; the rest of M3's case is unchanged (`parseUri` → error to the
  infobar, else `session.open(0, …)`).
- **View → Format…** (D28): before M3's code, `if (auto refusal = formatRefusal(streams()[0]
  .resolvedUri))` → `DVV_LOG_INFO("{}", *refusal)`, `showMessage(wxString::FromUTF8(*refusal),
  wxICON_INFORMATION)`, return; nothing is reopened. Every other stream keeps M3's path (an `ffmpeg`
  stream reopens with `applyChoice(resolvedUri, choice)`: `impl=ffmpeg` stays, `fmt`/`size`/`fps`
  equal the stream's, the colour keys apply; no FFmpeg special case, D28).
- **Unavailable FFmpeg in the GUI** (design v2 §4.6, §7): `onError(error)` uses
  `openErrorText(error, "", startup.ffmpeg)` for the log line and the infobar text (the Reopen
  refinements of M3 stay as they are); `onFormatNeeded(...)` logs `DVV_LOG_INFO("{}",
  ffmpegStatusLine(startup.ffmpeg))` before the dialog when `!startup.ffmpeg.core` (a container file
  without FFmpeg is "any unknown file": the format dialog of M3 D21 plus the status in the log).
- **`showModal` overloads** (code below): one template body for the three dialog types; the hook is
  moved out first (one shot) and runs from a `CallAfter` queued before `ShowModal`, then
  `EndModal(wxID_OK)`.
- **`smoke` additions** (design v2 D29, §8.5; inserted after M3's `infobar` step, before
  `appearance`):
  1. `about`: start: `dialogHooks().about = [st](wxGenericAboutDialog&) { st->aboutHookRan = true; };`
     `st->about0 = frame.aboutShown(); frame.execute(Command::About);` (the hook closes the dialog);
     pass when `st->aboutHookRan`, `frame.aboutShown() > st->about0` and `lastAboutText()` contains
     `"Licence: MIT"`, `"THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt"`, `"Vulkan: "` and `"FFmpeg"`.
  2. `lavfi through Open URI` — added only when `app.startup().ffmpeg.lavfi`: start:
     `st->presented0 = presented(frame.viewport()); dialogHooks().uri = [](wxTextEntryDialog& d) {
     d.SetValue("lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p"); }; frame.execute(Command::OpenUri);`
     pass when `frame.streams().size() == 1`, its `resolvedUri` starts with
     `"lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48"`, the DisplayState snapshot has `frames.count == 1` and
     `presented(frame.viewport()) > st->presented0`.
  3. `Format... on a lavfi stream` — only with step 2: start: `st->streamUri =
     frame.streams()[0].resolvedUri; frame.execute(Command::Format);` pass when
     `frame.lastMessage()` contains `"setparams"`, one stream with the same `resolvedUri`, and no
     format dialog was shown (`formatDialogsShown()` unchanged).
  When `ffmpeg.lavfi` is false: with `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` a step `FFmpeg required` is added whose
  `passed` sets `seen = ffmpegStatusLine(…)` and returns false (the scenario fails with the
  reason); otherwise one Info log line "selftest: FFmpeg not available, lavfi steps skipped" and
  no step.
- **`player` additions** (§8.5; inserted before "close stream and clean up"; only when
  `app.startup().ffmpeg.core` and `RAWREEL_TEST_MKV` names an existing file; otherwise the same
  `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` rule as `smoke` (the reason names the variable or the missing file)):
  1. `drop the FFV1 MKV`: start: `st->dialogs0 = frame.formatDialogsShown();
     st->presented0 = presented(frame.viewport()); dropFiles(frame.viewport(), {<RAWREEL_TEST_MKV as
     a path through pathFromUtf8>});` pass when `streamIs(64, 48, "yuv420p10le")`, the stream's
     `resolvedUri` contains `"impl=ffmpeg"`, `formatDialogsShown() == st->dialogs0` (no format
     dialog) and `presented(...) > st->presented0`.
  2. `play the MKV 5 frames`: start `frame.execute(Command::PlayPause)`; pass when
     `status().position >= 5`.
  3. `pause the MKV`: start `frame.execute(Command::PlayPause)` when playing; pass when
     `!status().playing`.

**Code** (binding: the hook mechanics on the modal loops):

```cpp
// src/gui/selftest.cpp — one body for the three dialogs; the M3 FormatDialog overload becomes a
// call of it.
namespace {
template <class Dialog>
int showModalWith(Dialog& dialog, std::move_only_function<void(Dialog&)>& slot) {
    if (slot) {
        // One shot; CallAfter needs a copyable functor, so the hook is shared.
        auto hook = std::make_shared<std::move_only_function<void(Dialog&)>>(std::move(slot));
        slot = nullptr;
        dialog.CallAfter([&dialog, hook] {
            (*hook)(dialog);
            dialog.EndModal(wxID_OK);
        });
    }
    return dialog.ShowModal();
}
}  // namespace

int showModal(FormatDialog& dialog) { return showModalWith(dialog, dialogHooks().format); }
int showModal(wxTextEntryDialog& dialog) { return showModalWith(dialog, dialogHooks().uri); }
int showModal(wxGenericAboutDialog& dialog) { return showModalWith(dialog, dialogHooks().about); }
```

```cpp
// src/gui/about.cpp
#include <wx/wx.h>  // first (M1b R11)

#include "gui/about.h"

#include <wx/aboutdlg.h>
#include <wx/generic/aboutdlgg.h>

#include "core/startup.h"
#include "core/version_text.h"
#include "gui/app.h"
#include "gui/main_frame.h"
#include "gui/selftest.h"

namespace rawreel {

void showAbout(MainFrame& frame) {
    VersionReport report{packageVersions(), vulkanStatusLine(frame.vulkanStatus()),
                         ffmpegStatusLine(wxGetApp().startup().ffmpeg)};
    const std::string text = aboutText(report);
    wxAboutDialogInfo info;
    info.SetName("rawreel");
    info.SetVersion(wxString::FromUTF8(report.packages.front().second));
    info.SetDescription(wxString::FromUTF8(text));
    wxGenericAboutDialog dialog(info, &frame);   // owned by the frame on every platform (FIX 11)
    frame.noteAbout(text);                        // lastAboutText(), aboutShown()
    showModal(dialog);                            // the selftest hook closes it with EndModal
}

}  // namespace rawreel
```
(`MainFrame::vulkanStatus()` builds the `VulkanStatus` from `context_` as described. Discarding `showModal`'s result
needs a `static_cast<void>` or a named variable for `[[nodiscard]]`.)

  `CallAfter` + `EndModal` mechanism as M3's format hook, so the real `wxTextEntryDialog` is
