- Note (plan choice): `formatRefusal` lives in `format_choice` (wx-free, unit-tested); M3's
  `applyChoice`, `validateChoice` and `FormatDialog` are unchanged (D28).
- Note (plan choice): the `smoke` step `Format... on a lavfi stream` is not listed in D29; it tests
  the only new GUI rule of D28 at the cost of one step.
- Note (plan choice): the GUI's `onError` passes an empty URI to `openErrorText` (the Session's
  error event carries none), so the status line is appended when FFmpeg is unavailable as a whole,
  which is the only case in practice (all six libraries are checked together, D3).
- Cut by the design (not built here): `CapabilitiesPanel`, the *Open URI* presets (D15), `--diag`,
  the Open dialog's FFmpeg filter, `--ffmpeg-dir` in `gui_arguments`, any FFmpeg rule in the format
  dialog, the `analysis` scenario.
- Note (U-WIN): the WSLg run of plan v2 is dropped. If the About hook ever fails on GTK when Linux
  GUI tests return (v1.x), M3's documented fallback applies (queue the hook with a second
  `CallAfter` from inside the first).

### Task 9: CI and release workflow

**Goal:** Require FFmpeg in the Windows CI build steps, keep the oracle tests out of the sanitizer
jobs, and add rawreel's `release.yml` (dry run and real run, `workflow_dispatch` only; one Windows
build job, U-WIN). Check the workflows with actionlint and static checks, and check every script
call of `release.yml` against Task 8's planned interface. Nothing runs on GitHub before Task 12's
Gate A. The Linux release job is v1.x; the existing CI Linux jobs are left as they are (U-WIN).

**Place in the order (user decision U-M4ORDER):** Task 9 runs after Task 7b (and Task P when it
ran) and before Task 10-alpha; Task 8 (packaging) runs later, after Task 10-final. So this task
defines `release.yml` against the script interface that Task 8's Produces fixes, but the packaging
scripts do not exist yet and the workflow's commands cannot run here. The verbatim run of the
`windows` job's commands and the rehearsal of the `publish` job's checksum lines (plan v2's Steps
4 and 5 of this task) are Task 8's Steps 9b and 9c; the workflow is first used there and first
run on GitHub in Task 12.

**Files:**
- rawreel-workspace:
  - Modify `.github/workflows/dvv-ci.yml`: step-level `env: DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG: "1"` on the
    `build` job's step "Build and test (Windows)" (the Linux steps stay unchanged, U-WIN).
  - Modify `.github/workflows/workspace.yml`: step-level `env: DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG: "1"` on the
    `integration` job's step "Build and test everything (Windows)". Nothing else: the job
    bootstraps from `repos.txt`, which lists `dvv-components` since Task 3, so design §9
    "workspace.yml gains dvv-components" is met.
  - Modify `cmake/DvvPresets.json` (only if Step 1 finds oracle tests registered in a sanitizer
    configuration): exclude the label `oracle` in the `vs2026-asan`, `ninja-clang-asan` and
    `ninja-clang-tsan` test presets (all three, so the presets stay consistent; only `vs2026-asan`
    runs locally).
  - Modify `CMakeLists.txt` (only if Step 1 finds `dvv_components` missing from the workspace
    build): add `dvv_components` to `FetchContent_MakeAvailable` and `dvv_check_siblings`.
- rawreel:
  - Create `.github/workflows/release.yml` (complete content below).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 8's planned interface (its Produces in this plan; Task 8 runs later, U-M4ORDER):
    `packaging/verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake`, `packaging/make-packages.cmake`,
    `packaging/package-smoke.cmake` with the parameters listed there; the output names; the
    `package-info-<platform>.cmake` files. The files do not exist yet; `release.yml` names them
    exactly as Task 8's Produces does.
  - Task 3: `repos.txt` lists `dvv-components`; `dvv-versions.cmake` declares `dvv_components`;
    the workspace presets set `FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_COMPONENTS`; dvv-components has a
    `ci.yml` calling `dvv-ci.yml@main`.
  - Task 6: the oracle tests (`dvv_components_oracle_tests`, label `ffmpeg;oracle`) are registered
    only `if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)`, like the GUI selftests. Step 1 checks it; if they are registered,
    the preset filter of Files applies.
  - Tasks 7a and 8: rawreel's unit and CLI tests are every rawreel CTest entry without the labels
    `gpu`, `display`, `gui`, `oracle` (`rawreel.core_unit`, `rawreel.core_session`,
    `rawreel.cli_unit`, `rawreel.cli_e2e.*` incl. the FFmpeg e2e entries,
    `rawreel.no_ffmpeg_imports`, and `rawreel.packaging_scripts` once Task 8 adds it); Step 1
    lists the current ones with `ctest -N`, and Task 8 Step 9b lists the final set.
  - Tasks 10-beta and 10-final: `rawreel/docs/release-notes-<version>.md` (needed by a real run
    only; 10-beta writes the draft, 10-final the final text; both run after this task).
  - M1b: `dvv-ci.yml`, `workspace.yml` structure (Task 13a of M1b; the build matrix entries carry
    `toolchain: msvc|clang`, and the Windows and Linux build steps are separate steps named
    "Build and test (Windows)" / "Build and test everything (Windows)"; Step 1 confirms the names),
    actionlint at `_build/tools/actionlint/bin/actionlint.exe`, the runner images `windows-2025`
    and `ubuntu-24.04` (the latter only for the `check` and `publish` jobs here), the third-party
    cache key.
- Produces: `release.yml` with inputs `version` (string, required) and `dry_run` (boolean,
  default `true`); jobs `check`, `windows`, `publish`; artifacts `windows` (packages, the sources
  archive when bundled, `package-info-windows.cmake`) and `release-files` (release assets +
  `SHA256SUMS`); in a real run, build attestations of every release file and a **draft** release
  `v<version>` in rawreel. Task 8 runs its `windows` job's commands and the `publish` job's
  checksum lines locally first (Steps 9b, 9c); Task 12 runs it on GitHub (dry run at Gate A, real
  run at Gate B).

**Behaviour** (§9 `release.yml`, D16, D20, D27; v2 arbiter FIX 20, 24):
- `workflow_dispatch` only. `RELEASE_REF` = `main` for a dry run, `v<version>` otherwise; every
  repository is checked out explicitly at that ref into its sibling folder (`bootstrap.ps1` has no
  ref parameter), then `bootstrap.ps1 -NoClone`.
- The `check` job rejects a version that is not `<digits>.<digits>.<digits>`. User input reaches
  scripts only through `env:`, never as `${{ inputs.* }}` inside a `run:` script.
- `windows` (runner `windows-2025`, the CI image): restore the third-party cache (CI's key; no
  save), bootstrap, verify the custom_ffmpeg archive (SHA256 + attestation, `GH_TOKEN`), build
  Release, run the unit and CLI tests (`-LE "gpu|display|gui|oracle"`; no oracle: CI ran it on the
  same refs), package with the sources archive (`SOURCES=ON`: bundled only; from the attested
  commit), run the package smoke (`EXPECTED_VERSION` = the input, `REQUIRE_SOURCES=ON`, the GUI
  smoke with `ALLOW_GUI_SKIP=ON`: GPU-less runner), upload. No Linux job (U-WIN: the Linux package
  and its job are v1.x); the GUI smoke on a GPU runs in the local dry runs (Tasks 8, 11, after
  this task, U-M4ORDER) and again in Task 12's pre-flight and dry-run checks on NVIDIA.
- `publish` (after `windows`): downloads the artifacts, drops `package-info-*.cmake`, writes
  `SHA256SUMS` over `rawreel-*`, uploads `release-files` (both kinds of run); only when not a dry run:
  `actions/attest-build-provenance` over every file and `gh release create v<version> --draft
  --verify-tag` with the release notes and all files.
- Permissions: workflow default `contents: read`; the build jobs add `attestations: read`; only
  `publish` holds `contents: write`, `id-token: write`, `attestations: write` (the design's three).
  **Note (plan choice):** narrowed per job; the jobs that run third-party build code hold no write
  token.
- Every action is pinned by a full commit SHA with the tag in a comment. The majors are those of
  `dvv-ci.yml` (`actions/checkout` v5, `actions/setup-python` v6, `actions/cache` v5) and the
  current majors of `actions/upload-artifact`, `actions/download-artifact` and
  `actions/attest-build-provenance`; each SHA is the commit of that major's newest release tag
  (Step 2 resolves them).
- `dvv-ci.yml` and `workspace.yml`: `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG: "1"` at step level on the Windows build
  steps only (the lint job builds only; the Linux build steps are left as they are, U-WIN: they
  still run the FFmpeg tests when the clang prefix has FFmpeg, but a skip there does not fail).
  No other change; the cache key already hashes `deps/`. The sibling repos call `dvv-ci.yml@main`,
  so this reaches them when Gate A pushes the workspace `main` first.

**Code** (binding).

`rawreel/.github/workflows/release.yml` (complete; replace each `<sha …>` with the 40-hex commit
of Step 2 and keep the tag comment):

```yaml
# Release of rawreel (spec §13.4; design M4 v2 §9, D16, D20, D27). Started by hand only.
# A dry run builds the main branches, runs the unit and CLI tests, packages, runs the package
# smoke tests and keeps the files as workflow artifacts. A real run does the same from the
# v<version> tags of every repository, attests the files and creates a draft release; a maintainer
# reviews and publishes it.
name: release

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      version:
        description: Version to release, without the v (for example 1.0.0)
        required: true
        type: string
      dry_run:
        description: Build from main and keep the files as workflow artifacts only
        required: true
        type: boolean
        default: true

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: release
  cancel-in-progress: false

env:
  # Every repository is checked out at this ref: main for a dry run, the release tag otherwise.
  RELEASE_REF: ${{ inputs.dry_run && 'main' || format('v{0}', inputs.version) }}
  DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG: "1"

jobs:
  check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 5
    steps:
      - name: Check the version input
        env:
          VERSION: ${{ inputs.version }}
        run: |
          if [[ ! "$VERSION" =~ ^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
            echo "::error::version must look like 1.0.0"
            exit 1
          fi

  windows:
    needs: check
    runs-on: windows-2025
    timeout-minutes: 180
    permissions:
      contents: read
      attestations: read
    env:
      GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/rawreel-workspace
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: rawreel-workspace
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/dvv-base
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: dvv-base
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/dvv-registry
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: dvv-registry
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/dvv-platform
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: dvv-platform
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/dvv-gpu
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: dvv-gpu
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/dvv-media
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: dvv-media
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/dvv-components
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: dvv-components
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          repository: ${{ github.repository_owner }}/rawreel
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: rawreel
      - uses: actions/setup-python@<sha setup-python> # <tag>
        with:
          python-version: "3.13"
      - name: Install tools
        run: python -m pip install -r rawreel-workspace/tools/requirements.txt
      - name: Check Visual Studio 2026
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          $vswhere = Join-Path ${env:ProgramFiles(x86)} 'Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe'
          $version = & $vswhere -latest -property catalog_productDisplayVersion
          if (-not $version.StartsWith('18.')) { throw "Visual Studio 2026 (18.x) required, found $version" }
          Write-Host "Visual Studio $version"
      # CI's cache entry (same key as dvv-ci.yml); a miss builds the prefix. Never saved from here.
      - name: Restore third-party
        uses: actions/cache/restore@<sha cache> # <tag>
        with:
          path: _third_party/msvc
          key: third-party-${{ runner.os }}-msvc-${{ hashFiles('rawreel-workspace/deps/**', 'rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvThirdParty.cmake', 'rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvSpdx.cmake') }}
      - name: Bootstrap
        shell: pwsh
        run: ./rawreel-workspace/bootstrap.ps1 -Toolchain msvc -NoClone
      - name: Verify the custom_ffmpeg archive (SHA256 and attestation)
        run: cmake -DPREFIX=_third_party/msvc "-DWORK_DIR=${{ runner.temp }}/ffmpeg-check" -P rawreel/packaging/verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake
      - name: Build (Release)
        working-directory: rawreel
        run: |
          cmake --preset vs2026
          cmake --build --preset vs2026-release
      - name: Unit and CLI tests
        working-directory: rawreel
        run: ctest --preset vs2026-release -LE "gpu|display|gui|oracle"
      # The sources archive is built in this job only, from a --recurse-submodules checkout of the
      # custom_ffmpeg tag whose commit equals the attestation's sourceRepositoryDigest.
      - name: Package (and the third-party sources when FFmpeg is bundled)
        run: cmake -DBUILD_DIR=_build/rawreel/vs2026 -DCONFIG=Release "-DOUT_DIR=${{ runner.temp }}/out" -DSOURCES=ON "-DFFMPEG_COMMIT_FILE=${{ runner.temp }}/ffmpeg-check/custom_ffmpeg-commit.txt" -P rawreel/packaging/make-packages.cmake
      # The runner has no GPU: the GUI smoke may exit 77 here; the local dry runs run it on a GPU.
      - name: Package smoke tests
        env:
          VERSION: ${{ inputs.version }}
        run: cmake "-DOUT_DIR=${{ runner.temp }}/out" -DPLATFORM=windows "-DWORK_DIR=${{ runner.temp }}/smoke" "-DEXPECTED_VERSION=$env:VERSION" -DGUI_SMOKE=windows -DALLOW_GUI_SKIP=ON -DREQUIRE_SOURCES=ON -P rawreel/packaging/package-smoke.cmake
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@<sha upload-artifact> # <tag>
        with:
          name: windows
          path: |
            ${{ runner.temp }}/out/rawreel-*
            ${{ runner.temp }}/out/package-info-windows.cmake
          if-no-files-found: error

  # No Linux job in v1.0 (user decision U-WIN): the Linux package and its job are v1.x.

  publish:
    needs: [windows]
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    permissions:
      contents: write
      id-token: write
      attestations: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@<sha checkout> # <tag>
        with:
          ref: ${{ env.RELEASE_REF }}
          path: rawreel
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@<sha download-artifact> # <tag>
        with:
          path: dist
          merge-multiple: true
      - name: Checksums
        working-directory: dist
        run: |
          rm -f package-info-*.cmake
          sha256sum rawreel-* > SHA256SUMS
          cat SHA256SUMS
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@<sha upload-artifact> # <tag>
        with:
          name: release-files
          path: dist/*
          if-no-files-found: error
      - name: Attest the release files
        if: ${{ !inputs.dry_run }}
        uses: actions/attest-build-provenance@<sha attest-build-provenance> # <tag>
        with:
          subject-path: dist/*
      - name: Create the draft release
        if: ${{ !inputs.dry_run }}
        working-directory: dist
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          VERSION: ${{ inputs.version }}
        run: |
          notes="../rawreel/docs/release-notes-${VERSION}.md"
          if [ ! -f "$notes" ]; then
            echo "::error::the release notes $notes are missing"
            exit 1
          fi
          gh release create "v${VERSION}" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --draft --verify-tag \
            --title "rawreel ${VERSION}" --notes-file "$notes" -- rawreel-* SHA256SUMS
```
(The `windows` job runs `make-third-party-sources.cmake` through `make-packages.cmake`; the
runner image has `git` with long-path support, which the script enables per command.)

**Tests first:** before the file exists, Step 3's actionlint call fails (`release.yml` missing) and
the static checks of Step 3 fail. After writing it, they pass, and Step 4 checks the script calls
against Task 8's planned interface. The Windows job's commands run verbatim, and the publish job's
checksum lines are rehearsed, in Task 8 (Steps 9b and 9c), once the scripts exist (U-M4ORDER).

**Steps** (from the workspace root; PowerShell tool on Windows; git only in PowerShell; nothing is
pushed, no workflow runs on GitHub):
- [ ] **Step 1: Inventory.**
  - Oracle under sanitizers: `Push-Location dvv-components; cmake --preset vs2026-asan *> $null; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -N -L oracle | Select-Object -Last 1; Pop-Location`.
    Expected `Total Tests: 0` (assumption holds). Otherwise add to each sanitizer test preset in
    `rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvPresets.json` (`vs2026-asan`, `ninja-clang-asan`,
    `ninja-clang-tsan`): `"filter": { "exclude": { "label": "oracle" } }`, and repeat (expected 0
    run, the listing still shows them as excluded by the filter).
  - CI step names: `Select-String rawreel-workspace/.github/workflows/dvv-ci.yml, rawreel-workspace/.github/workflows/workspace.yml -Pattern 'name: Build and test'`.
    Expected: the Windows steps "Build and test (Windows)" and "Build and test everything
    (Windows)"; if they are named otherwise, use the real names.
  - Workspace build: `Select-String rawreel-workspace/CMakeLists.txt -Pattern 'dvv_components'`.
    Expected: present in `FetchContent_MakeAvailable` and `dvv_check_siblings` (Task 3); otherwise
    add it to both.
  - Record both findings in the task report.
- [ ] **Step 2: Resolve the action SHAs** (read-only network): for each of `actions/checkout`
  (v5), `actions/setup-python` (v6), `actions/cache` (v5), `actions/upload-artifact`,
  `actions/download-artifact`, `actions/attest-build-provenance` (current majors):
  `git ls-remote --tags https://github.com/<action>.git` — take the newest `vX.Y.Z` of the major;
  use the `^{}` (peeled) line's commit when the tag is annotated. Write the SHAs and tags into the
  task report.
- [ ] **Step 3: Write the files** (release.yml; the two step-level `env` additions; Step 1's
  conditional edits). Check:
  `_build/tools/actionlint/bin/actionlint.exe rawreel-workspace/.github/workflows/dvv-ci.yml rawreel-workspace/.github/workflows/workspace.yml rawreel/.github/workflows/release.yml rawreel/.github/workflows/ci.yml; "exit $LASTEXITCODE"`
  (expected no output, `exit 0`; if the exe is missing, use the M1a venv recipe), then the static
  checks:
  ```powershell
  $y = Get-Content rawreel/.github/workflows/release.yml
  ($y | Select-String 'uses: ' | Where-Object { $_.Line -notmatch 'uses: [\w./-]+@[0-9a-f]{40} # v\d' }) ; "unpinned above (expect none)"
  ($y | Select-String '\$\{\{[^}]*inputs\.' | Where-Object { $_.Line -notmatch '^\s+(RELEASE_REF|VERSION): \$\{\{|^\s+if: \$\{\{' }) ; "inputs outside env/if above (expect none)"
  ($y | Select-String 'runs-on:' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() } | Sort-Object -Unique)
  ($y | Select-String 'actions/checkout@' | Measure-Object).Count
  ```
  Expected: nothing listed for the first two; `runs-on:` values exactly `ubuntu-24.04` (`check`,
  `publish`) and `windows-2025`; 9 checkout steps (8 + 1); no `linux` job and no `bootstrap.sh`
  in the file.
- [ ] **Step 4: The script calls against Task 8's interface** (read-only; U-M4ORDER: the scripts
  do not exist yet, so nothing runs):
  `Select-String rawreel/.github/workflows/release.yml -Pattern 'packaging/|bootstrap\.ps1|ctest ' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() }`.
  Expected: three script calls, `verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake` (`PREFIX`, `WORK_DIR`),
  `make-packages.cmake` (`BUILD_DIR`, `CONFIG`, `OUT_DIR`, `SOURCES`, `FFMPEG_COMMIT_FILE`) and
  `package-smoke.cmake` (`OUT_DIR`, `PLATFORM=windows`, `WORK_DIR`, `EXPECTED_VERSION`,
  `GUI_SMOKE=windows`, `ALLOW_GUI_SKIP=ON`, `REQUIRE_SOURCES=ON`), each parameter named in Task
  8's Produces "Scripts" block with that spelling; the upload paths match Task 8's output names
  (`rawreel-*`, `package-info-windows.cmake`); `bootstrap.ps1 -Toolchain msvc -NoClone`; `ctest
  --preset vs2026-release -LE "gpu|display|gui|oracle"`. A difference is fixed in `release.yml`
  (the plan's Task 8 interface is binding) and Step 3 repeats. The verbatim run is Task 8 Step
  9b; the checksum rehearsal is Task 8 Step 9c.
- [ ] **Step 5: Commit** (each with the trailer):
  ```powershell
  git -C rawreel-workspace add -A
  @'
  ci: require FFmpeg in the Windows CI build steps

  DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1 in the Windows build steps of dvv-ci.yml and of the nightly integration
  job, so a missing FFmpeg fails the FFmpeg tests instead of skipping them. The Linux steps stay
  as they are (v1.0 supports Windows only). Takes effect when published (workspace main first).

  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  '@ | git -C rawreel-workspace commit -F -
  git -C rawreel add .github/workflows/release.yml
  @'
  ci: add the release workflow

  release.yml runs by hand only. A dry run builds the main branches of every repository on
  Windows, runs the unit and CLI tests, packages and runs the package smoke tests, keeping the
  files as workflow artifacts. A real run builds the v<version> tags, verifies the custom_ffmpeg
  attestation, builds the third-party sources archive from the attested commit, writes SHA256SUMS,
  attests every file and creates a draft release. Actions are pinned by commit.

  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  '@ | git -C rawreel commit -F -
  ```
  (Add the preset or workspace CMake change of Step 1 to the workspace commit's body when made.)
- [ ] **Step 6: Final checks.** Privacy lint, commits mode, on both repositories:
  `foreach ($r in 'rawreel','rawreel-workspace') { cmake "-DREPO=$r" -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake }`.
  When Step 1 changed `DvvPresets.json` or the workspace `CMakeLists.txt`, also run the workspace
  workflow (one background call, `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` set):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
  No C++ changes in this task, so its Per-task clang-format does not apply; the workflows are
  checked by Steps 3 and 4 and, when the workspace changed, by the workflow above. The Windows
  job's commands run in Task 8 Step 9b (U-M4ORDER). The Linux build and clang-tidy of Task P's
  repository, when P ran, are the Step 8 checkpoint (the Linux presets of `DvvPresets.json`
  change only by the same JSON filter, if at all; that filter is checked by the Task 11
  workspace Linux sanity build).
- [ ] **Step 7: Record** in the M4 plan file's `## Changes during execution` ("Task 9: CI and
  release workflow"): Step 1's findings, the action tags (not the SHAs: they are in the file), the
  Step 3 and Step 4 results, and the note "Windows-job run and checksum rehearsal: Task 8 Steps 9b
  and 9c (U-M4ORDER)". Commit (`docs: record the M4 release workflow checks`, rawreel-workspace,
  with the trailer); privacy lint again.
- [ ] **Step 8: Checkpoint checks (Task P if it ran, Task 9; Global Constraints).** Task 9 is a
  checkpoint. After U-M4ORDER it no longer covers Task 8, which now runs after Task 10-final and
  falls under the Task 11 final checkpoint. Since the Task 7b checkpoint, Task 9 changed only
  workflow files (rawreel `.github/workflows/release.yml`; rawreel-workspace `dvv-ci.yml`,
  `workspace.yml`) and, when Step 1 needed them, `DvvPresets.json` or the workspace
  `CMakeLists.txt`. Workflow files are no build input, so they are checked by Steps 3, 4 and 6,
  not by a build; the preset or CMake change is checked by Step 6's workspace workflows. So the
  checkpoint commands below run only for the repository Task P changed, when Task P ran
  (`<cp-base>` = that repository's head at the Task 7b checkpoint). When Task P did not run, the
  checkpoint records "no build input changed since the Task 7b checkpoint; workflow files checked
  by Steps 3, 4 and 6" and ends. For the `<repo>` of Task P, one background Windows call and, in
  parallel, one background WSL call:

```powershell
$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; ctest --preset vs2026-release --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|AddressSanitizer'; Pop-Location
wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <cp-base> HEAD -- ":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/<repo>/ninja-clang --quiet && echo TIDY-OK'
```

  (dvv-components, if P changed it: its own pathspecs, Global Constraints.) When Task P ran, its
  ASan race check ×10 follows the ASan workflow of its repository (the regex Task P names):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location <repo>; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R "<regex>" --repeat until-fail:10 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`.
  When P changed a dvv repository, rawreel (which consumes it) also gets the Windows call. The
  Linux sanity build of Task 8's `packaging.cmake` is part of the Task 11 final checkpoint now
  (U-M4ORDER). Expected: Release (with the GUI selftests) and `vs2026-asan` `100% tests passed`,
  no `Skipped` line for a `gpu` or `ffmpeg` test, no sanitizer report, the race check ×10 green;
  `TIDY-OK`. A compile or link error in our code is fixed; a toolchain or third-party failure is
  recorded and reported. A failure is fixed (a `fix:` commit, then the Per-task checks and the
  failed check again) before Task 10-alpha starts.

**Expected results:** actionlint `exit 0`; the static checks list nothing unexpected; Step 4 finds
every script call and output name equal to Task 8's planned interface; privacy lint clean.
Checkpoint: Release and `vs2026-asan` green and `TIDY-OK` for Task P's repository when P ran,
otherwise the "no build input changed" record. The Windows-job run and the checksum rehearsal are
Task 8's (Steps 9b, 9c). No GitHub run exists yet (E6 is Task 12).

**Commit:** `ci: require FFmpeg in the Windows CI build steps` (rawreel-workspace), `ci: add the
release workflow` (rawreel), `docs: record the M4 release workflow checks` (rawreel-workspace).

**Notes:**
- Design references: §9 (`release.yml`, CI), §8.6, §8.7, D16, D20, D27; v2 arbiter FIX 20, 24, 28.
- **Note (plan choice):** the release workflow restores CI's third-party cache and never saves
  one, so a release run cannot change what CI uses.
- **Note (plan choice):** "unit + CLI tests" = every rawreel CTest entry without the labels
  `gpu|display|gui|oracle`; on the GPU-less Windows runner nothing else could run. The GUI smoke
  of the packaged build runs on NVIDIA in the local dry runs (Tasks 8, 11) and in Task 12's checks
  of the CI-built files (U-WIN: plan v2's Linux job with its Xvfb smoke is v1.x).
- **Note (U-WIN):** the sources archive moves from the Linux job to the `windows` job (the only
  build job); the checkout count drops from 17 to 9.
- **Note (plan choice):** the `check` job validates the version before two long jobs start; the
  package smoke's `EXPECTED_VERSION` additionally catches a version input that differs from
  `PROJECT_VERSION`. The pre-release versions `1.0.0-alpha` and `1.0.0-beta` of Tasks 10-alpha
  and 10-beta are never packaged or released (U-M4ORDER: Task 8 packages 1.0.0), so the
  `<digits>.<digits>.<digits>` rule stays.
- **Note (U-M4ORDER):** this task defines `release.yml`; Task 8 first uses it (the Windows-job
  commands verbatim, the checksum lines) and Task 12 first runs it on GitHub. A mismatch Task 8
  finds is fixed there in `release.yml` or in the script, whichever differs from Task 8's planned
  interface.
- R14: if `gh release create` or the attestation fails in rawreel, the fallback is a manual upload
  of the `release-files` artifact (Task 12 asks the user first).
- R15: CI failures that only GitHub shows are handled by Task 12's Gate A fix loop.

### Task 10: Release preparation in three stages (U-M4ORDER)

User decision U-M4ORDER (2026-10-25, binding) splits release preparation into **10-alpha**,
**10-beta** and **10-final**. They run one after the other, after Task 9 and before Task 8
(packaging); each runs in its own fresh session. The dispatch of each stage includes this overview
(the table and "Version label") together with the stage's text. Every item of the former Task 10
is in exactly one stage:

| Former Task 10 item | Stage |
|---|---|
| Version requests (Step 1 inventory), `project(VERSION 1.0.0)`, the `1.0` package requests, the version label mechanism | 10-alpha (label `alpha`) |
| Label `beta` | 10-beta |
| Label removed: version `1.0.0` | 10-final |
| Spec §2 FFmpeg row, §7.1/§7.2, §7.3 (FFmpeg sources, U-FF); the spec's `rawreel.json` text (U-IO2-config) | 10-alpha |
| rawreel README item 2 "FFmpeg sources" and item 7 "Configuration"; run instructions for a development build (new) | 10-alpha |
| Spec §1.1/§15, §3.1, §4, §6.1/§6.2/§6.4, §9.1, §9.3, §10.2, §10.3, §11, §12, §13.2, §13.4, §13.5, §15, §16, §17 | 10-beta |
| `rawreel-workspace/README.md`, `dvv-components/README.md`, the dvv-platform and dvv-media README checks, rawreel README items 1, 3 ([U4]), 4, 5, 6 | 10-beta |
| The [U4] accept and decline texts; the licence notes | 10-beta |
| Release notes `rawreel/docs/release-notes-1.0.0.md` | draft: 10-beta; final text: 10-final |
| The stale-text check (spec) | 10-beta; the U-FF check: 10-alpha on its texts, 10-beta on all |
| CLI examples checked as written | the stage that writes them |
| The package commands of the docs checked against the dry-run outputs | Task 8 Step 9d (the packages exist only after Task 8) |
| Per-task checks (full `ctest --preset vs2026` in every code repository) | each stage, on its own commits |
| Ledger (M4 section, heads for Task 11's Debug reuse) | 10-final |

**Version label (all three stages; D23, U-M4ORDER).** CMake's `project(VERSION)` accepts only
numeric components, so `project(<name> VERSION 1.0.0 …)` stays numeric through all three stages,
and the package version files (`COMPATIBILITY SameMinorVersion`), `find_dependency(<dvv
package> 1.0)` and `find_package(<pkg> 1.0 REQUIRED)` use it unchanged. The semver string with
the pre-release label lives in one variable per code repository, set in its top-level
`CMakeLists.txt` right after `project()`:
```cmake
# Version with the release stage (user decision U-M4ORDER): 1.0.0-alpha, 1.0.0-beta, then 1.0.0.
set(<project>_VERSION_STRING "${PROJECT_VERSION}-alpha")
```
(`<project>` = `dvv_base`, `dvv_registry`, `dvv_platform`, `dvv_gpu`, `dvv_media`,
`dvv_components`, `rawreel`; 10-beta writes `-beta`, 10-final writes `"${PROJECT_VERSION}"`.)
Each dvv package configuration (`cmake/<pkg>Config.cmake.in`) sets
`set(<pkg>_VERSION_STRING "@<pkg>_VERSION_STRING@")`, so an installed package carries it too.
rawreel's `rawreel_dvv_version()` reads `<package>_VERSION_STRING` in place of
`<package>_VERSION` (both of its lookups: the variable of an installed package, and
`get_directory_property(… DEFINITION <package>_VERSION_STRING)` of an in-tree one), and
`RAWREEL_VERSION` is `"${rawreel_VERSION_STRING}"`, so `rawreel-cli --version` and About show
`rawreel 1.0.0-alpha` and every dvv package at `1.0.0-alpha`. A test that compares a version with
`PROJECT_VERSION` or `<pkg>_VERSION` (10-alpha Step 1 finds them) compares with
`<pkg>_VERSION_STRING` instead. The package file names of Task 8 use `PROJECT_VERSION`; Task 8
runs only after 10-final, when both are `1.0.0`.

### Task 10-alpha: Version 1.0.0-alpha, FFmpeg sources and `rawreel.json` in the docs

**Goal:** Set every code repository to version `1.0.0-alpha`; bring the spec and the rawreel
README up to date for the FFmpeg sources with the lossless codec scope of user decision U-FF and
for `rawreel.json` (U-IO2-config); write run instructions for a development build, so the user
can try the alpha. Depends on Tasks 1–9 (and P if it ran). **Not a review stop:** when the
checks pass, the controller gives the user the run instructions and execution goes on to 10-beta
without waiting. Feedback that arrives later is handled in 10-beta.

**Files:**
- `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media`, `dvv-components`, `rawreel`:
  `CMakeLists.txt` (`project(<name> VERSION 1.0.0 …)` and the `<project>_VERSION_STRING` line of
  "Version label" above); `cmake/<pkg>Config.cmake.in` (`find_dependency(<dvv package> 1.0)` for
  every dvv dependency, and the `<pkg>_VERSION_STRING` line); `tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`
  (`find_package(<pkg> 1.0 REQUIRED)`); any other `0.1` / `0.<x>` version request of a dvv package
  found in Step 1; rawreel also `rawreel_dvv_version()`, the `RAWREEL_VERSION` definition and any
  test that compares a version with `PROJECT_VERSION`.
- `rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rawreel-design.md` (the alpha items below).
- `rawreel/README.md` (items 2 and 7 and "Running a development build").

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the code and records of Tasks 0–9 (and P); `decisions.md` (path in the execution
  context; the U-FF, U-FF review, U-IO2 and U-IO2-config lines, and U-M4ORDER); Task 0's
  codec-scope record; Task 5a's allowlist and its error text; Task 5b's `LAVFI_FILE_FILTERS` and
  its two refusal texts; M3.1's `rawreel.json` (`core/config`, `--config`, `io.fileReads`) and
  M3.1's README section "Configuration"; Task 4's search order and `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR` rules; Task
  1's `dvv_ffmpeg_sdk` (`DVV_FFMPEG_RUNTIME_DIR`).
- Produces: version `1.0.0-alpha` everywhere (`rawreel-cli --version` lists `rawreel 1.0.0-alpha`
  and every dvv package at `1.0.0-alpha`); `<project>_VERSION_STRING` in every code repository and
  package configuration; the spec's FFmpeg-source and `rawreel.json` text; README items 2, 7 and
  "Running a development build"; the run instructions in the task report. `dvv-versions.cmake`
  keeps `GIT_TAG main` and `FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1` until Gate B (Task 12 step 9); the workspace
  presets use `FETCHCONTENT_TRY_FIND_PACKAGE_MODE=NEVER`, so the mismatch has no effect before
  then.

**Behaviour:**
- **Versions (D23):** `project(VERSION 1.0.0)` and `<project>_VERSION_STRING`
  `"${PROJECT_VERSION}-alpha"` in the seven code repositories ("Version label" above); package
  configs and package tests ask for `1.0` (the version files keep `COMPATIBILITY
  SameMinorVersion`). The workspace project has no version and keeps none.
- **Spec updates, alpha part** (design §10 "Spec updates (Task 10)"; content, not exact
  replacements; short sentences in the spec's style; find each item's current sentence first):
  - §2 FFmpeg row: six libraries, the search order with final explicit sources (`DVV_FFMPEG_DIR`
    absolute; `<exe dir>/ffmpeg`, `<exe dir>/../lib/ffmpeg`), the checks (versions, licence, no
    `--enable-gpl`/`--enable-nonfree`), `DllDirectory`, `protocol_whitelist=file` for the `ffmpeg`
    provider's own `AVFormatContext`, lavfi graphs with command-line trust except that a filter
    which opens files or URLs (`movie`, `amovie`, …; Task 5b's list) is refused; libvmaf only v1.x;
    U-FF: any container FFmpeg demuxes, lossless video codecs only (the allowlist), lossy codecs
    out of scope for v1.0.
  - §7.1/§7.2: FFmpeg/lavfi behaviour (D5 stream choice, the U-FF codec allowlist checked at open
    (`rawvideo`, `v210`, `v410`, `r210`, `ffv1`, `huffyuv`, `ffvhuff`, `utvideo`, `magicyuv`,
    `png`, `qtrle`; any other codec `Unsupported` "codec <name> is not supported in v1.0 (lossless
    codecs only)"), index, `Sequential` for streams with packets without `pts` or with equal `pts`,
    with the packet-count `frameCount` and the reopen restart, the frame check; D6 graph end),
    the confidence rule incl. the extension rule, the ring capacity, colour keys on `ffmpeg` URIs
    (D28, the Y4M rule). Any sentence that names MPEG-2/`.m2v`, H.264 or another lossy codec as a
    supported input or a test fixture is rewritten (U-FF).
  - §7.3: the `pix_fmt` name mapping and the `yuvj*` aliases.
  - The spec's `rawreel.json` text (U-IO2, as M3.1 left it; find it with
    `Select-String -Path rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rawreel-design.md -Pattern 'rawreel\.json|fileReads'`):
    keep it and add, in one or two sentences, that the zip ships no `rawreel.json` and the
    built-in defaults apply until the user creates one (U-IO2-config), and that FFmpeg container
    sources read through FFmpeg's own file I/O and `lavfi:` graphs read no files, so
    `io.fileReads` applies to the raw and Y4M providers only.
- **rawreel README, alpha part** (new or rewritten sections; the numbers are the order of the
  final README, 10-beta adds items 1, 3–6):
    2. "FFmpeg sources": `file:` containers (any container FFmpeg demuxes, e.g. AVI, MKV, MOV, MP4,
       NUT) with lossless video codecs only (U-FF): the list `rawvideo`, `v210`, `v410`, `r210`,
       `ffv1`, `huffyuv`, `ffvhuff`, `utvideo`, `magicyuv`, `png`, `qtrle`; any other codec, e.g.
       H.264, HEVC, MPEG-1/2/4 or another lossy codec, is refused at open with "codec <name> is
       not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)". Then the stream choice, `Sequential` streams
       (no usable timestamps) with slow backward seeks (R17), `lavfi:` URIs with examples that run
       (`lavfi:testsrc2=s=1280x720:r=25,format=yuv420p`,
       `lavfi:testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25,format=yuv420p10le,setparams=colorspace=bt709:range=pc`), the
       file rule (a graph may not use a filter that opens files or URLs: `movie`, `amovie`,
       `subtitles`, `sendcmd`, `drawtext`, `metadata` and the others of Task 5b's list are refused
       with "lavfi: movie/amovie are not supported; open the file directly" or "lavfi: filter
       <name> opens files or URLs …"; open the file as a `file:` URI instead), colour
       keys on `ffmpeg` URIs (`?matrix=bt601&range=full`; `fmt`/`size`/`fps` only when equal to the
       stream's), View → Format… on a `lavfi:` stream (set the colour in the graph with
       `setparams`), `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR` (absolute, final; an empty value counts as unset), the search
       order, the FFmpeg status line in `rawreel-cli --version` and About.
    7. "Configuration" (U-IO2, U-IO2-config; keep and complete M3.1's section, never drop it): the
       optional `rawreel.json` in the folder of `rawreel-gui.exe`/`rawreel-cli.exe`, found through
       the running executable; the zip ships none, so the built-in defaults apply until the user
       creates one (a missing file is silent; an unreadable or invalid one gives one warning and the
       defaults). The key `io.fileReads` (`auto` (default; unbuffered on Windows with the buffered
       fallback), `unbuffered`, `buffered`) applies to raw and Y4M files; FFmpeg container sources
       read through FFmpeg's own file I/O and `lavfi:` graphs read no files. `--config <file>` on
       both executables reads another file. The example `{ "io": { "fileReads": "auto" } }`. Unzip
       to a folder you can write to if you want to keep a `rawreel.json` there (not under Program
       Files).
    8. "Running a development build" (new; for developers and for trying a pre-release): from the
       workspace root after `bootstrap.ps1 -Toolchain msvc`, build Release
       (`Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; Pop-Location`);
       find the exes with
       `Get-ChildItem -Recurse _build/rawreel/vs2026 -Filter rawreel-gui.exe | Where-Object FullName -Match '\\Release\\'`;
       a build tree has no `ffmpeg/` folder next to the exes, so point rawreel at the prefix's
       FFmpeg (absolute path, found at run time):
       `$env:DVV_FFMPEG_DIR = (Get-ChildItem -Recurse _third_party/msvc/custom_ffmpeg -Filter 'avutil-*.dll' | Select-Object -First 1).DirectoryName`;
       then `rawreel-cli --version` (its FFmpeg line names that folder) and `rawreel-gui`. No
       machine path, user name or version number in the text (the version comes from
       `--version`).
- **Run instructions for the user** (task report, relayed by the controller): the README item 8
  commands, a `lavfi:` example to open with *Open URI…*, and where feedback goes (to the
  controller; 10-beta applies it). No absolute paths: the commands resolve them at run time.

### Task 10-beta: Version 1.0.0-beta, full docs, [U4] texts, licence notes, release-notes draft

**Goal:** Set every code repository to version `1.0.0-beta`; apply the fixes for any feedback on
the alpha; bring the rest of the spec and every README to the v1.0 state (packages, the [U4]
text, the licence notes); write the draft of the hand-made release notes. Depends on 10-alpha.

**Files:**
- `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media`, `dvv-components`, `rawreel`:

       message names the format); the sample aspect ratio is ignored, so anamorphic files show
       stretched in fit; backward seeks on `Sequential` streams restart from the start; below 1:8 the
       fit view still undersamples (M3).

       captured.
    6. "Verifying downloads": `Get-FileHash <file> -Algorithm SHA256` compared with `SHA256SUMS`
       (or `sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS` where coreutils exist), and
       `gh attestation verify <file> --repo BarabashkaD/rawreel`.

      licence text first and add only what is missing.
  - **[U4] accept text** (README "FFmpeg in the package"):
    > The Windows zip includes the FFmpeg libraries avutil, avcodec, avformat, avfilter, swscale
    > and swresample of custom_ffmpeg <cf version> (FFmpeg <ffmpeg version>, LGPL 2.1 or later) in
    > `ffmpeg/`. rawreel loads them at run time and is not linked
    > with them; you may replace them with your own LGPL shared build of FFmpeg 8.x with the same
    > file names, or delete the folder. Their complete source is the release asset
    > `rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources.tar.xz` (custom_ffmpeg with its submodules and build scripts,
    > and LAME 3.100); `ffmpeg/SOURCE.txt` says the same. These libraries contain decoders for
    > codecs such as H.264, HEVC and AAC, which may be covered by patents in some countries;
    > licensing them is a matter for the user and the distributor. rawreel itself opens only
    > lossless video codecs. This is not legal advice.

    U-FF (Task 0's record): with "U4 patent note: dropped" the three sentences from "These
    libraries contain decoders" up to "This is not legal advice." are left out. With "LAME:
    absent" the text reads "(custom_ffmpeg with its submodules and build scripts)". When a
    lossless-only build was pinned, `<cf version>` names its asset's release.
  - **[U4] decline text:**
    > The Windows zip does not include FFmpeg. To open container files and `lavfi:` graphs, put an
    > LGPL shared build of FFmpeg 8.x (avutil, avcodec, avformat, avfilter, swscale and swresample,
    > with the file names of FFmpeg 8: <the six Windows names of Task 0>) into `ffmpeg/` next to
    > `rawreel-gui.exe`, or set `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR` to its absolute path. GPL builds are refused.
    > Whatever the build, rawreel opens only lossless video codecs (see "FFmpeg sources").

### Task 10-final: Version 1.0.0 and the final release notes

**Goal:** Set every code repository to version `1.0.0`, complete the release notes and update the
ledger, so Task 8 packages the final version. Depends on 10-beta.

**Files:**
- `dvv-base`, `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media`, `dvv-components`, `rawreel`:
  the `<project>_VERSION_STRING` line of `CMakeLists.txt` (`"${PROJECT_VERSION}"`, no label).
- `rawreel/docs/release-notes-1.0.0.md` (final text); `rawreel/README.md` only if the [U4] state
  changed since 10-beta.
- `rawreel-workspace/.git/sdd/progress.md` (ledger; not committed).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 10-beta's commits and report (the release-notes draft, the [U4] state used); the [U4]
  state now (`decisions.md`); the code and records of Tasks 0–9 (and P).
- Produces: version 1.0.0 everywhere (`rawreel-cli --version` lists `rawreel 1.0.0` and every dvv
  package at `1.0.0`; `PROJECT_VERSION` and `<project>_VERSION_STRING` equal, as Task 8 Step 1
  checks); the final `docs/release-notes-1.0.0.md`; the ledger with the heads on which Step 7 ran
  green (Task 11 reuses those Debug results for unchanged heads).

**Behaviour:**
- **Versions:** `<project>_VERSION_STRING` `"${PROJECT_VERSION}"` in the seven code repositories
  ("Version label" above). No `-alpha` or `-beta` remains in any repository.
- **Final release notes:** remove the draft line; check every item of 10-beta's list against the
  state now (the [U4] text equal to the README's; the downloads for the variant to publish: three
  assets when bundled, two otherwise, and `SHA256SUMS`; the version `1.0.0` only); short; no
  personal names, no machine paths.
- **Ledger** (`rawreel-workspace/.git/sdd/progress.md`): an M4 section with the commit heads of
  every repository on `m4`, the tasks done (and P if run; 10-alpha, 10-beta and 10-final), the
  decisions taken during execution (U-M4ORDER included), the alpha feedback and its outcome, the
  open items for Tasks 8, 11 and 12.

search of Step 4 lists the `-beta` lines and the draft line; after the edits both pass.
