NVIDIA, FFmpeg tests not skipped; `FORMAT-OK` everywhere; eight clean privacy lints; the final
release notes; the ledger updated.

**Commit:** `build: version 1.0.0` (seven repositories); `docs: finalise the 1.0.0 release notes`
(rawreel).

**Notes:**
- Design references: D23, §10 "Spec updates (Task 10)", R16; U-M4ORDER.
- **Note (U-M4ORDER):** Task 8 runs next and packages this version; its Step 1 stops if a label
  is left.

---

### Task 8: Packaging (both bundle variants, dry run)

**Goal:** Build the v1.0 Windows packages from a Release build: the Windows zip with an app-local
VC++ runtime, the Windows symbols zip and, when FFmpeg is bundled, the third-party sources archive
(the LGPL source offer of the bundled FFmpeg libraries, so it stays with the Windows zip). Prove
them with `package-smoke.cmake` (manifest, shipped notices, privacy scan, CLI and GUI smoke from
the extracted package, the sources archive) in a local dry run on Windows: `ON` with the sources
archive, `OFF` once. Nothing is published. The Linux `.tar.xz`, its FFmpeg bundling (sonames),
its manifest, smoke and dry run are v1.x (U-WIN); the packaging CMake stays portable (it skips
the package rules on other platforms).

**Note (U-DIST1, 2026-10-26):** superseded in part: the v1.0 zip ships no VC++ runtime; the user installs the Microsoft Visual C++ Redistributable (x64) (spec §13.5).

**Place in the order (user decision U-M4ORDER):** Task 8 runs after Task 10-final and before
Task 11. It packages version 1.0.0 (`PROJECT_VERSION` 1.0.0, pre-release label empty) with the
final README, `LICENSE` and release notes of 10-final. Task 9 already wrote `release.yml` against
the script interface of Produces below; this task makes the scripts and is the first to run the
workflow's Windows-job commands (Step 9b) and its checksum lines (Step 9c). It also checks the
package commands that 10-beta wrote into the docs against the dry-run outputs (Step 9d). Its
Release, ASan, clang-tidy and Linux sanity checks run at the Task 11 final checkpoint.

**Files:**
- rawreel:
  - Create `packaging/symbols.cmake`: Release debug information and the PDB link options (MSVC).
  - Create `packaging/packaging.cmake`: install rules (component `rawreel`, Release only), the
    VC++ runtime, the bundled FFmpeg files, `SOURCE.txt`, CPack, and the generated
    `packaging-info-<config>.cmake`; on other platforms a status message and no rules.
  - Create `packaging/SOURCE.txt.in`: the source offer that ships in `ffmpeg/`.
  - Create `packaging/scan-artifact-privacy.cmake`: module with `rawreel_scan_privacy` (D16).
  - Create `packaging/check-package.cmake`: module with `rawreel_check_manifest`,
    `rawreel_check_notices`, `rawreel_check_pdb_reference`, `rawreel_attestation_source`.
  - Create `packaging/make-packages.cmake`: script; runs CPack, names the archives, builds the
    symbols zip and (on request) the sources archive, writes `package-info-<platform>.cmake`.
  - Create `packaging/package-smoke.cmake`: script; §8.6 on the archives in an output folder.
  - Create `packaging/make-third-party-sources.cmake`: script; D27 archive.
  - Create `packaging/verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake`: script; downloads the pinned custom_ffmpeg
    archive, checks its SHA256 and its GitHub attestation, writes the source commit.
  - Create `packaging/write-manifest.cmake`: maintenance script; writes manifest lines from an
    extracted package.
  - Create `packaging/dry-run.cmake`: script; the local rehearsal of `release.yml` (Windows, under
    a `subst` drive).
  - Create `packaging/manifest-windows.txt` (generated once in Step 6, reviewed, committed).
  - Create `tests/packaging/test_packaging_scripts.cmake` and the binary fixture
    `tests/packaging/data/utf16le-user-path.bin`.
  - Modify `CMakeLists.txt` (include `packaging/symbols.cmake` before `FetchContent_MakeAvailable`;
    `rawreel_release_symbols(rawreel_gui)` and `rawreel_release_symbols(rawreel_cli)` after the
    targets; `include(packaging/packaging.cmake)` at the end when `NOT DVV_SANITIZE`) and
    `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (CTest `rawreel.packaging_scripts`).
  - Only if Step 9b or 9d finds a mismatch: `.github/workflows/release.yml` (Task 9),
    `README.md` and `docs/release-notes-1.0.0.md` (10-beta, 10-final); see those steps.
- rawreel-workspace: the record of Step 11 (Task 1 writes the prefix's state file); `README.md`
  only if Step 9d finds its "Releases" paragraph wrong.

**Two sessions (task size):** the controller dispatches this task as two fresh sessions on the same
branch, one after the other. **8a** = Steps 1–4 (the two modules and their 23 cases) and ends with
the commit `build: add the package checks and the artifact privacy scan` (rawreel; here-string to
`git -C rawreel commit -F -` with the trailer; privacy lint) and a short report. **8b** = Steps 1
(re-read, including 8a's commit) and 5–11 (Steps 9b–9d included); Step 8's commit then holds the
rest. The full checks of
Step 10 (the Per-task checks) run once, at the end of 8b.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - **Task 0** (records in the M4 plan file's `## Changes during execution`; find the file with
    `Get-ChildItem rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans -Filter '*m4*'`): the custom_ffmpeg
    archive layout (where `bin/`, `lib/`, `share/licenses/` and `build-info.txt` sit; Step 1 finds
    `include/libavutil/ffversion.h` in the prefix itself), the six library file names and majors (review of the v1
    design read Windows `avutil-60.dll avcodec-62.dll avformat-62.dll avfilter-11.dll swscale-9.dll
    swresample-6.dll`), `dumpbin /dependents`
    (expected `MSVCP140`, `VCRUNTIME140`, `VCRUNTIME140_1`), the DLLs' linker version (the minimum
    VC++ runtime file version below), whether the local MSVC prefix's wx libraries contain
    user-home paths (→ `OWN_PREFIX` of the dry run), the custom_ffmpeg attestation result (commit
    and `sourceRepositoryRef` form) and the [U4] answer if given.
  - **Task 1:** the superbuild cache option `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` (default `ON`) that sets
    custom_ffmpeg's `SHIPPED`; `<prefix>/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt` with the `SHIPPED` entries only
    plus the MSVC runtime paragraph (entry header format of `deps/write-notices.cmake`: a line of
    `=` characters, then `<name> <version>`, `Licence: …`, `Source: …`, then `--- <file> ---`
    licence blocks; on Windows the paragraph after the last entry: a separator line, then exactly
    `Microsoft Visual C++ runtime`, `Files: …`, `Licence: …`, `Source: …`); custom_ffmpeg
    installed as `PREBUILT` under `<prefix>/custom_ffmpeg/`; the LAME 3.100 source pin (URL +
    SHA256).
  - **Task 1's state file** `<prefix>/dvv-third-party-state.cmake` (produced by Task 1, read here
    with `include()`), with exactly these variables:
    ```cmake
    set(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG ON)                       # the superbuild option's value
    set(DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY "Vulkan-Headers;VulkanMemoryAllocator;custom_ffmpeg;nlohmann_json;vk-bootstrap;wxWidgets")
    set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION "1.0.0")
    set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_URL "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/custom_ffmpeg/releases/download/v1.0.0/<platform asset>")
    set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_SHA256 "<64 hex>")
    set(DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/custom_ffmpeg")
    set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL "<URL of lame-3.100.tar.gz>")
    set(DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256 "<64 hex>")
    ```
    (`DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY` is the platform's `SHIPPED` set, sorted; `custom_ffmpeg` only when
    bundled.)
  - **Task 4:** the FFmpeg default search `<exe dir>/ffmpeg` (the Windows zip's folder; the
    `<exe dir>/../lib/ffmpeg` entry serves the v1.x Linux package); `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR` is absolute
    and final; an existing empty directory makes FFmpeg "not available".
  - **Task 7a:** `rawreel-cli --version` prints `rawreel <version>` as its first line, one FFmpeg
    status line (`FFmpeg <ffmpeg version> (LGPL version 2.1 or later) from <dir>` or
    `FFmpeg: not available (<reason>)`) and, without a Vulkan loader or device,
    `Vulkan: not available (<reason>)` with exit 0; `info <uri> --json` and `dump` with the M2
    options (`--frame`, `--count`, `--view original|planes`, `--via cpu|gpu|auto`, `--out`).
  - **Task 7b:** `rawreel-gui --selftest smoke` opens `lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p`
    through the Open URI hook when `ffmpeg.lavfi` is available (required under
    `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1`) and opens and closes About; exit codes 0/1/4/6/77 (M1b).
  - **M1b–M3:** targets `rawreel_gui` (`OUTPUT_NAME rawreel-gui`, not built under `DVV_SANITIZE`)
    and `rawreel_cli` (`OUTPUT_NAME rawreel-cli`); the presets `vs2026`, `vs2026-release`
    (binary dir `_build/rawreel/vs2026`) and `ninja-clang` (the Task 11 checkpoint build);
    `README.md` and `LICENSE` in the rawreel root.
  - **Task 9:** `rawreel/.github/workflows/release.yml`, whose `windows` job calls
    `verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake`, `make-packages.cmake` and `package-smoke.cmake` with the
    parameters of Produces below, and whose `publish` job writes `SHA256SUMS` with
    `sha256sum rawreel-*`. Task 9 did not run these commands; Steps 9b and 9c do.
  - **Tasks 10-alpha to 10-final:** version 1.0.0 in every code repository (`project(… VERSION
    1.0.0 …)`, `rawreel_VERSION_STRING` `1.0.0`, so `rawreel-cli --version` prints
    `rawreel 1.0.0`); the final `README.md` (sections "Packages", "FFmpeg in the package",
    "Bug reports", "Verifying downloads", "Configuration"), `docs/release-notes-1.0.0.md` and the
    rawreel-workspace README "Releases" paragraph, written from this task's planned interface.
- Produces (Task 9's `release.yml`, written before this task against this interface, and Tasks
  11 and 12 call these exactly):
  - **File names** (`<v>` = `PROJECT_VERSION`): `rawreel-<v>-windows-x64.zip`,
    `rawreel-<v>-windows-x64-symbols.zip` (`rawreel-gui.pdb`, `rawreel-cli.pdb`),
    `rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources.tar.xz` (bundled only). Each archive has one top-level folder:
    `rawreel-<v>/` (the zip), `rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources/` (sources); the symbols zip has the
    two PDBs at its root.
  - **Package layout** (D17): `rawreel-<v>/{rawreel-gui.exe, rawreel-cli.exe, the VC++ runtime
    DLLs, README.md, LICENSE, THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt, ffmpeg/}`. When bundled, `ffmpeg/` holds
    exactly the six libraries, `licenses/` (the archive's `share/licenses/` tree),
    `build-info.txt` and `SOURCE.txt`. No `ffmpeg/` folder when not bundled. (D18's Linux layout
    is v1.x, U-WIN.) No `rawreel.json` (controller decision on U-IO2-config): the zip ships no
    config file, and M3.1's built-in defaults apply when it is missing (`io.fileReads` `auto`). A
    user creates `rawreel.json` next to the exes to override them; the manifest has no line for it.
    Note (U-DIST1, 2026-10-26): the layout has no VC++ runtime DLLs (Microsoft Visual C++ Redistributable (x64) installed by the user).
  - **Scripts** (all `cmake -P`; every failure is `message(FATAL_ERROR "<script>: <check>: …")`):
    ```
    cmake -DBUILD_DIR=<rawreel build dir> [-DCONFIG=Release] -DOUT_DIR=<dir> [-DWORK_DIR=<dir>]
          [-DSOURCES=ON -DFFMPEG_COMMIT_FILE=<file>] -P packaging/make-packages.cmake
    cmake -DOUT_DIR=<dir> -DPLATFORM=windows -DWORK_DIR=<dir> [-DEXPECTED_VERSION=<v>]
          [-DGUI_SMOKE=off|windows] [-DALLOW_GUI_SKIP=ON] [-DREQUIRE_SOURCES=ON]
          [-DEXTRA_TERMS=<a;b>] -P packaging/package-smoke.cmake
    cmake -DPREFIX=<third-party prefix> -DWORK_DIR=<dir> [-DOUT_FILE=<file>]
          -P packaging/verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake
    cmake -DSTATE=<prefix>/dvv-third-party-state.cmake -DCOMMIT=<40 hex> -DVERSION=<v>
          -DOUT_DIR=<dir> -DWORK_DIR=<dir> -P packaging/make-third-party-sources.cmake
    cmake -DROOT=<extracted package dir> -DVERSION=<v> -DOUT=<file> -P packaging/write-manifest.cmake
    cmake -DWORKSPACE_ROOT=<workspace root> [-DBUNDLE=ON|OFF] [-DOWN_PREFIX=ON|OFF] [-DSOURCES=ON]
          [-DDRIVE=<letter>:] [-DGUI_SMOKE=…] [-DEXTRA_TERMS=<a;b>]
          -P rawreel/packaging/dry-run.cmake
    ```
  - `make-packages.cmake` writes `<OUT_DIR>/package-info-windows.cmake` (no absolute paths; the
    file name keeps the `<platform>` part for v1.x):
    `RAWREEL_VERSION`, `RAWREEL_PLATFORM`, `RAWREEL_PACKAGE` (file name), `RAWREEL_BUNDLED`,
    `RAWREEL_SHIPPED`, `RAWREEL_FFMPEG_VERSION` (e.g. `n8.1.3`), `RAWREEL_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION`,
    `RAWREEL_MIN_VCRT_VERSION`, `RAWREEL_SYMBOLS` (file name or empty), `RAWREEL_SOURCES` (file
    name or empty). `package-smoke.cmake` reads it.
  - `verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake` writes `<WORK_DIR>/custom_ffmpeg-commit.txt` (default `OUT_FILE`)
    containing the 40-hex `sourceRepositoryDigest` of the attestation.
  - Module functions (`include(packaging/check-package.cmake)`,
    `include(packaging/scan-artifact-privacy.cmake)`):
    ```cmake
    # Every file under ROOT matches exactly one applicable manifest line and every applicable line
    # exactly one file. ERRORS receives one message per problem (empty list = pass); CLASSES
    # receives "<kind>|<relative path>" for every file (for the later checks).
    rawreel_check_manifest(ROOT <dir> MANIFEST <file> VERSION <v> BUNDLED <bool>
                           ERRORS <var> [CLASSES <var>])
    # The notice entries (names) equal SHIPPED exactly; with MSVC_RUNTIME (Windows) the Visual C++
    # runtime paragraph must be present, without it it must be absent.
    rawreel_check_notices(FILE <notices file> SHIPPED <name>... [MSVC_RUNTIME] ERRORS <var>)
    # Every string ending in ".pdb" in the exe is a bare file name (no '/', '\' or ':').
    rawreel_check_pdb_reference(FILE <exe> ERRORS <var>)
    # From `gh attestation verify --format json` output: element 0's
    # verificationResult.signature.certificate.sourceRepositoryDigest and .sourceRepositoryRef.
    rawreel_attestation_source(JSON <text> COMMIT <var> REF <var> ERRORS <var>)
    # MODE full: user-profile paths, /home/<name> other than runner, non-noreply e-mail addresses,
    # IPv4 addresses, EXTRA_TERMS. MODE paths: the two path rules only. MODE notices: full on the
    # lines outside licence blocks, paths on every line. BINARY: also UTF-16LE strings.
    # FINDINGS receives "<file name>: <kind>" items, never the matched text.
    rawreel_scan_privacy(FILE <file> MODE full|paths|notices [BINARY] [EXTRA_TERMS <term>...]
                         FINDINGS <var>)
    ```
  - CTest `rawreel.packaging_scripts` (label `unit`), every preset.

**Behaviour:**
- **Debug information (D16).** `packaging/symbols.cmake` is included before
  `FetchContent_MakeAvailable`, so every target of the build (the dvv libraries included) compiles
  with `/Zi`-style program databases in Release too; `rawreel_release_symbols(<exe>)` links
  Release with `/DEBUG /PDBALTPATH:%_PDB% /OPT:REF /OPT:ICF` (the exe names its PDB by file name
  only; `/OPT:*` restores what `/DEBUG` turns off). Not under `DVV_SANITIZE`; nothing changes on
  Linux.
- **Other platforms (U-WIN):** `packaging.cmake` checks `WIN32` right after the state-file check;
  elsewhere it prints `rawreel: packages are built on Windows only in v1.0 (the Linux package is
  v1.x)` and returns, so a Linux configure stays clean and adds no install rule, CPack file or
  `packaging-info` file. `make-packages.cmake` needs a Windows build tree (only there is a
  `packaging-info` file; otherwise its missing-file message applies); `package-smoke.cmake`
  accepts only `PLATFORM=windows` (else `package-smoke: PLATFORM: only windows in v1.0 (the Linux
  package is v1.x)`); `dry-run.cmake` stops on a non-Windows host with `dry-run: Windows only in
  v1.0 (the Linux package is v1.x)`.
- **Install rules and CPack** (Code below): component `rawreel`, `CONFIGURATIONS Release`;
  CPack installs only that component (`CPACK_INSTALL_CMAKE_PROJECTS`), so the install rules of the
  dvv subprojects never reach a package. `CPACK_PACKAGE_FILE_NAME` is `rawreel-<v>`, which is also
  the archive's top-level folder; `make-packages.cmake` renames the file to the platform name.
  **Note (plan choice):** the rename keeps the `rawreel-<v>/` top folder with the
  `…-windows-x64` file name without relying on `CPACK_ARCHIVE_FILE_NAME` for a non-component
  package.
- **The bundled FFmpeg files** come from the prefix: the avutil library is found by a recursive
  glob under `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_DIR` (exactly one match: `avutil-<digits>.dll`); its folder is the
  library folder, that folder's parent the FFmpeg root (`share/licenses/`, `build-info.txt`,
  `include/libavutil/ffversion.h`). Each of the six libraries must match exactly once
  (`<name>-<digits>.dll`), else configuration fails naming it. avdevice, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe` and
  the headers are never installed. (Linux bundling with soname files is v1.x, U-WIN.)
- **`SOURCE.txt`** (configured, `@ONLY`, LF) names the libraries, the custom_ffmpeg and FFmpeg
  versions, the LGPL, the replaceability rule and the sources archive of the same release:
  ```text
  FFmpeg libraries in this folder

  avutil, avcodec, avformat, avfilter, swscale and swresample are the unchanged "lgpl" shared
  libraries of custom_ffmpeg @CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION@ (FFmpeg @FFMPEG_VERSION@). They are licensed
  under the GNU Lesser General Public License, version 2.1 or later; the licence texts are in the
  licenses folder, and build-info.txt shows how they were configured.

  rawreel loads these libraries at run time and is not linked with them. You may replace them with
  your own LGPL shared build of FFmpeg 8.x that uses the same file names, or delete this folder
  (rawreel then opens no container files and no lavfi: graphs). GPL builds are refused.

  The complete corresponding source code is in @RAWREEL_SOURCES_ARCHIVE@, attached to the same
  release as this package:
  https://github.com/BarabashkaD/rawreel/releases/tag/v@RAWREEL_VERSION@
  It holds custom_ffmpeg @CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION@ with its submodules and build scripts (the configure
  line included) and the LAME 3.100 source release.
  ```
  (`RAWREEL_SOURCES_ARCHIVE` = `rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources.tar.xz`, set before
  `configure_file`. A template must not put a closing `@` of a variable directly before a
  hyphenated file name with a dot: the privacy lint reads the pair as an e-mail address.)
- **`make-packages.cmake`:** reads `<BUILD_DIR>/packaging-info-<CONFIG>.cmake` (missing → "build
  <CONFIG> of a non-sanitizer configuration whose prefix has dvv-third-party-state.cmake first");
  `WORK_DIR` defaults to `<OUT_DIR>/_work` and is emptied; removes earlier outputs of the same
  names; runs `cpack --config <build>/rawreel-CPackConfig.cmake -C <CONFIG> -B <WORK_DIR>/cpack`
  (cpack is found next to `CMAKE_COMMAND`); moves `rawreel-<v>.zip` to
  `<OUT_DIR>/<package name>.zip`; copies the two PDBs (`RAWREEL_PDBS`) into
  `<WORK_DIR>/symbols` and runs `cmake -E tar cf <OUT_DIR>/rawreel-<v>-windows-x64-symbols.zip
  --format=zip rawreel-gui.pdb rawreel-cli.pdb` there; with `SOURCES=ON` and bundled, runs
  `make-third-party-sources.cmake` with the commit read from `FFMPEG_COMMIT_FILE` (required then);
  with `SOURCES=ON` and not bundled it prints "FFmpeg is not bundled: no sources archive"; writes
  `package-info-windows.cmake`; prints one line per output: name, bytes, SHA256.
- **`verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake`** (D20): includes the state file; downloads
  `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_URL` with `file(DOWNLOAD … EXPECTED_HASH SHA256=<pin> TLS_VERIFY ON)` into
  `WORK_DIR`; takes `<owner>/<repo>` from the URL (`https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>/releases/…`);
  runs `gh attestation verify <file> --repo <owner>/<repo> --format json` (`gh` required; a non-zero
  exit → "the attestation of <file> could not be verified"); `rawreel_attestation_source` gives the
  commit and the ref; the ref must equal `refs/tags/v<DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION>` (or the form Task 0
  recorded); writes the commit to `OUT_FILE`.
- **`make-third-party-sources.cmake`** (D27; U-WIN: runs on Windows, in the Windows dry run and
  the Windows job of `release.yml`): clone URL = `https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>.git`
  from `DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_URL`; `git -c core.autocrlf=false -c core.longpaths=true clone --branch
  v<cf version> --depth 1 --recurse-submodules --shallow-submodules <url>
  <stage>/custom_ffmpeg-<cf version>` (`-c core.longpaths=true`: deep submodule paths on Windows);
  `git rev-parse HEAD` must equal `COMMIT` ("the tag v<…> is <x>, the attestation names <y>");
  the output of `git submodule status --recursive` is kept for the README; the symbolic links of
  the tree (`git ls-files -s --recurse-submodules` lines with mode `120000`), which a Windows
  checkout writes as small text files, are listed for the README (`none` when there are none);
  then every `.git` file and folder below the
  clone is removed (`file(GLOB_RECURSE … LIST_DIRECTORIES true "<clone>/.git")` plus the clone's own
  `.git`); LAME: `file(DOWNLOAD <DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL> <stage>/<file name of the URL> EXPECTED_HASH
  SHA256=<DVV_LAME_SOURCE_SHA256>)`; `<stage>/README.txt`:
  ```text
  Third-party sources for rawreel @VERSION@

  The complete corresponding source of the FFmpeg libraries in the rawreel @VERSION@ package
  (folder ffmpeg/ in the Windows zip):
  - custom_ffmpeg-@CF_VERSION@/: custom_ffmpeg @CF_VERSION@, tag v@CF_VERSION@, commit @COMMIT@, with
    every submodule and the build scripts (the configure line included). Submodules:
  @SUBMODULES@
    Symbolic links of the tree, stored here as text files that hold the link target:
  @SYMLINKS@
  - @LAME_FILE@: the LAME 3.100 source release that custom_ffmpeg builds (SHA256 @LAME_SHA256@).
  Upstream: @REPO_URL@
  ```
  (stage = `<WORK_DIR>/rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources`); then `cmake -E tar cJf
  <OUT_DIR>/rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources.tar.xz rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources` in `WORK_DIR`.
  **Note (plan choice):** the clone is shallow (`--depth 1 --shallow-submodules`) and without
  `.git` data: the archive must hold the source and build scripts, not the history.
- **`package-smoke.cmake`** (§8.6), in this order, each step printing `-- package-smoke: <step>: ok`:
  1. `PLATFORM` must be `windows` (U-WIN; message above). Reads
     `<OUT_DIR>/package-info-windows.cmake`; with `EXPECTED_VERSION`, `RAWREEL_VERSION` must equal
     it.
  2. Extracts the package into `<WORK_DIR>/extract` (emptied first, `cmake -E tar xf`); the
     archive must hold exactly one top-level entry, `rawreel-<v>`.
  3. **Manifest:** `rawreel_check_manifest` with `manifest-windows.txt` next to the script.
  4. **Shipped notices:** `rawreel_check_notices` on the `notices`-kind file with
     `RAWREEL_SHIPPED` and `MSVC_RUNTIME`.
     Note (U-DIST1, 2026-10-26): superseded: without `MSVC_RUNTIME` (no Visual C++ runtime paragraph; the zip holds no runtime DLL).
  5. **VC++ runtime:** every `vcrt` file named `vcruntime140*.dll` or `msvcp140*.dll` has a
     file version `VERSION_GREATER_EQUAL RAWREEL_MIN_VCRT_VERSION` (read with
     `pwsh -NoProfile -Command "(Get-Item -LiteralPath '<file>').VersionInfo.FileVersionRaw.ToString()"`;
     `powershell` when `pwsh` is missing).
     Note (U-DIST1, 2026-10-26): superseded: the zip holds no `vcrt` file, and this step checks that no VC runtime DLL is in it.
  6. **Privacy scan (D16):** `ours` and `ours-bundled` files: `MODE full` (exes `BINARY`), with
     `EXTRA_TERMS`; the `notices` file: `MODE notices`; `rawreel_check_pdb_reference` on both
     exes. `ffmpeg` files are covered by their SHA256 (step 3), `vcrt` files by step 5. Any
     finding fails and lists `<file>: <kind>` only.
  7. **CLI** (every run through `cmake -E env --unset=DVV_FFMPEG_DIR --unset=DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG
     DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail …`, timeout 120 s): `rawreel-cli --version` exits 0, has the line
     `rawreel <v>`, and one line starting with `FFmpeg`. Bundled: that line matches
     `^FFmpeg <ffmpeg version> \(LGPL.*\) from (.+)$` and the directory, normalised
     (`\` → `/`, `cmake_path(NORMAL_PATH)`, compared case-insensitively), equals
     `<extract>/rawreel-<v>/ffmpeg`; then
     `info "lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p" --json` exits 0 with `width` 64 and `height`
     48 (`string(JSON)`); `dump "lavfi:testsrc2=s=64x48:r=25,format=yuv420p" --frame 2 --via cpu
     --out <WORK_DIR>/frame2.yuv` exits 0 and writes 4608 bytes; with
     `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR=<WORK_DIR>/empty-ffmpeg` (an existing empty folder, absolute) `--version` has
     a line starting with `FFmpeg: not available` although the package's FFmpeg folder exists. Not
     bundled: the line starts with `FFmpeg: not available`.
  7b. **Config defaults** (U-IO2-config; both variants): `<extract>/rawreel-<v>/rawreel.json` must
     not exist (`rawreel.json must not be shipped`). The script writes a 4608-byte file
     `<WORK_DIR>/config-check.yuv` (`string(REPEAT "A" 4608 …)`, `file(WRITE …)`; any bytes are a
     valid 64×48 `yuv420p` frame) and runs `info "file:<WORK_DIR>/config-check.yuv?fmt=yuv420p&size=64x48" --json`
     with the step 7 environment and no `--config`: exit 0, `frames` 1, the JSON `reader` value is
     not empty (M3.1's read mode, `unbuffered` or a `buffered` text), and stderr has no line
     containing `config` (a missing default `rawreel.json` stays silent, M3.1 Task B3). The CLI and
     the GUI step 8 therefore run with the built-in defaults.
  8. **GUI** (`GUI_SMOKE`): `windows` runs `<extract>/rawreel-<v>/rawreel-gui.exe --selftest
     smoke`; `off` (default) skips (the `xvfb` and `display` modes of the Linux package are v1.x).
     Environment: `DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail`, `DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1` unless
     `ALLOW_GUI_SKIP`, `DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` when bundled, and `DVV_VK_VALIDATION`,
     `DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED`, `DVV_FFMPEG_DIR` unset (no validation layer in a package);
     timeout 300 s. Exit 0 passes; 77 passes only with `ALLOW_GUI_SKIP=ON` (GPU-less runners) and is
     reported as "GUI smoke skipped (no GPU)".
  9. **Symbols:** `RAWREEL_SYMBOLS` exists, extracts to exactly `rawreel-gui.pdb` and
     `rawreel-cli.pdb`, and both pass `rawreel_scan_privacy(MODE paths BINARY)`.
  10. **Sources** (when bundled and `REQUIRE_SOURCES=ON`): `RAWREEL_SOURCES` exists; `cmake -E tar
      tf` lists `rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources/README.txt`, an entry below
      `rawreel-<v>-third-party-sources/custom_ffmpeg-<cf version>/` and one ending in
      `lame-3.100.tar.gz` (the LAME entry only when the state file has `DVV_LAME_SOURCE_URL`, i.e.
      Task 0 recorded "LAME: present"; with "LAME: absent" the archive has no LAME entry, and the
      sources script skips its LAME download and README line).
- **Manifest format** (`packaging/manifest-windows.txt`, `#` comments; the `-<platform>` name is
  kept for v1.x):
  `<kind> <path> [<sha256>]`, path relative to the archive root, `@V@` = the version. Kinds:
  `ours` (built here; must exist), `ours-bundled` (`SOURCE.txt`; only when bundled), `notices`
  (`THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt`), `vcrt` (app-local VC++ runtime), `ffmpeg` (a bundled
  third-party file with its SHA256 from the prefix; only when bundled). Lines not applicable to the
  variant are ignored, so their files count as unexpected. Problems are reported as `missing file
  <path>`, `unexpected file <path>`, `SHA256 mismatch <path>`.
- **`write-manifest.cmake`** classifies an extracted ON-variant package: `…/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt`
  → `notices`; `…/SOURCE.txt` inside the FFmpeg folder → `ours-bundled`; any other file inside
  `ffmpeg/` → `ffmpeg <sha256>`; a file name matching
  `^(vcruntime|msvcp|concrt|vccorlib)140.*\.dll$` → `vcrt`; everything else → `ours`. It writes the
  lines sorted, with the version folder replaced by `@V@`, under the format comment above.
  Note (U-DIST1, 2026-10-26): the v1.0 manifest has no `vcrt` line; such a file in the zip is an error.
- **Notices check:** a separator is a line made only of `=` (at least 20). An entry is a separator
  whose next line is `<name> <version>` and whose line after that starts with `Licence:`; its name
  is the first word. The Visual C++ runtime paragraph is a separator followed by exactly the line
  `Microsoft Visual C++ runtime` (its next line is `Files: …`, so it is never an entry). Any other
  separator (for example a long `=` underline inside a licence text) is ignored. The set of entry
  names must equal `SHIPPED` (`missing notice <name>`, `unexpected notice <name>`); with
  `MSVC_RUNTIME` the paragraph must be present (`missing notice Microsoft Visual C++ runtime`),
  without it absent (`unexpected notice Microsoft Visual C++ runtime`). Step 1 confirms in
  `rawreel-workspace/deps/write-notices.cmake` that the line after an entry's name starts with
  `Licence:`; if it has another prefix, the rule and cases 7–9, 22 and 23 use that prefix.
- **Privacy rules** (D16; copied from `rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`, narrowed):
  - user-profile path: `[A-Za-z]:[\/]+[Uu][Ss][Ee][Rr][Ss][\/]+[A-Za-z0-9._-]`;
  - home path: `/home/<name>` with `<name>` ≠ `runner`;
  - e-mail: `[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z]+` unless it ends with
    `@users.noreply.github.com` or `@example.(com|org|net)` or equals `noreply@anthropic.com` or
    `noreply@github.com`;
  - IPv4: a maximal run of digits and dots that is exactly four parts, each ≤ 255 and ≤ 3 digits,
    other than `127.0.0.1` and `0.0.0.0`; an exact-string allowlist (empty at first) may name a
    version-like string of a statically linked library, each entry with a comment naming its origin
    (an arbiter decision);
  - `EXTRA_TERMS`: terms of 3 or more characters, case-insensitive substring (local runs pass the
    user name and the computer name; CI passes none).
  - Absolute drive paths outside user profiles (the runners' generic build roots, the `subst`
    drive locally) and the runner's own home folder are accepted (D16: runners build in generic
    roots).
  - For speed, candidate strings are pre-filtered with `file(STRINGS <file> <var> REGEX <one
    alternation of the rule patterns>)` (`ENCODING UTF-8` for text; for `BINARY` once with the
    default encoding and once with `ENCODING UTF-16LE`); only the hits are checked rule by rule in
    CMake. Text-file line numbers are not reported.
  - `MODE notices`: a licence block runs from a line `--- <path> ---` to the next separator that
    starts an entry or the Visual C++ runtime paragraph (the notices check's rule; a long `=` line
    inside a licence text does not end it) or the end of the file; the full rules apply to every line outside licence blocks,
    the path rules to every line.
- **`dry-run.cmake`** (D16 local dry run, design FIX 15; Windows only, U-WIN: on another host it
  stops with the message of "Other platforms"):
  - Outer part: picks `DRIVE` (default: the first of `R: S: T: U: V: W: Y: Z:` that is not
    a directory), runs `subst <DRIVE> <WORKSPACE_ROOT>`, runs itself again as a child
    (`-DINNER=ON -DWORKSPACE_ROOT=<DRIVE>/` and the other arguments), then always runs `subst <DRIVE>
    /D`, and fails with the child's result.
  - Inner part, `N` = the neutral root `<DRIVE>/`: the dry-run area is `<N>/_dryrun/`; toolchain
    `msvc`.
  - Prefix: the developer prefix `<WORKSPACE_ROOT>/_third_party/msvc` when `OWN_PREFIX=OFF`
    and its state's `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` equals `BUNDLE`; otherwise a prefix of its own,
    `<N>/_dryrun/third_party/msvc-<on|off>`, built by the superbuild exactly as `bootstrap`
    does (`-G "Visual Studio 18 2026" -A x64`) into `<N>/_dryrun/build/third-party-msvc-<on|off>`
    with `-DDVV_THIRD_PARTY_PREFIX=<that prefix> -DDVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG=<BUNDLE>` and `cmake --build …
    --config Release --parallel`. `OWN_PREFIX` defaults to `ON` exactly when Task 0 recorded
    user-home paths in the MSVC prefix's wx libraries.
  - rawreel: `cmake --preset vs2026 -B <N>/_dryrun/build/rawreel-msvc-<on|off>
    -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH=<prefix>` in `<WORKSPACE_ROOT>/rawreel`;
    `cmake --build <b> --config Release --parallel`; `cmake -E env DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail
    DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1 ctest --test-dir <b> -C Release -LE "gpu|display|gui|oracle"
    --output-on-failure` (the unit and CLI tests of `release.yml`).
  - With `SOURCES=ON`: `verify-ffmpeg-archive.cmake` (`WORK_DIR` `<N>/_dryrun/ffmpeg-check`), then
    `make-packages.cmake -DSOURCES=ON -DFFMPEG_COMMIT_FILE=…`; else `make-packages.cmake` alone; out
    dir `<N>/_dryrun/out/msvc-<on|off>`.
  - `package-smoke.cmake` with `PLATFORM=windows`, `GUI_SMOKE`, `EXTRA_TERMS`,
    `REQUIRE_SOURCES=<SOURCES>`, `WORK_DIR <N>/_dryrun/smoke/msvc-<on|off>`.
  - Prints the output file names, sizes and SHA256 at the end.
  - **Note (plan choice):** `cmake --preset <p> -B <dir>` overrides the preset's `binaryDir`; the
    separate build tree is needed because the developer tree's cache names the real path, not the
    `subst` drive. If CMake refuses the override, configure the same tree without `--preset`
    (generator, `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH`, `FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_*` and
    `FETCHCONTENT_TRY_FIND_PACKAGE_MODE=NEVER` as in `cmake/DvvPresets.json`) and record it.
  - **Note (plan choice):** while the developer prefix says `ON` (its notices list custom_ffmpeg),
    the `OFF` variant builds a prefix of its own; after a [U4] decline the developer prefix says
    `OFF` and the Prefix rule above governs unchanged. It runs once, as the design asks.
  - **Note (U-WIN):** the Linux inner part of plan v2 (`NEUTRAL_ROOT`, `-ffile-prefix-map`, the
    clang prefix) is v1.x; the `ON` dry run now also builds the sources archive (`SOURCES=ON`),
    which plan v2 built in WSL.

**Code** (binding: the tricky CMake parts).

`packaging/symbols.cmake` (complete):

```cmake
# Release symbols for the Windows minidumps (design M4 v2 D16, D22). Included by CMakeLists.txt
# before FetchContent_MakeAvailable, so every target of this build (the dvv libraries included)
# gets a program database in Release too. The exes embed only the PDB file name
# (/PDBALTPATH:%_PDB%, a documented linker switch); /OPT:REF and /OPT:ICF keep the Release
# optimisations that /DEBUG would otherwise turn off. Nothing changes under DVV_SANITIZE or on Linux.
include_guard(GLOBAL)

if(MSVC AND NOT DVV_SANITIZE)
    set(CMAKE_MSVC_DEBUG_INFORMATION_FORMAT
        "$<$<CONFIG:Debug,RelWithDebInfo,Release>:ProgramDatabase>")
endif()

function(rawreel_release_symbols target)
    if(MSVC AND NOT DVV_SANITIZE)
        target_link_options(${target} PRIVATE
            "$<$<CONFIG:Release>:/DEBUG;/PDBALTPATH:%_PDB%;/OPT:REF;/OPT:ICF>")
    endif()
endfunction()
```

`packaging/packaging.cmake` (complete except the Task 0 value):

```cmake
# Packages of rawreel v1.0 (design M4 v2 D16-D19, D27, §8.6): install rules for the component
# "rawreel" (Release only), the app-local VC++ runtime on Windows, the bundled FFmpeg libraries when
# the third-party prefix ships them, and CPack. make-packages.cmake drives it. Included by
# CMakeLists.txt only without DVV_SANITIZE (rawreel-gui is not built there).
include_guard(GLOBAL)

# Lowest file version of vcruntime140.dll/msvcp140.dll the custom_ffmpeg DLLs accept: the toolset
# that linked them (Task 0 record, "major.minor").
set(RAWREEL_MIN_VCRT_VERSION "<Task 0>")

find_file(RAWREEL_THIRD_PARTY_STATE dvv-third-party-state.cmake
          PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} NO_DEFAULT_PATH NO_CACHE)
if(NOT RAWREEL_THIRD_PARTY_STATE)
    message(STATUS "rawreel: the third-party prefix has no dvv-third-party-state.cmake; "
                   "no package rules")
    return()
endif()
include("${RAWREEL_THIRD_PARTY_STATE}")
cmake_path(GET RAWREEL_THIRD_PARTY_STATE PARENT_PATH rawreel_prefix)

# v1.0 ships a Windows zip only (user decision U-WIN); the Linux .tar.xz is v1.x. Other platforms
# configure without package rules.
if(NOT WIN32)
    message(STATUS "rawreel: packages are built on Windows only in v1.0 (the Linux package is v1.x)")
    return()
endif()

set(rawreel_platform windows)
set(rawreel_package_name "rawreel-${PROJECT_VERSION}-windows-x64")
set(rawreel_extension zip)
set(rawreel_ffmpeg_dir "ffmpeg")

install(TARGETS rawreel_gui rawreel_cli
        RUNTIME DESTINATION . COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)
install(FILES README.md LICENSE "${rawreel_prefix}/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt"
        DESTINATION . COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)

# D17: the VC++ runtime next to the exes (the UCRT comes with Windows 10 and later). The module
# only collects the files; the rule below gives them the component and the configuration.
set(CMAKE_INSTALL_SYSTEM_RUNTIME_LIBS_SKIP ON)
set(CMAKE_INSTALL_UCRT_LIBRARIES OFF)
include(InstallRequiredSystemLibraries)
install(PROGRAMS ${CMAKE_INSTALL_SYSTEM_RUNTIME_LIBS}
        DESTINATION . COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)

set(rawreel_ffmpeg_version "")
if(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE rawreel_avutil LIST_DIRECTORIES false "${DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_DIR}/avutil-*.dll")
    list(FILTER rawreel_avutil INCLUDE REGEX "/avutil-[0-9]+\\.dll$")
    list(LENGTH rawreel_avutil rawreel_count)
    if(NOT rawreel_count EQUAL 1)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "rawreel packaging: expected one avutil library below "
                            "${DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_DIR}, found ${rawreel_count}")
    endif()
    cmake_path(GET rawreel_avutil PARENT_PATH rawreel_ffmpeg_libdir)
    cmake_path(GET rawreel_ffmpeg_libdir PARENT_PATH rawreel_ffmpeg_root)
    foreach(library IN ITEMS avutil avcodec avformat avfilter swscale swresample)
        file(GLOB found LIST_DIRECTORIES false "${rawreel_ffmpeg_libdir}/${library}-*.dll")
        list(FILTER found INCLUDE REGEX "/${library}-[0-9]+\\.dll$")
        list(LENGTH found rawreel_count)
        if(NOT rawreel_count EQUAL 1)
            message(FATAL_ERROR "rawreel packaging: expected one ${library} library in "
                                "${rawreel_ffmpeg_libdir}, found ${rawreel_count}")
        endif()
        install(FILES "${found}" DESTINATION "${rawreel_ffmpeg_dir}"
                COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)
    endforeach()
    install(DIRECTORY "${rawreel_ffmpeg_root}/share/licenses/"
            DESTINATION "${rawreel_ffmpeg_dir}/licenses" COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)
    install(FILES "${rawreel_ffmpeg_root}/build-info.txt"
            DESTINATION "${rawreel_ffmpeg_dir}" COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)
    file(STRINGS "${rawreel_ffmpeg_root}/include/libavutil/ffversion.h" line
         REGEX "#define FFMPEG_VERSION \"")
    string(REGEX REPLACE ".*FFMPEG_VERSION \"([^\"]+)\".*" "\\1" rawreel_ffmpeg_version "${line}")
    set(CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION "${DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION}")
    set(FFMPEG_VERSION "${rawreel_ffmpeg_version}")
    set(RAWREEL_VERSION "${PROJECT_VERSION}")
    set(RAWREEL_SOURCES_ARCHIVE "rawreel-${PROJECT_VERSION}-third-party-sources.tar.xz")
    configure_file(packaging/SOURCE.txt.in "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/packaging/SOURCE.txt"
                   @ONLY NEWLINE_STYLE LF)
    install(FILES "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/packaging/SOURCE.txt"
            DESTINATION "${rawreel_ffmpeg_dir}" COMPONENT rawreel CONFIGURATIONS Release)
endif()

set(CPACK_PACKAGE_NAME rawreel)
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VERSION "${PROJECT_VERSION}")
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_FILE_NAME "rawreel-${PROJECT_VERSION}")  # also the top folder; renamed later
set(CPACK_GENERATOR ZIP)
set(CPACK_INCLUDE_TOPLEVEL_DIRECTORY ON)
set(CPACK_INSTALL_CMAKE_PROJECTS "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR};rawreel;rawreel;/")
set(CPACK_ARCHIVE_THREADS 0)
set(CPACK_OUTPUT_CONFIG_FILE "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/rawreel-CPackConfig.cmake")
set(CPACK_SOURCE_OUTPUT_CONFIG_FILE "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/rawreel-CPackSourceConfig.cmake")
include(CPack)

set(rawreel_pdbs "$<TARGET_PDB_FILE:rawreel_gui>;$<TARGET_PDB_FILE:rawreel_cli>")
file(GENERATE OUTPUT "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/packaging-info-$<CONFIG>.cmake" CONTENT
"set(RAWREEL_VERSION \"${PROJECT_VERSION}\")
set(RAWREEL_PLATFORM \"${rawreel_platform}\")
set(RAWREEL_PACKAGE_NAME \"${rawreel_package_name}\")
set(RAWREEL_EXTENSION \"${rawreel_extension}\")
set(RAWREEL_BUNDLED ${DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG})
set(RAWREEL_SHIPPED \"${DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY}\")
set(RAWREEL_FFMPEG_VERSION \"${rawreel_ffmpeg_version}\")
set(RAWREEL_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION \"${DVV_CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION}\")
set(RAWREEL_MIN_VCRT_VERSION \"${RAWREEL_MIN_VCRT_VERSION}\")
set(RAWREEL_CPACK_CONFIG \"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/rawreel-CPackConfig.cmake\")
set(RAWREEL_THIRD_PARTY_STATE \"${RAWREEL_THIRD_PARTY_STATE}\")
set(RAWREEL_PDBS \"${rawreel_pdbs}\")
")
```

`packaging/dry-run.cmake`, the host check and the outer part (binding: the `subst` drive is
removed even when the inner run fails):

```cmake
if(NOT CMAKE_HOST_WIN32)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dry-run: Windows only in v1.0 (the Linux package is v1.x)")
endif()
if(NOT INNER)
    if(NOT DRIVE)
        foreach(letter IN ITEMS R S T U V W Y Z)
            if(NOT IS_DIRECTORY "${letter}:/")
                set(DRIVE "${letter}:")
                break()
            endif()
        endforeach()
    endif()
    if(NOT DRIVE)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dry-run: no free drive letter for subst; pass -DDRIVE=<letter>:")
    endif()
    cmake_path(NATIVE_PATH WORKSPACE_ROOT root_native)
    execute_process(COMMAND subst "${DRIVE}" "${root_native}" RESULT_VARIABLE result)
    if(NOT result EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dry-run: subst ${DRIVE} failed")
    endif()
    set(forward "")
    foreach(name IN ITEMS BUNDLE OWN_PREFIX SOURCES GUI_SMOKE EXTRA_TERMS)
        if(DEFINED ${name})
            list(APPEND forward "-D${name}=${${name}}")
        endif()
    endforeach()
    execute_process(COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -DINNER=ON "-DWORKSPACE_ROOT=${DRIVE}/"
                            "-DREAL_ROOT=${WORKSPACE_ROOT}" ${forward}
                            -P "${DRIVE}/rawreel/packaging/dry-run.cmake"
                    RESULT_VARIABLE inner_result)
    execute_process(COMMAND subst "${DRIVE}" /D)
    if(NOT inner_result EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dry-run: failed (${inner_result})")
    endif()
    return()
endif()
```
(`EXTRA_TERMS` contains `;`: pass it to the child as `"-DEXTRA_TERMS=${EXTRA_TERMS}"` in one
argument, as above. The inner part uses `REAL_ROOT` only to find the developer prefix's state file
for the `OWN_PREFIX`/`BUNDLE` comparison; every path it builds with is below the drive.)

**Tests first** (`tests/packaging/test_packaging_scripts.cmake`, run as
`cmake -DWORK_DIR=<dir> -P …`, CTest `rawreel.packaging_scripts`, label `unit`, every preset). It
includes `../../packaging/check-package.cmake` and `../../packaging/scan-artifact-privacy.cmake`,
builds every tree and file inside `WORK_DIR` (emptied first), prints `PASS <case>` per case and
stops with `FATAL_ERROR` on the first failed assertion. Every source line that holds a deliberate
privacy pattern ends with `# privacy-lint: allow` (the repository lint skips such lines; the
artifact scan never sees the comment). Test paths use the drive letter `X:` and fake names.
1. `manifest: exact tree passes` — tree `rawreel-1.2.3/{a.exe, THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt,
   ffmpeg/x.dll, ffmpeg/SOURCE.txt}`, manifest `ours rawreel-@V@/a.exe`, `notices
   rawreel-@V@/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt`, `ffmpeg rawreel-@V@/ffmpeg/x.dll <sha256 of its content>`,
   `ours-bundled rawreel-@V@/ffmpeg/SOURCE.txt`; `VERSION 1.2.3 BUNDLED ON` → `ERRORS` empty;
   `CLASSES` holds `ours|rawreel-1.2.3/a.exe` and `ffmpeg|rawreel-1.2.3/ffmpeg/x.dll`.
2. `manifest: extra file fails` — add `rawreel-1.2.3/evil.dll` → `ERRORS` contains
   `unexpected file rawreel-1.2.3/evil.dll`.
3. `manifest: missing file fails` — remove `a.exe` → `missing file rawreel-1.2.3/a.exe`.
4. `manifest: changed third-party file fails` — change one byte of `x.dll` →
   `SHA256 mismatch rawreel-1.2.3/ffmpeg/x.dll`.
5. `manifest: not bundled` — `BUNDLED OFF`, tree without `ffmpeg/` → empty; with `ffmpeg/` present →
   `unexpected file rawreel-1.2.3/ffmpeg/x.dll` and `unexpected file rawreel-1.2.3/ffmpeg/SOURCE.txt`.
6. `manifest: version placeholder` — the case-1 tree checked with `VERSION 1.2.4` → `missing file
   rawreel-1.2.4/a.exe` and `unexpected file rawreel-1.2.3/a.exe`.
7. `notices: exact shipped set passes` — a file in `write-notices.cmake`'s format (62-character
   separators) with entries `alpha 1.0` and `beta 2.0` (each with `Licence:` and `Source:` lines
   and a `--- LICENSE ---` block whose text contains a line of `=` signs shorter than 20) and
   `SHIPPED alpha beta` → empty.
8. `notices: missing entry fails` — `SHIPPED alpha beta gamma` → `missing notice gamma`.
9. `notices: extra entry fails` — `SHIPPED alpha` → `unexpected notice beta`.
Cases 10–19 use these literal test strings (each line of this list carries the lint's allow marker,
as the test file's lines must):
- `P1` = `X:\Users\someone\src\a.cpp`, `P2` = `x:/users/someone/a.cpp`, `P3` = `X:\Users\bob\a` <!-- privacy-lint: allow -->
- `H1` = `/home/alice/src/a.cpp`, `R1` = `/home/runner/work/rawreel`, `D1` = `X:\a\rawreel` <!-- privacy-lint: allow -->
- `E1` = `dev@corp.io`, `E2` = `jloup@gzip.org` (a licence text's author address) <!-- privacy-lint: allow -->
- `N1` = `noreply@anthropic.com`, `N2` = `someone@users.noreply.github.com` <!-- privacy-lint: allow -->
- `I1` = `10.1.2.3`, `I2` = `300.1.2.3`, `I3` = `1.2.3`, `I4` = `1.2.3.4.5`, `I5` = `127.0.0.1` <!-- privacy-lint: allow -->
10. `privacy: clean text` — one line `built in R1 and D1, version I4, N1, N2, I5` (`MODE full`) →
    no findings.
11. `privacy: user profile path` — `P1` in one file and `P2` in a second → each one finding of kind
    `Windows user profile path`.
12. `privacy: home path` — `H1` → `home directory path`.
13. `privacy: e-mail` — `contact E1` → `e-mail address that is not a noreply address`.
14. `privacy: IPv4` — `I1` → `IPv4 address`; `I2` and `I3` → none.
15. `privacy: extra term` — `EXTRA_TERMS machinex`, text `built on MACHINEX-07` → `local user or
    host name`; `EXTRA_TERMS ab` (shorter than 3) → none.
16. `privacy: paths mode` — `E1 I1` with `MODE paths` → none; `P3` → one finding.
17. `privacy: notices mode` — a notices file whose licence block contains `E2` and whose generated
    lines are clean → none; the same file with `P3` inside the licence block → one finding; with
    `E1` on an entry's `Source:` line → one finding.
18. `privacy: UTF-16LE binary` — `tests/packaging/data/utf16le-user-path.bin` (the bytes `00 01`
    followed by `P1` in UTF-16LE) with `BINARY` → one `Windows user profile path` finding; without
    `BINARY` → none (the ASCII pass cannot see it).
19. `privacy: findings never repeat the text` — in cases 11–18, no finding item contains
    `someone`, `alice`, `bob`, `corp.io`, the digits of `I1` or `machinex`.
20. `pdb reference` — a file holding the strings `rawreel-gui.pdb` and `msvcrt.dll` → empty; one
    holding `X:\a\rawreel\rawreel-gui.pdb` → `the exe names its PDB with a directory`.
21. `attestation: source commit and ref` — JSON
    `[{"verificationResult":{"signature":{"certificate":{"sourceRepositoryDigest":"<40 × a>",
    "sourceRepositoryRef":"refs/tags/v1.0.0"}}}}]` → `COMMIT` = 40 × `a`, `REF` =
    `refs/tags/v1.0.0`, `ERRORS` empty; `[]` → `attestation output has no sourceRepositoryDigest`;
    a digest of 39 characters → `attestation output has no sourceRepositoryDigest`.
22. `notices: the Visual C++ runtime paragraph` — the case-7 file with the paragraph of Task 1's
    `write-notices.cmake` appended (separator, `Microsoft Visual C++ runtime`, `Files: …`,
    `Licence: …`, `Source: …`): `SHIPPED alpha beta MSVC_RUNTIME` → empty; `SHIPPED alpha beta`
    without `MSVC_RUNTIME` → `unexpected notice Microsoft Visual C++ runtime`; the case-7 file
    (no paragraph) with `MSVC_RUNTIME` → `missing notice Microsoft Visual C++ runtime`.
23. `notices: a long = line inside a licence text is no entry` — the case-7 file where beta's
    licence block contains a line of 62 `=` signs followed by `Copyright 2020 Example` and a text
    line: `SHIPPED alpha beta` → empty (no `unexpected notice Copyright`).
The whole script fails first: `include` cannot find the two modules.

**Steps** (from the workspace root; PowerShell tool on Windows; Linux (clang-format only) through
`wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && …'`; git only in PowerShell,
except the read-only `git diff --name-only` inside the WSL format command; long runs with
`run_in_background: true`; nothing is pushed):
- [ ] **Step 1: Read the inputs.** In the M4 plan file, read Task 0's records listed under
  Consumes and Tasks 1, 4, 7a, 7b's "Changes during execution". Read the notices format:
  `Select-String rawreel-workspace/deps/write-notices.cmake -Pattern '====|Licence|Source|---'`.
  Check the custom_ffmpeg layout in the prefix:
  `Get-ChildItem -Recurse _third_party/msvc/custom_ffmpeg -Include 'avutil-*.dll','build-info.txt','ffversion.h' | ForEach-Object FullName`.
  Record in the task report: the notices line prefixes, `RAWREEL_MIN_VCRT_VERSION`, the
  `OWN_PREFIX` value.
  Check the version (U-M4ORDER: packaging runs only after 10-final):
  `Select-String rawreel/CMakeLists.txt -Pattern '^project\(|_VERSION_STRING'`.
  Expected: `project(rawreel VERSION 1.0.0 …)` and `rawreel_VERSION_STRING` equal to
  `${PROJECT_VERSION}` (no `-alpha` or `-beta` label). Otherwise stop and report: 10-final has not
  run. The package file names use `PROJECT_VERSION`, and `release.yml` accepts only
  `<digits>.<digits>.<digits>`, so v1.0 packages no pre-release version.
- [ ] **Step 2: Check Task 1's state file** (nothing is written here):
  `Get-Content "_third_party/msvc/dvv-third-party-state.cmake" | Select-String '^set\(DVV_(BUNDLE_FFMPEG|SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY|CUSTOM_FFMPEG_VERSION|LAME_SOURCE_SHA256)' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }`.
  Expected: the file exists with `set(DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG ON)`, `custom_ffmpeg` in
  `DVV_SHIPPED_THIRD_PARTY`, version `1.0.0` and a 64-hex LAME SHA256. A missing file means the

  and check again; never write the file from this task.

**Notes:**
- Design references: D3 (shipping, exact library set), D16 (symbols, privacy scan), D17, D19,
  D20 (attestation), D22, D27, §8.6, §9 (`DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG`), R6, R12; v2 arbiter FIX 2, 15, 20,
  21, 28, 31. D18 (the Linux `.tar.xz`) and R11 (the Linux baseline) are v1.x (U-WIN).
- **Note (plan choice):** the VC++ runtime files are not hash-pinned (they come from the build
  machine's Visual Studio, not from a pinned archive); the manifest names them and step 5 checks
  their file version (FIX 31). A different Visual Studio on the runner that installs a different set
  of runtime DLLs fails the manifest loudly; the fix is a manifest update.
- **Note (plan choice):** README and LICENSE ship as the repository files (`README.md`, `LICENSE`);
  Tasks 10-alpha and 10-beta wrote the README's user sections before this task (U-M4ORDER), so
  the zip carries the final text.
- **Note (plan choice):** the release workflow (Task 9) and this dry run call the same
  `make-packages.cmake` and `package-smoke.cmake`, so the rehearsal and the release share one code
  path. Task 9 defined the workflow; it is first used here (Steps 9b and 9c) and first run on
  GitHub in Task 12 (U-M4ORDER).
- **Note (U-M4ORDER):** the packaging code is in no checkpoint before Task 11; Task 11, the final
  checkpoint, runs right after this task and covers it (Release, ASan, clang-tidy, Linux sanity
  build).
- **Note (plan choice):** `EXTRA_TERMS` (user and computer name) join the full-mode rules locally,
  as in the repository privacy lint; CI passes none.
- R6: if a bundled FFmpeg library needs a further DLL from the archive, it is added to the install
  rules and the manifest (`ffmpeg` kind) and recorded.
- **Note (U-WIN):** the sources archive stays in v1.0: the Windows zip bundles the LGPL FFmpeg
  libraries, and `SOURCE.txt` points at the archive as their corresponding source. It is now built
  on Windows (the `ON` dry run and the Windows job of `release.yml`); Risk: a Windows checkout
  writes symbolic links as text files and needs long paths, hence `core.longpaths=true` and the
  symbolic-link list in its README.
- **Note (U-WIN):** the session cut is unchanged (8a = Steps 1–4, 8b = Steps 1 and 5–11); 8b is
  smaller than in plan v2 (no Linux build, manifest, dry run), but the dry runs still take long.
- Risk: CMake may canonicalise the `subst` drive back to the real path in some places; the privacy
  scan of the dry-run exes is the check. A user-profile path found there means the root itself lies
  in a user profile: then run with `-DOWN_PREFIX=ON` and record it.
- `_dryrun/` lies in the workspace folder (not a repository) and stays until Task 12's pre-flight
  step has used its artifacts; it is deleted after Task 12 (or when the user asks).

### Task 11: Final verification ("v1.0.0 release candidate")

**Goal:** Prove every ungated exit criterion (E1–E5) on the final commits: the final checkpoint
(U-PROC: `vs2026` Debug and Release, `vs2026-asan`, clang-tidy on all sources after each
repository's `ninja-clang` build), the milestone's workspace Linux sanity build (U-WIN), the package smoke on the
final exes for the variant to be published, and the E5 manual checklist with the user on the
packaged Windows build. End with the state "v1.0.0 release candidate: every ungated exit
criterion met, dry-run packages verified" in the ledger and in the workspace `CLAUDE.md`.

**Files:**
- Fixes only (any repository), each with a failing test first where the defect can be reproduced
  without real input.
- `rawreel-workspace`: the M4 plan file's `## Changes during execution` ("Task 11").
- `rawreel-workspace/.git/sdd/progress.md` (ledger) and the workspace root `CLAUDE.md` "State"
  section (local files, not in a repository).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything of Tasks 0–9, 10-alpha, 10-beta, 10-final and 8 (and P); Task 8's
  `dry-run.cmake` and `package-smoke.cmake`;
  M3's E6 checklist procedure (M3 plan Task 11a: the files `clip.yuv`, `clip.y4m`,
  `my_yuv420p.json`, the .NET screen capture into the session scratchpad); M3.1's `--selftest perf`,
  when M3.1 has run
  (M3.1 plan Task 11b: the 4K file `perf_3840x2160_yuv420p10le_60.yuv`, three runs, the E7 rule
  `actualFps ≥ 59.5`, `stalls == 0`, `overwritten ≤ 6`); the [U4] state (decides the variant).
- Produces: the E1–E5 evidence, the Linux sanity build result, the "release candidate" state, and
  the local dry-run artifacts that Task 12's pre-flight step 4 re-checks.

**Behaviour:**
- **Variant:** `BUNDLE=ON` unless [U4] was declined (then `OFF`, and the decline text is in the
  README).
- **Linux sanity build** (U-WIN; the milestone's final one): the WSL `ninja-clang` configure +
  build of `rawreel-workspace` (its `ninja-clang` preset builds every repository in-tree, so one
  configure + build covers them all; no tests), next to the per-repository checkpoint builds of
  Step 3. Our portability bugs are fixed (Step 7's fix
  loop); toolchain or third-party failures are recorded and reported, not fixed.
- **E5 checklist** (design v2 §1 E5; on the extracted Windows zip with the local NVIDIA GPU; every
  item is done by the user with real input; the agent prepares files, starts the program, takes a
  screenshot when the user says an item is on screen, shows it, and records the user's verdict;
  the WSLg run and the GTK drag-and-drop item of plan v2 are v1.x, U-WIN):
  1–10. The M3 E6 list on the packaged builds (M3 plan Task 11a items 1–10: drag `clip.yuv` without
     size → format dialog; drop `clip.y4m`; Load JSON… with `my_yuv420p.json`; play/pause, step,
     seek, loop with hotkeys and the transport bar; resize; View → Format… BT.709 → BT.601 and range
     limited → full; matrix Custom Kr 0.212 Kb 0.087; Open URI of the PQ gradient (tone-mapped);
     `--appearance dark`; HiDPI when the display scales).
  11. Drag `clip.mkv` (the FFV1 fixture below) onto the window: opens without the format dialog;
      play, pause, step, seek.
  12. Codec scope (U-FF): (a) drag `clip.avi` (huffyuv in AVI, below): opens without the format
      dialog; play, pause, step, seek; if the user has a real lossless file (e.g. a capture in
      huffyuv, utvideo, magicyuv, FFV1 or v210), the same with it. (b) Drag a lossy file: a real
      H.264 MP4 the user names, else `clip-lossy.mp4` (or `clip-lossy.avi`) below. Expected: the
      open is refused with
      "codec h264 is not supported in v1.0 (lossless codecs only)" (`mpeg4` for the synthetic
      clip); the window, the previous stream and the log stay usable; no crash. The refusal is
      the same for any file of that codec, so the synthetic clip closes the item.
  13. *Open URI…* `lavfi:testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25,format=yuv420p`: plays; step forward and back works
      (backward is slow, R17).
  14. View → Format… on `clip.mkv`: matrix BT.709 → BT.601: a visible colour change; the Info pane
      updates.
  15. View → Format… on the `lavfi:` stream: the infobar/log says "set the colour in the graph
      (setparams=…)"; nothing reopens.
  16. Help → About: versions (1.0.0), MIT licence, the notices file name, the FFmpeg status line
      naming the package's FFmpeg folder.
  17. Windows only, **real TDR** (M3 R14, Y10): while `clip.mkv` plays, the user forces a GPU
      timeout: `dxcap -forcetdr` from an elevated prompt (Windows Graphics Tools; the agent checks
      `Get-Command dxcap -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue` and tells the user if the optional feature
      "Graphics Tools" is missing). Expected: within 5 s the infobar shows the error with Reopen;
      Reopen presents frames again at the kept position (a first Reopen may fail while the driver
      drains; a retry passes, R14). If the user cannot run it, the item is recorded as "real TDR:
      not checked (reason)" and listed for Task 12's pre-flight report. Two known gaps go with this
      check (M3 final review), so it does not rediscover them: (a) `Session::requestFailed`
      (rawreel `src/core/session.cpp`, about l.537) treats a non-`DeviceLost` outer error on a lost
      context as a read error first (two errors, the first without Reopen; fix: also take the
      device-lost path when `stack_.context && stack_.context->lost()`); (b) `waitForTimeline`
      (dvv-gpu `submitter.cpp`, about l.45-52) waits a full 1 s slice per in-flight submission
      before it checks `lost()`, so `~ExecutionContext` takes K seconds with a silent driver.
      Fix (a) in Step 7 when this item shows it; record (b) with the item's result.
  18. **perf re-run** (spec §12), **only if M3.1 has run** (Task 0 recorded "M3.1 run: yes"; the
      user picks the order of M3.1 and M4). Otherwise the item is recorded as "perf re-run: not
      applicable until M3.1" and skipped; it does not fail E5 (the packaged `rawreel-gui --selftest
      perf` would exit 6, "scenario perf is not available"). When M3.1 has run: the 4K
      `yuv420p10le` 600-frame file written with the packaged
      `rawreel-cli dump`, then `--selftest perf` with the packaged `rawreel-gui` three times (M3.1
      Task 11b procedure, validation off); the median by `actualFps`; the E7 rule; compared with
      M3.1's numbers. A miss goes to the arbiter with the named bottleneck (it is not fixed here).
  Then read the stderr files: no validation message (none can appear: packages have no layer) and
  no leak report.
- **Fixtures for 11–12** (scratch folder `$env:TEMP/rawreel-e5`, never in a repository), made with
  the prefix's `ffmpeg` program (found with
  `Get-ChildItem -Recurse _third_party/msvc/custom_ffmpeg -Filter ffmpeg.exe`):
  `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25 -frames:v 100 -c:v ffv1 -pix_fmt yuv420p10le -color_primaries bt709 -color_trc bt709 -colorspace bt709 clip.mkv`
  and `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25 -frames:v 100 -pix_fmt yuv422p -c:v huffyuv clip.avi`;
  only when the user has no lossy file, and only when Task 0 recorded "mpeg4 encoder: yes":
  `ffmpeg -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25 -frames:v 25 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:v mpeg4 clip-lossy.mp4`
  (without that encoder: `clip-lossy.avi`, a copy of `clip.avi` whose `HFYU` FourCCs in the first
  4096 bytes are replaced by `XVID` with Task 0 Step 7's PowerShell loop; it opens as codec
  `mpeg4`, so item 12 (b) always has a lossy input).
- **Exit criteria evidence** (quoted in the report):
  - E1: the `ffmpeg`-labelled dvv-components tests (FFV1/MKV, the lossless AVI/MKV/MOV/NUT and
    the lossless `Sequential` `framemd5` cases, the lossy-codec refusal (U-FF), lavfi,
    the lavfi file-filter refusal, the no-FFmpeg entries; `dvv_components.ffmpeg` reports 28
    doctest cases) and the import checks
    (`dvv_components.no_ffmpeg_imports`, `rawreel.no_ffmpeg_imports` on `rawreel-cli` and
    `rawreel-gui`) passed in Debug, Release and ASan.
  - E2: the `ffmpeg;oracle` tests ran and passed in `vs2026` (not in `vs2026-asan`).
  - E3: the rawreel FFmpeg e2e entries passed in Debug, Release and ASan; `rawreel.gui_selftest.smoke` (with
    the lavfi and About steps), `player` (with the MKV drop), `lost` passed on Windows (NVIDIA);
    the crash e2e left one `.dmp` (Windows, non-ASan).
  - E4: the dry runs of Step 4 (variant to publish) exit 0 with every `package-smoke` step ok; the
    `OFF` variant evidence of Task 8 stays valid if no packaging file changed since (else rerun it).
  - E5: the checklist record.
- **State text** for the ledger and `CLAUDE.md`: "M4: v1.0.0 release candidate: every ungated exit
  criterion met, dry-run packages verified (<date>). Next: Task 12, publication, behind the user's
  Gate A and Gate B."

**Tests first:** no feature. The tests are the final checkpoint runs, the dry runs and the checklist. Every defect
gets a failing unit case, e2e case or selftest step first when it can be reproduced without real
input; then the fix.

**Checkpoint task (U-PROC):** Task 11 is the milestone's final checkpoint and covers everything:
every code repository (Release and ASan, clang-tidy on all sources), the workspace workflows, the
ASan race check of Task P when it ran, the workspace Linux sanity build and the privacy lint on all
eight repositories. Task 12 adds no code and has no checkpoint.

**Steps** (from the workspace root; PowerShell; Linux (the checkpoint builds and the workspace
sanity build only) through `wsl … env.sh`; git only in PowerShell, except the read-only
`git ls-files` inside the WSL tidy commands; long runs in the background; nothing is pushed):
- [ ] **Step 1: Windows Debug, Release and ASan of every code repository and the workspace Linux
  sanity build.** For each `<repo>` of `dvv-base dvv-registry dvv-platform dvv-gpu dvv-media
  dvv-components rawreel` (one background call per repository, one at a time):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|AddressSanitizer'; Pop-Location`
  (the `vs2026` workflow is Debug and Release). A repository whose head is unchanged since Task
  10-final Step 8 may keep Task 10-final Step 7's Debug result (quote it) and run only
  `cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; ctest --preset vs2026-release --output-on-failure` in
  place of the `vs2026` workflow. When Task P ran, its ASan race check ×10 follows its
  repository's ASan workflow:
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location <repo>; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R "<regex>" --repeat until-fail:10 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`.
  In parallel, the workspace Linux sanity build (Behaviour; every repository in-tree; no tests):
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel-workspace && cmake --preset ninja-clang > /dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 20; echo "exit=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"'`
  (expected `exit=0`; a portability bug goes to Step 7, a toolchain or third-party failure is
  recorded).
- [ ] **Step 2: G3 workspace workflow** (background):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; $env:DVV_REQUIRE_FFMPEG='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
  Plus the workspace script tests:
  `Push-Location rawreel-workspace; Get-ChildItem tests -Recurse -Filter 'test_*.cmake' | ForEach-Object { cmake "-DWORK_DIR=$env:TEMP/ws-$($_.BaseName)" -P $_.FullName }; Pop-Location`.
- [ ] **Step 3: Checkpoint lint and privacy lint.** For each C++ `<repo>` of `dvv-base
  dvv-registry dvv-platform dvv-gpu dvv-media dvv-components rawreel` (background, in parallel with
  the Windows runs), the full clang-format and clang-tidy on all sources after the repository's
  `ninja-clang` configure + build with no tests (these builds are part of the Linux sanity build):
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git ls-files -z "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 8 clang-format --dry-run --Werror && git ls-files -z ":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/<repo>/ninja-clang --quiet && echo LINT-OK'`
  (dvv-components with its own clang-tidy file list, Global Constraints). Expected `LINT-OK` each.
  Then
  `foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','dvv-media','dvv-components','rawreel') { cmake "-DREPO=$r" -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake }`.
- [ ] **Step 4: Dry run on the final exes** (`run_in_background: true`; `<variant>` = `ON`, or
  `OFF` after a [U4] decline; `<own>` = the `OWN_PREFIX` value Task 8 recorded; `-DSOURCES=ON`
  only with `ON`; log into the session scratchpad):
  `cmake "-DWORKSPACE_ROOT=$((Resolve-Path .).Path)" -DBUNDLE=<variant> -DOWN_PREFIX=<own> -DSOURCES=ON -DGUI_SMOKE=windows "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel/packaging/dry-run.cmake *> <scratchpad>/dryrun-win.log; "exit=$LASTEXITCODE"`
  When the variant is `ON`, Task 8's `OFF` dry run is repeated only if a packaging file changed
  since Task 8: the same command with `-DBUNDLE=OFF` and without `-DSOURCES=ON`. Expected `exit=0`
  and every `package-smoke` step ok (the sources step with `ON`); record the output SHA256s.
- [ ] **Step 5: Prepare the E5 files** on Windows (`$env:TEMP/rawreel-e5`): extract the Step 4
  zip there; make `clip.yuv`, `clip.y4m` and `my_yuv420p.json` with the extracted `rawreel-cli` (M3
  plan Task 11a Step 6 commands with `$cli` = the extracted exe) and `clip.mkv`, `clip.avi` (and,
  if needed, `clip-lossy.mp4` or `clip-lossy.avi`) with the prefix's `ffmpeg`; check them with the
  extracted `rawreel-cli info` (the lossy clip must give the U-FF refusal text).
- [ ] **Step 6: E5 on Windows with the user.** Ask the user for a real lossy file such as an
  H.264 MP4, and optionally a real lossless file (paths given in the conversation only). Start
  `Start-Process (Join-Path $e5 'rawreel-<v>/rawreel-gui.exe') -RedirectStandardError (Join-Path $e5 'gui.err.txt')`
  and Explorer on the folder; walk through items 1–18 with screenshots (.NET capture of the
  primary screen into the session scratchpad, shown to the user); the perf runs (item 18, only when
  M3.1 has run) follow
  M3.1 plan Task 11b Steps 5–6 with `$cli`/`$gui` = the extracted exes
  (`$env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='off'`).
- [ ] **Step 7: Fixes.** For each defect: failing test first (when automatable), fix, `fix: …`
  commit with the trailer in its repository, then that repository's Step 1 runs (Debug, Release,
  ASan), its Step 3 lint, the workspace Linux sanity build of Step 1 again when the fix touches
  code that Linux compiles, and Step 4 again if a packaged file changed.
- [ ] **Step 8: Record and state.** In the M4 plan file's `## Changes during execution` add "Task
  11: final verification": Steps 1–4 results (per repository and preset, the Linux sanity build
  result with any recorded toolchain or third-party failure), E1–E5 evidence, each E5 item with
  the user's verdict, the perf numbers (GPU model, refresh rate, the three JSON lines, the median,
  the E7 verdict, the comparison with M3.1; or "perf re-run: not applicable until M3.1"), the open
  items (real TDR; E5 item 12 (b) only if the refusal itself failed: a lossy input always exists,
  the user's file, `clip-lossy.mp4` or `clip-lossy.avi`) and the fixes. No
  machine names, user names or paths. Commit (`docs: record the v1.0.0 release candidate checks`,
  rawreel-workspace, with the trailer); privacy lint. Update the ledger and the workspace
  `CLAUDE.md` "State" with the state text (Behaviour). Delete `$env:TEMP/rawreel-e5` and the 4K
  perf folder (when item 18 ran); keep `_dryrun/` for Task 12.

**Expected results:** Debug, Release and `vs2026-asan` green in every repository and in the
workspace workflow (GPU tests run on NVIDIA; FFmpeg and oracle tests run, not skipped; no sanitizer
report; Task P's race check ×10 green when it ran); the Linux sanity build of the workspace passes
(or only recorded toolchain/third-party failures remain); `LINT-OK` for every C++ repository; eight
clean privacy lints; the dry run green; E5 recorded with the user's verdicts; the state "v1.0.0 release
candidate" written.

**Commit:** `docs: record the v1.0.0 release candidate checks` (rawreel-workspace) and any `fix: …`
commits, each with the trailer.

**Notes:**
- Design references: E1–E5, §8.5, §8.6, §8.7, spec §12 (perf), M3 R14, Y10, Y11; v2 arbiter FIX 21,
  26.
- **Note (plan choice):** the E5 GUI items run on the packaged build (the extracted zip), not on
  the build tree, so the checklist also checks the package's own FFmpeg folder and runtime DLLs.
- **Note (U-WIN):** plan v2's WSL matrix, Xvfb leg, WSL dry run, WSLg E5 run and GTK
  drag-and-drop item are dropped (Linux v1.x); the E5 items are renumbered (perf is item 18) and
  the steps after Step 6 move up by one. The workspace sanity build is the milestone's final Linux
  build (the checkpoints built each changed repository before, U-PROC).
- **Note (U-FF, replaces the arbiter decision on item 12):** H.264 is out of scope for v1.0, so
  item 12 no longer checks that a real H.264 file plays. It checks that a lossless AVI plays and
  that a lossy file is refused with the allowlist message. The refusal depends only on the codec
  id, so the synthetic `clip-lossy.mp4` closes the item, and the NVENC clip is no longer needed.
  Without an `mpeg4` encoder the FourCC-patched `clip-lossy.avi` serves (review fix), so item 12
  (b) no longer stays open for want of a lossy file. (Task 12's "12 (b) open: no lossy file" text
  then never applies.)
- **Note (arbiter decision, narrowed by U-PROC):** Step 1 reuses Task 10-final Step 7's Debug
  results for repositories whose head did not change since (U-M4ORDER: Task 8 changes rawreel and
  rawreel-workspace after 10-final, so those two run in full); Release and ASan run for every
  repository, because no task after the Task 9 checkpoint ran them.
- A real-TDR failure that is not the known R14 retry goes to the arbiter before Task 12.

### Task 12: Publication (GATED: [U5] Gate A, Gate B)

**Goal:** Publish rawreel v1.0: publish all repositories
with green CI and a green `release.yml` dry run (Gate A), then tag v1.0.0 and create the release
with the Windows zip, the symbols zip, the sources archive (when bundled), `SHA256SUMS` and
attestations (Gate B). **Nothing in this task pushes, creates, tags, runs a workflow or
publishes before the user's explicit go-ahead for that gate and then for that single step, each
asked at that time.** Ends with "v1.0 published".

**Note (U-DIST1, 2026-10-26):** the Windows zip asset ships no VC++ runtime; its release text names the Microsoft Visual C++ Redistributable (x64) as a prerequisite.

**Files:**
- `rawreel-workspace/cmake/dvv-versions.cmake` (Gate B pins), the spec §17 ([U4], [U5] answers).
- Only on a [U4] decline: the `DVV_BUNDLE_FFMPEG` default `OFF` in its three places
  (rawreel-workspace `deps/CMakeLists.txt` `option(… ON)`, `bootstrap.ps1` `$BundleFfmpeg = 'ON'`,
  `bootstrap.sh` `bundle_ffmpeg=ON`; bootstrap always passes the value, so CI and `release.yml`
  follow only the bootstrap defaults), `rawreel/README.md` and
  `rawreel/docs/release-notes-1.0.0.md` (the decline text of Task 10-beta).
- Fixes found by CI (any repository).
- Ledger, workspace `CLAUDE.md` "State", `decisions.md` (the answers).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11's state and dry-run artifacts; Task 9's `release.yml` (inputs `version`,
  `dry_run`; artifacts `windows`, `release-files`); Task 8's `package-smoke.cmake`;
  Task 10-beta's [U4] texts; the remotes `origin` of rawreel-workspace and dvv-base (exist since M0); the
  pre-push hook `tools/githooks/pre-push` (privacy lint in prepush mode); `gh` authenticated as the
  project's GitHub account.
- Produces: public repositories `BarabashkaD/{rawreel-workspace, dvv-base, dvv-registry,
  dvv-platform, dvv-gpu, dvv-media, dvv-components, rawreel}` with `main` and the `v1.0.0` tags; the
  v1.0.0 release of rawreel.

**Behaviour** (design §10.1, D23, E6; CLAUDE.md "GitHub" and "Privacy"):
- **Gate A question** (asked once, verbatim apart from the bracketed parts; [U4] is added only when
  it is still open):
  > **Gate A: publish the repositories.** May I now:
  > 1. push `main` of `rawreel-workspace` (first: the other repositories' CI uses its workflow) and
  >    of `dvv-base`;
  > 2. create the public repositories `dvv-registry`, `dvv-platform`, `dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media`,
  >    `dvv-components` and `rawreel` under `BarabashkaD` with these descriptions and the topics

  > 3. push fixes to `main` of these repositories while CI is red (each scanned for private data
  >    before the push);
  > 4. run the nightly workspace workflow once, and rawreel's `release.yml` as a dry run
  >    (`dry_run=true`: no tags, no release, no attestations)?
  > I will still ask you right before each of these writes (each push, repository creation,
  > workflow run). Tags and the release are not included; I will ask again for them (Gate
  > B).
  >
  > [**[U4] FFmpeg in the packages** (needed now): the packages carry the six custom_ffmpeg LGPL
  > libraries (with decoders for patent-encumbered codecs such as H.264/HEVC/AAC, although rawreel
  > opens only lossless codecs; the README says
  > patent licensing is the user's and the distributor's matter), and the release carries
  > `rawreel-1.0.0-third-party-sources.tar.xz` (custom_ffmpeg with submodules and build scripts,

  > FFmpeg sources work out of the box) or **decline** (the packages ship without FFmpeg and without

  approve the plan of writes. Every push, tag, repository create, topic change,
  workflow run and release create or delete below is still preceded by its own `STOP: ask the

  the changed `bootstrap.sh` default is a Linux-only build file; this run only refreshes the clang

  (`exit=0`, no `ffmpeg/` folder, no sources archive), then continue with Gate A's steps.
  After a decline, Step 8b also checks that the CI-built `package-info-windows.cmake` contains
  `set(RAWREEL_BUNDLED OFF)`, that the extracted zip has no `ffmpeg/` folder, and that

- **Privacy before every push** (CLAUDE.md): the commits being pushed are scanned (the pre-push
  hook does it automatically; run it explicitly too) and the identity check of pre-flight 2 holds.

  local Qt prototype folder outside the workspace.

**Tests first:** none (no code). The checks are the pre-flight, CI and the release asset
verification.

**Steps** (from the workspace root; PowerShell; `gh` and git from PowerShell only):
- [ ] **Step 1 (pre-flight, local, no network writes): `main` = `m4` head.** For each repository:

  `git -C <r> branch -f main m4` only when `git -C <r> merge-base --is-ancestor main m4` succeeds or
  `main` does not exist (the chain m1b → m2 → m3 → m4 is linear); otherwise stop and report.
  `git -C <r> switch main`.

  `foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','dvv-media','dvv-components','rawreel') { cmake "-DREPO=$r" -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake; git -C $r log --all --format='%an|%ae|%cn|%ce' | Sort-Object -Unique }`.
  Expected: eight `clean (commits)` lines; the identities are only the GitHub handle with its noreply
  address (and `GitHub|noreply@github.com` as committer where applicable).
- [ ] **Step 3 (pre-flight): repository contents.** Each repository has `LICENSE` (MIT), `README.md`,
